kaḷaviyal

92. iṉpamum poruḷu maṟaṉu meṉṟāṅ
kaṉpoṭu puṇarnta vaintiṇai maruṅkiṟ
kāmak kūṭṭaṅ kāṇuṅ kālai
maṟaiyōr tēettu maṉṟa leṭṭaṉuṭ
ṭuṟaiyamai nalyāḻt tuṇaimaiyō riyalpē.
iv ōttuk kaḷavu kaṟpeṉṉuṅ kaikōḷ iraṇṭiṉuṅ kaḷavu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṟ kaḷaviyaleṉṉum peyarttāyiṟṟu: piṟarkkuritteṉṟu irumutu kuravarāṟ koṭaiyetirnta talaiviyai avarkoṭuppak koḷḷātu iruvaruṅ karanta uḷḷattōṭu etirppaṭṭup puṇarnta kaḷavātaliṉ itu piṟarkkuriya poruḷai maṟaiyiṟkoḷḷuṅ kaḷavaṉṟāyiṟṟu. itu vētattai maṟainūleṉṟāṟ pōlakkoḷka. “kaḷaveṉap paṭuva tiyāteṉa viṉaviṉ – vaḷaikeḻu muṉkai vaḷaṅkeḻu kūntaṉ – muḷaiyeyiṟ ṟamarnakai maṭanal lōḷoṭu – taḷaiyaviḻ taṇṭārk kāma ṉaṉṉōṉ – viḷaiyāṭ ṭiṭameṉa vēṟumalaic cāraṉ – māṉiṉaṅ kuruviyoṭu kaṭintu viḷaiyāṭu – māyamun tōḻiyu maruvinaṉ kaṟiyā – māyap puṇarcci yeṉmaṉār pulavar.” ikkaḷavaik “kāmappuṇarcciyum” eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya nāṉkuvakaiyālum mēṟkūṟumāṟu uṇarka. iṉpattiṟkup potuvilakkaṇam akattiṇaiyiyaluṭkūṟi ataṟkiṉamākiya poruḷum aṟaṉuṅkūṟum puṟattiṇaiyai ataṉpuṟattu nikaḻtaliṟ puṟattiṇaiyiyaluṭ kūṟi īṇṭu avviṉpattiṉai virittuc ciṟappilakkaṇaṅ kūṟutaliṉ itu akattiṇaiyiyalōṭu iyaipuṭaittāyiṟṟu. vaḻakkunāṭi eṉṟaliṉ itu ulakiyaleṉappaṭum, ulakattu maṉṟalāvatu kuravar koṭuppataṟkumuṉṉar oruvaṟkum oruttikkuṅ kaṇṇum maṉamun tammuḷ iyaivatēyeṉa vētamuṅ kūṟiṟṟātaliṉ. iccūttiraṅ kaḷaveṉappaṭṭa oḻukkam ulakattup poruḷ palavaṟṟuḷḷum iṉpampaṟṟit tōṉṟumeṉavum aḵtu iṉṉatāmeṉavuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉum eṉṟāṅku = iṉpamum poruḷum aṟaṉumeṉṟu muṟkūṟiya mūvakaipporuḷkaḷuḷ: aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇai maruṅkiṉ = oruvaṉōṭu oruttiyiṭait tōṉṟiya aṉpōṭu kūṭiya iṉpattiṉ pakutiyākiya puṇartaṉ mutaliya aivakai oḻukkattiṉuḷ: kāmak kūṭṭaṅ kāṇuṅ kālai = puṇartalum puṇartaṉimittamumeṉappaṭṭa kāmappuṇarcciyai ārāyuṅkālattu: maṟaiyōr tēettu maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ = vētam ōriṭattukkūṟiya maṇameṭṭaṉuḷ: tuṟaiamai nal yāḻttuṇaimaiyōr iyalpu = tuṟai amainta nalyāḻiṉaiyuṭaiya piriviṉmaiyōratu taṉmai.   e-ṟu.
aṉpāvatu “aṭumaran tuñcutō ḷāṭavaru māynta – paṭumaṇip paimpū ṇavarun – taṭumāṟik – kaṇṇetir nōkkotta kārikaiyiṟ kaikalan – tuṇṇekiḻac cērvatā maṉpu.” maṉṟal eṭṭāvaṉa:  piramam, pirācāpattiyam, āriṭam, teyvam, kāntaruvam, acuram, irākkatam, paicācam eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ piramamāvatu:  otta kōttirattāṉāy nāṟpatteṭṭiyāṇṭu piramacariyaṅ kāttavaṉukkup paṉṉīrāṭṭaip paruvattāḷāyp pūppu eytiyavaḷaip peyarttu iraṇṭām pūppu eytāmai aṇikalaṉ aṇintu tāṉamākak koṭuppatu. “kayalē ramaruṇkaṭ kaṉṉipūp peyti – yayalpē raṇikalaṉkaḷ cērtti – yiyali – ṉiralotta vantaṇaṟku nīriṟ koṭuttal – piramamaṇa meṉṉum peyarttu.” pirācāpattiyamāvatu:  makaṭkōṭaṟku uriya kōttirattār koṭutta paricattu iraṭṭi tammakaṭku īntu koṭuppatu. “arimata ruṇka ṇāyiḻai yeytutaṟ – kuriyavaṉ koṭutta voṇporu ḷiraṭṭi – tiriviṉ ṟantai tiṇṇitiṟ cērtti – yariyataṉ kiḷaiyō ṭamaivarak koṭuttal – pirita lillāp pirācā pattiyam.” āriṭamāvatu:  takkāṉ oruvaṟku āvum āṉēṟum poṟkōṭṭup poṟkuḷampiṉavākac ceytu avaṟṟiṭai niṟīip poṉ aṇintu nīrum ivaipōṟ polintu vāḻvīreṉa nīriṟkoṭuppatu. “taṉakkotta voṇporu ṭaṉmakaḷaic cērtti – maṉaikkotta māṇpuṭaiyāṟ pēṇi – yiṉakkotta – vīriṭat tāvai niṟīiyiṉi tīvatē – yāriṭattār kaṇṭamaṇa mām.” teyvamāvatu:  peruvēḷvi vēṭpikkiṉṟār palaruḷ oruvaṟku avvēḷvittīmuṉṉart takkaṇaiyākak koṭuppatu. “nīḷi neṭunakar neypeytu pāritta – vēḷvi viḷaṅkaḻaṉ muṉṉiṟīik – kēḷviyāṟ – kaivaittām pūṇāḷaik kāmuṟṟāṟ kīvatē – teyva maṇattār tiṟam.” acuramāvatu:  kollēṟukōṭal tiripaṉṟiyeytal villēṟṟutal mutaliyaṉa ceytu kōṭal. “mukaiyaviḻ kōtaiyai muḷḷeyiṟ ṟarivaiyait – takainalaṅ karutun tarukkiṉi ruḷareṉi – ṉivaiyivai ceytāṟ keḷiyaṇmaṟ ṟivaḷeṉat – tokainilai yuraitta piṉṟaip pakaivalit – taṉṉavai yāṟṟiya vaḷavaiyiṟ – ṟoṉṉilai yacuran tuṇinta vāṟē.” irākkatamāvatu:  talaimakaṭaṉṉilun tamarilum peṟātu valitiṟ koḷvatu. “malipoṟpaim pūṇāḷai māluṟṟa maintar – valitiṟkoṇ ṭāḷvatē yeṉpa – valitiṟ – parākkatañ ceytuḻalum pāḻi nimirtō – ḷirākkatattār maṉṟa liyalpu.” paicācamāvatu:  mūttōr kaḷittōr tuyiṉṟōr puṇarcciyum iḻintōḷai maṇañceytalum āṭaimāṟutalum piṟavumām. “eccārk keḷiya riyainta kāvalar – poccāp peytiya poḻutuko ḷamaiyattu – meyccār peytiya mikupukaḻ naṇpi – ṉucāvārk kutavāk kēṇmaip – picācar pēṇiya perumaicā liyalpē.” “iṭaimayakkañ ceyyā viyalpiṉi ṉīṅki – yuṭaimayakki yuṭkaṟutta leṉpa – vuṭaiya – tucāvārk kutavāta vūṉilā yākkaip – picācattār kaṇṭamaṇap pēṟu.” iṉik kantaruvamāvatu: kantaruva kumāraruṅ kaṉṉiyarun tammuḷ etirppaṭṭuk kaṇṭu iyaintatupōlat talaivaṉun talaiviyum etirppaṭṭup puṇarvatu. “atirppilpaim pūṇāru māṭavarun tammu – ḷetirppaṭṭuk kaṇṭiyaita leṉpa – katirppoṉyāḻ – muntiruvar kaṇṭa muṉivaṟu taṇkāṭcik – kantaruvar kaṇṭa kalappu.” eṉa ivaṟṟāṉuṇarka.
kaḷavoḻukkam potuvākaliṉ nāṉkuvaruṇattārkkum āyarmutaliyōrkkum urittu. mālaicūṭṭutalum itaṉpāṟpaṭum. villēṟṟutaṉ mutaliyaṉa perumpāṉmai aracarkkurittu. avaṟṟuḷ ēṟutaḻuvutal āyarkkē ciṟantatu. irākkatam antaṇaroḻintōrkku urittu; valitiṟ paṟṟip puṇartaliṉ aracarkku itu peruvaraviṟṟaṉṟu. pēy iḻintōrkkē urittu. kantaruvariṉ makkaḷ ciṟitu tiripuṭaimaiyiṟ cēṭpaṭai mutaliyaṉa uḷavāmeṉṟuṇarka. aṟattiṉāṟ poruḷākki apporuḷāṉ iṉpanukartaṟ ciṟappāṉum ataṉāṉ illaṟaṅ kūṟalāṉum iṉpam muṟkūṟiṉār. aṟaṉum iṉpamum poruḷāṟ peṟappaṭutaliṉ ataṉai iṭaivaittār. pōkamum vīṭumeṉa iraṇṭuñ ciṟattaliṟ pōkam īṇṭukkūṟi vīṭupeṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam muṟkūṟiṉār. oḻinta maṇaṅ kaikkiḷaiyum peruntiṇaiyumāy aṭaṅkutaliṉ itaṉai aṉpoṭeṉṟār. poruḷāṟ koḷḷum maṇamum iruvarcuṟṟamum iyaintuḻit tāmum iyaitaliṟ kantaruvappāṟpaṭum. aintiṇaip puṟattavākiya veṭcimutaliyavaṟṟiṟkum aṉpoṭu puṇartaluṅ koḷḷappaṭum. “aṟattiṟkē yaṉpucār peṉpa vaṟiyār – maṟattiṟku maḵtē tuṇai.” eṉṟaliṉ. kantaruvarkkuk kaṟpiṉṟi amaiyavum peṟum. īṇṭuk kaṟpiṉṟik kaḷavē amaiyāteṉṟaṟkut tuṟaiyamai eṉṟār.
93. oṉṟē vēṟē yeṉṟiru pālvayi
ṉoṉṟi yuyarnta pāla tāṇaiyi
ṉotta kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyuṅ kāṇpa
mikkō ṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭivarai yiṉṟē.
itu muṟkūṟiya kāmakkūṭṭattiṟku uriya kiḻavaṉuṅ kiḻattiyum etirppaṭum nilaṉum avvetirppāṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇamum aṅṅaṉam etirppaṭutaṟku uriyōr peṟṟiyuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟu iru pāl vayiṉ = iruvarkkum ōriṭamum vēṟṟiṭamum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa iruvakai nilattiṉ kaṇṇum: oṉṟi uyarnta pālatu āṇaiyiṉ = ummaikkālattu ellāppiṟappiṉum iṉṟiyamaiyātu uyiroṉṟi orukālaikkorukāl aṉpumutaliyaṉa ciṟattaṟku ētuvākiya pālvarai teyvattiṉ āṇaiyālē: otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇpa = piṟappu mutaliyaṉa pattum otta talaivaṉun talaiviyum etirppaṭupa: mikkōṉ āyiṉum kaṭivarai iṉṟē = aṅṅaṉam evvāṟṟāṉum ovvātu talaivaṉ uyarntōṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭiyappaṭā.  e-ṟu. eṉṟirupālvayiṟkāṇpa eṉap pāl vaṉpāl meṉpālpōla niṉṟatu. uyarntapālai nōytīrnta maruntupōṟ koḷka. orunilam ātalai muṟkūṟiṉār, ivvoḻukkattiṟku ōtiyatu kuṟiñcinilamoṉṟumē ātaṟciṟappu nōkki. vēṟunilam ātalaip piṟkūṟiṉār, kuṟiñcitaṉṉuḷḷum iruvarkkum malaiyum ūrum vēṟātalumaṉṟit tiṇaimayakkattāṉ marutam neytaleṉṉum nilappakutiyuḷ orutti aritiṉ nīṅkivantu etirppaṭutal uḷavātalumeṉa vēṟupaṭṭa pakuti palavum uṭaṉkōṭaṟku. orunilattuk kāmappuṇarccip paruvattāḷāyiṉāḷai āyattiṉ nīṅkit taṉittu ōriṭattu eḷitiṟ kāṇṭal ariteṉṟaṟkup pālatāṇaiyiṟkāṇpa eṉṟār. eṉavē, vēṟṟunilattiṟkāyiṉ vēṭṭai mēliṭṭut tirivāṉ aṅṅaṉan taṉittuk kāṇuṅkāṭci arumaiyāṟ pālatāṇai vēṇṭumāyiṟṟu. um. “ivaṉiva ḷaimpāl paṟṟavu mavaḷivaṉ – puṉṟalai yōri vāṅkunaḷ pariyavuṅ – kātaṟ ceviliyar tavirppavun tavirā – tētil ciṟuceru vuṟupa maṉṉē – nallaimaṉ ṟamma pālē melliyaṟ – ṟuṇaimalarp piṇaiya laṉṉavivar – maṇamaki ḻiyaṟkai kāṭṭi yōyē.” itu ōrūr eṉṟatām. “kāmañ ceppātu kaṇṭatu moḻimō” eṉṟatu eṉ nilattu vaṇṭātaliṉ eṉakkākak kūṟātē col eṉṟaliṟ kuṟiñcinilam oṉṟāyiṟṟu. “ilaṅku maruvit tilaṅku maruvittu – vāṉi ṉilaṅku maruvittē tāṉuṟṟa – cūḷpēṇāṉ poyttāṉ malai.” eṉpuḻip poyttavaṉ malaiyum ilaṅkum aruvitteṉa viyantukūṟit tamatu malaikku naṉṟi iyalpeṉṟaliṟ kuṟiñciyuḷḷum malai vēṟāyiṟṟu. “cevviral civappūrac cēṭceṉṟā yeṉṟavaṉ – pauvanīrc cāykkoḻutip pāvaitan taṉaittaṟkō – kauvainō yuṟṟavar kāṇātu kaṭuttacol – lovvāteṉ ṟuṇarāynī yorunilaiyē yuraittatai.” itu marutattut talaivi kaḷavoḻukkaṅ kūṟuvāḷ pauvanīrc cāyppāvai tantāṉ oruvaṉeṉa neytaṉilattu etirppaṭṭamai kūṟiyatu. āṇaiviti kaikōḷiṉ mutaṟkaṭ kūṟutaliṟ kaṟpiṉkāṟum oṉṟum vēṟuñ cellum pālatu. āṇaiyum avvāṟām.
mikutalāvatu:  kulaṅ kalvi pirāyam mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ mikutal. eṉavē, antaṇar aracarmutaliya varuṇattup peṇkōṭaṟkaṇ uyartalum aracarmutaliyōrum ammuṟai uyartaluṅ koḷka. itaṉāṉē antaṇar mutaliyōr aṅṅaṉam peṇkōṭaṟkaṭ piṟantōrkkum ivvoḻukkam uritteṉṟu koḷka. kaṭi, mikuti. avar aṅṅaṉaṅ kōṭaṟkaṇ ottamakaḷir peṟṟa putalvarōṭu oḻinta makaḷir peṟṟa putalvar ovvāreṉpatu uṇarttaṟkup peritum varaiyappaṭāteṉṟār. patiṉāṟutoṭaṅki irupattunāṉku īṟākakkiṭanta yāṇṭoṉpatum oru peṇkōṭaṟku mūṉṟiyāṇṭāka antaṇaṉ uyaruṅ kantaruvamaṇattu; oḻintōrāyiṉ attuṇai uyarār. irupattunāṉkiraṭṭi nāṟpatteṭṭātal piramamutaliyavaṟṟāṉ uṇarka. valleḻuttu mikutal eṉṟāṟpōla mikutalaik koḷḷavē pirāyam iraṭṭiyāyiṟṟu. kiḻatti mikutal aṟakkaḻivām. kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti eṉavē palapiṟappiṉum oruvarkku oruvar urimai eytiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam orumai kūṟiṟṟēṉum “orupāṟkiḷavi” eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉ nālvakai nilattu nāṉkuvaruṇattōrkaṇṇum āyar vēṭṭuvar mutaliyōrkaṇṇuṅkoḷka. iccūttiram “muṉṉaiya nāṉkum” eṉakkūṟiya kāṭcikku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṟṟeṉṟuṇarka. um. “karuntaṭaṅkaṇ vaṇṭākac cevvāy taḷirā – varumpivar meṉmulai tottāp – perumpaṇaittōṭ – peṇṭakaip polinta pūṅkoṭi – kaṇṭēṅ kāṇṭaluṅ kaḷittaveṅ kaṇṇē.” ikkāṭcikkaṭ talaivaṉaippōṟ talaivi viyantu kūṟutal pulaṉeṟivaḻakkaṉmai uṇarka. (2)
94. ciṟantuḻi yaiyañ ciṟanta teṉpa
viḻintuḻi yiḻipē cuṭṭa lāṉa.
iḵtu eytātateytuvittu eytiyatu vilakkiṟṟu, muṉṉaiya nāṉkum eṉṟataṉāṟ kūṟiya aiyan talaivaṉkaṇṇatē eṉavun talaivikku nikaḻumō eṉṉum aiyattai vilakkutaluṅ kūṟaliṉ. (i-ḷ.) ciṟantuḻi aiyam ciṟantatu eṉpa = aṅṅaṉam etirppāṭṭiṉ iruvaruḷḷuñ ciṟanta talaivaṉkaṇṇē aiyam nikaḻtal ciṟantateṉṟu kūṟuvār āciriyar: iḻintuḻi iḻipē cuṭṭalāṉa = attalaivaṉiṉ iḻinta talaivikkaṇ aiyam nikaḻumāyiṉ iṉpattiṟku iḻivē avaḷkarutum ātalāṉ.  e-ṟu. talaivaṟkut teyvamō allaḷōveṉa nikaḻnta aiyam nūṉmutaliyavaṟṟāl nīkkit teyvamaṉmai uṇartaṟku aṟivuṭaiyaṉātalun, talaivikku murukaṉō iyakkaṉō makaṉōveṉa aiyam nikaḻiṉ ataṉai nīkki uṇartaṟkuk karuviyilaḷ ākalāṉum iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉār. talaivikku aiyam nikaḻiṉ accamēyaṉṟik kāmakkuṟippu nikaḻātām. makaṭūuviṉ āṭūucciṟattal paṟṟic ciṟantuḻi eṉṟār. um. “aṇaṅkuko lāymayil kollō kaṉaṅkuḻai – mātarkoṉ mālumeṉ ṉeñcu.” eṉavarum. (3)
94. vaṇṭē yiḻaiyē vaḷḷi pūvē
kaṇṇē yalamara limaippē yaccameṉ
ṟaṉṉavai piṟavu māṅkava ṇikaḻa
niṉṟavai kaḷaiyuṅ karuvi yeṉpa.
iḵtu aiyuṟṟut teḷiyuṅkāl iṭaiyatu ārāycciyātaliṉ ārāyuṅ karuvi kūṟukiṉṟatu. vaṇṭu mutaliyaṉa vāṉakattaṉavaṉṟi maṇṇakattaṉavātal nūṟkēḷviyāṉum uyttuṇarcciyāṉum talaimakkaḷ uṇarpa. (i-ḷ.) vaṇṭē = payiṉṟataṉ mēlallatu cellāta tātu ūtum vaṇṭu: iḻaiyē = oruvarāl iḻaikkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ: vaḷḷi = mulaiyiṉun tōḷiṉum eḻutun toyyiṟkoṭi: pūvē = kaikkoṇṭu mōntu uyirkkuṅ kaḻunīrppū: kaṇṇē = vāṉkaṇṇallāta ūṉkaṇ: alamaral = kaṇṭu aṟiyāta vaṭivukaṇṭa accattāṟ piṟanta taṭumāṟṟam: imaippē = akkaṇṇiṉ itaḻ imaittal: accam = āṇmakaṉaik kaṇṭuḻi maṉattiṟ piṟakkum accam: eṉṟu aṉṉavai piṟavum = eṉṟu avveṇvakaipporuḷum avai pōlvaṉa piṟavum: avaṇ nikaḻa niṉṟavai = avvetirppāṭṭiṉkaṭ tāṉ muṉpu kaṇṭa varaiyaramakaḷ mutaliya piḻampukaḷāy īṇṭut taṉmaṉattu nikaḻaniṉṟa appiḻampukaḷai: āṅkuk kaḷaiyum karuvi eṉpa = muntunūṟkaṇṇē avvaiyam nīkkuṅ karuviyāmeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar.  e-ṟu. eṉavē, eṉakkum atu karutteṉṟār. ivaiyellām makkaṭkuriyaṉavāy nikaḻavē teyvappakutimēṟ ceṉṟa aiyam nīṅkit tuṇiyum uḷḷam piṟattaliṟ tuṇivum uṭaṉkūṟiṟṟēyāyiṟṟu. iṉi aṉṉapiṟa āvaṉa kāl nilantōytalum niḻalīṭum viyarttalum mutaliyaṉa. “tirunutal vērarumpun tēṅkōtai vāṭu – mirunilañ cēvaṭiyun tōyu – mariparanta – pōkita ḻuṇkaṇu mimaikku – māku maṟṟava ḷakaliṭat taṇaṅkē.” eṉavarum. ikkāṭci mutaliya nāṉkum akaṉaintiṇaikkuc ciṟappuṭaimaiyum ivai kaikkiḷaiyāmāṟum “muṉṉaiyanāṉkum” eṉpuḻik kūṟiṉām. iṅṅaṉam aiyantīrntuḻit talaiviyai viyantu kūṟutaluṅ koḷka. (4)
96. nāṭṭa miraṇṭu maṟivuṭam paṭuttaṟkuk
kūṭṭi yuraikkuṅ kuṟippurai yākum.
iḵtu aṅṅaṉam makkaḷuḷḷāḷeṉat tuṇintuniṉṟa talaivaṉ piṉṉarp puṇarcci vēṭkai nikaḻntuḻit talaiviyaik kūṭaṟkuk karuti urainikaḻttuṅkāṟ kūṟṟumoḻiyāṉ aṉṟik kaṇṇāṉ urainikaḻttumeṉpatūum atu kaṇṭu talaiviyum akkaṇṇāṉē taṉatu vēṭkai pulappaṭuttik kūṟumeṉpatūuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu; eṉavē, itu puṇarcci nimittamākiya vaḻinilaikkāṭci kūṟukiṉṟatāyiṟṟu. (i-ḷ.) aṟivu = talaivaṉ aṅṅaṉam makkaḷuḷḷāḷeṉṟu aṟinta aṟivāṉē: uṭampaṭuttaṟku = talaiviyaik kūṭṭattiṟku uṭampaṭuttaṟku: nāṭṭam iraṇṭum kūṭṭi uraikkum = taṉṉuṭaiya nōkkam iraṇṭāṉuṅ kūṭṭi vārttaicollum: kuṟippurai nāṭṭam iraṇṭum ākum = avvēṭkai kaṇṭu talaivi taṉatu vēṭkai pulappaṭuttik kūṟuṅ kūṟṟun taṉṉuṭaiya nōkkam iraṇṭāṉumām.  e-ṟu. nāṭṭam iraṇṭiṭattuṅ kūṭṭuka. ummai virikka. iṅṅaṉam itaṟkup poruḷkūṟal āciriyarkkuk karuttātal “pukumukam purital” eṉṉum meyppāṭṭiyaṟ cūttirattāṉuṇarka. ataṟkup pērāciriyar kūṟiya uraiyāṉumuṇarka. oṉṟu oṉṟai ūṉṟi nōkkutaliṉ nāṭṭameṉṟār. nāṭṭutalum nāṭṭamum okkum. um. “nōkkiṉā ṇōkketir nōkkuta ṟākkaṇaṅku – tāṉaikkoṇ ṭaṉṉa tuṭaittu.” eṉavarum. itu pukumukam purital eṉṉum meyppāṭu kūṟiyatu. (5)
97. kuṟippē kuṟittatu koḷḷu māyi
ṉāṅkavai nikaḻu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu puṇarcciyamaiti kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) kuṟittatu = talaivaṉ kuṟitta puṇarcci vēṭkaiyaiyē: kuṟippuk koḷḷumāyiṉ = talaivi karuttut tirivupaṭāmaṟ koḷḷavaṟṟāyiṉ: āṅku = akkuṟippaik koṇṭa kālattu: avai nikaḻum eṉmaṉār pulavar = pukumukam puritaṉ mutalāy iruvakaiyumeṭuttal īṟākakkiṭanta meyppāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭaṉuṭ poṟinutal veyarttal mutaliya patiṉoṉṟum muṟaiyē nikaḻumeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar.  e-ṟu. aṅkavaiyum pāṭam. paṉṉiraṇṭām meyppāṭākiya iruvakaiyumeṭuttal kūṟavē muyakkamum uyttuṇarak kūṟiyavāṟukāṇka. ammeyppāṭṭiyaluṭ kūṟiya mūṉṟu cūttirattaiyum īṇṭuk kūṟiyuṇarka. um. “kaṇṇoṭu kaṇṇiṇai nōkkokkiṉ vāyccoṟkoṇ – ṭeṉṉa payaṉu mila.” “kaṇkaḷavu koḷḷuñ ciṟunōkkaṅ kāmattiṟ – cempāka maṉṟu peritu.” eṉavarum. itaṉai nāṉkuvaruṇam oḻintōrkkuṅ koḷka. um. “pāṉalan taṇkaḻip pāṭaṟintu taṉṉaimār – nūṉala nuṇvalaiyā ṉoṇṭeṭutta – kāṉaṟ – paṭupulāl kāppāḷ paṭaineṭuṅka ṇōkkaṅ – kaṭipollā veṉṉaiyē kāppu.” eṉavarum. iṉi muyaṅki makiḻntu kūṟuvaṉa. “kōṭa letirmukaip pacuvī mullai – nāṟitaḻk kuvaḷaiyō ṭiṭaippaṭa viraii – yaitutoṭai māṇṭa kōtai pōla – naṟiya nallōṇ mēṉi – muṟiyiṉum vāyatu muyaṅkaṟku miṉitē.” “tammi liruntu tamtupāt tuṇṭaṟṟā – lammā varivai muyakku.” eṉavarum. uraiyiṟ kōṭalāṉ moḻikēṭka virumputaluṅ kūṭṭiya teyvattai viyantu kūṟutalum vantuḻikkāṇka.
98. perumaiyu muraṉu māṭūu mēṉa.
ittuṇai meyyuṟupuṇarccikku uriyaṉavē kūṟi iṉi uḷḷappuṇarcciyē nikaḻntu viṭum pakkamum uṇṭeṉpatūum ivviruvakaip puṇarccippiṉṉark kaḷaviṉṟi varaintukōṭal kaṭitil nikaḻtaluṇṭeṉpatūum uṇarttukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) perumaiyum = aṟivum āṟṟalum pukaḻuṅ koṭaiyum ārāytalum paṇpum naṇpum paḻipāvamañcutalum mutaliyaṉavāy mēṟpaṭum perumaippakutiyum: uraṉum = kaṭaippiṭiyum niṟaiyuṅ kalaṅkātu tuṇitalum mutaliya valiyiṉ pakutiyum: āṭūu mēṉa = talaivaṉkaṇṇatu.  e-ṟu. itaṉāṉē uḷḷappuṇarcciyē nikaḻntu varaintukoḷḷum ulakavaḻakkum meyyuṟupuṇarcci nikaḻntuḻiyuṅ kaḷavu nīṭṭiyātu varaintukōṭalum uḷḷañ ceṉṟuḻiyellām nekiḻntōṭātu ārāyntu oṉṟu ceytalum melinta uḷḷattāṉāyun tōṉṟāmaṉ maṟaittalun tīviṉaiyāṟṟiya pakutiyiṟ ceṉṟa uḷḷam mīṭṭalun talaivaṟku uriyaveṉṟu koḷka. u-m. “ceṉṟa viṭattāṟ celaviṭā tītorīi – naṉṟiṉ pāluyppa taṟivu.” eṉavarum. perumai nimittamāka uraṉ piṟakkum. avvuraṉāṉ meyyuṟupuṇarcci ilaṉātalum uriyaṉeṉa ituvum ōr viti kūṟiṟṟu. talaivikku meyyuṟupuṇarcci naṭakkum vēṭkai nikaḻāmaikkuk kāraṇam mēṟkūṟupa. iṉi iyaṟkaippuṇarcci iṭaiyīṭu paṭṭuḻi iṭantalaippāṭṭiṉkaṇ vēṭkai taṇiyātu niṉṟu kūṟupaveṉṟum āṇṭu iṭaiyīṭu paṭṭuḻip pāṅkaṉāṟ kūṭupaveṉṟum uraippōrum uḷar; avar aṟiyārāyiṉār; eṉṉai? avviraṇṭiṭattum iyaṟkaip puṇarccikku uriyameyppāṭukaḷ nikaḻntē kūṭavēṇṭutaliṉ avaṟṟaiyum iyaṟkaippuṇarcciyeṉap peyarkūṟalaṉṟik kāmappuṇarcciyum iṭantalaippāṭum pāṅkoṭutaḻāalum eṉa āciriyar vēṟuvēṟupeyar kūṟāreṉṟuṇarka. (7)
99. accamu nāṇu maṭaṉumun tuṟutta
niccamum peṇpāṟ kuriya veṉpa.
itu mēlatē pōlvatōr vitiyai uḷḷappuṇarcci paṟṟit talaivikkuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) accamum = aṉpu kāraṇattiṟ tōṉṟiya uṭkum: nāṇum = kāmakkuṟippu nikaḻntavaḻip paṭuvatōr uḷḷavoṭukkamum: maṭaṉum = ceviliyar koḷuttakkoṇṭu koṇṭatuviṭāmaiyum: muntuṟutta immūṉṟum mutaliyaṉa: niccamum peṇpāṟku uriya eṉpa = eññāṉṟum peṇpālārkku uriyaveṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar.  e-ṟu. muntuṟutta eṉṟataṉāṟ kaṇṭaṟiyātaṉa kaṇṭuḻi maṉaṅ koḷḷāta payirppum, ceyattakuvatu aṟiyāta pētaimaiyum, niṟuppataṟku neñcuṇṭām niṟaiyuṅ koḷka. maṭaṉ kuṭippiṟantōr ceykaiyātaliṉ accamum nāṇumpōla meyyuṟupuṇarcciyai vilakkuvatām. talaivi uṭaiyaḷeṉavē talaivaṉ perumaiyum uraṉum uṭaiyaṉāy vēṭkaimītūravum peṟumāyiṟṟu. ivai ivaṭku eṉṟum uriyavāyiṉ iyaṟkaippuṇarccikku uriyaḷallaḷāmāyiṉum ivvilakkaṇattiṟ tiriyātu niṉṟēyum puṇarccikku uriyaḷāmeṉṟaṟkup paṉṉiraṇṭu meyppāṭuṅ kūṟiṉār. ivaṟṟāṉē puṇarccippiṉṉarp peṟutumeṉat talaivaṉaippōla āṟṟuvāḷāyiṟṟu. iruvar kaṇṇuṟṟuk kātalkūrntavaḻiyellāṅ kantaruvamām. ātalāṉ ataṟku ētuvākiya perumaiyum uraṉum accamum nāṇum pōlvaṉa kūṟiṉār. iccūttiram iraṇṭum nāṭakavaḻakkaṉṟip perumpāṉmai ulakiyalvaḻakkē kūṟaliṉ ikkantaruvam ikkaḷaviyaṟkuc ciṟappiṉṟu, iṉik kūṟuvaṉa meyyuṟupuṇarccipaṟṟiya kaḷavoḻukkamātaliṉ. (8)
100. vēṭkai yorutalai yuḷḷutaṉ melita
lākkañ ceppa ṉāṇuvarai yiṟatta
ṉōkkuva vellā mavaiyē pōṟaṉ
maṟattaṉ mayakkañ cākkā ṭeṉṟac
ciṟappuṭai marapiṉavai kaḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu mutalākak kaḷavilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvār itaṉāṉ iyaṟkaippuṇarccimutaṟ kaḷavu veḷippaṭuntuṇaiyum iruvarkkum uḷavām ilakkaṇam ivvoṉpatumeṉap potuvilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār. (i-ḷ.) orutalaivēṭkai = puṇarātamuṉṉum puṇarntapiṉṉum iruvarkkum iṭaiviṭṭu nikaḻātu orutaṉmaittāki nilaipeṟum vēṭkai: orutalai uḷḷutal = iṭaiviṭātu oruvar oruvaraic cintiyāniṟṟal: melital = aṅṅaṉam uḷḷutal kāraṇattāṉ uṭampuvāṭutal: ākkañ ceppal = yātāṉum ōr iṭaiyūṟu kēṭṭavaḻi ataṉai ākkamāka neñciṟkuk kūṟik kōṭal: nāṇuvaraiyiṟattal = āṟṟun tuṇaiyum nāṇi allātavaḻi ataṉvaraiyiṟattal: nōkkuva ellām avaiyē pōṟal = piṟar tammai nōkkiya nōkkellān tam maṉattuk karantu oḻukukiṉṟavaṟṟai aṟintu nōkkukiṉṟāreṉat tiriyakkōṭal: maṟattal = viḷaiyāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟai maṟattal: mayakkam = ceytiṟaṉ aṟiyātu kaiyaṟṟup puḷḷum māvum mutaliyavaṟṟōṭu kūṟal: cākkāṭu = maṭalēṟutalum varaipāytalum pōlvaṉa kūṟal: eṉṟu acciṟappu uṭai marapiṉavai kaḷavu eṉa moḻipa = eṉṟu collappaṭṭa antac ciṟappuṭaittāṉa muṟaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya oṉpatuṅ kaḷavoḻukkameṉṟu kūṟupa.  e-ṟu. iyaṟkaippuṇarccikku iyaipuṭaimaiyiṉ vēṭkai muṟkūṟiṉār.
cēṭpaṭuttavaḻit talaivaṉ ataṉai aṉpeṉṟu kōṭalum iyaṟkaippuṇarcci puṇarntu nīṅkiyavaḻit talaivi ataṉai aṉpeṉṟu kōṭalum pōlvaṉa ākkañceppal. talaivaṉ pāṅkaṟkun tōḻikkum uraittalun talaivi tōḻikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟalum pōlvaṉa nāṇuvaraiyiṟattal. kaḷavatikāramātaliṉ avaiyeṉṉuñ cuṭṭuk kaḷavai uṇarttum. kaiyuṟaipuṉaitalum vēṭṭaimēliṭṭuk kāṭṭuttiritalun talaivaṟku maṟattal; kiḷiyum pantum mutaliyaṉakoṇṭu viḷaiyāṭutalait tavirntatu talaivikku maṟattal. cākkāṭāvaṉa:  “aṇiṟpal laṉṉa koṅkumutir muṇṭakattu – maṇikkē ḻaṉṉa mānīrc cērppa – vimmai māṟi maṟumai yāyiṉu – nīyā kiyareṅ kaṇavaṉai – yāṉā kiyarniṉ ṉeñcunēr pavaḷē.” “niṟaintōrt tēru neñcamoṭu kuṟaintōrp – payaṉiṉ maiyiṟ paṟṟuviṭ ṭorūu – nayaṉiṉ mākkaḷ pōla vaṇṭiṉañ – cuṉaippū nīttuc ciṉaippūp paṭara – maiyaṉ māṉiṉa maruḷap paiyeṉa – ventāṟu poṉṉi ṉanti pūppa – vaiyaṟi vakaṟṟuṅ kaiyaṟu paṭarō – ṭakaliru vāṉa mamma mañciṉam – pakalāṟṟup paṭutta paḻaṅkaṇ mālaik – kātalarp pirinta pulampi ṉōtaka – vāraña ruṟuna raruniṟañ cuṭṭik – kūreḵ keṟiñari ṉalaitta lāṉā – teḷḷaṟa viyaṟṟiya niḻalkāṇ maṇṭilat – tuḷḷutara vīyiṟ paipaya nuṇaṅki – matukai māytal vēṇṭum peritaḻin – titukol vāḻi tōḻi yeṉṉuyir – vilaṅkuvēṅ kaṭuvaḷi yeṭuppat – tulaṅkumāp puḷḷiṟ ṟuṟakkumap poḻutē.” ivai talaivi cākkāṭāyiṉa. maṭalēṟuvaleṉak kūṟutaṉ māttiraiyē talaivaṟkuc cākkāṭu. ivai ciṟappuṭaiyaveṉavē kaḷavu ciṟappuṭaittām. ivai kaṟpiṟku ākā. iruvarkkum ivai taṭumāṟi varutaliṉ marapiṉavai yeṉap paṉmaikūṟiṉār. (9)
101. muṉṉilai yākkal colvaḻip paṭutta
ṉaṉṉaya muraitta ṉakainaṉi yuṟāa
tannilai yaṟitaṉ melivuviḷak kuṟutta
ṟannilai yuraitta ṟeḷivakap paṭuttaleṉ
ṟiṉṉavai nikaḻu meṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu iṉpamum iṉpanilaiyiṉmaiyumākiya puṇartal pirital kūṟiya muṟaiyāṉē iyaṟkaippuṇarcci muṟkūṟi ataṉ piṉṉarp piritalum piritaṉimittamumāy attuṟaippaṭuvaṉa vellān tokuttut talaivaṟku uriyaveṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) muṉṉilaiyākkal = muṉṉilaiyākāta vaṇṭu neñcu mutaliyavaṟṟai muṉṉilaiyākkikkōṭal: colvaḻippaṭuttal = accollātavaṟṟaic colluvaṉapōlak kūṟutal: naṉṉayam uraittal = avai colluvaṉavāka avaṟṟiṟkut taṉ kaḻiperuṅkātal kūṟuvāṉāyt taṉṉayappuṇarttutal: nakainaṉi uṟāatu annilai aṟital = talaivi makiḻcci mikavum eytāmaṟ puṇarccikkiṉamākiya pirivunilaikūṟi avaḷ āṟṟuntaṉmai aṟital: melivu viḷakkuṟuttal = ippirivāṟ taṉakkuḷḷa varuttattait talaivi maṉaṅkoḷḷak kūṟutalun talaivi varuttaṅ kuṟippāṉ uṇarntu atu tīrak kūṟutalum: tam nilai uraittal = niṉṉoṭu paṭṭa toṭarcci eḻumaiyum varukiṉṟateṉat tamatunilai uraittal: teḷivu akappaṭuttal = niṉṉiṟpiriyēṉ piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉ piriyiṉ aṟaṉallatu ceykēṉāvaleṉat talaivi maṉattut tēṟṟampaṭak kūṟutal: eṉṟu iṉṉavai nikaḻum eṉmaṉār pulavar = eṉṟu ikkūṟiya ēḻum payiṉṟuvarum iyaṟkaippuṇarccippiṉ talaivaṟku.  e-ṟu. muṟkūṟiya mūṉṟum nayappiṉ kūṟu. itu aṟivaḻintu kūṟātu talaivi kēṭpatu kāriyamāka vaṇṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku uvakaipaṟṟik kūṟuvatu. naṉṉayam eṉavē evariṉun tāṉ kātalaṉāka uṇarttum. itaṉpayaṉ puṇarcciyeytiniṉṟāṭku ivaṉ evviṭattāṉkollō iṉṉum itu kūṭuṅkollō ivaṉ aṉpuṭaiyaṉkollō eṉanikaḻum aiyanīṅkutal. itu piritaṉimittam. ivaṉ piriyāviṭiṉ ivvoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉām āṇṭu yām iṟantupaṭutaliṉ ivaṉum iṟantupaṭuvaṉeṉak karutap, piriveṉpatum oṉṟu uṇṭeṉat talaivaṉ kūṟutal avaṭku makiḻcciyiṉṟeṉpatu nakai naṉi yuṟāteṉṟār. “puṇartal pirital” eṉakkūṟiya cūttirattiṟ puṇartalai muṟkūṟi ēṉaip pirivai annilaiyeṉṟu īṇṭuc cuṭṭik kūṟiṉār. itaṉāṟ talaivikkup pirivaccaṅkūṟiṉār. taṇṇīr vēṭṭu ataṉai uṇṭu uyirpeṟṟāṉ itaṉāṉ uyirpeṟṟēmeṉak karuti ataṉmāṭṭu vēṭkai nīṅkātavāṟupōlat, talaivimāṭṭu vēṭkaiyeyti avaḷai aritiṟkūṭi uyirpeṟṟāṉātaliṉ, ivaḷāṉ uyirpeṟṟēmeṉṟuṇarntu, avaṇmēl nikaḻkiṉṟa aṉpuṭaṉē piriyumātaliṟ talaivaṟkum pirivaccam uḷatāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam aṉpu nikaḻavum piṟar aṟiyāmaṟ pirikiṉṟēṉeṉpataṉait talaivikku maṉaṅkoḷḷak kūṟumeṉṟaṟku viḷakkuṟuttaleṉṟār. itaṉāṉē vaṟpuṟuttal peṟṟām. atu aṇittu emmiṭameṉṟum piṟavāṟṟāṉum vaṟpuṟuttalām. mēlaṉavum piritaṉimittam. 
um. “koṅkutēr vāḻkkai yañciṟait tumpi – kāmañ ceppātu kaṇṭatu moḻimō – payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpiṉ mayiliyaṟ – ceṟiyeyiṟ ṟarivai kūntali – ṉaṟiyavu muḷavōnī yaṟiyum pūvē.” itaṉuṭ tumpi eṉṟatu muṉṉilaiyākkal; kaṇṭatumoḻimō eṉṟatu colvaḻippaṭuttal; kūntaliṉ naṟiyavum uḷavō eṉṟatu naṉṉayamuraittal; kāmañ ceppātu eṉṟatu eṉṉilattu vaṇṭātaliṉ eṉakkākak kūṟātu meykūṟeṉat taṉ iṭam atuvākak kūṟaliṉ iṭamaṇitteṉṟatu; payiliyatu naṭpu eṉṟatu tannilaiyuraittal. “pūviṭaip paṭiṉum yāṇṭu kaḻintaṉṉa – nīruṟai makaṉṟiṟ puṇarcci pōlap – pirivari tākiya taṇṭāk kāmamō – ṭuṭaṉuyir pōkuka tilla kaṭaṉaṟin – tiruvē mākiya vulakat – toruvē mākiya puṉmainā muyaṟkē.” muṟpiṟappil iruvēmāyk kūṭip pōntaṉam, ivvulakilē ippuṇarccikku muṉṉar yām vevvēṟāyuṟṟa tuṉpattu iṉṟu nāmē nīṅkutaṟkeytiya pirivaritākiya kāmattuṭaṉē iruvarkkum uyirpōvatāka, iḵteṉakku viruppameṉṟāṉ; eṉpataṉāṟ tannilaiyuraittalum pirivaccamuṅ kūṟiṟṟu. “kuvaḷai nāṟuṅ kuvaiyiruṅ kūnta – lāmpa ṉāṟun tēmpoti tuvarvāyk – kuṇṭunīrt tāmaraik koṅki ṉaṉṉa – nuṇpaḵ ṟutti māa yōyē – nīyē, añca leṉṟa veñcolañ calaiyē – yāṉē, kuṟuṅkā laṉṉaṅ kuvavumaṇaṟ cēkkuṅ – kaṭalcūḻ maṇṭilam peṟiṉum – viṭalcū ḻilaṉyā ṉiṉṉuṭai naṭpē.” itu nayappum pirivaccamum vaṉpoṟaiyuṅ kūṟiṟṟu. “yāyum yāyum yārā kiyarō – ventaiyu nuntaiyu memmuṟaik kēḷir – yāṉu nīyu mevvaḻi yaṟituñ – cempulap peyaṉīr pōla – vaṉpuṭai neñcan tāṉkalan taṉavē.” itu pirivareṉak karutiya talaivi kuṟippuṇarntu talaivaṉ kūṟiyatu. “melliya larivainiṉ ṉallakam pulampa – ṉiṟṟuṟat tamaikuve ṉāyi ṉeṟṟuṟan – tiravalar vārā vaikal – palavā kukayāṉ celavuṟu takavē.” “aṟattā ṟaṉṟeṉa moḻinta toṉṟupaṭu kiḷavi – yaṉṉa vāka veṉṉuḷam pōla.” ivai teḷivakappaṭuttal. “ammel lōti vimmuṟ ṟaḻuṅka – lemmalai vāḻna rirumpuṉam paṭukkiya – varanti ṉaviyaṟut tuṟutta cāntanum – parantēn talkuṟ ṟiruntutaḻai yutavum – paṇpiṟ ṟeṉpa vaṇmai yataṉāṟ – palkāl vantunam paruvara ṟīra – valkalum poruntuva mākali – ṉolkā vāḻkkait tākumeṉ ṉuyirē.” ituvum aṇittu emmiṭameṉa āṟṟuvittatu. payiṉṟeṉavē payilātu varum āyattuyttalum yāṉpōvaleṉak kūṟutalum maṟaintu avaṭkāṇṭaluṅ kaṇṭuniṉṟu avaṇilai kūṟuvaṉavum avaḷarumai yaṟintu kūṟuvaṉavum pōlvaṉa piṟavuṅkoḷka. “yāṉṟaṟkāṇṭoṟum” eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷuḷ “nīyaṟin tilaiyā ṉeñcē – yāṉaṟin tēṉatu vāyā kutalē.” eṉa maṟaintu avaṭkaṇṭuniṉṟu talaivaṉ avaḷoṭu nikaḻntatu niṉaii neñciṟkuk kūṟiyatu. “kāṇā marapiṟ ṟuyireṉa moḻivōr – nāṇilar maṉṟa poymmoḻin taṉarē – yāaṅ kāṇṭummē varumpeṟa luyirē – collu māṭu meṉmela viyaluṅ – kaṇaikkā ṉuṇukiya nucuppiṉ – maḻaikkaṇ mātar paṇaipperun tōṭṭē.” āyattoṭu pōkiṉṟāḷaik kaṇṭu kūṟiyatu. itaṉkaṇ āyattuyttamaiyum peṟṟām. iṉi “vēṭkai yorutalai” eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟai meyyuṟupuṇarcci mēl nikaḻttutaṟku avattai kūṟiṉāreṉṟum iccūttirattait talaiviyai nōkki muṉṉilaiyākkaṉ mutaliyaṉa kūṟip piṉṉar iyaṟkaippuṇarcci puṇarumeṉṟuṅ kūṟuvārum uḷar. avar aṟiyār; eṉṉai? īṇṭu avattai kūṟip piṉṉarp puṇarccinikaḻumeṉiṉ āṇṭuk kūṟiya meyppāṭu paṉṉiraṇṭum vēṇṭāvām. aṉṟiyum, āṟām avati kaṭantu varuvaṉa akamaṉmai meyppāṭṭiyaliṟ kūṟaliṟ pattām avattaiyākiya cākkāṭeyti meyyuṟupuṇarcci naṭattal poruntāmaiyuṇarka. iṉit talaiviyai muṉṉilaiyākkaṉ mutaliyaṉa kūṟip piṉṉarp puṇarumeṉiṉ muṉṉark “kūṭṭiyuraikkuṅ kuṟippuraiyākum” eṉak kaṇṇāṟ kūṟik kūṭumeṉṟalum “irukaiyumeṭuttal” eṉap piṉpu kūṟutalum poruntātām. aṉṟiyum nayappum pirivaccamum vaṉpoṟaiyum kūṟip piriyavēṇṭutalāṉum atu poruntātām. (10)
102. meytoṭṭup payiṟal poypā rāṭṭa
liṭampeṟṟut taḻāa liṭaiyūṟu kiḷatta
ṉīṭuniṉain tiraṅkal kūṭuta luṟutal
colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻit
tīrāt tēṟṟa muḷappaṭat tokaiip
pērāc ciṟappi ṉirunāṉku kiḷaviyum
peṟṟavaḻi makiḻcciyum pirintavaḻik kalaṅkalu
niṟpavai niṉaii nikaḻpavai yuraippiṉuṅ
kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpiṉum
peṭṭa vāyilpeṟ ṟiravuvali yuṟuppiṉu
mūrum pēruṅ keṭutiyum piṟavu
nīriṟ kuṟippi ṉirampak kūṟit
tōḻiyaik kuṟaiyuṟum pakutiyun tōḻi
kuṟaiyavaṭ cārtti meyyuṟak kūṟalun
taṇṭā tirappiṉu maṟṟaiya vaḻiyuñ
collavaṭ cārttaliṟ pulliya vakaiyiṉu
maṟintōḷ yāppi ṉavvaḻi maruṅkiṟ
kēṭum pīṭuṅ kūṟalun tōḻi
nīkkali ṉākiya nilaimaiyu nōkki
maṭaṉmā kūṟu miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē.
itu mēl iyaṟkaippuṇarccip pakutiyellāṅ kūṟi ataṉvaḻit tōṉṟum iṭantalaippāṭum ataṉvaḻit tōṉṟum pāṅkaṟkūṭṭamum avaṟṟuvaḻittōṉṟun tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭamum nikaḻumiṭattut talaivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻttumāṟum āṟṟāmai kaiyikantu kalaṅkiyavaḻi avaṉ maṭaṉmā kūṟumāṟuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. itaṉuḷ irunāṉkukiḷaviyum eṉṉun tuṇaiyum iṭantalaippāṭum vāyilpeṭpiṉum eṉṉun tuṇaiyum pāṅkaṟkūṭṭamum oḻintaṉa tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭamumām. (i-ḷ.) meytoṭṭuppayiṟal = talaivaṉ talaiviyai meyyaittīṇṭip payilāniṟkunilaimai: eṉṟāl iyaṟkaippuṇarccippiṉṉarp perunāṇiṉaḷākiya talaimakaḷ etirniṟkumōveṉiṉ tāṉ piṟantakuṭikkuc ciṟantavoḻukkat tiṟkut takātatu ceytāḷātaliṉ maṟaiyiṟṟappā maṟaiyōṉoruvaṉai maṟaiyiṟ ṟappiya maṟaiyōṉ pōlavum vēṭkaimikutiyāṉ veytuṇṭu puṉkūrntār pōlavum neñcum niṟaiyun taṭumāṟi iṉic ceyaṟpālatiyāteṉṟum āyattuḷḷē varuvāṉkol eṉṉum accaṅkūravum vārāṉkol eṉṉuṅ kātalkūravum pulaiyaṉ ṟīmpāl pōṉ maṉaṅkoḷḷā aṉantaruḷḷam uṭaiyaḷāy nāṇu maṟantu kātalīrppac cellum; ceṉṟu niṉṟāḷait talaivaṉ ivvoḻukkam puṟattār ikaḻap pulaṉāy vēṟupaṭṭāḷ kollō eṉavum aṅṅaṉam maṟaipulappaṭutaliṉ itaṉiṉūṅku varaintukoḷḷiṉaṉṟi immaṟaikku uṭampaṭāḷōveṉavuṅ karutumāṟu muṉpupōl niṉṟa talaiviyai meyyuṟat tīṇṭi niṉṟu kuṟippaṟiyumeṉṟaṟkut toṭumeṉṉātu payiṟaleṉṟār.
poy pārāṭṭal = aṅṅaṉan tīṇṭiniṉṟuḻit talaivi kuṟippaṟintu avaḷai ōtiyum nutalum nīvip poyceyyāniṉṟu puṉainturaittal: citaiviṉṟēṉuñ citaintaṉapōṟ tiruttaliṟ poyyeṉṟār.
iṭampeṟṟut taḻāal = avviraṇṭaṉāṉun talaiviyai mukampeṟṟavaṉ avaṇōkkiya nōkkiṉait taṉṉiṭattilē cērttikkoṇṭu kūṟal: iṭaiyūṟu kiḷattal = avaḷ perunāṇiṉaḷātaliṉ iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟak kēṭṭuk kūṭṭattiṟku iṭaiyūṟākac cila nikaḻtti avaṟṟait talaivaṉ kūṟal: avai kaṇputaittaluṅ kompāṉuṅ koṭiyāṉuñ cārtalumām: nīṭuniṉaintiraṅkal = puṇarccinikaḻntu poḻutu nīṇṭataṟku iraṅki irakkantōṉṟak kūṟal: kūṭutal uṟutal = nīṭittateṉṟu iraṅkiṉāṉeṉpatu aṟintōḷ ivaṉ āṟṟāṉāki iṟantupaṭuvaṉeṉap perunāṇuk kaṭitunīṅkutal: colliya nukarcci vallēpeṟṟuḻi = talaivaṉ tāṉ muṟkūṟiya nukarcciyai viraiyap peṟṟavaḻi: peṟṟavaḻi eṉpataṉaip peṟutaleṉap peyarppaṭuttal akkaruttāṟ peṟutum. tīrāt tēṟṟam uḷappaṭat tokaii = eññāṉṟum piriyāmaikkuk kāraṇamākiya cūḷuṟutal akappaṭat tokuttu: puṇarccinikaḻntuḻiyallatu tēṟṟaṅkūṟal ākāteṉṟaṟku vallē peṟṟuḻit tīrāt tēṟṟameṉṟār. muṉṟeḷivakappaṭuttapiṉ nikaḻnta āṟṟāmai tīrtaṟkutteyvattoṭucārttic cūḷuṟutaliṉ ittēṟṟamum vēṇṭiṟṟu.
pērāc ciṟappiṉ irunāṉku kiḷaviyum = kuṟaiyāc ciṟappiṉavākiya ivveṭṭum: tāmēkūṭum iṭantalaippāṭum pāṅkaṉāṟ kuṟitalaippeyyum iṭantalaippāṭum otta ciṟappiṉavātaṟkup pērācciṟappiṉeṉṟār. eṉavē iṭantalaippāṭu iraṇṭāyiṟṟu. tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭampōlap pāṅkaṉ uraiyāṭi iṭainiṉṟu kūṭṭāmaiyiṟ pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam eṉṟataṉait talaimakaṉ pāṅkaṉaik kūṭuṅ kūṭṭameṉṟu koḷka. um. “uṟutō ṟuyirtaḷirppat tīṇṭalāṟ pētaik – kamiḻti ṉiyaṉṟaṉa tōḷ.” itu eṉ kaiceṉṟuṟuntōṟum iṉṉuyir taḷirkkumpaṭi yāṉ tīṇṭappaṭutaliṉeṉap poruḷkūṟavē meytīṇṭalāyiṟṟu. “tīṇṭalu miyaivatu kollō māṇṭa – villuṭai yiḷaiyar kalliṭu peṭutta – naṉantalaik kāṉat tiṉantalaip pirinta – puṉkaṇ maṭamā ṉērpaṭa nacaiic – cilaimāṇ kaṭuvicai talainiṟat taḻuttik – kurutiyoṭu paṟitta ceṅkōl vāḷi – māṟukoṇ ṭaṉṉa vuṇka – ṇāṟiruṅ kūntaṟ koṭicci tōḷē.” kaḻaṟiya pāṅkaṟkuk kūṟun talaivaṉ ivaṉāṉ ikkuṟai muṭiyātu, nerunal iṭantalaippāṭṭiṟ kūṭiyāṅkuk kūṭuval, atu kūṭuṅkoleṉṟu kūṟuvāṉ aṟṟaiñāṉṟu meytoṭṭuppayiṉṟatē kūṟiṉāṉ. “colliṟ colletir koḷḷā yāḻaniṉ – ṟirumuka miṟaiñci nāṇuti katumeṉak – kāmaṅ kaimikiṟ ṟāṅkuta leḷitō – koṭuṅkē ḻirumpuṟa naṭuṅkak kuttip – puliviḷai yāṭiya pulavunāṟu vēḻattiṉ – ṟalaimarup pēyppak kaṭaimaṇi civantaniṉ – kaṇṇē katuva valla naṇṇā – rāṇṭalai matila rākavu muracukoṇ – ṭōmparaṇ kaṭanta vaṭupōrc ceḻiyaṉ – perumpeyark kūṭa laṉṉaniṉ – karumpuṭait tōḷu muṭaiyavā laṇaṅkē.” itu aṅṅaṉam meytīṇṭi niṉṟavaṉ yāṉ taḻīikkoṇṭu kūṟil ataṉai ēṟṟukkoḷḷāyāy iṟaiñci niṉṟu nāṇattāṟ kaṇṇaip putaittiyeṉa iṭaiyūṟukiḷattal kūṟik kāmaṅ kaimmikiṟ ṟāṅkuta leḷitōveṉa nīṭuniṉaintiraṅkal kūṟip puliyiṭait tōyntu civanta kōṭupōla eṉṉiṭait tōyntu kāmakkuṟippiṉāṟ civanta kaṇṇeṉak kūṭutaluṅ kūṟiṟṟu. iṉit taṉiyēvantaṉa. “kaṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvarniṉ ṉaiya – ruṭalpuk kuyirkoṉṟu vāḻvaimaṉ ṉīyu – miṭalpuk kaṭaṅkāta vemmulaiyō pāra – miṭarpuk kiṭuku miṭaiyiḻival kaṇṭāy. itu poypārāṭṭal.” “kolyāṉai veṇmaruppuṅ kolval puliyataḷu – nalyāṇar niṉṉaiyar kūṭṭuṇṭu – celvārtā – mōrampi ṉāleytu pōkkuvaryāṉ pōkāmai – yīrampi ṉāleytā yiṉṟu.” itu iṭampeṟṟuttaḻāal. “ilaṅku vaḷaiteḷirppa valava ṉāṭṭi – mukamputait tuppi ṉikaiñciniṉ ṟōḷē – pulampukoṇ mālai maṟaiya – nalaṅkē ḻāka nalkuva ḷeṉakkē.” itu kūṭutaluṟutal. “vēṭṭa poḻuti ṉavaiyavai pōlumē – tōṭṭār katuppiṉā ṭōḷ.” itu nukarccipeṟṟatu. “emmaṇaṅ kiṉavē makiḻnaṉai muṟiya – naṉaimutir puṉkiṉ pūttāḻ veṇmaṇal – vēlaṉ puṉainta veṟiyayar kaḷantoṟuñ – cennel vāṉpori citaṟi yaṉṉa – vekkar naṇṇiya vemmūr viyaṉṟuṟai – nēriṟai muṉkai paṟṟic – cūrara makaḷiro ṭuṟṟa cūḷē.” itaṉuṭ tīrāt tēṟṟattaip piṉṉorukāṟ talaivi tērntu teḷivoḻittuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. iyaṟkaippuṇarccikkaṭ talaivi karuttellām uṇarntāṉāyiṉuṅ kāvaṉmikutiyāṉuṅ karavuḷḷattāṉum iraṇṭāṅ kūṭṭattiṉum avaḷnilai teḷiyātu aiyuṟutaluṅ kūṟi mūṉṟāvatāy mēl nikaḻum pāṅkaṟkūṭṭattiṉpiṉ nikaḻum iṭantalaippāṭṭiṉum aiyuṟavu uritteṉṟu meytoṭṭuppayiṟaṉ mutaliyaṉa ataṟkuṅ kūṟiṉār. aṉṟiyum makkaḷellām mutal iṭai kaṭaiyeṉa mūvakaippaṭutaliṉ avarkkellām itu potuvitiyākalāṉum amaiyum. “eṇṇiya tiyaiyā tāṅkol kaṇṇi – yavvuṟu marapi ṉukarcci peṟukeṉa – varivaṇ ṭārkkum vāypuku kaṭāatta – vaṇṇal yāṉai yeṇṇaruñ cōlai – viṇṇuyar veṟpiṉem maruḷi niṉṉi – ṉakali ṉakalume ṉuyireṉat tavali – varuntuya ravalamo ṭaṇittem miṭameṉap – pirintuṟai vamaintavem pulampunaṉi nōkkik – kavarvuḷu neñcamoṭu kavaluṅ kollō – vāya nāppaṇ varukuvaṉ kolleṉa – vuyavumeṉ ṉuḷḷat tayarvumika lāṉē.” itu varuvāṉ koleṉṉum accamum vārāṉkoleṉṉuṅ kātaluṅ kūrntu talaivi kūṟiyatu. iṉic colliyaveṉṟataṉāṉē iṉṉum ippuṇarcci kūṭuṅkoleṉak kūṟuvaṉavum iṉṉun teyvantarumeṉak kūṟuvaṉavun talaiviyai etirppaṭṭa iṭaṅkaṇṭuḻi avaḷākak kūṟuvaṉavuṅ kāṭcikku nimittamākiya kiḷḷaiyai vāḻttuvaṉavun talaivi taṉitta nilaimai kaṇṭu viyappaṉavum muṉṉart tāṉ nīṅkiya vaḻip piṟanta varuttaṅ kūṟuvaṉavum aṇiyaṇintu viṭuttalum ivaipōlvaṉa piṟavum iṭantalaippāṭṭiṟkuk koḷka. um. “veḷḷa varampi ṉūḻi pōkiyuṅ – kiḷḷai vāḻiya palavē yoḷḷiḻai – yirumpala kūntaṟ koṭicci – peruntōṭ kāval kāṭṭiya vammē.” itu kiḷḷai vāḻttu. “nīṅkiṟ ṟeṟūuṅ kuṟukuṅkāṟ ṟaṇṇeṉṉun – tīyāṇṭup peṟṟā ḷivaḷ.” itu nīṅkiyavaḻip piṟanta varuttaṅ kūṟiyatu. “alkupaṭa ruḻanta varimatar maḻaikkaṭ – palpūm pakaittaḻai nuṭaṅku malkuṟ – ṟirumaṇi puraiyu mēṉi maṭavōḷ – yārmakaḷ kolliva ṭantai vāḻiyar – tuyara muṟīiyiṉa ḷemmē yakalvaya – larivaṉa rarintun taruvaṉa peṟṟun – taṇceṟu tāya mataṉuṭai nōṉṟāṭ – kaṇpō ṉeytal poraviṟ pūkkun – tiṇṭērp poṟaiyaṉ ṟoṇṭit – teṉṟiṟam perukaviva ḷīṉṟa tāyē.” eṉpatum atu. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkuc cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷ varuvaṉa uḷavēṟ kāṇka.
peṟṟavaḻi makiḻcciyum = colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟuḻi avaṉ maṉam makiḻum makiḻcciyum: pērāc ciṟappiṉeṉṟataṉāṟ pāṅkaṉāl nikaḻum iṭantalaippāṭṭiṟkum itukoḷka. um. “oṭuṅkī rōti yoṇṇutaṟ kuṟumaka – ṇaṟunta ṇīra ḷāraṇaṅ kiṉaḷē – yiṉaiya ḷeṉṟavaṭ puṉaiyaḷa vaṟiyēṉ – cilamel liyavē kiḷavi – yaṇaimel liyaḷyā muyaṅkuṅ kālē.” eṉavarum.
pirintavaḻik kalaṅkalum = aṅṅaṉam puṇarntu pirintuḻi aṉpumikutiyāṟ tāṉ maṟaintu avaṭ kāṇuṅkāl āyattiṭaiyuñ cīṟūriṭaiyuṅ kaṇṭu iṉik kūṭutal ariteṉa iraṅkalum. um. “kuṇakaṭaṟ ṟiraiyatu paṟaitapu nārai – tiṇṭērp poṟaiyaṉ ṟoṇṭi muṉṟuṟai – yayirai yāriraik kaṇvan tāṅkuc – cēya ḷariyōṭ paṭāti – nōyai neñcē nōyppā lōyē.” “illō ṉiṉpaṅ kāamuṟ ṟāaṅ – karituvēṭ ṭaṉaiyā ṉeñcē kātali – nalla ḷākuta laṟintāṅ – kariya ḷākuta laṟiyā tōyē.” eṉavarum. vaḻiyeṉṟataṉāṟ piriyaluṟuvāṉ kūṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. “vāḻta luyirkkaṉṉa ḷāyiḻai cāta – lataṟkaṉṉa ṇīṅku miṭattu.” itu mūṉṟāṅ kūṭṭattiṉaiyuṅ karutaliṉ īṇṭu vaittār. itu mutalākap pāṅkaṟkūṭṭamām. kalaṅkalu meṉavē akkalakkattāṉ nikaḻvaṉavellāṅ koḷka. avai talaivaṉ pāṅkaṉai niṉaittalum akkalakkaṅ kaṇṭu pāṅkaṉ viṉāvuvaṉavum atuvē paṟṟukkōṭāka uṟṟaturaittalum piṟavumām. um. “paṇṭaiyai yallainī yiṉṟu parivoṉṟu – koṇṭa maṉattai yeṉavuṇarval – kaṇṭāyā – ṉiṉṉuṟṟa tellā maṟiya vuraittiyāṟ – piṉṉuṟṟa naṇpiṉāy pērttu.” eṉavum, “vañcamē yeṉṉum vakaittālōr māviṉāyt – tañcan tamiyēṉāyc ceṉṟave – ṉeñca – nalaṅkoṇṭār pūṅkuḻalā ṇallāyat taṉṟi – valaṅkoṇṭāḷ koṇṭā ḷiṭam.” “eluva ciṟāa rēmamuṟu naṇpa – pulava tōḻa kēḷā yattai – mākkaṭa ṉaṭuva ṇeṇṇāṭ pakkattup – pakuveṇ ṭiṅka ṭōṉṟi yāṅkuk – katuppayal viḷaṅkuñ ciṟunutal – putukkōḷ yāṉaiyaip piṇittaṟṟā lemmē.” eṉavum varum.
niṟpavai niṉaii nikaḻpavai uraippiṉum = niṟpavai niṉaii uraippiṉum nikaḻpavai uraippiṉum eṉa uraippiṉeṉpataṉai muṉṉuṅ kūṭṭuka; atu kēṭṭa pāṅkaṉ ulakattu nilainiṟkiṉṟa naṟkuṇaṅkaḷai avaṉai niṉaippittuk kaḻaṟik kūṟiṉum, akkaḻaṟiyavaṟṟai maṟuttut taṉ neñciṉ nikaḻum varuttaṅkaḷai avaṟkuk kūṟiṉum: um. “kuṉṟa muruḷiṟ koṭittērōy kuṉṟiyu – ḷoṉṟu moḻiva tuḷatāmō – naṉṟaṟintu – tāmuṟaiyē ceyvār takavilavē ceytakkāl – yārmuṟai ceypavō maṟṟu.” eṉavum, “terōṉ ṟeṟukatir maḻuṅkiṉun tiṅka – ṭīrā vemmaiyoṭu ticainaṭuk kuṟuppiṉum – peyarāp peṟṟiyiṟ ṟiriyāc cīrcāl – kulattiṟ ṟiriyāk koḷkaiyuṅ koḷkaiyoṭu – nalattiṟ ṟiriyā nāṭṭamu muṭaiyōy – kaṇṭata ṉaḷavaiyiṟ kalaṅkuti yeṉiṉim – maṇṭiṉi kiṭakkai mānila – muṇṭeṉak karuti yuṇaralaṉ yāṉē.” eṉavum ivai niṟpavai niṉaiik kaḻaṟiṉa. “kāmaṅ kāma meṉpa kāma – maṇaṅkum piṇiyu maṉṟē niṉaippiṉ – mutaiccuvaṟ kalitta muṟṟāvi ḷampuṉa – mūtā taivan tāṅku – viruntē kāmam peruntō ḷōyē.” ituvum atu. “nayaṉu naṇpu nāṇunaṉ kuṭaimaiyum – payaṉum paṇpum pāṭaṟin toḻukalu – nummiṉu maṟikuveṉ maṉṉē kammeṉa – vetirtta titti yēriḷa vaṉamulai – vitirttu viṭṭaṉṉa vantuṇ cuṇaṅki – ṉaimpāl vakutta kūntaṟ cempoṟ – ṟirunutal polinta tēmpā yōti – mutunī rilañcip pūtta kuvaḷai – yetirmalarp piṇaiya laṉṉaviva – ḷarimatar maḻaikkaṇ kāṇā vūṅkē.” “iṭikkuṅ kēḷīr nuṅkuṟai yāka – niṟukka lāṟṟiṉō naṉṟumaṟ ṟilla – ñāyiṟu kāyum vevvaṟai maruṅkiṟ – kaiyi lūmaṉ kaṇṇiṟ kākkum – veṇṇai yuṇaṅkal pōlap – parantaṉ ṟinnōy moṇṭukoḷaṟ karitē.” ivai nikaḻpavai uraittaṉa. ippaṉmaiyāṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellāṅ koḷka.
kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya vāyil peṭpiṉum = aṅṅaṉan talaivaṟku nikaḻuṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷai veḷippaṭak kāṭṭiya pāṅkaṉ avaṉ āṟṟāmai mikuti kaṇṭu ataṉai nīkkutaṟku virumpiṉum: atu niṉṉāṟ kāṇappaṭṭa uru evviṭattu ettaṉmaittu eṉaviṉāvum. um. “paṅkayamō tuṅkap paṉitaṅku mālvaraiyō – vaṅkaṇ vicumpō valaikaṭalō – veṅkōvic – cevvaṇṇa mālvaraiyē pōlun tirumēṉi – yivvaṇṇañ ceytārk kiṭam.” eṉavarum. atukēṭṭut talaivaṉ kaḻiyuvakai mītūrntu iṉṉaviṭattu ittaṉmaittu eṉṉum. “kaḻaipā ṭiraṅkap palliyaṅ kaṟaṅka – vāṭumaka ṇaṭanta koṭumpuri nōṉkayiṟ – ṟatavat tīṅkiṉi yaṉṉa cemmukat – tuyttalai manti vaṉpaṟaḻ tūṅkak – kaḻaikka ṇirumporai yēṟi niṉṟu – kuṟakkuṟu mākka ṭāḷaṅ koṭṭumak – kuṉṟakat tatuvē kuḻumiḷaic cīṟūr  cīṟū rōḷē nāṟumayirk koṭicci – koṭicci kaiyakat tatuvēpiṟar – viṭuttaṟ kākātu piṇittaveṉ ṉeñcē.” itu iṭaṅkūṟiṟṟu. “kēḷir vāḻiyō kēḷir nāḷumeṉ – ṉeñcu piṇikkoṇṭa vañci lōti – perunā ṇaṇinta ciṟumel lāka – morunāḷ puṇarap puṇari – ṉaraināḷ vāḻkkaiyum vēṇṭalaṉ yāṉē.”  itu taṉmai kūṟiṟṟu. mīṭṭuṅ kuṟṟaṅ kāṭṭiya eṉṟataṉāṉē ikkūṭṭattiṟkuriya kūṟṟākic cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ vēṟupaṭavaruvaṉavellāṅ koḷka. “kāyā vīṉṟa kaṇvī nāṟṟa – māyā muṉṟil varuvaḷi turakku – mākōḷ vāḻnar ciṟukuṭi yāṭṭi – vēyēr meṉṟōḷ vilakkunar – yārō vāḻinī yavvayiṟ celiṉē.” itu pāṅkaṉai nī yāṇṭuc cellavēṇṭumeṉṟatu. “eṉṉuṟunōy tīrttaṟ kirupiṟappi ṉāṉmaṟaiyōṉ – ṟaṉṉuṟunōy pōlat taḷarkiṉṟā – ṉiṉṉiṉiya – cuṟṟattiṟ ṟīrnta cuṭuriḻaiyaic cevviyāṟ – ṟeṟṟeṉak kaṇṇuṟuṅko leṉṟu.” itu kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟa pāṅkaṉ avviṭattuk kāṇuṅkoleṉṟu aiyuṟṟatu. “aḷitō tāṉē yaruṇmika vuṭaittē – kaḷivāy vaṇṭiṉaṅ kavarntuṇ ṭāṭu – moḷitār mārpi ṉōṅkeḻiṟ kuricilaip – piriyāt tuyaramoṭu pētuyirt takaṉṟa – kiḷiyō maḻalaik kēḻkiḷar māta – rārnta cuṟṟamoṭu tamari ṉīkkat – tāṉē tamiyaḷ kāṭṭiya – vāṉōr teyvam vaṇaṅkuval yāṉē.” itu kuṟivaḻic ceṉṟa pāṅkaṉ talaiviyai eḷitiṟ kāṭṭiya teyvattai vaṇaṅkiyatu. “kaṇṇeṉa malaruṅ kuvaḷaiyu maṭiyeṉat – taṇṇeṉun taṭamalar tayaṅku tāmaraiyu – mulaiyeṉa muraṇiya kōṅkamum vakaiyeḻiṉ – miṉṉeṉa nuṭaṅku maruṅkulu maṇiyeṉa – vayiṉvayi ṉimaikkum pāṅkupal luruviṟ kāṇṭaku kamaḻkoṭi pōlumeṉ – ṇāṇṭakai yaṇṇalai yaṟivutolait tatuvē.” itu ivaḷpōlum iṟaivaṉai varuttiṉāḷeṉap pāṅkaṉ aiyuṟṟatu. “kaṇṇē, kaṇṇayaṟ piṟanta kavuḷaḻi kaṭāatta – vaṇṇal yāṉai yāriyarp paṇitta – viṟaṟpōr vāṉavaṉ kolli mīmicai – yaṟaikkāṉ māccuṉai yaviḻnta nīlam – pallē, pallaraṇ kaṭanta pacumpūṭ pāṇṭiyaṉ – malkunīr varaippiṟ koṟkai muṉṟuṟai – yūtai yīṭṭiya vuyarmaṇa laṭaikarai – yōta veṇṭirai yutaitta mutta – niṟaṉē, tiṟalviḷaṅ kavuṇar tūṅkeyi leṟinta – viṟaṉmiku muraciṉ velpōrc cōḻa – ṉalaṉaṇi yaraṅkiṟ pōkiya māvi – ṉuruva nīḷciṉai yoḻukiya taḷirē – yeṉṟavai payantamai yaṟiyār naṉṟu – maṭavar maṉṟavik kuṟavar makka – ṭēmpoḻi kiḷavi yivaḷai – yāmpayan tēmem makaḷeṉ pōrē.” itu talaiviyai viyantatu. “paṇṇātu paṇmēṟṟeṉ pāṭuṅ kaḻikkāṉa – leṇṇātu kaṇṭāruk kēraṇaṅkā – meṇṇātu – cāvārcāṉ ṟāṇmai tirintilār maṟṟivaṉaik – kāvār kayiṟurīi viṭṭu.” pāṅkaṉ talaivaṉai viyantatu. “pūnta ṇirumpuṉattup pūcal piriyātu pūḻi yāṭik – kāntaṭ kamaḻkulaiyāṟ kātaṉ maṭappiṭitaṉ kavuḷvaṇ ṭōppa – vēntaṉpō ṉiṉṟa viṟaṟkaḷiṟṟai villiṉāṟ kaṭivār taṅkai – yēnteḻi lāka miyaiyā tiyaintanō yiyaiyum pōlum.” itu talaivaṟku varuttan takumeṉa avaṉai viyantatu. “vemmuṟu tuyaramoṭu” eṉṉuñceyyuḷuṅ koḷka. iṉip pāṅkaṉ talaivitaṉmai talaivaṟkuk kūṟuvaṉavum iṭaṅkāṭṭuvaṉavuñ cāṉṟōrceyyuḷuḷ varumvaḻik kāṇka. āṇṭuc ceṉṟa talaivaṉ iṭantalaippāṭṭiṟ kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē kūṭutalkoḷka. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭi niṉṟu avaṉ makiḻntukūṟuvaṉavum piṟavuṅ koḷka. “vēṭṭa poḻuti ṉavaiyavai pōlumē – tōṭṭār katuppiṉā ṭōḷ.” “emakkunayan taruḷiṉai yāyiṟ paṇaittō – ḷoṇṇuta larivaiyoṭu meṉmela viyali – vanticiṉ vāḻiyō maṭantai – toṇṭi yaṉṉaniṉ paṇpupala koṇṭē.” itu pāṅkaṟkūṭṭaṅ kūṭi nīṅkun talaivaṉ nī varumiṭattu niṉtōḻiyōṭum varalvēṇṭu meṉat talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu. “neyvaḷa raimpā ṉēriḻai mātarai – meynnilai tiriyā mētaku cuṟṟamo – ṭeytuta lariteṉ ṟiṉṉaṇa miraṅkik – kaiyaṟu neñcamoṭu kavaṉṟunaṉi peyarntaveṉ – paita luḷḷam parivu nīkkit – teyvat taṉṉa teriyiḻai meṉṟō – ḷeytat tanta vēntalo ṭeṉṉiṭai – naṟpāṟ kēṇmai nāṭoṟu meyta – vappāṟ piṟappiṉum peṟukamaṟ ṟemakkē.” aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭiniṉṟu talaivaṉ pāṅkaṉai uṇmakiḻntu uraittatu. ivaṉ perumpāṉmai pārppāṉām. ittuṇaiyum pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam.
peṭṭavāyil peṟṟu iravu valiyuṟuppiṉum = aṅṅaṉam avaṉaip puṇaipeṟṟuniṉṟa talaivaṉ talaivikku vāyilātaṟku uriyārai yārāyntu palaruḷḷun talaiviyāṟ pēṇappaṭṭāḷ taṉakku vāyilān taṉmaiyaiyuṭaiya tōḻiyai avaḷ kuṟippiṉāṉ vāyilākap peṟṟu ivaḷai irantu piṉṉiṟpaleṉa valippiṉum: maṟaintu talaiviyaik kaṇṭuniṉṟāṉ avaṭku avaḷ iṉṟiyamaiyāmai kaṇṭu avaḷai vāyileṉat tuṇiyum. um. “talaippuṇaik koḷiṉē talaippuṇaik koḷḷuṅ – kaṭaippuṇaik koḷiṉē kaṭaippuṇaik koḷḷum – puṇaikai viṭṭup puṉalō ṭoḻuki – ṉāṇṭum varukuvaḷ pōlu māṇṭa – mārip pittikattu nīrvār koḻumukaic – cevveri nuṟaḻuṅ koḻuṅkaṭai maḻaikkaṭ – ṭuḷitalait talaiiya taḷiraṉ ṉōḷē.” itu talaivi avaṭku iṉaiyaḷeṉṟu karuti avaḷai vāyilākat tuṇintatu. aṉṟit tōḻi kūṟṟeṉiṟ talaiviyai arumaikūṟiṉaṉṟi ikkuṟai muṭippaleṉa ēṟṟukkoḷḷāḷ taṉakku ētamāmeṉṟu añci; aṉṟiyun tāṉē kuṟaiyuṟukiṉṟāṟku itukūṟip payantatūumiṉṟu. “maruntiṟ ṟīrā maṇṇi ṉākā - taruntavam” eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ iravuvaliyuṟṟatu.
ūrum peyarum keṭutiyum piṟavum nīriṟ kuṟippiṉ nirampak kūṟit tōḻiyaik kuṟaiyuṟum pakutiyum = tōḻiyai irantu piṉṉiṟṟalai valitta talaivaṉ, talaiviyun tōḻiyum oruṅku talaippeyta cevvi pārttāyiṉun, tōḻi taṉittuḻiyāyiṉum, numpatiyum peyarum yāvaiyeṉavum īṇṭu yāṉ keṭuttavai kāṭṭumiṉeṉavum aṉaiyaṉa piṟavaṟṟaiyum, akatteḻuntatōr iṉṉīrmai tōṉṟum ikkūṟṟu vēṟōr karuttu uṭaitteṉa avaḷ karutumāṟṟāṉum amaiyac collit, tōḻiyait taṉ kuṟaiyaṟivikkuṅ kūṟupāṭum: viṉāvuvāṉ ētilarpōla ūriṉai muṉ viṉāyc ciṟitu uṟavu tōṉṟap peyariṉaip piṉ viṉāy avviraṇṭiṉum māṟṟam peṟātāṉ oṉṟu keṭuttāṉākavum ataṉai avar kaṇṭār pōlavum kūṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ eṉṉiṉāyatōr kuṟaiyuṭaiyaṉeṉṟu avaḷ karutak kūṟumeṉpār nirampa veṉṟār. keṭutiyāvaṉa yāṉai puli mutaliyaṉavum neñcum uṇarvum iḻantēṉ avai kaṇṭīrōveṉavum viṉāvuvaṉa palavumām. piṟavumeṉṟataṉāl vaḻiviṉātalun taṉṉoṭu avariṭai uṟavu tōṉṟaṟpālaṉavuṅ kūṟutaluṅ koḷka. um. “aruvi yārkkum peruvarai naṇṇik – kaṉṟukāl yātta maṉṟap palaviṉ – vērkkoṇṭu tūṅkuṅ koḻuñcuḷaip perumpaḻaṅ – kuḻavic cētā mānti yayalatu – vēypayi liṟumpi ṉāmuṟal parukum – peruṅkal vēlic ciṟukuṭi yāteṉac – collavuñ collī rāyiṟ kalleṉak – karuvi māmaḻai vīḻnteṉa veḻunta – ceṅkē ḻāṭiya ceḻuṅkuraṟ ciṟutiṉaik – koypuṉaṅ kāvalu numatō – kōṭen talku ṉīṭō ḷīrē.” ūrum piṟavum viṉāyatu. “kalluṟṟa nōyvaruttak kālu naṭaiyaṟṟē – ṉelliṟ ṟiyāṉum varuntiṉēṉ – villuṟṟa – pūṅka ṇimaikkum puruva matimukattī – rīṅkituvō nummuṭaiya vūr.” itu ūr viṉāyatu. “ceṟikura lēṉaṟ ciṟukiḷi kāppī – raṟikuvē ṉummai viṉāa – yaṟipaṟavai – yaṉṉa nikarkkuñcī rāṭamai meṉṟōḷi – reṉṉa peyarirō nīr.” itu peyar viṉāyatu. “naṟaiparanta cānta maṟaveṟintu nāḷā – luṟaiyetirntu vittiyavū ḻēṉaṟ – piṟaiyetirnta – tāmaraipōla vāṇmukattut tāḻkuḻalīr kāṇīrō – vēmarai pōntaṉa vīṇṭu” “taṅkuṟippi ṉōrun talaicceṉṟu kaṇṭakkā – leṅkuṟippi ṉōmeṉ ṟikaḻntirār – nuṅkuṟippiṉ – veṉṟi paṭarneṭuṅkaṇ vēyttōḷīr kūṟīrō – vaṉṟi paṭarnta vaḻi.” vaṉṟi, paṉṟi. “taṇṭu puraikatirt tāḻkuraṟ centiṉai – maṇṭu pukavaru māṇṭakiḷi māṟṟu – moṇṭoṭip paṇaittō ḷoṇṇuta liḷaiyīr – kaṇṭaṉi rāyiṟ karavā turaimiṉ – koṇṭaṉa kuḻuvi ṉīṅki maṇṭiya – vuḷḷaḻi pakaḻiyō ṭuyaṅkiyōr – puḷḷi māṉkalai pōkiya neṟiyē.” ivai keṭutiviṉāyiṉa. “mellilaip parappiṉ viruntuṇ ṭiyāṉumik – kalleṉ ciṟukuṭit taṅkiṉmaṟ ṟevaṉō.” eṉavum, “illuṭaik kiḻamai yemmoṭu puṇariṟ – ṟītumuṇṭō māta rīrē.” eṉavum varuvaṉa piṟavumeṉṟataṉāṟ koḷka. kuṟaiyuṟūumpakuti kuṟaiyuṟupakuti eṉavumām; eṉavē, kuṟaiyuṟuvār collumāṟṟāṉē kaṇṇi mutaliya kaiyuṟaiyōṭu cēṟaluṅ koḷka. pakutiyeṉa varaiyātu kūṟaliṟ taṉittuḻip pakuti mutaliyaṉavum iruvarumuḷvaḻi ivaṉtalaippeytiyuṭaiyaṉ eṉat tōḻi uṇarumāṟum viṉāvutal koḷka. ivai kuṟaiyuṟavuṇartalum iruvarumuḷvaḻi avaṉ varavuṇartalumām. muṉṉuṟavuṇartal “nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamum” eṉpuḻik kūṟupa. matiyuṭampāṭu mūvakaiyaveṉa mēṟkūṟupa.
tōḻi kuṟai avaṭ cārtti meyyuṟak kūṟalum = tōḻi ivaṉ kūṟukiṉṟa kuṟai talaiviyiṭattēyāy iruntateṉṟu avaṇmēlē cērtti itaṉai uṇmaiyeṉṟu uṇarat talaivaṉ kūṟutalum: um. “karuṅkaṭa luṭkalaṅka nuṇvalai vīci – yoruṅkuṭaṉ ṟaṉṉaimār tanta koḻumī – ṉuṇaṅkalpuḷ ḷōppu moḷiyiḻai māta – raṇaṅkāku māṟṟa vemakku.” “paṇpum pāyaluṅ koṇṭaṉa ṭoṇṭit – taṇkamaḻ putumalar nāṟu moṇṭoṭi – yaitamarn takaṉṟa valkuṟ – koytaḷir mēṉi kūṟumati tavaṟē.” ivai veḷippaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupāṭu uṇarka. “kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ kuṉṟattuk kavāaṟ – paiñcuṉaip pūtta pakuvāyk kuvaḷaiyu – mañci lōti yacaiyiyaṟ koṭicci – kaṇpōṉ malarta laritiva – ṭaṉpōl cāyaṉ maññaikku maritē.” itu iruvarum uḷvaḻi vanta talaivaṉ talaivitaṉmai kūṟavē ivaḷ kaṇṇatu ivaṉ vēṭkaiyeṉṟu tōḻi kuṟippāṉ uṇarak kūṟiyatu. kuṉṟanāṭaṉ, murukaṉ; avaḷ tantaiyumām. “uḷḷiṟ kāṇpeṉ pōlvaṉ muḷḷeyiṟ – ṟamiḻta muṟuñ cevvāyk kamaḻaki – lāra nāṟu maṟalpōl kūntaṟ – pēramar maḻaikkaṇ maṭantai – mūraṉ muṟuvaloṭu mataiiya nōkkē.” ituvum atu. itu mutaliyavaṟṟait talaivaṉ kūṟṟākavē kūṟātu tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷākak kūṟi avviṭattut talaivaṉ maṭaṉmā kūṟumeṉṟu poruḷ kūṟiṉ “nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamum” eṉṉuñ cūttirattut tōḻi ivaṟṟaiyē kūṟiṉāḷeṉṟal vēṇṭāvām, atu kūṟiyatu kūṟalāmākaliṉ.
taṇṭātu irappiṉum = iṭantalaippāṭu mutaliya kūṭṭaṅkaḷāṉ amaiyātu piṉṉum pakaṟkuṟiyaiyum iravukkuṟiyaiyum vēṇṭiṉum: um. “koṇṭaṉ māmaḻai koṭkupu kuḻaitta – ciṟukō liṇara peruntaṇ cāntam – vakaicē raimpāṟ kaipeṟa vārip – pularviṭat tutirtta tukaḷpaṭu kūḻaip – peruṅkaṇ ṇāya muvappat tantai – neṭuntēr vaḻaṅku nilavumaṇaṉ muṟṟattup – pantoṭu peyarum parivi lāṭṭi – yaruḷiṉu maruḷā ḷāyiṉum peritaḻintu – piṉṉilai muṉiyaṉmā neñcē yeṉṉatūu – maruntuya ravalan tīrkku – maruntupiṟi tillai yāṉuṟṟa nōykkē.” parivilāṭṭiyaiyeṉa iraṇṭāvatu virikka. “kaṭuntē rēṟiyuṅ kāṉaliṟ ceṉṟuṅ – koṭuṅkaḻi maruṅki ṉaṭumpamalar koytuṅ – kaitai tūkkiyu neytal kuṟṟum – puṇarntām pōla vuṇarnta neñcamoṭu – vaikalu miṉaiya mākavuñ ceytārp – pacumpūṇ vēnta raḻinta pācaṟai – yoḷiṟuvē laḻuvattuk kaḷiṟupaṭap poruta – perumpuṇ ṇuṟunarkkup pēey pōlap – piṉṉilai muṉiyā namvayi – ṉeṉṉeṉa niṉaiyuṅkol paratavar makaḷē.” tōḻi namvayiṟ paratavar makaḷaiyeṉṉeṉa niṉaiyuṅkoleṉka. “pālotta veḷḷaruvi pāyntāṭip palpūppey – tālotta vaivaṉaṅ kāppāḷkaṇ – vēlotta – neñcamvāyp pukkoḻivu kāṇpā ḷevaṉkolō – vañcāyaṟ kēnōval yāṉ.” ivai pakaṟkuṟi irantaṉa. “ellu melliṉ ṟacaivuperi tuṭaiyēṉ – mellilaip parappiṉ viruntuṇa voruvaṉ” eṉat talaivaṉ iravukkuṟi vēṇṭiyataṉait tōḻi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. iṉṉum iraṭṭuṟamoḻital eṉpataṉāṟ taṇṭātu eṉpataṟkut tavirātu irappiṉumeṉap poruḷkūṟik kaiyuṟai koṇṭuvantu kūṟuvaṉavum nīrēvuvaṉa yāṉ ceyvēṉeṉak kūṟuvaṉavun tōḻi niṉṉāṟ karutappaṭuvāḷai aṟiyēṉeṉṟuḻi avaṉ aṟiyak kūṟuvaṉavum piṟavum vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉkaṇ aṭakkuka. um. “kavaḷak kaḷiyiyaṉmāl yāṉaiciṟ ṟāḷi – tavaḻattā ṉillā tatupōṟ – pavaḻak – kaṭikai yiṭaimuttaṅ kāṇṭoṟu nillā – toṭikai yiṭaimuttan tokku.” niṉ vāyitaḻaiyum eyiṟṟaiyuṅ kāṇuntōṟum nillā eṉ kaiyiṭattil irukkiṉṟa pavaḻakkoṭiyum muttum eṉka. “naṟavukkama ḻalari nāṟa vāyvirintu – niṟantikaḻ kamaḻu miṇaivāy neytaṟ – kaṇṇit talaiyar karuṅkaip paratavar – niṉṉaiya rallarō neṟitā ḻōti – yoṇcuṇaṅ kiḷamulai yoruñāṉṟu puṇari – ṉuṇka yiṟṟu valai numaroṭu vāṅkik – kaitai vēli yivvūrc – ceyṟūṭ ṭēṉō ceṟitoṭi yāṉē.” “aṟika vaḷaiya viṭaimaṭavā yāyañ – ciṟitavaḷcel lāḷiṟumeṉ ṟañcic – ciṟitava – ṇalkumvāy kāṇātu nainturuki yeṉṉeñca – molkumvā yolka luṟum.” eṉavarum.
maṟṟaiya vaḻiyum = kuṟiyetirppaṭṭuṅ kaiyuṟai maṟukkappaṭṭuṅ koṭukkappeṟṟum irantu piṉṉiṉṟāṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ kuṟiyetirppāṭiṉṟi āṟṟāṉāy irantu piṉṉiṟṟalai māṟumavviṭattum: um. “niṉceyal cettu nīpala vuḷḷip – perumpuṉa paitalai varunta laṉṟiyu – malaimicait toṭutta malintuce ṉīttan – talaināṇ māmalar taṇṭuṟaip parappiṟ – kaṭalkarai melikkuṅ kāvirip pēriyāṟ – ṟaṟalvār neṭuṅkayat tarunilai kalaṅka – māliru ṇaṭunāṭ pōkit taṉṉaiyar – kālait tanta kaṇaikkōṭṭu vāḷai – kataṟuva vāṅkun tīñcoṟ pāṇmaka – ṇeṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅku naṟavumali maṟukiṟ – paḻañcen nelliṉa mukavai koḷḷāḷ – kaḻaṅkuṟaḻ muttamoṭu nalkalam peṟūum – payaṅkeḻu vaippiṟ palavē levvi – nayampuri naṉmoḻi yaṭakkavu maṭaṅkā – ḷīṉpoṉ ṉiṇarnaṟu malarppuṉṉai veḵkit – titiyoṭu poruta vaṉṉi pōla – viḷikuvai kollō nīyē kiḷiyeṉac – ciṟiya miḻaṟṟuñ cevvāyp periya – kayaleṉa vamartta vuṇkaṭ puyaleṉap – puṟantāḻ piruḷiya piṟaṅkukura laimpāṉ – miṉṉēr maruṅkuṟ kuṟumakaḷ – piṉṉilai viṭāa maṭaṅkeḻu neñcē.” eṉavum, “māykatil vāḻiya neñcē nāḷu – melliyaṟ kuṟumaka ṇallaka nacaii – yaravirai tēru mañcuvaru ciṟuneṟi – yiraveṉa nīyum peṟāa yaruḷavar – pulleṉ kaṇṇai pulampukoṇ ṭulakat – tuḷḷavark kellām perunakai yākak – kāmaṅ kaimika vuṟutara – vāṉā varumpaṭar talaittan tōyē.” eṉavum varuvaṉa, taṉṉeñciṉai iravu vilakkiyaṉa.
collavaṭ cārttaliṟ pulliya vakaiyiṉum = tāṉ varuntik kūṟukiṉṟa kūṟṟiṉait talaiviyaic cārttit talaivaṉ kūṟaliṉ ivvāṟu āṟṟāṉāy iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉāṉeṉṟu añcit tōḻi uṇarāmaṟ talaivitāṉē kūṭiya pakutiyiṉum: “kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviya tākum” eṉpataṉāṟ talaiviyāṟ kuṟipeṟṟun tōḻiyai irakkum. um. “ayattu vaḷarpaiñ cāymurunti ṉaṉṉa – nakaippolin tilaṅku meyiṟukeḻu tuvarvā – yākat tarumpiya mulaiyaḷ paṇaittōṇ – māttāṭ kuvaḷai malarpiṇait taṉṉa – māyitaḻ maḻaikkaṇ māa yōḷoṭu – pēyu maṟiyā maṟaiyamai puṇarcci – pūcaṟ ṟuṭiyiṟ puṇarvupirin ticaippak – kāynta kāppoṭu nāñcelaṟ karumaiyiṟ – kaṭumpuṉaṉ malinta kāvirippēr yāṟṟu – neṭuñcuḻi nītta maṇṇunaḷ pōla – naṭuṅkañar petira muyaṅki neruna – lāka maṭaitan tōḷē veṉvēṟ – kaḷiṟukeḻu tāṉaip poṟaiyaṉ kolli – yoḷiṟunī raṭukkattu viyalakam poṟpak – kaṭavu ḷeḻutiya pāvaiyiṉ – maṭavatu māṇṭa māa yōḷē.” “aṇaṅkuṭaip paṉittuṟait toṇṭi yaṉṉa – maṇaṅkamaḻ poḻiṟkuṟi nalkiṉa ṇuṇaṅkiḻaip – poṅkari paranta vuṇka – ṇaṅkaluḻ mēṉi yacaiyiya lemakkē.” eṉavarum. vakaiyeṉṟataṉāṉē itaṉiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. “taḷircēr taṇṭaḻai taii nuntai – kuḷirvāy viyaṉpuṉat teṟpaṭa varukō – kuṟuñcuṉaik kuvaḷai yaṭaiccinām puṇariya – naṟuntaṇ kāṉa lāṭukam varukō – viṉcoṉ mēvalaip paṭṭave ṉeñcuṇak – kūṟiṉi maṭantainiṉ kūreyi ṟuṇkeṉa – yāṉṟaṉ moḻitaliṉ moḻiyetir vantu – tāṉcey kuṟinilai yiṉiya kūṟi – yēṟupiri maṭappiṇai pōla vēṟupaṭ – ṭuṟukaḻai nivappiṟ ciṟukuṭi peyaruṅ – koṭicci celpuṟa nōkki – viṭutta neñcam viṭalol lāvē.” “iraṇṭaṟi kaḷaviṉaṅ kāta lōḷē – muraṇko ṭuppiṟa cevvēṉ malaiyaṉ – muḷḷūrk kāṉa nāṟa vantu – naḷḷeṉ kaṅku ṉammō raṉṉaḷ – kūntal vēynta viravumala rutirttuc – cāntuḷar naṟuṅkatup peṇṇey nīvi – yamarā mukatta ḷākit – tamarō raṉṉaḷ vaikaṟai yāṉē.” eṉavarum.
aṟintōḷ ayarppiṉ avvaḻi maruṅkiṟ kēṭum pīṭuṅ kūṟalum = matiyuṭampaṭṭa tōḻi nīr kūṟiya kuṟaiyai yāṉ maṟantēṉeṉak kūṟumāyiṉ avviṭattut taṉṉoṭu kūṭāmaiyāṟ talaivimaruṅkiṟ piṟanta kēṭṭaiyum avaḷ ataṉai āṟṟiyirunta perumaiyaiyuṅ kūṟutalum: um. “oḷḷiḻai makaḷirō ṭōraiyu māṭāy – vaḷḷitaḻ neytaṟ ṟoṭalaiyum puṉaiyāy – viripūṅ kāṉa loruciṟai niṉṟō – yārai yōniṟ ṟoḻutaṉam viṉavutuṅ – kaṇṭār taṇṭā nalattait teṇṭiraip – peruṅkaṭaṟ parappi ṉamarntuṟai yaṇaṅkō – viruṅkaḻi maruṅki ṉilaipeṟ ṟaṉaiyō – colliṉi maṭantai yeṉṟaṉe ṉataṉetir – muḷḷeyiṟṟu muṟuvalun tiṟantaṉa – pallita ḻuṇkaṇum parantavāṟ paṉiyē.” “taṇṭaḻai cerīiyun taṇṇeṉa vuyirttuṅ – kaṇkaluḻ muttaṅ katirmulai yuṟaittu – māṟṟiṉa ḷeṉpatu kēṭṭaṉa māṟṟā – veṉṉiṉu mavaḷiṉu mikanta – viṉṉā mākka ṭaṭinaṉṉa rūrē.” “tōḷuṅ kūntalum palapā rāṭṭi – vāḻta lollumō maṟṟē ceṅkōṟ – kuṭṭuvaṉ ṟoṇṭi yaṉṉaveṟ – kaṇṭu nayantunī nalkāk kālē.” eṉavarum. ayarttatu avaḷ arumai tōṉṟutaṟku.
tōḻi nīkkaliṉākiya nilaimaiyum nōkki = tōḻi ivviṭattuk kāvalar kaṭiyareṉak kūṟic cēṭpaṭa niṟuttaliṟ taṉakku uṇṭākiya varuttattaiyum pārttu: ummai, ciṟappu. ituvē maṭaṉmā kūṟutaṟku ētuvāyiṟṟu. “nallurai yikantu pullurait tāayp – payanīrk kēṟṟa pacuṅkalam pōla – vuḷḷan tāṅkā veḷḷa nīnti – yaritayar vuṟṟaṉai neñcē yeṉṟum – peritā lammaniṉ pūcaluyar kōṭṭu – makavuṭai manti pōla – vakaṉuṟat taḻīik kēṭkunarp peṟiṉē.” “paṇaittōṭ kuṟumakaḷ pāvai taiiyum – pañcāyp paḷḷañ cūḻntu maṟṟiva – ḷurutteḻu vaṉamulai yoḷipeṟa veḻutiya – toyyil kāppō raṟitalu maṟiyār – muṟaiyuṭai yaracaṉ ceṅkōl vaiyat – tiyāṉṟaṟ kaṭaviṉ yāṅkā vatukol – peritum pētai maṉṟa – vaḷitō tāṉēyiv vaḻuṅka lūrē.” “uraitticiṟ ṟōḻiyatu puraittō vaṉṟē – turukkaṅ kamaḻu meṉṟō – ṭuṟappa veṉṟi yiṟīi yareṉṉuyirē.” eṉavarum.
maṭaṉmākūṟum iṭaṉumāruṇṭē = accēṭpaṭaiyāṉ maṭalēṟuvaleṉak kūṟum iṭaṉum uṇṭu. e-ṟu. nōkki maṭaṉmā kūṟumeṉka. um. “viḻuttalaip peṇṇai viḷaiyaṉ māmaṭaṉ – maṇiyaṇi peruntār mārpiṟ pūṭṭi – veḷḷeṉ paṇintupiṟa reḷḷat tōṉṟi – porunāṇa maruṅkiṟ perunā ṇīkkit – teruvi ṉiyaluvun taruvatu kollō – kaliṅkavi racainaṭaip pētai – melintila ṇāmviṭaṟ kamainta tūtē.” itu neñcoṭu kiḷattal. “nāṇāka nāṟu naṉaikuḻalā ṇalkatataṉ – pūṇāka nōvaḷavum pōkātu – pūṇāka – moṉṟēṉ ṟiraṇṭāva tuṇṭō maṭaṉmāmē – ṉiṉṟēṉ maṟukiṭaiyē nērntu.” itu tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu. ivai cākkāṭu kuṟittaṉa. “māveṉa maṭalu mūrpa pūveṉak – kuvimuki ḻerukkaṅ kaṇṇiyuñ cūṭupa – maṟuki ṉārkkavum paṭupa – piṟitu mākupa kāmaṅkāḻk koḷiṉē.” itaṉuṭ piṟitumākupa eṉṟatu varaipāytalai. iṭaṉeṉṟataṉāṟ tōḻi periyōrkkut takāteṉṟavaḻit talaivaṉ maṟuttuk kūṟuvaṉavum piṟavum vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. “nāṇoṭu nallāṇmai paṇṭuṭaiyē ṉiṉṟuṭaiyēṉ – kāmuṟṟā rēṟu maṭal.” eṉavarum. iṉi iṭaṉumeṉṟa ummaiyaip pirittu niṟīi irunāṉku kiḷaviyum makiḻcciyuṅ kalaṅkaluṅ kūṟum iṭaṉum uṇṭu, uraippiṉum peṭpiṉu muvappiṉu mirappiṉum vakaiyiṉuṅ kūṟum iṭaṉum uṇṭu, pakutikkaṇṇum maṟṟaiyavaḻikkaṇṇuṅ kūṟum iṭaṉum uṇṭu, meyyuṟakkūṟalum pīṭuṅkūṟalum uṇṭeṉa muṭikka. (11)
103. paṇpiṟ peyarppiṉum parivuṟṟu meliyiṉu
maṉpuṟṟu nakiṉu mavaṭpeṟṟu maliyiṉu
māṟṟiṭai yuṟutalu mavviṉaik kiyalpē.
ituvum iṟantupiṉṉiṟpuḻit talaivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) paṇpiṟ peyarppiṉum = tōḻi talaiviyatu iḷamai mutaliya paṇpukūṟi avvēṭkaiyai mīṭpiṉum: um. “kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kātaṉ maṭamakaḷ – vaṇṭupaṭu kūntaṟ ṟaṇṭaḻaik koṭicci – vaḷaiyaṇi muṉkai vāleyiṟ ṟarivai – yiḷaiya ḷāyiṉu māraṇaṅ kiṉaḷē.” itu iḷaiyaḷeṉap peyarttuḻit talaivaṉ kūṟiyatu.
parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum = iruvakaik kuṟiyiṭattun talaiviyai etirppaṭum ñāṉṟum etirppaṭṭa ñāṉṟum etirppaṭāniṉṟa ñāṉṟum palavāyavaḻi avaṉ parinta uḷḷattaṉāy meliyiṉum: um. “āṉā nōyō ṭaḻipaṭar kalaṅkik – kāmaṅ kaimikak kaiyaṟu tuyaraṅ – kāṇavu nalkā yāyiṟ pāṇar – paricil peṟṟa viriyuḷai naṉmāṉ – kavikuḷampu poruta kaṉmicaic ciṟuneṟi – yiravalar meliyā tēṟum poṟaiya – ṉuraicā luyarvaraik kollik kuṭavayi – ṉakalilaik kānta ḷalaṅkukulaip pāyntu – paṟavai yiḻaitta palka ṇiṟāaṟ – ṟēṉuṭai neṭuvarait teyva meḻutiya – viṉaimāṇ pāvai yaṉṉōḷ – kolaicūḻn taṉaḷā ṉōkō yāṉē.” itu pakaṟkuṟiyiṟ parivuṟṟatu. “ōtamu moliyō viṉṟē yūtaiyun – tātuḷar kāṉaṟ ṟapaveṉ ṟaṉṟē – maṇaṉmali mūtū rakaṉeṭun teruviṟ – kūkaic cēval kurālō ṭēṟi – yāriruñ catukkat tañcuvarak kuḻaṟu – maṇaṅku kālkiḷaru mayaṅkiru ṇaṭunāṭ – pāvai yaṉṉa palarāy vaṉappiṟ – ṟaṭameṉ paṇaittōṇ maṭamiku kuṟumakaḷ – cuṇaṅkaṇi vaṉamulai muyaṅka luḷḷi – mīṉkaṇa ṭuñcum poḻutum – yāṉkaṇ ṭuñcēṉ yātuko ṉilaiyē.” itu iravukkuṟiyiṟ parivuṟṟatu. “maḻaivara vaṟiyā maññai yālu – maṭukka ṉallūra cainaṭaik koṭicci – tāṉem maruḷā ḷāyiṉum – yāṉṟa ṉuḷḷupu maṟantaṟi yēṉē.” eṉavum, “kaḷittoṟuṅ kaḷḷuṇṭal vēṭṭaṟṟāṟ kāmam – veḷippaṭun tōṟu miṉitu.” eṉavum varum. iṉṉum parivuṟṟumeliyiṉum eṉṟataṉāṉē puṇarntu nīṅkuntalaivaṉ āṟṟātu kūṟuvaṉavum, vaṟum puṉaṅ kaṇṭu kūṟuvaṉavum, iṟceṟippaṟivuṟuppa āṟṟāṉāyk kūṟuvaṉavun, tōḻi iṟpuṇarccikkaṭ taṉṉilaikkoḷīik kūṟuvaṉavum, iravukkuṟikkaṇ varukiṉṟāṉ talaiviyai aiyuṟṟup pāṅkaṟkuk kūṟuvaṉavum, piṟavum vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉkaṇ amaikka. “eṉṟu miṉiya ḷāyiṉum pirita – loṉṟu miṉṉā ḷaṉṟē neñcam – paṉimaruntu viḷaikkum parūukka ṇiḷamulaip – paṭucāntu citaiya muyaṅkuñ – ciṟukuṭik kāṉavaṉ perumaṭa makaḷē.” “kōṭāp pukaḻmāṟaṉ kūṭa laṉaiyāḷai – yāṭā vaṭakiṉuḷuṅ kāṇēṉpōr – vāṭāk – karuṅkōlvēṉ maṉṉar kalampukka kollō – maruṅkulkom paṉṉāṇ mayir.” “peṟuva tiyaiyā tāyiṉu muṟuvatoṉ – ṟuṇṭumaṉ vāḻiya neñcē tiṇṭērk – kaivaḷa rōri kāṉan tīṇṭi – yeṟivaḷi kamaḻu neṟipaṭu kūntaṉ – maii yīi rōti māayōḷ – vayiṉiṟai yaṉṉa naṭpi ṉiṉṉō – yiṟumuṟai yeṉavoṉ ṟiṉṟi – maṟumai yulakattu maṉṉutal peṟiṉē.” “nōyu nekiḻcciyum viṭacciṟan taṉavē – vēyvaṉap puṟṟa tōḷai nīyē – yeṉṉuḷ varutiyō veḻiṉaṭaik koṭicci – murukupuṇarn tiyaṉṟa vaḷḷi pōlaniṉ – ṉuruvukaṇ ṇeṟippa nōkkāṟ ṟalaṉē – pōkiya nākap pōkkaruṅ kavalaic – ciṟukaṭ paṉṟip peruñciṉa voruttal – cēṟā ṭirumpuṟa nīṟoṭu civaṇa – veḷvacip paṭīiya moyttaveṉpa vaḻīik – kōṉāy koṇṭa koḷḷaik – kāṉavar peyarntārtañ ciṟukuṭi yāṉē.” “mayilkoṉ maṭavāḷkoṉ mānīrt tiraiyuṭ – payilvatōr teyvaṅkol kēḷīr – kuyilpayiluṅ – kaṇṇi yiḷañāḻaṟ pūmpoḻi ṉōkkiya – kaṇṇiṉ varuntumeṉ ṉeñcu.” eṉavarum.
aṉpu uṟṟu nakiṉum = tōḻi kuṟaimaṟuppuḻi aṉputōṉṟanakiṉum: um. “nayaṉiṉ maiyiṟ payaṉitu veṉṉātu – pūmpoṟip polinta vaḻalumi ḻakaṉpaip – pāmpuyi raṇaṅki yāṅku mīṅkitu – takāatu vāḻiyō kuṟumaka ṇakāa – turaimati yuṭaiyumeṉ ṉuḷḷañ cāraṟ – koṭuviṟ kāṉavaṉ kōṭṭumā tolaiccip – paccūṉ peyta pakaḻi pōlac – cēyari paranta māyitaḻ maḻaikka – ṇuṟāa nōkka muṟṟaveṉ – paita ṉeñca muyyu māṟē.” itu aṉpuṟṟu nakkuḻit talaivaṉ kūṟiyatu.
avaṭ peṟṟu maliyiṉum = tōḻi uṭampāṭupeṟṟu maṉam makiḻiṉum: um. “emakkunayan taruḷiṉai yāyiṟ paṇaittō – ḷoṇṇuta larivaiyoṭu meṉmela viyali – vanticiṉ vāḻiyō maṭantai – toṇṭi yaṉṉaniṉ paṇpupala koṇṭē.” itu avaṭpeṟṟu malintu talaivaṉ kūṟiyatu. iṉṉum avaṭpeṟṟu maliyiṉum eṉṟataṟku iraṭṭuṟamoḻitaleṉṟataṉāṟ talaiviyaip pakaṟkuṟiyiṉum iravukkuṟiyiṉum peṟṟu makiḻiṉum eṉṟu poruḷuraikka. “naṉṟē ceyta vutavi naṉṟuterin – tiyāmevaṉ ceykuva neñcē kāmar – melliyaṟ koṭicci kāppap – palkura lēṉaṟ pāttaruṅ kiḷiyē.” itu kiḷi puṉattiṉkaṭ paṭikiṉṟateṉṟu talaiviyaik kākka ēviyataṉai aṟinta talaivaṉ avaḷaip peṟṟēmeṉa makiḻntu kūṟiyatu. “kāṇiṟ kuvaḷai kaviḻntu nilaṉōkku – māṇiḻai kaṇṇovvē meṉṟu.” itu pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ avaṭpeṟṟu malintatu. “kūṟuvaṅ kollō kūṟalēṅ kolleṉak – karanta kāmaṅ kainiṟuk kallātu – nayantu nāmviṭṭa naṉmoḻi nampi – yaraināḷ yāmattu viḻumiḻai karantu – kārvirai kamaḻuṅ kūnta ṟūviṉai – nuṇṇū lākam poruntiṉaḷ veṟpi – ṉiṉamaḻai cūḻnta maṭamayil pōla – vaṇṭu vaḻippaṭarat taṇmalar vēyntu – vilvakup puṟṟa nalvāṅku kuṭaccū – lañcilam poṭukki yañciṉaḷ vantu – tuñcūr yāmattu muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvō – ḷāṉṟa kaṟpiṟ cāṉṟa pēriya – lammā varivaiyō valla ṭeṉāa – tāynta naṉṉāṭ ṭaṇaṅkuṭaic cilampiṟ – kaviram periya vurukeḻu kālāa – ṉērmalar niṟaiccuṉai yuṟaiyuñ – cūrmakaṇ mātō veṉṉumeṉ ṉeñcē.” eṉavarum. “viṇṇakam viḷakkal vēṇṭi nammiṟ – piriyiṉum piriyumō peruntōṭ koṭicci – vāṉañ cūṭiya tilakam pōla – vōṅkiru vicumpiṉuṅ kāṇṭu – mīṅkuṅ kāṇṭu mivaḷciṟu nutalē.” ituvum atu.
āṟṟiṭai uṟutalum = talaivaṉ cellum neṟikkaṇ iṭaiyūṟu tōṉṟiṉa iṭattum: eṉṟatu, talaiviyun tōḻiyum varuvaḻiyarumai kūṟiyavaḻit talaivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻumeṉṟavāṟu: um. “kuruti vēṭkai yurukeḻu vayamāṉ – valimiku muṉpiṉ maḻakaḷiṟu pārkku – marampayil cōlai maliyap pūḻiya – ruruvat turuvi ṉāṇmēya lāru – māri yeṇkiṉ malitaru nīḷiṭai – nīnayantu varuta levaṉeṉap palapulan – taḻutaṉai yuṟaiyu mammā varivai – payaṅkeḻu palaviṉ kollik kuṭavaraip – pūtam puṇartta putitiyal pāvai – virikati riḷaveyiṟ ṟōṉṟi yaṉṉaniṉ – ṉāynala muḷḷi variṉemak – kēma māku malaimuta lāṟē.” eṉat talaivi āṟṟiṉatarumai kūṟiyataṟkut talaivaṉ kūṟiyatu. iraṭṭuṟamoḻitaleṉpataṉāṉ āṟṟiṭaiyuṟutaṟku varaiviṭaivaittup pirintāṉ āṟṟiṭai varuttamuṟṟuk kūṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. atu pōkiṉṟāṉ kūṟuvaṉavum mīṇṭavaṉ pāṅkaṟkuk kūṟuvaṉavumām. “ōmpumati vāḻiyō vāṭai pāmpiṉ – ṟūṅkutōl kaṭukkun tūveḷ ḷaruvik – kalluyar naṇṇi yatuvē nelli – maraiyiṉa māru muṉṟiṟ – pulvēy kurampai nallō ḷūrē.” “kavalai keṇṭiya vakalvāyc ciṟukuḻi – koṉṟai poṉvī tāayc celvar – poṉcey pēḻai mūytiṟan taṉṉa – kāretir puṟaviṉatu vēyuyarn tōrkku – nīroṭu corinta miccil yāvarkkum – varaikō ḷaṟiyāc coṉṟi – niraikōṟ kuṟuntoṭi tantai yūrē.” eṉavarum.
avviṉaikku iyalpē = attōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṭattut talaivaṉkūṟṟu nikaḻvatākiya ilakkaṇamām. e-ṟu. (12)
104. pāṅkaṉi mittam paṉṉiraṇ ṭeṉpa.
itu mēṟ pāṅkinimittaṅ kūṟiya atikārattāṉē pāṅkaṉimittaṅ kūṟukiṉṟār. vāyilpeṭpiṉumeṉṟa pāṅkaṉimittam pōlātu itu vēṟupaṭakkūṟaliṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti vakuttatu; eṉṉai? pāṅkaṉ talaiviyai etirppaṭṭu vantu talaivaṟku uraittalaṉṟik kāḷaiyaroṭu kaṉṉiyarai ulakiyalāṟ puṇarkkumāṟu puṇarkkun tuṇaiyēyākaliṉ. (i-ḷ.) akaṉaintiṇaiyum allātavaḻip pāṅkaṉkaṇṇavākiya nimittam paṉṉirupakutiyavām. e-ṟu. eṇvakai maṇattiṉum iṭainiṉṟu puṇarkkum pārppāṉ iruvakaik kōttiram mutaliyaṉavun tāṉaṟintu iṭainiṉṟu puṇarttal vaṉmai avarpuṇartaṟku nimittamātaliṉ avai avaṉkaṇṇaveṉappaṭum. ivaṉaip pirācāpatiyeṉpa. nimittamuṅ kāraṇamum oṉṟu. kāraṇam paṉṉiraṇṭeṉavē kāriyamum paṉṉiraṇṭām: avai eṇvakai maṇaṉumātaliṉ avaṟṟaik kaikkiḷai mutaliya ēḻutiṇaikkum iṉṉavāṟu uriyaveṉa varukiṉṟa cūttiraṅkaḷāṟ paṉṉirupakutiyum aṭaṅkak kūṟupa. avvāṟṟāṉē piramam pirācāpattiyam āriṭan teyvam eṉavum, mullai kuṟiñci pālai marutam neytal eṉavum, acuram irākkatam pēy eṉavum paṉṉiraṇṭām. piramam mutaliya nāṉkaṟkum pāṅkaṉ ētuvākaliṉ ivvāṟu piramacariyaṅ kāttāṉeṉavum, ivaṉ iṉṉa kōttirattāṉ ākaliṉ ivaṭku uriyaṉeṉavum, ivaḷai iṉṉavāṟṟāṟ koṭukkat takumeṉavum, iṉṉōṉai ācāriyaṉākak koṇṭu vēḷviceytu maṟṟikkaṉṉiyaik koṭukkat takumeṉavuñ collip puṇarkkumeṉpatu. iṉi yāḻōr kūṭṭattuḷ aintiṇaiyumāyiṟ pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟa vakaiyāṉē mutal karu uripporuḷ varaiyaṟaipaṟṟi muṟaiciṟantu varutalum peyarkoḷap peṟāmaiyum uṭaiyavaṉṟē, avvāṟaṉṟi īṇṭuk koḷkiṉṟa yāḻōrkūṭṭattu aintiṇaiyumāyiṉ avvavanilattiyalpāṉum piṟapāṭaiyoḻukkattāṉum vēṟupaṭṭa vēṟupāṭupaṟṟiyuñ cuṭṭiyoruvarp peyarkoṭuttum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa ulakiyalāṉ ellāraiyum iṭainiṉṟu puṇarppāruḷvaḻi avvavanimittaṅkaḷum vēṟuvēṟāki varum pāṅkaṉ nimittaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iṅṅaṉam aintiṇaippakutiyum pāṅkaṉimittamāṅkāl vēṟupaṭumeṉavē pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṟpaṭṭa iruvakaikkaikōḷum pōlā ivaiyeṉpatūum, avvavanilattiṉ makkaṭkut takka maṉṟalum vēṟākaliṉ avarkkum pāṅkar uḷareṉpatūum, ivvāṟṟāṉ eṇvakaimaṇaṉum uṭaṉōtavē ivaiyum oḻinta eḻuvakaimaṇaṉum pōla akappuṟameṉappapaṭumeṉpatūuṅ koḷka. iṉi acurattaṉmaiyāḷaik kuravar iṉṉavāṟu koṭuppar nīyuñ cēṟiyeṉṟu oruvaṉ pāṅkupaṭak kūṟalum, ivaṉai avaṭkuk kāṭṭi ivaṉ iṉṉaṉeṉṟu oruvaṉ iṭainiṉṟu kūṟalum uṇmaiyiṉ atuvum pāṅkaṉimittamuṭaittu. irākkatattiṟkum ittaṉmaiyāḷ iṉṉuḻi iruntāḷeṉṟu pāṅkāyiṉār kūṟakkēṭṭu oruvaṉ valitiṟpaṟṟik kōṭaliṉ ituvum pāṅkaṉimittamuṭaittu. pēykkum poruntuvatu aṟiyātāṉ iṭainiṉṟu puṇarppiṉ ataṟkum atu nimittamām. ippaṉṉiraṇṭun toṉmaiyun tōlumeṉṟa vaṉappiṉuḷ varuvaṉa. (13)
105. muṉṉaiya mūṉṟuṅ kaikkiḷaik kuṟippē
piṉṉar nāṉkum peruntiṇai peṟumē.
mēṟ paṉṉiraṇṭeṉappaṭṭa eṇvakai maṇattiṉuḷ ēḻaṉai eḻutiṇaiyuḷ iṉṉatiṇaippāṟpaṭum eṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) muṉṉaiya mūṉṟum kaikkiḷaik kuṟippē = itaṟku muṉ niṉṟa acuramum irākkatamum paicācamuṅ kaikkiḷaiyeṉṟaṟkuc ciṟantilavēṉuṅ kaikkiḷaiyeṉac cuṭṭappaṭum: piṉṉar nāṉkum peruntiṇai peṟumē = piṉṉar niṉṟa piramam pirācāpattiyam āriṭan teyvameṉṉum nāṉkiṉaiyum peruntiṇai taṉakku iyalpākavē peṟumeṉavuṅ kūṟappaṭum. e-ṟu. maṉṟal eṭṭu eṉṟa varalāṟṟu muṟaiyāṉē vāḷātu paṉṉiraṇṭeṉṟār eṉpatē paṟṟi īṇṭum ammuṟaiyāṉē iṭavakaiyāṉ muṉṉaiya mūṉṟum piṉṉar nāṉkumeṉṟār; eṉavē, iṉik kūṟum yāḻōr mēṉa aintum oṉṟāka avviraṇṭaṟkum iṭaiyateṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. villēṟṟiyāyiṉuṅ kollēṟutaḻīiyāyiṉuṅ koḷvaleṉṉum uḷḷattaṉāvāṉ talaivaṉēyātaliṉ ataṉai muṟpaṭappiṟanta aṉpumuṟaipaṟṟi orutalaikkāmamākiya kaikkiḷaiyeṉṟār. irākkatam valitiṉ maṇañceytalātaliṉ atuvum appāṟpaṭum; pēyum appāṟpaṭum. ivai muṉṉaiya mūṉṟuṅ kaikkiḷaiyāyavāṟu. kāmañcālā iḷamaiyōḷvayiṟ kaikkiḷai ciṟappuṭaitteṉṟaṟkup pullittōṉṟumeṉak kūṟi itaṉai vāḷātu kuṟippeṉṟār; āṇṭup piṟkālattaṉṟik kāṭcikkaṇ maṇam ataṟkiṉmaiyiṉ īṇṭu maṇaṅ kūṟumvaḻik kūṟātu akattiṇaiyiyaluṭ kūṟiṉār. ēṟiya maṭaṉmā mutaliyavaṟṟaip peruntiṇaikkuṟippēyeṉak kūṟi īṇṭup peruntiṇai peṟumēyeṉṟār, avai ciṟappila ivai ciṟappuṭaiyaveṉṟaṟku. innāṉkum orutalaikkāmam paṟṟi nikaḻāmaiyāṉum oruvaṉōṭoruttiyai etirniṟīi avaruṭampāṭṭōṭu puṇarkkuṅ kantaruvamaṉmaiyāṉum avaṟṟiṉ vēṟākiya peruntiṇaiyām. (14)
105. mutaloṭu puṇarnta yāḻōr mēṉa
tavalaruñ ciṟappi ṉainnilam peṟumē.
itu appaṉṉiraṇṭaṉuḷ iṭaiyatāy oḻinta aintuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) mutaloṭu puṇarnta yāḻōr mēṉa = mēṟkūṟiya naṭuvaṇaintiṇaiyun tamakkumutalāka avaṟṟōṭu poruntivaruṅ kantaruvamārkkam aintum: tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉ ainnilam peṟumē = keṭalaruñ ciṟappoṭu poruntiya aivakai nilaṉum peṟutaliṉ avai ainteṉappaṭum. e-ṟu. eṉavē, mutaṟkantaruvam aintumēyaṉṟi avaṟṟōṭu poruttamuṭaiya kantaruvam ivvaintumeṉa vēṟupaṭuttiṉār. ivai appaṉṉiraṇṭaṉuṭ kūṟāniṉṟa aintum mutaloṭu puṇarntaveṉṟē oḻiyātu piṉṉum yāḻōrmēṉa veṉṟār ivaiyuṅ kantaruvamē eṉṟaṟku. ivaiyum oruvaṉ oruttiyetirniṉṟu uṭampaṭuttaloppumaiyuṭaiya keṭalaruñ ciṟappapeṉavē mutal karu uripporuḷāṉuṅ kaḷaveṉṉuṅ kaikōḷāṉum pāṅki puṇarttaliṉmaiyāṉum ilakkaṇaṅ kuṟaippaṭṭatēṉuñ cuṭṭiyoruvarp peyarkoḷḷappaṭṭuk kaṟpiyalākiya ilvāḻkkaiyum peṟṟuvarutaṟ ciṟappuṭaiya ivaiyum ainnilam peṟutalēyaṉṟiyeṉṟāṉām. itu pulaṉeṟiyaṉṟi ulakiyalākaliṉ ulakiyalāṟ pālainilaṉum āṇṭuvāḻvārkku maṉṟalum uḷatākaliṟ pālaiyuṅ kūṟiṉār. eṉavē, aimpulattu vāḻvār maṇamuñ ceyyuḷuṭ pāṭiyakkāl iḻukkiṉṟeṉṟār. (15)
106. iruvakaik kuṟipiḻaip pākiya viṭattuṅ
kāṇā vakaiyiṟ poḻutunaṉi yikappiṉun
tāṉakam pukāaṉ peyarta liṉmaiyiṟ
kāṭci yācaiyiṟ kaḷampukkuk kalaṅki
vēṭkaiyiṉ mayaṅkiya kaiyaṟu poḻutiṉum
pukāak kālaip pukketirp paṭṭuḻip
pakāa viruntiṉ pakutik kaṇṇum
vēḷā ṇetirum viruntiṉ kaṇṇum 
vāḷā ṇetirum pirivi ṉāṉu
nāṇuneñ calaippa viṭuttaṟ kaṇṇum
varaital vēṇṭit tōḻi ceppiya
puraitīr kiḷavi pulliya vetirum
varaivuṭaṉ paṭutalu māṅkataṉ puṟattup
puraipaṭa vanta maṟuttaloṭu tokaiik
kiḻavōṇ mēṉa veṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu mēṟ talaivaṟkuriya kiḷavikūṟip pāṅkaṉimittam avaṉkaṇ nikaḻum pakutiyuṅ kūṟi ammuṟaiyāṉē talaivikkuriya kiḷavi kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) iruvakaikkuṟi piḻaippu ākiya iṭattum = iravukkuṟiyum pakaṟkuṟiyum piḻaittaviṭattum um. “muḻavumuta laraiya taṭavunilaip peṇṇaik – koḻumaṭa liḻaitta ciṟukōṟ kuṭampaik – karuṅkā laṉṟiṟ kāmar kaṭuñcūl – vayavuppeṭai yakavum pāṉāṭ kaṅkuṉ – maṉṟam pōḻu miṉamaṇi neṭuntēr – vārā tāyiṉum varuvatu pōlac – cevimuta licaikku maravamoṭu – tuyiṟuṟan taṉavāṟ ṟōḻiyeṉ kaṇṇē.” “koṉṉūr tuñciṉum yāntuñ calamē – yemmi layala tēḻi lumpar – mayilaṭi yilaiya mākkura ṉocci – yaṇimiku meṉkom pūḻtta – maṇimaruḷ pūviṉ pāṭunaṉi kēṭṭē.” “ēṟiraṅ kiruḷiṭai yiraviṉiṟ patampeṟāṉ – māṟiṉē ṉeṉakkūṟi maṉaṅkoḷḷun tāṉeṉpa – kūṭutal vēṭkaiyāṟ kuṟipārttuk kuraṉoccip – pāṭōrkkuñ ceviyōṭu paitalēṉ yāṉāka.” eṉavarum. “iruḷvī neyta litaḻakam poruntik – kaḻutukaṇ paṭukkum pāṉāṭ kaṅku – lemmiṉu muyavuti centalai yaṉṟil – kāṉalañ cērppaṉ pōla niṉpū – neṟṟic cēvalum poyttaṉṟō kuṟiyē.” itu taṉṉuṭkaiyā ṟeytiṭukiḷavi. “puṉkaṇkūr mālaip pulampumeṉ kaṇṇēpōṟ – ṟuṉpa muḻavāy tuyilap peṟutiyā – liṉkaḷavāy neytāṉī yeytuṅ kaṉaviṉuḷ – vaṉkaṇār kāṉal varakkaṇ ṭaṟitiyō.” eṉavum ivai kuṟipiḻaittuḻit taṉvayiṉurimaiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattaimaiyumpaṭak kūṟiyaṉavām. kuṟipiḻaittalāvatu puṉalolippaṭuttalum puḷḷeṭupputalum mutaliyaṉa. kuṟiyeṉak kuṟittavaḻi avaṉāṉaṉṟi avai vēṟōr kāraṇattāṉ nikaḻntuḻi ataṉaik kuṟiyeṉa niṉaintuceṉṟu avai avaṉ kuṟiyaṉmaiyiṉ akaṉṟumāṟutalām. pakaṟkuṟikku utāraṇam vantuḻikkāṇka.
kāṇā vakaiyiṟ poḻutu naṉi ikappiṉum = kuṟivaḻic cellun talaivaṉai iṟṟaiñāṉṟiṟ kāṇṭal ariteṉṟu kaiyaṟuvatōrāṟṟāṟ poḻutu cēṭkaḻiyiṉum: eṉṟatu, tāy tuñcāmai ūrtuñcāmai kāvalarkaṭukutal nilavuveḷippaṭutal nāytuñcāmai pōlvaṉavaṟṟāṟ talaivaṉkuṟiyiṉkaṇ talaivi varappeṟāmal nīṭṭittalām. um. “irumpiḻi makāariv vaḻuṅkaṉ mūtūr – viḻaviṉ ṟāyiṉun tuñcā tāku – malla lāvaṇa maṟukuṭaṉ maṭiyiṉ – valluraik kaṭuñco laṉṉai tuñcāḷ – piṇiko ḷaruñciṟai yaṉṉai tuñciṟ – ṟuñcāk kaṇṇar kāvalar kaṭukuva – rilaṅkuvē liḷaiyar tuñciṉ vaiyeyiṟṟu – valañcurit tōkai ñāḷi maruḷu – maravavāy ñamali kuraiyātu maṭiyiṟ – pakaluru vuṟaḻa nilavukāṉṟu vicumpi – ṉakalvāy maṇṭila niṉṟuviri yummē – tiṅka ḷakalcērpu kaṉaiyiṟuṇ maṭiyi – ṉilleli valci valvāyk kūkai – kaḻutuvaḻaṅ kiyāmat taḻitakak kuḻaṟum – vaḷaikaṭ cēval vāḷātu maṭiyiṉ – maṉaicceṟi kōḻi māṇkura liyampu – mellā maṭinta kālat torunā – ṇillā neñcat tavarvā ralarē – yataṉāl, aripey puṭṭi lārppap pariciṟan – tāti pōkiya pāypari naṉmā – nocci vēlit tītta ṉuṟantaik – kaṉmutir puṟaṅkāṭ ṭaṉṉa – paṉmuṭ ṭiṉṟāṟ ṟōḻinaṅ kaḷavē.” “karuṅkāl vēṅkai vīyuku tuṟuka – lirumpulik kuruḷaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟuṅ kāṭṭiṭai – yelli varunar kaḷaviṟku – nallai yallai neṭuveṇ ṇilavē.” “vāḷvari vēṅkai vaḻaṅkuñ ciṟuneṟiyeṅ – kēḷvarum pōḻti leḻāalvāḻi veṇṭiṅkāḷ – kēḷvarum pōḻti leḻātāyk kuṟāliyarō – nīḷvari nākat teyiṟēvāḻi veṇṭiṅkāḷ.” ituvum atu.
tāṉakam pukāaṉ peyartaliṉmaiyiṟ kāṭci ācaiyiṟ kaḷampukkuk kalaṅki vēṭkaiyiṉ mayaṅkiya kaiyaṟu poḻutiṉum = aṅṅaṉaṅ kāṇāvakaiyiṟ poḻutu naṉiyikantu talaivi kuṟi tappiyakkālun talaivaṉ kuṟiyiṭam pukuntallatu peyarāṉeṉpatu tāṉ aṟiyumātaliṉ āṇṭup pukuntavaṉ tāṉ vantu nīṅkiṉamai aṟitaṟku ōr kuṟi ceytaṉṟi vāḷātu peyarāṉaṉṟē akkuṟikāṇuṅ kāṭciviruppiṉāṟ talaivi piṟṟaiñāṉṟu viṭiyaliṟceṉṟu āṇṭaik kuṟikaṇṭu kalaṅki avaṉai etirppaṭutal vēṭkaiyaḷākic ceyvataṟiyātu mayakkattōṭu kaiyaṟavu eytumpoḻutiṉkaṇṇum: tāṉ eṉṟatu talaivaṉai. iravukkuṟiyiṉai akameṉṟār, iravukkuṟi eyilakattatu eṉpataṉāl. kuṟiyiṟceṉṟu nīṅkuvaṉeṉavē kāṭci avaṉmēṟṟaṉṟik kuṟimēṟṟām. kuṟi mōtiram mālai muttam mutaliyaṉa kōṭṭiṉuṅ koṭiyiṉum iṭṭuvaittaṉavām; ivai varuttattiṟku ētuvām. itu viṭiyal nikaḻumeṉṟaṟkup poḻuteṉṟāṉ; eṉavē kāṇpaṉa viṭiyaliṟ kāṇumeṉṟār. mayaṅkum eṉṟataṉāṟ tōḻiyum uṭaṉmayaṅkum. atu “ikkāntaṇ meṉmukaimēl vaṇṭaṉṟaḵ timmukaiyiṟ – kaikkāntaṇ melviralāy kāṇitō – pukkuc – ceṟintatupōṟ ṟōṉṟun toṭupoṟi yāmpaṇ – yaṟintatoṉ ṟaṉṉa tuṭaittu.”
pukāak kālaip pukku etirppaṭṭuḻip pakāa viruntiṉ pakutikkaṇṇum = uṇṭikkālattut talaiviyillattut talaivaṉ pukketirppaṭṭavaḻi nīkki niṟuttāta viruntu ēṟṟukkoḷḷum pakutikkaṇṇum: eṉavē, maṉaiyakam pukutaṟku ovvātamikka talaivaṉ pukuntāl iḵtoṉṟuṭaitteṉat tērātu tāy avaṉai viruntēṟṟu nīkki niṟuttaṟpakutiyun taḻīiyiṉavāṟāyiṟṟu. “pukākkālamātaliṟ pakāvirunteṉṟāṉ; viṭiyaṟkālamāyiṟ talaivaṉ pukāṉeṉavum pukākkālattup pukkañāṉṟāyiṉ avar viruntēṟṟukkōṭal orutalaiyeṉṟu pukumeṉṟuṅ koḷka. talaivi kāṭciyācaiyiṟ kalaṅkiya ataṟkēṟpat talaivaṟkuṅ kāṭciyācai kūṟiṟṟu; atu “cuṭarttoṭīi kēḷāy teruviṉā māṭu – maṇaṟciṟṟil kāliṟ citaiyā vaṭarcciya – kōtai parintu varippantu koṇṭōṭi – nōtakka ceyyuñ ciṟupaṭṭi mēlainā – ḷaṉṉaiyum yāṉu miruntēmā villilē – yuṇṇunīr vēṭṭē ṉeṉavantāṟ kaṉṉai – yaṭarpoṟ cirakattāl vākkic cuṭariḻā yuṇṇunī rūṭṭivā veṉṟā ḷeṉayāṉun – taṉṉai yaṟiyātu ceṉṟēṉmaṟ ṟeṉṉai – vaḷaimuṉkai paṟṟi naliyat terumantiṭ – ṭaṉṉā yivaṉoruvaṉ ceytatukā ṇeṉṟēṉā – vaṉṉai yalaṟip paṭartarat taṉṉaiyā – ṉuṇṇunīr vikkiṉā ṉeṉṟēṉā vaṉṉaiyun – taṉṉaip puṟampaḻittu nīvamaṟ ṟeṉṉaik – kaṭaikkaṇṇāṟ kolvāṉpō ṉōkki nakaikkūṭṭañ – ceytāṉak kaḷvaṉ makaṉ.” itu pukākkālattup pukkāṉai viruntēṟṟukkoṇṭamai iṉṉorukālattut talaivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. “aṉṉai vāḻka palavē teṇṇī – riruṅkaṭal vēṭṭa mentai pukkeṉat – tārmaṇi neṭuntēr nīvi yāṉumō – rellamai viruntiṉe ṉeṉṟa – mellam pulampaṉait taṅkeṉ ṟōḷē.” itu tōḻikūṟṟumām. oṉṟiya tōḻiyeṉṟataṉāṟ tōḻikūṟṟu vantuḻik kāṇka. “maṇṇiya ceṉṟa voṇṇuta larivai – puṉṟaru pacuṅkāy tiṉṟataṉ ṟappiṟ – koṉpatiṟ ṟoṉpatu kaḷiṟṟō ṭavaṇiṟai – poṉcey pāvai koṭuppavuṅ koḷḷāṉ – peṇkolai purinta naṉṉaṉ pōla – varaiyā nirayattuc celiyarō vaṉṉai – yorunā ṇakaimuka viruntiṉaṉ vanteṉap – pakaimuka vūriṟ ṟuñcalō vilaḷē.” itu pukākkālattut talaimaimikka talaivaṉ pukkataṟku viruntēlātu cevili iravun tuyilātāḷait talaivi muṉintu kūṟiyatu.
vēḷāṇ etirum viruntiṉ kaṇṇum = aṅṅaṉam viruntātalēyaṉṟit talaivi vēḷāṇmaiceyya etirkoḷḷak karututal kāraṇattāṟ tōḻi avaṉai viruntēṟṟuk kōṭaṟkaṇṇum: eṉṟatu, talaivi avaṟku upakārañceyyak karuti ataṉaik kuṟippāṟ kūṟat tōḻi avaṉai viruntāyt taṅkeṉṉum. um. “nāḷvalai mukanta kōḷvala paratavar – nuṇaṅkumaṇa lāṅka ṇuṇaṅkap peymār – paṟikoḷ koḷḷaiyar maṟuka vukka – mīṉār kurukiṉ kāṉalam peruntuṟai – yellai taṇpoḻiṟ ceṉṟeṉac cēlīi – yātō pūṭṭayar vēeyār kōoṟ – ceṟitoṭi tiruttip pāṟumayir nīvic – celliṉi maṭantainiṉ ṟōḻiyoṭu maṉaiyeṉac – colliya vaḷavait tāṉperitu kaviḻntu – tīṅkā yiṉaḷiva ḷāvayiṟ ṟaṅkātu – notumalar pōlap piriyiṟ katumeṉap – piṟitoṉ ṟākalu mañcuva lataṉāṟ – cēṇiṉa varuvōy pōlap pēṇāp – peruṅkali yāṇareñ ciṟukuṭit tōṉṟiṉar – valletir koṇṭu mellitiṉ viṉaiit – tuṟaiyumā ṟaṉṟu poḻutē cuṟavu – mōtamali kaṭaliṉ māṟā yiṉavē – yelliṉṟu tōṉṟal cellā tīmeṉa – vemarkuṟai kūṟat taṅki yēmuṟa – liḷaiyarum puraviyu miṉpuṟa nīyu – milluṟai nalvirun tākuta – lollutum perumanī nalkutal peṟiṉē.” itaṉuṭ tāṉperitu kaluḻntu tīṅkāyiṉaḷeṉavē akkuṟipput talaivaṉ pōkāmaṟ taṭuppak kūṟiyateṉṟu uṇarntu tōḻi kūṟiṉāḷ.
vāḷāṇ etirum piriviṉāṉum = vāḷāṇmai ceytaṟku otta pirivu tōṉṟiyavaḻiyum: āṇṭut talaivimēṟṟuk kiḷavi. mūvakaippirivilum pakaivayiṟpirivai vitantōti atikārappaṭṭu varukiṉṟa kaḷaviṉuḷ avai nikaḻappeṟā ituvāyiṉ varaiviṭaivaittup piriyavumpeṟum aracarkku iṉṟiyamaiyāta pirivākaliṉ eṉpatu karuttu. ōtalun tūtum varaiviṭaivaittup piriviṟkuc ciṟantilaveṉṟāṉām. ippirivu aracarkku uritteṉpatu tāṉē cēṟalum eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṟ peṟutum. vāḷāṇmaikku ēṟṟa piriveṉavē muṭiyuṭai vēntarēvaliṟ piriyum aracarkaṇṇatu ippiriveṉka. ciṟupāṉmai avvēntaṟku urittu, “veḷippaṭai tāṉē” eṉpataṉuḷ ippirivillai eṉparātaliṉ. atu “pakaiveṉṟu tiṟaikoṇṭa pāytiṇṭēr micaiyavar – vakaikoṇṭa cemmaṉām vaṉappāra viṭuvatō – pukaiyeṉap putalcūḻntu pūvaṅkaṭ poticeyyā – mukaiveṇpa ṉutiporā muṟṟiya kaṭumpaṉi.” itaṉuṭ paṉiyetir paruvaṅ kuṟiñciyākaliṟ kaḷaviṟ pirintāṉ vāḷāṇetirum veṉṟi tōḻikkut talaivi kūṟiyavāṟu. itu avaṉvayiṟ parattaimai karutātatu.
nāṇu neñcu alaippa viṭuttaṟkaṇṇum = talaivikku iṉṟiyamaiyāta nāṇuttāṉ avaḷ neñciṉai alaittaliṉ avaḷ annāṇiṉaik kaiviṭutaṟkaṇṇum: atu uṭaṉpōkkiṉum varaivukaṭāvum vaḻiyum vēṭkai mītūrntu nāṇtuṟanturaittal pōlvaṉa. “aḷitō tāṉē nāṇē nammoṭu – naṉinī ṭuḻantaṉṟu maṉṉē yiṉiyē – vāṉpūṅ karumpi ṉōṅkumaṇaṟ ciṟuciṟai tīmpuṉa ṉeritara vīntuk kāṅkut – tāṅku maḷavai tāṅkik – kāma neritarak kainil lātē.” itu uṭaṉpōkku valittamaiyiṉ nāṇtuṟantu kūṟiyatu.
varaital vēṇṭit tōḻi ceppiya puraitīr kiḷavi pulliya etirum = varaital viruppiṉāṟ tōḻi talaivaṟku varaivukaṭāyk kūṟiya puraitīr kiḷaviyait talaivi poruntiniṉṟē iyaṟpaḻittaṟku maṟuttāḷpōl niṟkum etirmaṟaiyaiyum: puraitīrkiḷavi talaivaṉuyarpiṟku ēlātu iyaṟpaḻittu uraikkuṅkiḷavi. atu, “pāṭukam vāḻitōḻi” eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcikkaliyuḷ “ilaṅku maruvit tilaṅku maruvittu – vāṉi ṉilaṅku maruvittē tāṉuṟṟa – cūḷpēṇāṉ poyttāṉ malai.” eṉat tōḻi iyaṟpaḻitta vāypāṭṭāṉ varaivukaṭāva ataṉai uṭampaṭṭup paḻittaṟku uṭampaṭātāḷ “poyttaṟ kuriyaṉō poyttaṟ kuriyaṉō – vañcalōm peṉṟāraip poyttaṟ kuriyaṉō – kuṉṟaka naṉṉāṭaṉ vāymaiyiṟ poytōṉṟiṟ – ṟiṅkaḷuṭ ṭīttōṉṟi yaṟṟu.” eṉat talaivi iyaṟpaṭa moḻintu etirmaṟuttavāṟu kāṇka. “aruvi vēṅkaip peruvarai nāṭaṟ – kiyāṉevaṉ ceykō veṉṟi yāṉatu – nakaiyeṉa vaṇarē ṉāyi – ṉeṉṉā kuvaiko ṉaṉṉuta ṉīyē. ituvum iyaṟpaḻitta tōḻikkut talaivi iyaṟpaṭa moḻintatu.”
varaivu uṭaṉpaṭutalum = talaivaṟkut talaivi tamarvaraivuṭampaṭṭataṉait talaivi virumputalaiyum: um. “ilaiyamar taṇkuḷavi vēynta potumpiṟ – kulaiyuṭaik kānta ḷiṉavaṇ ṭimiru – malaiyaka nāṭaṉum vantāṉmaṟ ṟaṉṉai – yalaiyu malaipōyiṟ ṟiṉṟu.” “oṟuppa vōvalar maṟuppat teṟalar – tamiya ruṟaṅkuṅ kauvai yiṉṟā – yiṉiyatu kēṭṭiṉ puṟukaviv vūrē.” “muṉāa tiyāṉaiyuṇa kurukiṉ kāṉalam – peruntōṭṭa maḷḷa rārppicai verūuṅ – kuṭṭuvaṉ māntai yaṉṉaveṅ – kuḻaiviḷaṅ kāynutaṟ kiḻavaṉu māvaṉē.” tamarāṉ oṟukkappaṭṭu ōvārāyt tuyaruḻattal ākāteṉa āṟṟuvikkuñ coṟkaḷāṉ maṟutturaippavun tēṟārāyt taṉittu iruppār uṟakkaṅ kāraṇamāka eḻunta kauvai kēḷātu varainteytiya māṟṟaṅ kēṭṭu ivvūrum iṉpuṟuka eṉpatām. 
āṅkataṉ puṟattup puraipaṭavanta maṟuttaloṭu tokaii = avaṉ varaivuvēṇṭiṉaviṭattu avvaraivu puṟattatākiyavaḻit talaivi taṉṉuyarpu uṇṭākat tōṉṟiyatu maṟuttalōṭē muṟkūṟiyavaṟṟait tokuttu: ataṉpuṟameṉavē ataṟku ayalākiya notumalar varaivāyiṟṟu. talaivi taṉkuṭippiṟappuṅ kaṟpum mutaliya uyarccikku ēṟpa ataṉai maṟuttut talaivaṉ varaiyumāṟu nī kūṟeṉat tōḻikkuk kūṟumeṉṟaṟkup puraipaṭa vanta maṟuttaleṉṟār. “vāri neṟippaṭṭirumpuṟan tāḻnta – vōrip putalva ṉaḻutaṉa ṉeṉpavō vōrum putuva malartaii yemareṉ peyarāl – vatuvai yayarvāraik kaṇṭu vilakki matiyaṟiyā – vēḻaiyai yeṉṟakala nakkuvan tīyāynī – tōḻi yavaṉuḻaic ceṉṟu; ceṉṟiyā ṉaṟivēṉ kūṟuka maṟṟiṉic – collaṟiyāp pētai maṭavai maṟṟellā – niṉakkorūu maṟṟeṉ ṟakal; akalanī ṭiṉṟu niṉakkum – varutarak kāṇpā yaṉaittākac colliya – collum viyaṉkoḷak kūṟu; tarumaṇaṟ ṟāḻappey tilpūva lūṭṭi – yerumaip peṭaiyō ṭemariṅ kayarum – perumaṇa mellān taṉittē yoḻiya – varimaṇaṉ muṉṟuṟaic ciṟṟil puṉainta – tirunuta lāyattār tammuṭ puṇarnta – vorumaṇan tāṉaṟiyu māyi ṉeṉaittun – terumaral kaiviṭ ṭirukkō valarnta – virinī ruṭukkai yulakam peṟiṉu – maruneṟi yāyar makaḷirk – kirumaṇaṅ kūṭuta lilliyal paṉṟē.” eṉavarum. “maḷḷarkuḻīiyaviḻavu” eṉpatumām. iccūttirattu urupum eccamumāyavaṟṟaik kiḻavōṇmēṉa eṉpataṉōṭu muṭittu muṟṟiṟkuk kiḷaviyeṉa orupeyar veḷippaṭuttu muṭikka. pulliya etiraiyum uṭaṉpaṭutalaiyum maṟuttaluṭaṉ tokuttatu.
kiḻavōḷ mēṉa eṉmaṉār pulavar = talaiviyiṭattaṉa kiḷaviyeṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar  e-ṟu. (16)
108. kāmat tiṇaiyiṟ kaṇṇiṉṟu varūu
nāṇu maṭaṉum peṇmaiya vātaliṟ
kuṟippiṉu miṭattiṉu mallatu vēṭkai
neṟippaṭa vārā vavaḷvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu uḷḷappuṇarccikku uriyavāṟu meyyuṟupuṇarccik kaṇṇum nikaḻumeṉṟa nāṇum maṭaṉuṅ kuṟippiṉum iṭattiṉum varumeṉak kūṟutaliṉ “accamu nāṇum” eṉpataṟkup puṟaṉaṭaiyāyiṟṟu. itaṉai īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāṉ, iṭattiṉkaṇvarum nāṇum maṭaṉun tantaṉmai tirintuvarumeṉpatūum atu kūṟṟiṉkaṇ varumeṉpatūuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻkiṉṟa ivviṭattē kūṟavēṇṭutaliṉ. eṉavē, itumutaliya cūttiram mūṉṟum muṉṉart talaivikkuk kūṟṟunikaḻumeṉṟaṟkuk kūṟṟunikaḻuṅkāl nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkik kūṟum eṉṟu akkūṟṟiṟku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiṉavēyāyiṟṟu. (i-ḷ.) avaḷvayiṉ āṉa nāṇum maṭaṉum peṇmaiya ātaliṉ = talaiviyiṭattu uḷavākiya nāṇum maṭaṉum peṇmaipparuvattē tōṉṟutalaiyuṭaiyavātaliṉ: kāmattiṇaiyiṟ kaṇṇiṉṟu kuṟippiṉum varūum = apparuvattē tōṉṟiya kāmavoḻukkaṅ kāraṇamāka avai kaṇṇiṉkaṇiṉṟu kuṟippiṉum varum: vēṭkai neṟippaṭa iṭattiṉum varūum = aṉṟi vēṭkai taṉṟaṉmai tiriyātu vaḻippaṭutalālē karumanikaḻccikkaṇṇum varum: allatu vārā = avvīriṭattumallatu avai vārā  e-ṟu. iyaṟkaippuṇarccikkaṇ uriyavākak kūṟum paṉṉiraṇṭu meyppāṭṭāṉuṅ kuṟippiṉkaṇ nāṇum maṭaṉum nikaḻntavāṟuṇarka. “oruneṟip paṭṭāṅ kōriyaṉ muṭiyuṅ – karuma nikaḻcci yiṭameṉa moḻipa.” eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷiyaṟ cūttirattāṉ iṭameṉṟataṉaik karumanikaḻcci eṉṟuṇarka. aḵtāvatu iṭantalaippāṭum pāṅkoṭutaḻāalun tōḻiyiṟpuṇarvumām. ivaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum nāṇum maṭaṉum nikaḻumeṉṟāṉ, iṉit tōḻiyiṟpuṇarviṉkaṇvarum nāṇum maṭaṉun tantaṉmai tirintuvarumeṉa mēliṟ cūttirattāṟ kūṟukiṉṟāṉ. (17)
109. kāmañ collā nāṭṭa miṉmaiyi
ṉēmuṟa viraṇṭu muḷaveṉa moḻipa.
itu karumanikaḻccikkaṇ varum nāṇum maṭaṉun tantaṉmai tirintuvarumeṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) collāk kāmam iṉmaiyiṉ = karumanikaḻcciyiṭattuk kūṟṟu nikaḻāta kāmam pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṉkaṇiṉmaiyiṉ: iraṇṭum ēmuṟa nāṭṭam uḷaveṉamoḻipa = muṟkūṟiya nāṇum maṭaṉun tantaṉmai tirintuvaranāṭṭutal uḷaveṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar.  e-ṟu. eṉṟatu, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattut talaivi kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvaḷeṉpatūum nikaḻuṅkāl nāṇum maṭaṉum perumpāṉmai keṭṭu akkūṟṟu nikaḻumeṉpatūum aṅṅaṉaṅ keṭutalaiyum muntunūṟkaṇ āciriyar nāṭṭutal uḷaveṉpatūuṅ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. “tērē muṟṟaṉṟu niṉṉiṉum peritē – pērēmuṟ ṟāypōla muṉṉiṉṟu vilakkuvāy” eṉṟāṟpōla mayakkam uṇarttiṟṟu. iṉi nāṇum maṭaṉuṅ keṭṭakūṟṟut tōḻiyai nōkkik kūṟumeṉa mēṟkūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
110. colletir moḻita larumait tākali
ṉalla kūṟṟumoḻi yavaḷvayi ṉāṉa.
itu nāṇum maṭaṉum perumpāṉmai nikaḻāta kūṟṟut tōḻikkut talaivi kūṟumeṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) etir col = aṅṅaṉam nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkiya collai: avaḷ vayiṉ moḻital arumaittu alla ākaliṉ = tōḻiyiṭattukkūṟutal arumaiyuṭaittallavākaiyiṉālē: kūṟṟumoḻi āṉa = kuṟippāṉaṉṟik kūṟṟāṟ kūṟum moḻi talaivikkup poruntiṉa  e-ṟu. etirtal taṉṟaṉmai māṟupaṭutal. “oṉṟiya tōḻiyoṭu” eṉa akattiṇaiyiṟ kūṟutalāṉun “tāyatti ṉaṭaiyā” eṉap poruḷiyaliṟ kūṟutalāṉum avaḷvayiṉ nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkiya collaik kūṟutalum poruntumeṉṟāṉ: avai muṟkāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaḷuṭ “kūṭutalvēṭkaiyāṟ kuṟipārttu” eṉavum, “vaḷaimuṉkai paṟṟi naliyat terumantiṭṭu” eṉavum “kāmaneritarak kainillātē” eṉavuṅ kūṟiyavāṟṟāṉum mēṟkūṟukiṉṟa utāraṇaṅkaḷāṉum nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkik kūṟṟunikaḻntavāṟuṇarka. “cūttirat tupporu ḷiṉṟiyum    paṭumē” eṉpataṉāṉ ivvilakkaṇam peṟutaṟku immūṉṟu cūttirattiṟkum māṭṭuṟuppuppaṭap poruḷ kūṟiṉām. iṉik kūṟṟu nikaḻuṅkāl nāṇum maṭaṉum peṇmaiyavātaliṟ kuṟippiṉum iṭattiṉumaṉṟi vēṭkaineṟippaṭa vārāveṉṟu poruḷkūṟiṟ kāṭṭiya utāraṇaṅkaṭku māṟupāṭākalāṉuñ cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaḷellāṅ kuṟippum iṭaṉumaṉṟip perumpāṉmai kūṟṟāy varutalāṉum āciriyar talaivaṉkūṟṟun talaivikūṟṟun tōḻikūṟṟuñ cevilikūṟṟumeṉak kūṟṟuñ cērttu nūlceytalāṉum atu poruḷaṉmaiyuṇarka. (19)
111. maṟaintavaṟ kāṇṭa ṟaṟkāṭ ṭuṟuta
ṉiṟainta kātaliṟ colletir maḻuṅkal
vaḻipāṭu maṟuttaṉ maṟuttetir kōṭal
paḻitīr muṟuval ciṟitē tōṟṟal
kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiṉu nāṇumika variṉu
miṭṭuppiri viraṅkiṉu marumaicey tayarppiṉum
vantavaḻi yeḷḷiṉum viṭṭuyirt taḻuṅkiṉu
nontuteḷi voḻippiṉu macca nīṭiṉum
pirintavaḻik kalaṅkiṉum peṟṟavaḻi maliyiṉum
varuntoḻiṟ karumai vāyil kūṟiṉuṅ
kūṟiya vāyil koḷḷāk kālaiyu
maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkic citaintavaḻit tōḻikku
niṉaittal cāṉṟa varumaṟai yuyirttalu
muyirāk kālat tuyirttalu muyircela
vēṟṟuvaraivu variṉatu māṟṟutaṟ kaṇṇu
neṟipaṭu nāṭṭattu nikaḻntavai maṟaippiṉum
poṟiyiṉ yātta puṇarcci nōkki
yorumaik kēṇmaiyi ṉuṟukuṟai teḷintō
ḷarumai cāṉṟa nāliraṇṭu vakaiyiṟ
perumai cāṉṟa viyalpiṉ kaṇṇum
poytalai yaṭutta maṭaliṉ kaṇṇuṅ
kaiyaṟu tōḻi kaṇṇīr tuṭaippiṉum
veṟiyāṭ ṭiṭattu veruviṉ kaṇṇuṅ
kuṟiyi ṉoppumai maruṭaṟ kaṇṇum
varaivutalai variṉuṅ kaḷavaṟi vuṟiṉun
tamartaṟ kātta kāraṇa maruṅkiṉun
