taṉkuṟi taḷḷiya teruḷāk kālai
vantaṉaṉ peyarnta vaṟuṅkaḷa nōkkit
taṉpiḻaip pākat taḻīit tēṟalum
vaḻuviṉṟu nilaiiya viyaṟpaṭu poruḷiṉum
poḻutu māṟum puraiva taṉmaiyi
ṉaḻivutalai vanta cintaik kaṇṇuṅ
kāmañ ciṟappiṉu mavaṉaḷi ciṟappiṉu
mēmañ cāṉṟa vuvakaik kaṇṇun
taṉvayi ṉurimaiyu mavaṉvayiṟ parattaiyu
maṉṉavu muḷavē yōriṭat tāṉa.
itaṉuṭ talaivikūṟṟu nikaḻttumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ cila kūṟṟukkaḷuṭ taṉvayiṉ urimaiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattaiyumpaṭa nikaḻttavum peṟumeṉkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉvayiṉeṉavē taṉṉeṉṟatu talaiviyaiyām, urimai, kaḷivilē kaṟpukkaṭampūṇṭoḻukal; eṉavē, pulaviyuḷḷattāḷākavum peṟuṅ kaḷaviṉeṉpatu karutip parattaiyumuḷa eṉṟāṉ; ūṭalum uṇarttalum veḷippaṭa nikaḻāmaiyiṉ ivai pulavippōli. parattai, ayaṉmai. avaṉkaṭ parattaimaiyiṉṟēṉuṅ kātaṉmikutiyāṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ karututal peṇṭaṉmai. ummai etirmaṟaiyākaliṉ ivviraṇṭum ilavātalē perumpāṉmai. (i-ḷ.) maṟaintu avaṟ kāṇṭal = talaivaṉ puṇarntu nīṅkuṅkāṟ taṉkātaṉ mikutiyāl avaṉmaṟaiyuntuṇaiyum nōkkiniṉṟu aṅṅaṉam maṟaintavaṉaik kāṇṭaṟkaṇṇun tōḻikkuk kūṟṟāṟ kūṟutaluḷa: um. “kaḻippūk kuṟṟuṅ kāṉa lalkiyum – vaṇṭaṟ paravai varimaṇa layarntu – miṉpuṟap puṇarntu miḷivarap paṇintun – taṉṟuyar veḷippaṭa luṟīi nantuya – raṟiyā maiyi ṉayarnta neñcamoṭu – cellu maṉṉō mellam pulampaṉ – celvōṉ peyarpuṟat tiraṅki muṉṉiṉṟu – takaiiya ceṉṟave ṉiṟaiyi ṉeñca – meytiṉṟu kollō tāṉē yeytiyuṅ – kāmañ ceppa nāṇiṉṟu kollō – vutuva kāṇa varukuva tērē – kuppai veṇmaṇaṟ kuvavumicai yāṉu – mekkart tāḻai maṭalvayi ṉāṉu – māykoṭip pācaṭumpu pariyavūr piḻipu – ciṟukuṭip paratavar peruṅkaṭaṉ maṭutta – kaṭuñcelaṟ koṭuntimil pōla – nivantupaṭu tōṟṟamo ṭikantumā yummē.” aṟiyāmaiyiṉ ayarntaneñcamoṭu eṉpatu taṉvayiṉurimai; ikantumāyum eṉpatu avaṉvayiṟ parattaimai.
taṟkāṭṭuṟutal = taṉṉai avaṉ kāṇāvakai nāṇāṉ maṟaintu oḻukiṉun taṉpolivaḻiviṉai avaṟkuk kāṭṭal vēṇṭutaṟkaṇṇum: atu “iṉṉa ḷāyiṉa ṇaṉṉuta leṉṟavart – tuṉṉac ceṉṟu ceppunarp peṟiṉē – naṉṟumaṉ vāḻi tōḻinam paṭappai – nīrvār paimputaṟ kalitta – mārip pīrtta lācila koṇṭē.” iṉṉaḷāyiṉaḷeṉṟatu taṟkāṭṭuṟutal. ceppunarp peṟiṉēyeṉpataṉāṟ kaḷavāyiṟṟu, kaṟpiṟku vāyilkaḷ ceppuvār uḷarātaliṉ. itaṟku iraṇṭum uḷa.
niṟainta kātaliṟ col etir maḻuṅkal = talaivi kātaṉmikutiyāṟ talaivaṉ parattaimaiyai etirkūṟa niṉaintu kūṟṟeytātu kuṟaipaṭutaṟkaṇṇum: um. “piṟaivaṉap piḻanta nutalum yāḻani – ṉiṟaivarai nillā vaḷaiyu maṟaiyā – tūralar tūṟṟuṅ kauvaiyu nāṇiṭ – ṭuraiyavark kuraiyā vāyiṉu miraivēṭṭuk – kaṭuñcūl vayavoṭu kāṉaley tātu – kaḻaṉi yoḻinta koṭuvāyp peṭaikku – muṭamutir nārai kaṭaṉmī ṉoyyu – mellam pulampaṟ kaṇṭunilai cellāk – karappavuṅ kāppavuṅ kaimik – kuraitta tōḻi yuṇka ṇīrē.” itu yām uraiyāmāyiṉuṅ kaṇ uraittaṉa eṉṟaliṉ iraṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ.
vaḻipāṭu maṟuttal = varuttamikutiyāṟ talaivaṉai vaḻipaṭutalai maṟuttuk kūṟumiṭattum: um. “eṉṉa rāyiṉu miṉiniṉai voḻika – vaṉṉa vāka vuraiyaṟ ṟōḻiyā – miṉṉa mākat tuṟantōr naṭpeṉa – māṉā ruṭukkai malaiyuṟai kuṟava – raṟiyā taṟutta ciṟiyilaic cāntam – vaṟaṉuṟ ṟāra murukkip paiyeṉa – maramvaṟi tākac cōrntuk kāṅke – ṉaṟivu muḷḷamu mavarvayiṟ ceṉṟeṉa – vaṟitā likuḷaiyeṉ yākkai yiṉiyavar – variṉu nōymarun tallār vārā – tavaṇa rākuka kātala rivaṇaṅ – kāmam paṭaraṭa varuntiya – nōymali varuttaṅ kāṇaṉmā remarē.” “uḷḷi ṉuḷḷam vēmē yuḷḷā – tiruppiṉem maḷavait taṉṟē varutti – vāṉṟōy vaṟṟē kāmañ – cāṉṟō rallar yāmarīi yōrē” eṉavarum. “nīyuṭam paṭutaliṉ yāṉṟara vantu – kuṟiniṉ ṟaṉaṉē kuṉṟa nāṭa – ṉiṉṟai yaḷavai ceṉṟaikka ceṉṟi – kaiyuṅ kālu mōyvaṉa voḻukat – tīyuṟu taḷiri ṉaṭuṅki – yātu millaiyāṉ ceyaṟkuri yatuvē.” ittōḻi kūṟṟē ceṉṟaikkaveṉṟataṉāṟ talaivi maṟuttamai peṟṟām.
maṟuttu etirkōṭal = aṅṅaṉam vaḻipāṭu maṟutta talaiviyē avaṉai ēṟṟukkōṭalai virumpiyakkaṇṇum: atu “kauvai yañciṟ kāma meykku – meḷḷaṟa viṭiṉē yuḷḷatu nāṇē – peruṅkaḷiṟu vāṅka murintu nilampaṭar – nāḷuṭai yociya laṟṟē – kaṇṭiciṟ ṟōḻiyava ruṇṭave ṉalaṉē.” itu nāṇēyuḷḷatu kaṟpuppōmeṉṟaliṉ maṟuttetirkōṭalām.
paḻitīr muṟuval ciṟitē tōṟṟal = taṉkaṟpiṟku varum paḻitīrnta taṉmaiyāṟ taṉkaṭ tōṉṟiya makiḻcciyaic ciṟitē tōḻikkut tōṟṟuvittaṟkaṇṇum: talaivaṉāṟ tōṉṟiya nōyum pacalaiyum murukaṉāṟ tīrntateṉṟu avaṉ kēṭpiṟ kaṟpiṟkup paḻiyāmātaliṟ paḻiyeṉṟār. um. “aṇaṅkuṭai neṭuvarai yucciyi ṉiḻitaruṅ – kaṇaṅko ḷaruvik kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ – maṇaṅkamaḻ viyaṉmār paṇaṅkiya cella – lituveṉa vaṟiyāṇ maṟuvaraṟ poḻutiṟ – paṭiyōrt tēytta palpukaḻt taṭakkai – neṭuvēṭ pēṇat taṇikuva ḷivaḷeṉa – mutuvāyp peṇṭi ratuvāyk kūṟak – kaḷaṉaṉ kiḻaittuk kaṇṇi cūṭi – vaḷanakar cilampap pāṭip palikoṭut – turuvac centiṉai kurutiyoṭu tūuy – murukāṟṟup paṭutta vurukeḻu naṭunā – ḷāra nāṟa varuviṭart tatainta – cāraṟ palpū vaṇṭupaṭac cūṭik – kaḷiṟṟirai terīip pārva lotukki – ṉoḷittiyaṅku marapiṉ vayappuli pōla – naṉmaṉai neṭunakark kāvala raṟiyāmait – taṉṉacai yuḷḷattu naṉṉacai vāyppa – viṉṉuyir kuḻaippa muyaṅkutoṟu meymmalintu – nakkaṉe ṉallaṉō yāṉē, yeytta – nōytaṇi kātalar varavīṇ – ṭētila vēlaṟ kuvantamai kaṇṭē” itaṉuṭ paḻitīra avaṉ vantu uyirtaḷirppa muyaṅki nakkanilaiyait tōḻikkut talaivi kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiṉum = talaivikuṟippiṉaṉṟi etirppaṭṭa talaivaṉ oruvaḻi avaḷai akappaṭuttavaḻik kāṭciviruppiṉaḷāyiṉum appoḻutu avaḷkalaṅkiṉum: eṉavē, kāṭci viruppiṉai mītūrntu kalakkam pulappaṭṭatu, talaivaṉvayiṟ parattaimaikaruti. “koṭiyavuṅ kōṭṭavu nīriṉṟi niṟampeṟap – poṭiyaḻaṟ puṟantanta pūvāppūm polaṅkōtait – toṭiceṟi yāppamai yari” eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcikkaliyuḷ “ataṉā lallal kaḷaintaṉaṉ ṟōḻi” eṉak kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiya varuttattaik kaḷaintaṉaṉeṉat talaiviyuraiyeṉat tōḻikku uraittaṟkaṭ kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. ivvutāraṇam āṇṭukkāṭṭutum. “uṟukaḻi maruṅki ṉōtamoṭu malarnta” eṉṉum pāṭṭut talaivaṉ itaṉaik kūṟiyatu.
nāṇu mika variṉum = talaivaṉai etirppaṭṭa talaivi taṉ perunāṇuṭaimai kūṟit talaivaṉai ēṟṟukkoḷḷātu niṟpiṉum: um. “viḷaiyāṭ ṭāyamoṭu veṇmaṇa laḻutti – maṟantaṉen tuṟanta kāḻmuḷai yakaiya – neypey tīmpāl peytiṉitu vaḷarttatu – nummiṉuñ ciṟantatu kaitai yākumeṉ – ṟaṉṉai kūṟiṉaḷ puṉṉaiyatu ciṟappē – yamma nāṇutu nummoṭu nakaiyē – viruntiṟ pāṇar viḷaricai kaṭuppa – valampuri vāṉkōṭu naralu milaṅkunīrt – tuṟaikeḻu koṇkanī nalki – ṉiṟaipaṭu nīḻal piṟavumā ruḷavē.” itaṉuḷ amma nāṇutumeṉap putituvantatōr nāṇumikuti tōṉṟa maṟutturaittaliṟ taṉvayiṉurimaiyum avaṉvayiṟparattaimaiyuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ.
iṭṭup pirivu iraṅkiṉum = cēṇiṭaiyiṉṟi iṭṭitākap pirintuḻit talaivi iraṅkiṉum: kaṟpiṉuṭ collāta piritalaiyum iṭṭuppiriveṉpa, kaḷavupōla nikaḻporuḷ uṇarttippiritalarumaiyiṉ. um. “yāṉē yīṇṭai yēṉē yeṉṉalaṉē – yāṉō nōyoṭu kāṉa laḵtē – tuṟaivaṉ ṟammū rāṉē – maṟaiyala rāki maṉṟat taḵtē” tammūrāṉeṉṟaliṉ ōtaṉ mutaliya piriviṉṟi aṇittāyavaḻip pirintāṉeṉa avviraṇṭum payappak kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. “ciṟuveṇ kākkaic cevvāyp peruntōṭ – ṭeṟitirait tuvalai yīrmpuṟa naṉaippap – paṉipulan tuṟaiyum palpūṅ kāṉal – virinīrc cērppa ṉīppi ṉorunam – miṉṉuyi rallatu piṟitoṉ – ṟevaṉō tōḻi nāmiḻap patuvē.” ituvum atu. ivai kaḷaviṉuṭ pulavippōliyām.
arumai ceytu ayarppiṉum = muṟkūṟiya iṭṭuppirivēyaṉṟit talaivaṉ taṉṉai ariyaṉākac ceytukoṇṭu tammai maṟappiṉum: atu taṇṭātirattalai muṉinta maṟṟaiyavaḻit talaivaṉ tāṉum ariyaṉāy maṟantāṉ pōṉṟu kāṭṭiṉum avviraṇṭuṅ kūṟutalām. um. “tīmai kaṇṭōr tiṟattum periyōr – tāmaṟin tuṇarka veṉpa mātō – vaḻuvap piṇṭa nāppa ṇēmuṟ – ṟiruveti rīṉṟa vēṟṟalaik koḻumuṉai – cūṉmutir maṭappiṭi nāṇmeya lāru – malaikeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṇmai palaviṉ – mācciṉai tuṟanta kōṇmutir perumpaḻam – viṭaraḷai vīḻntuk kāṅkut toṭarpaṟac – cēṇuñ ceṉṟuk kaṉṟē yaṟiyā – tēka laṭukkat tiruṇmukai yirunta – kuṟiñci nallūrp peṇṭi – riṉṉu mōvā reṉṟiṟat talarē.” tīṅkuceytāraiyum poṟukkiṟpār nammait tuṟattaliṉ nām ariyēmākiyatu paṟṟit tāmum ariyarāyiṉār pōlumeṉa avviraṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. “neytaṟ paṭuvi ṉiṟaikaḻit taṇcērppaṉ – kaitaicūḻ kāṉaliṟ kaṇṭanāṭ pōlāṉāṟ – ceyta kuṟiyumpoy yāyiṉavāṟ cēyiḻā – yaiyako lāṉṟār toṭarpu.” ituvum atu.
vantavaḻi eḷḷiṉum = peritākiya iṭaiyīṭṭiṉuḷ aritākat talaivaṉ vantañāṉṟum peṟātañāṉṟait tuṉpamikutiyāṟ peṟṟataṉaiyuṅ kaṉavupōṉṟu koṇṭu ikaḻntiruppiṉum: um. “māṉaṭi yaṉṉa kavaṭṭilai yaṭampiṉ – ṟārmaṇi yaṉṉa voṇpūk koḻuti – yoṇṭoṭi makaḷir vaṇṭa layarum – puḷḷimiḻ peruṅkaṭaṟ cērppaṉai – yuḷḷēṉ ṟōḻi paṭiyareṉ kaṇṇē.” “vārākkāṟ ṟuñcā variṟṟuñcā vāyiṭai – yāraña ruṟṟaṉa kaṇ.” “iṉka ṇuṭaittavar pārval pirivañcum – puṉka ṇuṭaittāṟ puṇarvu.” variṟ ṟuñcāveṉavum puṉkaṇuṭait teṉavum varavum pirivum añci iraṇṭum nikaḻak kūṟiṉāḷ. itu muṉṉilaip puṟamoḻi. “kaṇṭiraṇ muttam payakku mirumunnīrp – paṇṭaṅko ṇāvāy vaḻaṅkun tuṟaivaṉai – muṇṭakak kāṉaluṭ kaṇṭē ṉeṉatteḷintē – ṉiṉṟa vuṇarvilā tēṉ.” itu muṉpu iṉpan taruvaṉeṉa uṇarntuniṉṟa uṇarvu īṇṭillāta yāṉ puṇarccivaruttan tarumeṉṟu teḷintēṉeṉṟāḷeṉka.
viṭṭu uyirttu aḻuṅkiṉum = karanta maṟaiyiṉait talaivi tamarkku uraittaṟkut tōḻikku vāyviṭṭuk kūṟi akkūṟiyataṉaiyē tamarkēṭpakkūṟātu taviriṉum: uyirttal, kūṟutal. um. “valanta vaḷḷi māṉōṅku cāraṟ – kiḷarnta vēṅkaic cēṇeṭum poṅkarp – poṉṉēr putumalar vēṇṭiya makaḷi – riṉṉā vicaiya pūcal payiṟṟali – ṉēka laṭukkat tiruḷaḷaic cilampi – ṉākoḷ vayappuli yākumaḵ teṉatta – malaikeḻu cīṟūr cilampak kalleṉac – cilaiyuṭai yiṭattar peyartaru nāṭa – ṉeñcā viyaṉmār puṭaitteṉa vaṉṉaik – kaṟivip pēṅko laṟiviyēṅ kolleṉa – virupāṟ paṭṭa cūḻcci yorupāṟ – cērntaṉṟu vāḻi tōḻi yākkai – yiṉṉuyir kaḻiva tāyiṉu niṉmaka – ḷāymala ruṇkaṭ pacalai – kāma nōyeṉac ceppā tīmē.” itu ciṟaippuṟam.
nontu teḷivu oḻippiṉum = varaivu nīṭṭittut talaivaṉ cūḷuṟṟavaḻi ataṟku nontu teḷiviṭai vilaṅkiṉum: um. “maṉṟat tuṟukaṟ karuṅkaṇ mucuvukaḷuṅ – kuṉṟaka nāṭaṉ ṟeḷitta teḷiviṉai – naṉṟeṉṟu tēṟit teḷintēṉ ṟalaiyaḻi – yeṉṟumaṟ ṟeṉṟu maṉaittu.” eṉavarum. “emmaṇaṅkiṉavē” eṉpatu talaivikūṟak kēṭṭut tōḻi kūṟiyatu. atuvum itaṉāṟ koḷka.
accam nīṭiṉum = teyvam accūḷuṟaviṟku avaṉai varuttumeṉṟun tantai taṉṉaiyar aṟikiṉṟārōveṉṟuṅ kūṭṭamuṇmai uṇarnta tōḻikkum uṇmai kūṟutaṟkum añciya accam nīṭṭippiṉum: um. “maṉṟa marā atta pēemutir kaṭavuḷ – koṭiyōrt teṟūu meṉpa yāvatuṅ – koṭiya rallareṅ kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭar – pacaiip pacantaṉṟu nutalē – ñekiḻi ñekiḻntaṉṟu taṭameṉ ṟōḷē.” “meṉṟiṉai mēynta ciṟukaṭ paṉṟi – vaṉka laṭukkattut tuñcu nāṭa – ṉentai yaṟita lañcikko – latuvē teyya vārā maiyē.” “puṉaiyiḻai nōkkiyum puṉalāṭap puṟañcūḻntu – maṇivari taiiyuna milvantu vaṇaṅkiyu – niṉaiyupu varuntumin neṭuntakai tiṟattivvū – riṉaiyaḷeṉ ṟeṭuttōtaṟ kaṉaiyaiyō nīyeṉa – viṉavuti yāyiṉ viḷaṅkiḻāy kēḷiṉi; cevviral civappūra    uraittatai; oṭuṅkiyām pukalollēm peyartara vavaṉ kaṇṭu – neṭuṅkaya malarvāṅki neṟittuttan taṉaittaṟkō – viṭuttavar virakiṉṟi yeṭuttacoṟ poyyōṭak – kaṭintatūu milaiyāynī kaḻaṟiya vantatai; varitēṟṟāy nīyeṉa vaṇaṅkiṟai yavaṉpaṟṟit – terivēyttōṭ karumpeḻutit toyyilcey taṉaittaṟkō – puriyunam māyattār poyyāka veṭuttaco – luritteṉa vuṇarāynī yulamantāy pōṉṟatai; eṉavāṅku, aritiṉi yāyiḻā yatutēṟṟal puriporuṅ – kaṉṟunum vatuvaiyu ṇamarceyva tiṉṟīṅkē – tāṉayan tiruntativ vūrāyi ṉevaṉkolō – nāñceyaṟ pāla tiṉi.” eṉavarum.
pirintavaḻik kalaṅkiṉum = kaḷavu alarākāmal yāṉ pirintuḻit talaivi kalaṅkuvaḷeṉṟu añcit talaivaṉ piriyātu uṟaitaliṟ pirivaik karutappeṟāta talaivi avvāṟaṉṟip pirintuḻik kalaṅkiṉum: um. “miṉṉuccey karuvip peyaṉmaḻai tūṅka – vicumpā ṭaṉṉa paṟainivan tāṅkup – polampaṭaip polinta voṇṭē rēṟik – kalaṅkukaṭaṟ ṟuvalai yāḻi naṉaippa – viṉicceṉ ṟaṉaṉē yiṭumaṇaṟ cērppaṉ – yāṅkaṟin taṉṟuko ṟōḻiyeṉ – ṟēṅkamaḻ tirunuta lūrum pacappē.” “kuṟunilaik kuraviṉ ciṟunaṉai naṟuvī – vaṇṭutaru nāṟṟam vaḷikalan tīyak – kaṇkaḷi peṟūuṅ kaviṉpeṟu kālai – yelvaḷai ñekiḻttōrk kalla luṟīiyar – ceṉṟa neñcañ ceyviṉaik kacāvara – voruṅkuvā ṉacaiyoṭu varuntuṅ kollō – varuḷā ṉātali ṉiḻintivaṇ vantu – toṉṉala ṉiḻantaveṉ poṉṉiṟa nōkki – yēti lāṭṭi yivaḷeṉap – pōyiṉṟu kollō nōytalai maṇantē.” “varuvatu kollō tāṉē vārā – tavaṇuṟai mēvali ṉamaivatu kollō – puṉavarko ḷaḷiyiṟ kalava maññai – yiruvi yirunta kuruvi veruvuṟap – pantāṭu makaḷiriṟ paṭartaruṅ – kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭaṉoṭu ceṉṟaveṉ ṉeñcē.” “porippuṟap pullic ciṉaiyīṉṟa puṉṉai – varippuṟa vārmaṇaṉmē lēṟit – terippuṟat – tāḻkaṭaṟ ṟaṇcērppaṉ ṟārakala nalkumē – lāḻiyāṟ kāṇāmō yām.” eṉavarum.
peṟṟavaḻi maliyiṉum = talaivi iṭaiyīṭiṉṟit talaivaṉai etirppaṭap peṟṟañāṉṟu putuvatu maliyiṉum: varaivu nīṭṭitta kālattup peṟṟavaḻimalivai veḷippaṭak kūṟutalum varaivu nīṭṭiyātavaḻip peṟṟavaḻimalivai veḷippaṭuttāmaiyum uṇarka. um. “iṉṉicai yurumoṭu kaṉaituḷi talaii – maṉṉuyir maṭinta pāṉāṭ kaṅkuṟ – kāṭutoṭu vaṭṭattu viḷiviṭam peṟātu – variyataṭ paṭutta cēkkait teriyiḻait – tēṉāṟu katuppiṟ koṭicciyar tantai – kūtiriṟ ceṟiyuṅ kuṉṟa nāṭa – vaṉaintuvā liḷamulai ñemuṅkap pullūu – viḷaṅkutoṭi muṉkai vaḷaintupuṟañ cuṟṟa – niṉmār paṭaitali ṉiṉitā kiṉṟē – nummil pulampā ṉuḷḷutoṟu naliyun – taṇvara lacaiiya paṇpil vāṭai – patampeṟu kallā kiṭampārttu nīṭiya – malaimara mociya voṟṟip – palarmaṭi kaṅku ṉeṭumpuṟa nilaiyē.” muyakkam iṉiteṉa makiḻntu kūṟuvāḷ nummil pulampāl vāṭaikku varuntiṉēmeṉṟaliṉ iraṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. “amma vāḻi tōḻi nalamika – nalla vāyiṉa vaḷiyameṉ ṟōḷkaṇ – malla liruṅkaḻi malarum – mellam pulampaṉ vanta vāṟē.” “amma vāḻi tōḻi paṉmā – ṇuṇmaṇa laṭaikarai nammō ṭāṭiya – taṇṇan tuṟaivaṉ marīi – yaṉṉai yaruṅkaṭi vantuniṉ ṟōṉē.” eṉavarum. ivai tōḻikkuk kūṟiyaṉa. peṟṟavaḻi maliyiṉumeṉap peṟuporuḷ iṉṉateṉavum iṉṉārkkuk kūṟuvateṉavum varaiyātu kūṟavē piṟarpeṟṟu malintu piṟarkkuk kūṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. “amma vāḻi tōḻi yaṉṉaik – kuyarnilai yulakamuñ ciṟitā lavarmalai – mālaip peyta maṇaṅkama ḻuntiyoṭu – kālai vanta muḻumutaṟ kāntaṇ – melviral kuḻaiya muyaṅkalu – milluyttu naṭuṅkaluṅ kaṭiyā tōṭkē.” itu peṟṟavaḻi makiḻntu tōḻikku uraittatu. itaṟkum iraṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ.
varum toḻiṟku arumai vāyil kūṟiṉum = talaivaṉ iṭaiviṭātu varutaṟku āṇṭu nikaḻum ētam palavāṟṟāṉum uḷatām arumaiyai vāyilākiya tōḻi kūṟiṉun talaivikkuk kūṟṟu nikaḻtal uḷa: atu “naḷḷeṉ ṟaṉṟē yāmañ collavin – tiṉitaṭaṅ kiṉarē mākkaṇ muṉiviṉṟu – naṉantalai yulakamun tuñcu – mōryāṉ maṉṟa tuñcā tēṉē.” itaṉuṭ poḻutu ceṉṟatillaiyeṉṟum mākkaḷ iṉṉun tuyiṉṟilareṉṟum arumaiyai vāyil kūṟiyavaḻit talaivi yāmamum naḷḷeṉṟum mākkaḷun tuyiṉṟum vantilareṉa varuntik kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. nām ēviya toḻil ēṟṟukkoṇṭu varukiṉṟavaṉ ōr kāraṇattāṉaṉṟi vārātoḻiyumōveṉṟu talaivi koḷḷumāṟu kūṟumeṉṟaṟkut toḻileṉṟār.
kūṟiya vāyil koḷḷāk kālaiyum = talaivaṟkuk kuṟai nērntu vāyilākik kuṟainayappak kūṟiyavaḻiyum piṟavaḻiyun tōḻikūṟṟiṉait talaivi ēṟṟuk koḷḷāta kālattukkaṇṇum: vāyil, tōḻi. um. “teruviṉkaṭ, kāraṇa miṉṟik kalaṅkuvārk kaṇṭunī – vāraṇa vācip patampeyara lētila – nīniṉmēṟ koḷva tevaṉ.” eṉat tōḻikūṟṟiṉai maṟuttatu. “tōḷē toṭinekiḻn taṉavē kaṇṇē – vāḷīr vaṭiviṉ vaṭiviḻan taṉavē – nutalum pacalai pāyiṉṟu titalaic – cilpoṟi yaṇinta palkā ḻalkuṉ – maṇiyē raimpāṉ māyōṭ keṉṟu – vevvāyp peṇṭir kavvai tūṟṟa – nāmuṟu tuyarañ ceyyila reṉṉuṅ – kāmuṟu tōḻi kātalaṅ kiḷavi – yirumpucey kollaṉ vevvulait teḷitta – tōymaṭaṟ ciṉṉīr pōla – nōymali neñciṟ kēmamāñ ciṟitē.” itu tōḻikūṟṟiṉai naṉkumatiyātu kūṟiṉāḷ.
maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkic citaintavaḻit tōḻikku niṉaittal cāṉṟa arumaṟai uyirttalum = kāppumikutikkaṇ maṉaiyakappaṭṭuk kalaṅki uṇarvu aḻintavaḻit talaivi ārāycciyuṭaittākiya arumaṟaiyiṉait tōḻikkuk kūṟutalum uḷa. um. “kēḷā yallai tōḻi yalkal – vēṇavā naliya veyya vuyirā – vēmāṉ piṇaiyiṉ varuntiṉe ṉākat – tuyarmaruṅ kaṟintāḷ pōla vaṉṉai – tuñcā yōveṉ kuṟumaka ḷeṉṟaliṟ – colveḷip paṭāmai mellave ṉeñciṟ – paṭumaḻai poḻinta pāṟai maruṅkiṟ – ciralvā yuṟṟa taḷaviṟ pālavaṟ – kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṟ paṭarntōrkkuk – kaṇṇum paṭumō veṉṟiciṉ yāṉē.” itaṉuṭ tuñcāyōveṉat tāy kūṟiyavaḻi maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiyavāṟum paṭarntōrkkeṉa maṟaiyuyirttavāṟuṅ kaṇpaṭāk koṭumai ceytāṉeṉap parattaimai kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka. “poḻutu melliṉṟu peyalu mōvātu – kaḻutukaṇ paṉippa vīcu mataṉṟalaip – pulippaṟ ṟālip putalvarp pulli – yaṉṉā yeṉṉu maṉṉaiyu maṉṉō – veṉmalain taṉaṉko ṟāṉē taṉmalai – yāra nāṟu mārpiṉaṉ – māri yāṉaiyiṉ vantuniṉ ṟaṉaṉē.” “palaviṉ paḻampeṟṟa paiṅkaṭ kaṭuva – ṉilaveṉ ṟiṉaiyayiru mekkalcūḻ veṟpaṉ – pulavuṅko ṟōḻi puṇarvaṟin taṉṉai – celavuṅ kaṭintāḷ puṉattu.” ivaiyum atu. iṉṉum maṉaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkiyeṉṟataṉāṟ kāppucciṟaimikka kaiyaṟukiḷavikaḷuṅ koḷka. “ciṟuveḷḷāṅ kurukē ciṟuveḷḷāṅ kurukē – tuṟaipō kaṟuvait tūmaṭi yaṉṉa – niṟaṅkiḷar tūvic ciṟuveḷḷāṅ kurukē – yemmūr vante moṇṭuṟait tuḻaiic – ciṉaikkeḷiṟ ṟārkaiyai yavarūrp peyarti – yaṉaiya vaṉpiṉaiyō perumaṟa viyaiyō – vāṅkaṭ ṭīmpuṉa līṅkaṭ parakkuṅ – kaḻaṉi nallūr makiḻnaṟkeṉ – ṉiḻainekiḻ paruvaral ceppā tōyē.” “kūrvāyc ciṟukurukē kuṇṭunī ruṭkiṭanta – vāra liraikaruti nittalu niṟṟiyā – ṉēriṇarp puṉṉaikkīḻk koṇkaṉ varumeṉap – pēruṇka ṇīrmalka niṉṟāṇmaṟ ṟeṉṉāyō.” “oṇṭūvi nārāyniṉ cēvalu nīyumāy – vaṇṭūtu pūṅkāṉal vaikaluñ cēṟirāṟ – peṇṭūtu vantē meṉavuraittuk kātalaraik – kaṇṭīr kaḻaṟiyakkāṟ kāṉal kaṭipavō.” ivai kāppucciṟaimikka kaiyaṟukiḷavi.
uyirāk kālattu uyirttalum = talaivaṉōṭu taṉṟiṟattu oruvarum oṉṟu uraiyātavaḻit taṉatāṟṟāmaiyāṟ taṉṉōṭum avaṉōṭum paṭṭaṉa cilamāṟṟan talaivitāṉē kūṟutalum uḷa.: tōḻi maṟaiveḷippaṭuttuk kōṭaṟku vāḷātu iruntuḻit talaivaṉ taṉmēṟ tavaṟiḻaittavaḻi iraṇṭumpaṭak kēṭpōriṉṟiyuṅ kūṟutalām. um. “uṟaipati yaṉṟit tuṟaikeḻu ciṟukuṭik – kāṉalañ cērppaṉ koṭumai yēṟṟi – yāṉāt tuyaramoṭu varuntip pāṉāṭ – ṭuñcā tuṟaiñaro ṭucāvāt – tuyiṟkaṇ mākkaḷoṭu neṭṭirā vuṭaittē.” “taḻaiyaṇi yalku ṟāṅkal cellā – nuḻaiciṟu nucuppiṟ kevva māka – vammel lāka niṟaiya vīṅkiya – kommai varumulai ceppuṭa ṉetiriṉa – yāṅkā kuvaḷkol pūṅkuḻai yeṉṉu – mavala neñcamo ṭucāvāk – kavalai mākkaṭṭip pētai yūrē.” eṉavarum. uyirātāḷ tōḻiyāyiṉāḷ; avaḷ talaivikūṟuvaṉa kēṭṭaṟkup poyttuyilkoḷḷum. uyirttalumeṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyavataṉāṟ tōḻikkuk kūṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. “pēṇupa pēṇār periyō reṉpatu – nāṇut takkaṉṟatu kāṇuṅ kālē – yuyirō raṉṉa ceyirtīr naṭpi – ṉiṉakkiyāṉ maṟaittal yāvatu mikapperi – taḻitak kaṉṟāṟ ṟāṉē koṇkaṉ – yāṉyā yañcuva leṉiṉun tāṉē – pirital cūḻāṉ maṉṉē yiṉiyē – kāṉa lāya maṟiyiṉu māṉai – talarva taṉṟuko leṉṉu mataṉāṟ – pularvatu kollavar naṭpeṉa – vañcuva ṟōḻiyeṉ ṉeñcat tāṉē.” eṉavarum.
uyircela vēṟṟu varaivu variṉ atu māṟṟutaṟkaṇṇum = iṟantupāṭu payakkumāṟṟāṟ taṉṟiṟattu notumalar varaiyak karutiyañāṉṟu ataṉai māṟṟutaṟkaṇṇum: um. “aṉṉai vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉai puṉṉai – poṉṉiṟam viriyum pūkkeḻu tuṟaivaṉai – yeṉṉai yeṉṟum yāmē yivvūr – piṟitoṉ ṟākak kūṟu – māṅku mākkumō vāḻiya pālē.” “palaviṟ cērnta paḻanti ṉiṉakkalai – cilaiyiṟ kāṉavaṉ centoṭai verīic – ceruvuṟu kutiraiyiṟ poṅkic cāra – liruvetir nīṭamai tayaṅkap pāyum – peruvarai yaṭukkattuk kiḻavō ṉeṉṟu – maṉṟai yaṉṉa naṭpiṉaṉ – putuvōrt tammavip paḻaṅkē ḷūrē.” eṉavarum.
neṟipaṭu nāṭṭattu nikaḻntavai maṟaippiṉum = tōḻi kūṭṭamuṇmai vaḻakkiyalāl nāṭukiṉṟa kālattuk kaṇcivappum nutalvēṟupāṭum mutaliya meyvēṟupāṭu nikaḻntuḻi avaṟṟait tōḻi aṟiyāmaluñ cevili aṟiyāmalun talaivitāṉ maṟaippiṉum: um. “kaṇṇun tōḷun taṇṇaṟuṅ katuppu – moṇṭoṭi makaḷir taṇṭaḻai yalkuluṅ – kāṇṭoṟuṅ kaviṉai yeṉṟi yatumaṟ – ṟīṇṭu maṟantaṉaiyāṟ peritē vēṇṭāy – nīyevaṉ mayaṅkiṉai tōḻi – yāyiṉuñ ciṟantaṉṟu nōyperi tuḻantē.” kātaṉmikutiyāṟ kaviṉaiyeṉaṟ pālāy vēṟupaṭṭaṉaiyeṉṟu eṟṟukku mayaṅkiṉaiyeṉat talaivi taṉvaruttam maṟaittāḷ. “tuṟaivaṉ ṟuṟanteṉat tuṟaiyirun taḻutaveṉ – mammar vāṇmuka nōkki yaṉṉaini – ṉavala muraiyeṉ ṟaṉaḷē kaṭaleṉ – pañcāyp pāvai koṇṭu – vaṇṭalañ ciṟumaṉai citaittateṉ ṟēṉē.” itu cevilikku maṟaittatu.
poṟiyiṉ yātta puṇarcci nōkki orumaik kēṇmaiyiṉ uṟukuṟai teḷintōḷ = tāṉ avaḷeṉṉum vēṟṟumaiyillāta naṭpiṉālē tōḻi taṉakku vantu kūṟiya kuṟaiyē poṟiyiṉōkkit teḷintōḷ, muṉṉart teyvappuṇarcci nikaḻntamai nōkki atukāraṇattāṉ muṭippatākat teḷinta talaivi: arumai cāṉṟa nāl iruvakai = tāṉ muṉ arumai amaintuniṉṟa nilaiyāṟ talaivaṉ taṉkaṇ nikaḻttiya meytoṭṭuppayiṟaṉ mutaliya eṭṭiṉālē: perumai cāṉṟa iyalpiṉkaṇṇum = taṉakku uḷatām perumai kūṟutaṟku amaintatōr iyalpiṉkaṇṇum: eṉṟatu, talaivaṉ ittuṇai iḷivantaṉa ceyyavum yāṉ nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkiṟṟilēṉeṉṟu taṉperumai tōḻikkuk kūṟutalām. um. “miṉṉoḷi raviraṟa liṭaipōḻum peyalēpōṟ – poṉṉakait takaivakir vakaineṟi vayaṅkiṭṭup – poḻiliṭai yiṭṭa kamaḻnaṟum pūṅkōtai – yiṉṉakai yilaṅkeyiṟṟut tēmoḻit tuvarccevvāy – naṉṉutā ṉiṉakkoṉṟu naviluvāṅ kēḷiṉi; nilleṉa niṟuttā ṉiṟuttē vantu – nutalu mukaṉun tōḷuṅ kaṇṇu – miyaluñ collu nōkkupu niṉaii – yaitēyn taṉṟu piṟaiyu maṉṟu – maitīrn taṉṟu matiyu maṉṟu – vēyamaṉ ṟaṉṟu malaiyu maṉṟu – pūvamaṉ ṟaṉṟu cuṉaiyu maṉṟu – mella viyalu mayilu maṉṟu – collat taḷaruṅ kiḷiyu maṉṟu; eṉavāṅ, kaṉaiyaṉa palapā rāṭṭip paiyeṉa – valaiyar pōlac cōrpata ṉōṟṟiyeṉ – ṉeñcu nekiḻnta cevvi kāṇūup – pulaiyar pōlap puṉka ṇōkkit – toḻalun toḻutāṉ ṟoṭalun toṭṭāṉ – kāḻvarai nillāk kaṭuṅkaḷi ṟaṉṉōṉ – ṟoḻūun toṭūu mavaṉṟaṉmai – yēḻait taṉmaiyō villai tōḻi.” itaṉuṭ pārāṭṭiyeṉap poypārāṭṭaluñ, cōrpataṉōṟṟiyeṉa neñcu nekiḻnta cevvi kūṟutaliṟ kūṭutaluṟutalum, pulaiyarpōla nōkkiyeṉa nīṭuniṉaintiraṅkalum, toḻalun toḻutāṉeṉa iṭampeṟṟuttaḻālun, toṭalun toṭṭāṉeṉa meytoṭṭuppayiṟalum, avaṉikaḻttiyavāṟuṅ kūṟi, matattāṟ parikkōlellaiyil nillāta kaḷiṟupōla vēṭkai mikutiyāṉ aṟiviṉellaiyil nillātavaṉeṉat tīrāt tōṟṟamum oruvāṟṟāṟkūṟit, taṉakkup perumaicāṉṟa iyalpaip piṉṉorukāṟ tōḻikkuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
iṉit talaivaṟkup perumai amaintaṉa eṭṭukkuṇameṉṟu kūṟi, avaṟṟai “iḷamaiyum vaṉappu milloṭu varavum – vaḷamaiyun taṟukaṇum varampil kalviyun – tēcat tamaitiyum mācil cūḻcciyum” eṉap poruḷkūṟiṉ, “aṟuvakaippaṭṭa pārppaṉappakkamum” eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya eṇkaḷ avai kūṟiya vaṭanūlkaḷil vēṟē eṇṇutaṟku uriyaṉa cila illāmal eṇṇiṉāṟpōla īṇṭum iḷamaimutaliya eṭṭum oḻiya vēṟeṇṇutaṟku uriyaṉa eṭṭut talaivaṟkiṉṟākak kūṟalvēṇṭum; īṇṭu avvāṟiṉṟit talaivaṟku uriyaṉavākap palavakaikaḷāṉ evveṭṭuḷavākak kūṟakkiṭantamaiyiṉ aṅṅaṉam āciriyar ilakkaṇaṅkūṟāreṉa maṟukka. aṉṟiyum eṭṭum eṭuttu ōtupaveṉṟum utāraṇamiṉṟeṉṟum maṟukka. iṉi muṭṭuvayiṟkaḻaṟaṉ, muṉivumeyniṟuttal, accattiṉakaṟal, avaṉpuṇarvumaṟuttal, tūtumuṉiviṉmai, tuñciccērtal, kātalkaimikal, kaṭṭuraiyiṉmai eṉpaṉa eṭṭumeṉṟu avaṟṟai īṇṭukkoṇarntu poruḷkūṟiṟ, kūṟṟuk kūṟukiṉṟaviṭattē koṇarntu kūṟṟiṉuḷ aṭaṅkak kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉṟu maṟukka.
poytalai aṭutta maṭaliṉkaṇṇum = poyyiṉait talaikkīṭākavuṭaiya maṭaliṉkaṇṇum: atu maṭaṉmā kūṟiyavaḻi ammaṭaliṉai meyyeṉak koṇṭāḷ ataṉaip poyyeṉak kōṭalām. um. “veḷḷāṅ kurukiṉ piḷḷaiyum palavē – yavaiyiṉum palavē ciṟukaruṅ kākkai – yavaiyiṉu mavaiyiṉum palavē kuvimaṭa – lōṅkirum peṇṇai mīmicait toṭutta – tūṅkaṇaṅ kurīik kūṭṭuḷa ciṉaiyē.” itu maṭaṉmā koḷḷak kuṟittōṉaip paṟavaikkuḻān tammai maṭalūra viṭāveṉa viḷaiyāṭṭuvakaiyāṟ poyyeṉṟu ikaḻntatu.
kaiyaṟu tōḻi kaṇṇīr tuṭaippiṉum = talaiviyai āṟṟuvittuk kaiyaṟṟa tōḻi talaivikaṇṇīrait tuṭaippiṉum: um. “yāmeṅ kāman tāṅkavun tāntaṅ – keḻutakai maiyiṉā ṉaḻutaṉa tōḻi – kaṉṟāṟṟup paṭutta puṉṟalaic ciṟāar – maṉṟa vēṅkai malarpata nōkki – yēṟā tiṭṭa vēmap pūcal – viṇṭōy viṭarakat tiyampuṅ – kuṉṟa nāṭaṟ kaṇṭaveṅ kaṇṇē.” taṉ āṟṟāmaikku āṟṟāta tōḻiyai āṟṟuvikkiṉṟāḷ avviraṇṭuṅ kūṟiyatu.
veṟiyāṭṭiṭattu veruviṉ kaṇṇum = talaivi vēṟupāṭu eṟṟiṉāṉāyiṟṟeṉṟu vēlaṉai viṉāy veṟiyāṭṭu eṭuttuḻit talaivi añcum accattiṉkaṇṇum: atu paṇṭēyun taṉparattaimaiyāṉ nekiḻntoḻukuvāṉ iṉṟu nam āṟṟāmaikku maruntu piṟitumuṇṭeṉṟaṟiyiṉ varaivu nīṭumeṉṟu añcutal. um. “paṉivarai nivanta payaṅkeḻu kavāaṟ – ṟuṉiyil koḷkaiyo ṭavarnamak kuvanta – viṉiya vuḷḷa miṉṉā vākki – muḷitta niṟutta nalka levvañ – cūruṟai veṟpaṉ mārpuṟat taṇita – laṟintaṉa ḷalla ḷaṉṉai vārkōṟ – ceṟintilaṅ kelvaḷai ñekiḻpata nōkkik – kaiyaṟu neñciṉaḷ viṉavaliṉ mutuvāyp – poyvala peṇṭir pirappuḷā virīi – muruka ṉāraṇaṅ keṉṟali ṉatucet – tōvat taṉṉa viṉaipuṉai nalliṟ – pāvai yaṉṉa palarāy māṇkaviṉ – paṇṭaiyiṟ ciṟakkaveṉ makaṭkeṉap paraiik – kūṭuko ḷiṉṉiyaṅ kaṟaṅkak kaḷaṉiḻait – tāṭaṇi yayarnta vāṉperum pantar – veṇpōḻ kaṭampoṭu cūṭi yiṉcī – raitamai pāṇi yirīikkai peyarāc – celvaṉ perumpeya rētti vēlaṉ – veṟiyayar veṅkaḷam poṟpa vallōḷ – poṟiyamai pāvaiyiṟ ṟūṅkal vēṇṭi – ṉeṉṉāṅ kollō tōḻi mayaṅkiya – maiyaṟ peṇṭirkku novva lāka – vāṭiya piṉṉum vāṭiya mēṉi – paṇṭaiyiṟ ciṟavā tāyi ṉimmaṟai – yalarā kāmaiyō varitē yatāaṉ – ṟaṟivaña ruṟuvi lallalkaṇ ṭaruḷi – veṟikamaḻ naṭuvē ṇalkuva ṉeṉiṉē – ceṟitoṭi yuṟṟa cellal piṟiteṉak – kāṉkeḻu nāṭaṉ kēṭpiṉ – yāṉuyir vāḻta lataṉiṉu maritē.” iṉṉāvākki niṟutta evvameṉpatu avaṉvayiṟ parattaimai, uyirvāḻtal ariteṉpatu taṉvayiṉurimai. avai veṟiyañciyavaḻi nikaḻntaṉa.
kuṟiyiṉ oppumai maruṭaṟ kaṇṇum = iravukkuṟi varun talaivaṉ ceyyuṅkuṟi piṟitoṉṟaṉāṉ nikaḻntu talaivaṉkuṟiyai ottavaḻi ataṉai meyyāka uṇarntu talaivi mayaṅkiyavaḻiyum: puṉalolippaṭuttaṉ mutaliya avaṉ ceyaṟkaiyāṉaṉṟi iyaṟkaiyāṉ nikaḻntuḻik kuṟiyiṉoppumaiyām. um. “meyyō vāḻi tōḻi cāraṉ – maipaṭ ṭaṉṉa māmuka mucukkalai – yāṟṟap pāyāt tappa lēṟṟa – kōṭṭoṭu pōki yāṅku nāṭaṉ – ṟāṉkuṟi vārāt tappaṟkut – tāmpacan taṉaveṉ ṟaṭameṉ ṟōḷē.” kōṭu āṟṟap pāyātu vēṇṭiyavāṟu pāyntu ataṉai muṟitta mucuppōla nāṅ kuṟipeṟuṅ kālattu vārātu puṭtāmē veṟittu iyampuntuṇaiyum nīṭṭittup piṉpu varutaliṟ kuṟivāyāttappu avaṉmēl ēṟṟi ataṟkut tōḷ pacantaṉaveṉṟu piṉṉorunāḷ avaṉvantuḻit tōḻiyai nōkki ivvaravu meyyōveṉavē avviraṇṭum peṟṟām. “aṇikaṭaṟ ṟaṇcērppaṉ ṟērpparimā pūṇṭa – maṇiyarava meṉṟeḻuntu pōntēṉ – kaṇivirumpu – puḷḷaravaṅ kēṭṭup peyarntē ṉoḷiyiḻā – yuḷḷuruku neñciṉēṉ yāṉ.” ituvum atu.
varaivu talaivariṉum = veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉarāyiṉum muṉṉarāyiṉum varainteytutaṟ ceykai talaivaṉkaṇ nikaḻiṉum: āṇṭu muṟkālattu nikaḻnta āṟṟāmaipaṟṟi avviraṇṭuṅ kūṟum. um. “naṉṉāṭ ṭalaivaru mellainam māṭṭilait – tannāṇṭān tāṅkuvā reṉṉōṟ ṟaṉarkol; puṉavēṅkait tātuṟaikkum poṉṉaṟai muṉṟi – ṉaṉaviṟ puṇarcci naṭakkumā maṉṟō; naṉaviṟ puṇarcci naṭakkalu māṅkē – kaṉaviṟ puṇarcci kaṭitumā maṉṟō.” eṉa nāṇtāṅki āṟṟuvārum uḷarō veṉavuṅ kaṉaviṟ puṇarcci kaṭitumeṉavum iraṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. “kollaip puṉatta vakilcumantu kalpāyntu – vāṉi ṉaruvi tatumpak kaviṉiya – nāṭa ṉayaṉuṭaiya ṉeṉpataṉā ṉīppiṉum – vāṭaṉ maṟantaṉa tōḷ.” nayaṉuṭaiyaṉ eṉpataṉāl varaivu talaivantamaiyum nīppiṉumeṉpataṉāl avaṉvayiṟ parattaimaiyuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ.
kaḷavu aṟivuṟiṉum = tam oḻukalāṟu puṟattārkkup pulaṉākat talaivaṉ oḻukiṉum: āṇṭum avviraṇṭuṅ kūṟum. um. “nāṇmaḻai talaiiya naṉṉeṭuṅ kuṉṟattu – mālkaṭaṟ ṟiraiyi ṉiḻitaru maruvi – yakaliruṅ kāṉat takalaṇi nōkkit – tāṅkavun takaivarai nillā nīrcuḻal – pōteḻiṉ maḻaikkaṇ kaluḻtali ṉaṉṉai – yevaṉcey taṉaiyōniṉ ṉilaṅkeyi ṟuṇkeṉa – melliya liṉiya kūṟaliṉ valvirain – tuyiriṉuñ ciṟanta nāṇumaṟan turaitta – luyntaṉeṉ vāḻi tōḻi cāraṟ – kānta ḷūtiya maṇiniṟat tumpi – tīntoṭai narampi ṉimirum – vāṉṟōy veṟpaṉ mārpaṇaṅ keṉavē.” yāṉ avaṉai etirppaṭṭa iṭaṅkaṇṭu aḻutēṉāka ataṉaikkaṇṭu nī evaṉ ceytaṉaiyeṉa viṉāya aṉṉaikku immaṟaiyiṉaik kūṟaluṟṟut tavirntēṉeṉat tāy kaḷavaṟivuṟṟavāṟu kūṟak karuti avaṉvayiṟ parattaimai kūṟiṟṟu.
tamar taṟ kātta kāraṇa maruṅkiṉum = aṅṅaṉaṅ kaḷavaṟivuṟṟa ataṉṟalaic cevilimutaliya cuṟṟattār talaiviyaik kāttaṟku ētuvākiya kāraṇappakutikkaṇṇum: āṇṭun tamarai nonturaiyātu avaṉvayiṟ parattaimai kūṟum. kāraṇamāvaṉa talaivi tōṟṟappolivum varuttamum ayalār kūṟum alarumām. um. “aṭampi ṉāymalar viraii neyta – ṉeṭuntoṭai vēynta nīrvār kūnta – lōrai makaḷi rañciyīr ñeṇṭu – kaṭaliṟ parikkun tuṟaivaṉo ṭorunā – ṇakku viḷaiyāṭaluṅ kaṭitaṉ – ṟaitemak kamma meytōy naṭpē.” itu vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu iṟceṟittamai taṉṉuḷḷē kūṟiyatu. “perunī raḻuvat tentai tanta – koḻumī ṉuṇaṅkaṟ paṭupuḷ ḷōppi – yekkarp puṉṉai yiṉṉiḻa lacaiic – cekkar ñeṇṭiṉ kuṇṭaḷai keṇṭi – ñāḻa lōṅkuciṉait toṭutta koṭuṅkaḻit – tāḻai vīḻkayiṟ ṟūca ṟūṅkik – koṇṭa liṭumaṇaṟ kuravai muṉaiyiṉ – veṇṭalaip puṇari yāyamo ṭāṭi – maṇippūm paintaḻai taii yaṇittakaip – palpūṅ kāṉa lalkiṉam varutal – kauvai nallaṇaṅ kuṟṟa vivvūrk – koṭitaṟīip peṇṭir coṟkoṇ ṭaṉṉai – kaṭikoṇ ṭaṉaḷē tōḻi peruntuṟai – yellaiyu miravu meṉṉā kutaleṉa – valava ṉāynta vaṇpari – nilavumaṇaṟ koṭkumōr tēruṇ ṭeṉavē.” “peruṅkaṭaṟ ṟiraiyatu ciṟuveṇ kākkai – kaḷiṟṟuc ceviyaṉṉa pācaṭai mayakkiya – paṉikkaḻi tuḻavum pāṉāṭ ṭaṉittōr – tērvantu peyaru meṉpa vataṟkoṇ – ṭōru malaikku maṉṉai piṟarum – piṉṉu viṭuttuppiṉ miṉṉiḻai makaḷi – riḷaiyaru mutiyaru muḷarē – yalaiyāt tāyaroṭu naṟpā lōrē” ivai piṟarkūṟṟāṟ tamar kāttaṉa. “mulaimukañ ceytaṉa muḷḷeyi ṟilaṅkiṉa – talaimuṭi cāṉṟa” eṉṟaṉa tōṟṟap polivāṟ kāttaṉa. itaṟkum avviraṇṭum uḷa.
taṉkuṟi taḷḷiya teruḷāk kālai vantaṉaṉ peyarnta vaṟuṅkaḷam nōkkit taṉ piḻaippākat taḻīit tēṟalum = talaivi taṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kuṟiyiṭaṅkaḷ iṟceṟippu mutaliya kāraṇaṅkaḷāṉ iḻakkappaṭṭaṉavaṟṟai ivai iḻakkumeṉa muntuṟavē uṇarāta kālattu muṟkūṟiya kuṟiyiṭamē iṭamāka vantu talaivaṉ kūṭātu peyartalāṟ tamakkup payampaṭāta vaṟuṅkaḷattai niṉaintu ataṉait talaivaṟku muntuṟavē kuṟipeyarttiṭappeṟāta tavaṟu taṉmēl ēṟṟikkoṇṭu tōḻiyaiyum atukūṟiṟṟilaḷeṉat taṉṉoṭu taḻīikkoṇṭu talaivi teḷitaṟkaṇṇum: ākavē, avaṉtavaṟṟait taṉtavaṟu ākkiṉaḷām. taḻīi, tōḻiyait taḻīi. attavaṟu avaṉkaṭ cellāmaṟ taṉatākat tēṟiṉāḷ. um. “viriyiṇar vēṅkai vaṇṭupaṭu kaṇṇiyaṉ – ṟeriyitaḻk kuvaḷait tēmpāy tāra – ṉañcilai yiṭava tāka veñcelaṟ – kaṇaivalan terintu tuṇaipaṭarn tuḷḷi – varutal vāyvatu vāṉṟōy veṟpaṉ – vantaṉa ṉāyi ṉantaḷirc ceyalaik – kāvi lōṅkuciṉait toṭutta vīḻkayiṟ – ṟūcaṉ māṟiya maruṅkum pāypuṭai – nāṭā maiyiṟ kaluḻpila tēṟi – nīṭitaḻt talaiiya kaviṉpeṟu nīlaṅ – kaṇṇeṉa malarnta cuṉaiyum vaṇpaṟai – maṭakkiḷi yeṭuttal cellāt taṭaṅkuraṟ – kuvavuppoṟai yiṟutta kōṟṟalai yiruvik – koytoḻi puṉamu nōkki neṭituniṉaintu – paitalaṉ peyarkuvaṉ kollō vaitē – kayaveḷ ḷaruvi cūṭiya vuyarvaraik – kūuṅ kaṇṇatem mūreṉa – vāṅkatai yaṟivuṟaṉ maṟanticiṉ yāṉē.” itaṉuḷ ūcaṉ māṟutalum puṉamun taṉkuṟi taḷḷiya iṭaṉ; maṟanticiṉ eṉṟatu teruḷākkālai; kūuṅkaṇṇatu ūreṉa uṇarttāmaiyiṉ, iṭaiyīṭu paṭuvataṉṟi avaṉkaṭ tavaṟuṇṭōveṉat taṉ piḻaippākat taḻīiyiṉāḷ. itu ciṟaippuṟamāka varaivukaṭāyatu.
vaḻuviṉṟu nilaiiya iyaṟpaṭu poruḷiṉum = vaḻuppaṭuttaliṉṟi niṉṟa iyaṟpaṭa moḻitaṟ poruṇmaik kaṇṇum. vaḻuviṉṟi nilaiiya eṉṟataṉāṟ tōḻi iyaṟpaḻittuḻiyē iyaṟpaṭa moḻivateṉka. talaivaṉ vaḻuvait tōḻi kūṟiyataṟkup poṟātu tāṉ iyaṟpaṭa moḻintatallatu taṉmaṉattu avaṉ parattaimai karututaluṭaimaiyiṟ poruḷ vēṟukuṟittāḷām. um. “aṭampamar neṭuṅkoṭu yuḷputain toḷippa – veṇmaṇal virikkun taṇṇan tuṟaivaṉ – koṭiya ṉāyiṉu māka – vavaṉē tōḻiyeṉ ṉuyirkā valaṉē.” “toṭinaṭan tiḻiyac cāayt tōḷavar – koṭumai kūṟiṉa vāyiṉuṅ koṭumai – nalvarai nāṭaṟ killai tōḻiyeṉ – ṉeñciṟ pirintatūu milarē taṅkuṟai – nōkkaṅ kaṭintatūu milarē – nilattiṉum peritē nērnta naṭpē.” eṉavarum. akaṉṟu uyarntu tāḻntavaṟṟuṭ peritākiya naṭpuṭaiyavaṉ eṉak kūṟiyatu. orukālaikku orukāṟ perukumeṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāḷāyiṉum namatu naṭpuppōl orukālē peruttatillaiyeṉa iraṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ.
poḻutum āṟum puraivatu aṉmaiyiṉ aḻivu talaivanta cintaikkaṇṇum = talaiviyun tōḻiyun talaivaṉ iravukkuṟi varuṅkāṟ poḻutāyiṉum neṟiyāyiṉum iṭaiyūṟākip poruntutaliṉmaiyiṉ aḻivu talaittalai ciṟappa vanta ārāyccikkaṇṇum: āṇṭum avviraṇṭum nikaḻum. um. “maṉṟupā ṭavintu maṉaimaṭin taṉṟē – koṉṟō raṉṉa koṭumaiyō ṭiṉṟē – yāmaṅ koḷavariṟ kaṇaiik kāmaṅ – kaṭaliṉu muraiik  ceṉṟa tiṉṟē.” kāmaṅ karaipoḻiyāniṟkavumeṉṉa naṉṟikaruti iruvaroṭuñ cūḻātu ceṉṟatu neñceṉa iraṇṭuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. maṉaimaṭintaṉṟeṉpatu poḻutu, ciṟuneṟiyeṉpatu āṟṟiṉṉāmai; itaṉaip poruḷiyaluṭ kūṟātu taṉvayiṉurimaiyum avaṉvayiṟ parattaimaiyumpaṟṟi īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāṉ. “kuṟaiyoṉ ṟuṭaiyēmāṟ ṟōḻi niṟaiyillā – maṉṉuyirk kēmañ – ceyalvēṇṭu miṉṉē – yaravaḻaṅku nīḷcōlai nāṭaṉai veṟpi – liravarā leṉpa turai.” “vaḷaivāyc ciṟukiḷi” eṉṉuṅ kuṟuntokaiyum atu.
kāmañ ciṟappiṉum = talaivi kāmañciṟantu tōṉṟiṉum: um. “oliyavin taṭaṅki yāma naḷḷeṉak – kalikeḻu pākkan tuyiṉmaṭin taṉṟē – toṉṟuṟai kaṭavuḷ cērnta parārai – maṉṟap peṇṇai vāṅkumaṭaṟ kuṭampait – tuṇaipuṇa raṉṟi luyavukkural kēṭṭoṟun – tuñcāk kaṇṇa ṭuyaraṭac cāay – namvayiṉ varuntu naṉṉuta leṉpa – tuṇṭukol vāḻi tōḻi teṇkaṭal – vaṇkaip paratava riṭṭa ceṅkōṟ – koṭumuṭi yavvalai pariyap pōkkik – kaṭumura ṇeṟicuṟā vaḻaṅku – neṭunīrc cērppaṉṟa ṉeñcat tāṉē.” “ñāyiṟu paṭṭa vakalvāy vāṉat – taḷiya tāṉē koṭuñciṟaip paṟavai – yiṟaiyuṟa vōṅkiya neṟiyayaṉ marāatta – piḷḷai yuḷvāyc cerīiya – viraikoṇ ṭavaiyum viraiyumāṟ celavē.” “koṭuntā ḷalava kuṟaiyā mirappē – moṭuṅkā volikaṭaṟcērppa – ṉeṭuntēr – kaṭanta vaḻiyaiyeṅ kaṇṇārak kāṇa – naṭantu citaiyāti nī.” “muṭamutir puṉṉaip paṭukōṭ ṭirunta – maṭamuṭai nāraik kuraittēṉ – kaṭaṉaṟintu – pāytiraic cērppaṉ parittēr varakkaṇṭu – nītakā teṉṟē niṟuttu.” eṉavarum.
avaṉ aḷi ciṟappiṉum = talaivikkuk kāmamikka kaḻipaṭar ciṟantāṟ pōlvatu talaivaṉkaṭ ciṟantuḻi atukāraṇattāl avaṉ aḷi ciṟantu tōṉṟiṉum: ivvāṟu aritiṉvarukiṉṟāṉ varaikiṉṟalaṉeṉa avviraṇṭun tōṉṟum. um. “iruḷkiḻippatupōṉ miṉṉi vāṉan – tuḷitalaik koṇṭa naḷipeya ṉaṭunāṇ – miṉmiṉi moytta murivuvāyp puṟṟam – poṉṉeṟi pitiriṟ cuṭara vāṅkik – kurumpi keṇṭu peruṅkai yēṟṟai – yirumpucey kollariṟ ṟōṉṟu māṅka – ṇāṟē yarumara piṉavē yāṟē – cuṭṭunarp paṉikkuñ cūruṭai mutalai – kaḻaimāy nīttaṅ kalporu tiraṅka – lañcuvan tamiya meṉṉātu mañcucuman – tāṭukaḻai naralu maṇaṅkuṭaik kavāa – ṉīruyirp piṇaviṉ vayavuppaci kaḷaiiya – viruṅkaḷi ṟaṭṭa peruñciṉa vuḻuvai – nāma nallarā katirpaṭa vumiḻnta – meymaṇi viḷakkiṟ pulāa līrkkum – vāṇaṭan taṉṉa vaḻakkaruṅ kavalai – yuḷḷuna ruṭkum kallaṭar ciṟuneṟi – yaruḷpuri neñcamō ṭeḵku tuṇaiyāka – vantōṉ koṭiyaṉu mallaṉ ṟanta – nītava ṟuṭaiyaiyu mallai niṉvayi – ṉāṉā varumpaṭar ceyta – yāṉē tōḻi tavaṟuṭai yēṉē.” vantōṉeṉpatu avaṉaḷi ciṟattal, tavaṟuṭaiyēṉeṉpatu taṉvayiṉurimai, koṭiyaṉumallaṉeṉpatu avaṉvayiṟ parattaimai. “cēṇōṉ māṭṭiya naṟumpukai ñekiḻi – vāṉa mīṉiṉ vayiṉvayi ṉimaikku – mōṅkumalai nāṭaṉ cāntupula rakala – muḷḷi ṉuṇṇōy malkum – pulliṉ māyva tevaṉko laṉṉāy.” ituvum atu.
ēmañ cāṉṟa uvakaik kaṇṇum = nālvakaip puṇarcciyāl nikaḻuṅ kaḷaviṉkaṇ eññāṉṟum iṭaiyīṭupaṭāmaṟ talaivaṉ vantu kūṭutal iṉpattiṟkup pātukāval amainta uvakaiyiṉait talaivi eytiyakkaṇṇum: atu eññāṉṟuṅ kūṭṭam peṟṟamaiyāṉ makiḻntu kūṟutalām. um. “nōyalaik kalaṅkiya mataṉaḻi poḻutiṟ – kāmañ ceppa lāṇmakaṟ kamaiyum – yāṉeṉ, peṇmai taṭpa nuṇṇitiṟ ṟāṅkik – kaival kammiyaṉ kaviṉpeṟak kaḻāa – maṇṇāp pacumut tēyppak kuviyiṇarp – puṉṉai yarumpiya pulavunīrc cērppa – ṉeṉṉa makaṉko ṟōḻi taṉvayi – ṉārva muṭaiya rāki – mārpaṇaṅ kuṟunarai yaṟiyā tōṉē.” maṇṇāp pacumuttēyppa nuṇṇitiṟ ṟāṅkip peṇmai taṭpaveṉa māṟik kaḻuvāta paciyamuttan taṉatu mikka oḷiyai maṟaittuk kāṭṭiṉāṟpōla yāmum puṇarcciyāl nikaḻnta mikka nalaṉaip pulappaṭāmal aritākat tāṅkip peṇmaiyāṟ takaittuk koḷḷumpaṭiyākat taṉmārpāl varuttamuṟṟāraik kaṇṭu aṟiyātōṉākiya cērppaṉai eṉṉamakaṉeṉṟu collappaṭumeṉa makiḻntu kūṟiṉāḷ. ārvamuṭaiyarāka vēṇṭi mārpaṇaṅkuṟunarai aṟiyātōṉeṉka. alarāmaṟ kuvinta kottaiyuṭaiya puṉṉaikkaṇṇē pulāṉāṟṟattaiyuṭaiya nīr teṟittarumpiya cērppaṉeṉṟataṉāṟ puṉṉaiyiṭattut tōṉṟiya pulāṉāṟṟattaip pūvirintu keṭukkumāṟupōla varaintu koṇṭu kaḷaviṉkaṇ vantakuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa oḻukuvaṉeṉpatu uḷḷuṟai. “iraṇṭaṟi kiḷavi” eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuṭ tōṟṟappolivai maṟaippaṉeṉat talaivaṉ kūṟiyavāṟum uṇarka.
maṟaintavaṟkāṇṭaṉ mutaliya āṟaṟkum ummaiyum urupum virittu ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum ummaivirikka; ummai virikka vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku ummaiyum, iraṇṭum virikka vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟiṟku iraṇṭum virittu, avaṟṟiṟkum ēṉai viṉaiyeccaṅkaṭkuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻtaluḷaveṉa muṭikka. kūṟṟu atikārattāṉ varum. uyirākkālattu uyirttalumuḷaveṉa muṭikka.
ōriṭattāṉa taṉvayiṉ urimaiyum avaṉ vayiṟ parattaiyum uḷa = ikkūṟṟu muppattāṟaṉuḷ orōviṭaṅkaḷilē taṉṉiṭattu aṉpiṟku urimaiyuṇṭākavum avaṉiṭattu ayaṉmai uṇṭākavuṅ kūṟṟu nikaḻtaluḷa: āṉ āṉaveṉa īṟu tirintatu.
aṉṉavum uḷa = avai pōlvaṉa piṟavum uḷa. e-ṟu. aṉṉapiṟaveṉṟataṉāṉ iṉṉun talaivikūṟṟāy ivaṟṟiṉ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉāṉ amaikka. “piṇiniṟan tīrntu perumpaṇaittōḷ vīṅka – vaṇimalai nāṭaṉ varuvāṉko ṟōḻi – kaṇiniṟa vēṅkai kamaḻntuvaṇ ṭārkku – maṇiniṟa mālaip poḻutu.” itu talaivi iravukkuṟi nayantu kūṟiyatu. “peyalkāṉ maṟaittaliṉ vicumpukā ṇalarē – nīrparan toḻukali ṉilaṅkā ṇalarē – yellai cēṟali ṉiruḷperitu paṭṭaṉṟu – pallōr tuñcum pāṉāṭ kaṅkul – yāṅkuvanta ṉaiyō vōṅkal veṟpa – vēṅkai kamaḻumeñ ciṟukuṭi – yāṅkaṟin taṉaiyō nōkō yāṉē.” itu iravukkuṟi vanta talaivaṉai nōkkik kūṟiyatu. “koṭuṅkural kuṟaitta cevvāyp paiṅkiḷi – yañca lōmpi yārpataṅ koṇṭu – niṉkuṟai muṭitta piṉṟai yeṉkuṟai – colla vēṇṭumār kaitoḻu tirappal – palkōṭ palaviṉ cāralavar nāṭṭu – niṉkiḷai maruṅkiṟ cēṟiyāyi – ṉammalai kiḻavōṟ kuraimati yimmalai – kāṉak kuṟavar maṭamaka – ḷēṉal kāva lāyiṉa ḷeṉavē.” eṉavum, “ōṅkaṟ ṟuṟuvaraimēṟ kāntaḷ kaṭikaviṉap – pāmpeṉa vōṭi yurumiṭittuk kaṇṭiraṅkum – pūṅkuṉṟa nāṭaṉ puṇarntavan nāṭpōla – nīṅki nekiḻnta vaḷai.” eṉavum, “maṉṟap palaviṉ cuḷaiviḷai tīmpaḻa – muṇṭuvantu manti mulaivaruṭak – kaṉṟamarn tāmā curakku maṇimalai nāṭaṉai – yāmāp pirita lilam.” eṉavum varum. “avaruṭai nāṭṭa vāyiṉu mavarpōṟ – pirita ṟēṟṟāy pēraṉ piṉavē – yuvakkā ṇeṉṟu muḷḷuva pōlac – centārc ciṟupeṭai taḻīip – paiṅkura lēṉaṟ paṭartaruṅ kiḷiyē.” pakaṟkuṟikkaṭ talaivaṉīṭa āṟṟātu tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu. (20)
112. vakaiviṭai vaitta kālattu varuntiṉum
varaiya nāḷiṭai vantōṉ muṭṭiṉu
muraiyeṉat tōḻik kuraittaṟ kaṇṇun
tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamu muḷavē.
ituvum atikārattāṟ talaivikūṟṟu iṉṉavāṟumām eṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) varaiviṭaivaitta kālattu varuntiṉum = varaivumāṭcimaippaṭāniṟkavum poruḷkāraṇattāṉ ataṟku iṭaiyīṭākat talaivaṉ nīkkivaittup pirinta kālattut talaivi varuttameytiṉum: āṇṭut tōḻi viṉavāmalun tāṉē kūṟumeṉṟāṉ, āṟṟuvittup pirital kaḷaviṟkup perumpāṉmai iṉmaiyiṉ. vaittaveṉṟatu nīkkapporuṭṭu. varuntutal āṟṟuvippāriṉmaiyiṉ varuttamikutalām.
varaiyā nāḷiṭai vantōṉ muṭṭiṉum = varaiyātoḻukun talaivaṉ oruñāṉṟu tōḻiyaiyāṉum āyattaiyāṉuñ ceviliyaiyāṉuṅ katumeṉa etirppiṉum: uraiyeṉat tōḻikku uraittaṟkaṇṇum = notumalar varaiviṟku maṇamuraciyampiyavaḻiyāṉum piṟāṇṭāṉun tōḻikku iṉṉavāṟu kūṭṭam nikaḻntateṉak kūṟi ataṉai namaraṟiyak kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉṟun talaivaṟku nam varuttamaṟiyak kūṟalvēṇṭumeṉṟuṅ kūṟutaṟkaṇṇum: tāṉē kūṟum kālamum uḷavē = immūṉṟu pakutiyiṉun tōḻi viṉāvāmaṟ talaivi tāṉē kūṟuṅ kālamum uḷa. e-ṟu. ummaiyāṟ tōḻi viṉaviya iṭattuk kūṟalē valiyuṭaittu. um. “arumpaṟa malarnta karuṅkāl vēṅkai – mēkkeḻu peruñciṉai yirunta tōkai – pūkkoy makaḷiriṟ ṟōṉṟu nāṭaṉ – ṟakāaṉ pōlat tāṉṟītu moḻiyiṉun – taṉkaṇ kaṇṭatu poykkuva taṉṟē – muttunirai yotta muḷḷēyiṟṟut tuvarvāy – varaiyāṭu vaṇpaṟaḻt tantaik – kaṭuvaṉ – ṟāṉatu poyppiṉ yāṉevaṉ ceykō – tiṉaittā ḷaṉṉa ciṟupacuṅkāla – voḻukunī rāral pārkkuṅ – kuruku muṇṭutā maṇanta ñāṉṟē.” ivaṟṟuṭ tuṟantāṉpōlavum maṟantāṉpōlavuṅ karutit tāṉ tītumoḻiyiṉumeṉavum yāṉevaṉ ceykōveṉavun tōḻi viṉavākkālattu avaṉtavaṟṟai varaiviṭaivaittaliṉ āṟṟāmaikku aṟivittāḷ. “pakaleri cuṭariṉ mēṉi cāyavum - pāmpūr matiyi ṉutaloḷi karappavu – meṉakkunī yuraiyā yāyiṉai niṉakkiyā – ṉuyirpakut taṉṉa māṇpiṉe ṉātali – ṉatukaṇ ṭiciṉāl yāṉē yeṉṟunaṉi – yaḻuta lāṉṟici ṉāyiḻai yolikura – lēṉal kāvali ṉiṭaiyuṟ ṟoruvaṉ – kaṇṇiyaṉ kaḻalaṉ ṟāraṉ ṟaṇṇeṉac – ciṟupuṟaṅ kavaiyiṉa ṉāka vataṟkoṇ – ṭaḵtē niṉainta neñcamo – ṭiḵtā kiṉṟiyā ṉuṟṟa nōyē.” itu tōḻi viṉāviyavaḻit talaivi kūṟiyatu. varaiviṭaivaittakālattu varuntiṉum eṉpataṉait tokuttamoḻiyiṉ vakuttaṉarkōṭal eṉṉun tantiravuttiyākakkoṇṭu ataṉkaṇ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellāṅkoḷka. “uraitticiṟ ṟōḻiyatu puraittō vaṉṟē – yaruntuya ruḻattalu māṟṟā mataṉṟalaip – perumpiṟi tāta lataṉiṉu mañcutu – maṉṉō piṉṉu naṉmalai nāṭaṉ – piriyā vaṉpiṉa riruvaru meṉṉu – malarataṟ kañciṉaṉ kollō palaruṭaṉ – ṟuñcūr yāmat tāṉumeṉ – ṉeñcat tallatu varavaṟi yāṉē.” itu varaviṭaik kavaṉṟu tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu. “atuko ṟōḻi kāma nōyē” eṉṉum pāṭṭum atu. “tōḻi vāḻi mēṉāṭ cāraṟ – koṭiyōra kuṉṟam paṉippa neṭituniṉṟu – puyaṟoṭaṅ kiṉṟē poyyā vāṉat – taṉaivara laḻituḷi talaii – vemmulai yāka naṉaikkumeṅ kaṇṇē.” itu varaiviṭaip paruvaṅkaṇṭu āṟṟātu tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu. “paṉipputa livarnta paiṅkoṭi yavaraik – kiḷivā yoppi ṉoḷiviṭu paṉmalar – verukkup palluruviṉ mullaiyo ṭuñala – vāṭai vantataṉ ṟalaiyu nōyporak – kaṇṭiciṉ vāḻi tōḻi teṇṭiraik – kaṭalāḻ kalattiṟ ṟōṉṟi – mālai maṟaiyumavar maṇineṭuṅ kuṉṟē.” itu paruvaṅkaṇṭu āṟṟātu tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu. “nōyuṅ kaimikap peritē meyyun – tīyumiḻ teṟaliṉ veytā kiṉṟē – poyyeṉac ciṟitāṅ kuyiriya paiyeṉa – muṉṟiṟ koḷiṉēr nantuvaḷ periteṉa – niraiyē neñcat taṉṉaik kuyttāṇ – ṭuraiyiṉi vāḻi tōḻi puraiyi – ṉuṇṇē relvaḷai ñekiḻttōṉ kuṉṟat – taṇṇa ṉeṭuvarai yāṭit taṇṇeṉa – viyalaṟai mūḻkiya vaḷiyeṉ – pacalai yākan tīṇṭiya ciṟitē.” itu varaiviṭai āṟṟāmai mikkuḻi avaṉvaraiyiṉ mūḻkiya kāṟṟu eṉmeykkaṭ paṭiṉum āṟṟalāmeṉṟatu. “amma vāḻi tōḻi yavāpō – ṉammuṭai vāḻkkai maṟantaṉṟu kollō – maṉaiyeṟi yulakkaiyiṟ ṟiṉaikiḷi kaṭiyuṅ – kāṉa nāṭaṉ pirinteṉat – tāṉum pirintaṉṟeṉ māmaik kaviṉē.” itu vaṉpuṟaiyetiraḻintatu. “ciṟupuṉ mālai ciṟupuṉ mālai – tīppaṉip paṉṉa taṇvaḷi yacaiic – cekkar koṇṭa ciṟupuṉ mālai – vaikalum varutiyā lemakkē – yoṉṟuñ collāyavar kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭṭē.” itu mālaippoḻutu kaṇṭu varuntik kūṟiyatu. iṉṉum vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉkaṇ aṭakkuka. “kēṭṭiciṉ vāḻi tōḻi yalkaṟ – poyva lāḷaṉ meyyuṟa marīi – vāyttakaip poykkaṉā maruṭṭa vēṟṟeḻun – tamaḷi taivan taṉaṉē kuvaḷai – vaṇṭupaṭu malariṟ cāayt – tamiyōṉ maṉṟa vaḷiyēṉ yāṉē.” varaitaṟkup piriya varuntukiṉṟatu eṉṉeṉṟāṭkuk kaṉavu nalivuraittatu. “āṭamaik kuyiṉṟa vavirtuḷai maruṅkiṟ – kōṭai yavvaḷi kuḻalicai yākap – pāṭiṉ ṉaruvip paṉinī riṉṉicai – tōṭamai muḻaviṉ ṟutaikura lākak – kaṇakkalai yikukkuṅ kaṭuṅkuraṟ ṟūmpoṭu – malaippūñ cāral vaṇṭiyā ḻāka – viṉpa limiḻicai kēṭṭuk kaḷiciṟantu – manti nallavai maruḷvaṉa nōkkak – kaḻaivaḷa raṭukkat tiyali yāṭumayil – viḻavukkaḷa viṟaliyiṟ ṟōṉṟu nāṭa – ṉuruvaval viṟpaṟṟi yampu terintu – ceruccey yāṉai ceṉṉeṟi viṉaiip – pularkura lēṉaṟ puḻaiyuṭai yoruciṟai – malartār mārpa ṉiṉṟōṟ kaṇṭōr – palarcilar vāḻi tōḻi yavaru – ḷāriruṭ kaṅku laṇaiyoṭu porunti – yōryā ṉākuva tevaṉko – ṉīrvār kaṇṇoṭu ñekiḻtō ḷēṉē.” avaṉai āyattār palaruṅ kaṇṭāreṉa vantōṉ muṭṭiyavāṟum avaruḷ nekiḻtōḷēṉ yāṉēyeṉat tāṉē kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka. “tāḻai kurukīṉun taṇṇan tuṟaivaṉai – māḻaimā ṉōkkiṉ maṭamoḻi – nūḻai – nuḻaiyu maṭamakaṉ yārkoleṉ ṟaṉṉai – puḻaiyu maṭaittāḷ katavu.” “nakainī kēḷāy tōḻi yalkal – vayanā yeṟintu vaṉpaṟaḻ taḻīi – yiḷaiya reytutaṉ maṭakkik kiḷaiyoṭu – nāṉmulaip piṇaval celiya kāṉoḻin – tarumpuḻai muṭukka rāṭkuṟittu niṉṟa – taṟukaṭ paṉṟi nōkkik kāṉavaṉ – kuṟukiṉaṉ ṟoṭutta kūrvāyp pakaḻi – maṭaicelaṉ muṉpiṟṟaṉ paṭaicelac cellā – taruvaḻi yilaṅkumeṉ peruviṟal pōṉmeṉa – veyyātu peyaruṅ kuṉṟa nāṭaṉ – ceṟivuya ṟoṭakkaliṟ parīippuri yaviḻntēntu – kuvavu moympiṟ pūccōr mālai – yeṟṟiyiṟ kayiṟṟi ṉeḻilvantu tuyalvara – vilvantu niṉṟōṟ kaṇṭaṉa ḷaṉṉai – vallē yeṉmuka nōkki – nallai maṉṉeṉa nakūuppeyarn tōḷē.” ivai vantōṉ ceviliyai etirttuḻik kūṟiyaṉa. “koṭiyavuṅ kōṭṭavu nīriṉṟi niṟampeṟap – poṭiyaḻaṟ puṟantanta pūvāppūm polaṅkōtait – toṭiceṟi yāppamai yarimuṉkai yaṉaittōḷā – yaṭiyuṟai yaruḷāmai yottatō niṉakkeṉa; narantanā ṟiruṅkūnta leñcātu naṉipaṟṟip – polampuṉai makaravāy nuṅkiya cikaḻikai – nalampeṟac cuṟṟiya kuralamai yorukāḻ – viraṉmuṟai cuṟṟi mōttalu mōntaṉaṉ; naṟāavaviḻn taṉaveṉmel viraṟpōtu koṇṭu – ceṟāac ceṅkaṇ putaiya vaittup – paṟāak kuruki ṉuyirttalu muyirttaṉaṉ; toyyi liḷamulai yiṉiya taivantu – toyyalan taṭakkaiyiṉ vīḻpiṭi yaḷikku – maiyal yāṉaiyiṉ maruṭṭa maruṭṭiṉaṉ; ataṉā, lalla kaḷaintaṉaṉ ṟōḻi naṉṉaka – raruṅkaṭi nīvāmaik kūṟi ṉaṉṟeṉa – niṉṉoṭu cūḻva tōḻi nayampurin – tiṉṉatu ceytā ḷivaḷeṉa – maṉṉā vulakattu maṉṉuvatu puraimē.” eṉak kaippaṭṭuk kalaṅkip puṇarcci nikaḻntamai kūṟi aruṅkaṭi nīvāmaik kūṟiṉ naṉṟeṉat tamarkkuk kūṟumāṟu tōḻikkut talaivi kūṟiṉāḷ. curitakattu irukāṟ tōḻiyeṉṟāḷ nāṇut taḷaiyāka maṟaikarantavāṟu tīrat tōḻikku mukamaṉāka. “eritakain taṉṉa centalai yaṉṟil – piriyiṉ vāḻā teṉpōṟ ṟeyya – tuṟaimel valampuri tuṇaicēt tōṭik – karuṅkāl veṇkuruku payiṟarum – peruṅkaṭaṟ paṭappaiyeñ ciṟunal lūrē.” itu aṉṟil piriyiṉ vāḻāteṉak kūṟeṉṟatu. “aiyō veṉayām paiyeṉak kūṟiṟ – kēṭkuvar kollō tāmē mākkaṭaṟ – parūuttirai tokutta nuṇkaṇ veṇmaṇa – liṉṉun tūrā kāṇavar – poṉṉi ṉeṭuntēr pōkiya neṟiyē.” avar iṉṉum pōvataṟkumuṉṉē namvaruttattai veḷippaṭak kūṟeṉṟatu. “eṉṉaiko ṟōḻi yavarkaṇṇu naṉkillai – yaṉṉai mukaṉu matuvākum – poṉṉalar – puṉṉaiyam pūṅkāṉaṟ cērppaṉait takkatō – niṉṉalla nilleṉ ṟurai.” ivai talaivaṟkuk kūṟeṉṟaṉa. ivai talaivi aṟattoṭu niṟṟaṟpakuti. tōḻikkē uraittaṟkut tōḻikkeṉṟār. (21)
113. uyiriṉuñ ciṟantaṉṟu nāṇē nāṇiṉuñ
ceyirtīr kāṭcik kaṟpucciṟan taṉṟeṉat
tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu
kāmak kiḻava ṉuḷvaḻip paṭiṉun
tāvi ṉaṉmoḻi kiḻavi kiḷappiṉu
māvakai piṟavun tōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē.
itu talaivikūṟṟiṟkuc ciṟappillaṉa kūṟi, avaiyum akapporuḷām eṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) uyiriṉum nāṇ ciṟantaṉṟu = ellāvaṟṟiṉuñ ciṟanta uyiriṉum makaḷirkku nāṇ ciṟantatu: nāṇiṉuñ ceyirtīr kaṟpuk kāṭci ciṟantaṉṟu = : annāṇiṉuṅ kuṟṟan tīrnta kaṟpiṉai naṉṟeṉṟu maṉattāṟ kāṇutal ciṟantatu: eṉat tollōr kiḷavi pulliya neñcamoṭu = eṉṟu muṉṉuḷḷōr kūṟiya kūṟṟiṉaip poruntiya neñcuṭaṉē: kāmak kiḻavaṉ uḷvaḻip paṭiṉum = talaivaṉ iruntaviṭattē talaivi tāṉē celliṉum: tāvil naṉmoḻi kiḻavi kiḷappiṉum = maṉavaliyiṉṟic celvāmeṉak kūṟum naṉmoḻiyiṉait talaivi tāṉē kūṟiṉum: poruḷ tōṉṟum = avai akapporuḷāyttōṉṟum: āvakai piṟavum maṉ poruḷ tōṉṟum = akkūṟṟiṉ kūṟupāṭṭilē piṟa kūṟṟukkaḷum mikavum akapporuḷāyt tōṉṟum.  e-ṟu. eṉṟatu, talaivi kūṟṟu, ciṟupāṉmai vēṟupaṭṭu varuvaṉavaṟṟaik kaṟpucciṟappa nāṇ tuṟantāluṅ kuṟṟam iṉṟeṉṟaṟkuc ceyirtīreṉṟār; naṉmoḻiyeṉṟār kaṟpiṟ tiriyāmaiyiṉ; avai iṉṉōraṉṉavaḻi neñcoṭukiḷattalpōlvaṉa. ivaḷkūṟṟut tōḻikkun talaivaṟkumē tōṉṟuvateṉka. maṉ ākkam, iḻinta poruḷum uyarat tōṉṟaliṉ. “maḷḷar kuḻīiya viḻavi ṉāṉu – makaḷir taḻīiya tuṇaṅkai yāṉu – māṇṭuṅ kāṇēṉ māṇṭak kōṉai – yāṉumō rāṭukaḷa makaḷē yeṉkaik – kōṭī rilaṅkuvaḷai ñekiḻtta – pīṭukeḻu kuricilu māṭukaḷa makaṉē.” āṇṭuṅ kāṇēṉeṉa avaṉuḷvaḻippaṭṭuk kūṟiṉamaiyiṟ kaṟpiṉpālatāyt tōḻiyun talaivaṉum peṇṭaṉmaiyiṟ tiriyak karutātu naṉkumatittavāṟu kāṇka. “aruṅkaṭi yaṉṉai kāva ṉīvip – peruṅkaṭai yiṟantu maṉṟam pōkip – pakalē palaruṅ kāṇa nāṇiṭ – ṭakalvayaṟ paṭappai yavaṉūr viṉavic – ceṉmō vāḻi tōḻi paṉṉāṭ – karuvi vāṉam peyyā tāyiṉu – maruvi yārkku mayantikaḻ cilampiṉ – vāṉṟōy māmalai nāṭaṉaic – cāṉṟō yallai yeṉṟaṉa mavaṟkē.” “kōṭī rilaṅkuvaḷai ñekiḻa nāṭoṟum – pāṭil kaluḻumaṅ kaṇṇoṭu pulampi – yīṅkiva ṇuṟaitalu muyaṅkuva lāṅkē – yeḻuviṉi vāḻi neñcē muṉātu – kullaik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉaiyatu – valvēṟ kāṭṭi ṉaṉṉāṭ ṭumpar – moḻipeyar tēetta rāyiṉum – vaḻipaṭal cūḻntici ṉavaruṭai nāṭṭē.” ivai tōḻikkum neñciṟkuṅ kūṟiyaṉa. “īyaṟ puṟṟat tīrmpuṟat tiṟutta – kurumpi valcip peruṅkai yēṟṟai  veṉṉunarp peṟiṉē.” eṉṉum akappāṭṭum atu. iṅṅaṉam vantuḻiyuṅ kaṟpuc ciṟantatām. iṉip piṟavāṟ koḷvaṉa varumāṟu: “poṉṉiṇar vēṅkai kaviṉiya pūmpoḻi – luṉṉamalai nāṭa ṉalampuṉaiya – meṉmulaiyāy – pōyiṉa ciṉṉāḷ puṉattu maṟaiyiṉā – lēyiṉā riṉṟi yiṉitu.” “kāṉalañ ciṟukuṭik kaṭaṉmēm paratavar – nīṉiṟap puṉṉaik koḻuniḻa lacaiit – taṇperum parappi ṉoṇpata nōkki – yaṅka ṇarilvalai yuṇakkun tuṟaivaṉō – ṭalarē yaṉṉai yaṟiyi ṉivaṇuṟai – variya vāku namakkeṉak kūṟiṟ – koṇṭuñ celvarko ṟōḻi yumaṇar – veṇka luppiṉ koḷḷai cāṟṟik – kaṇanirai kiḻikku neṭuneṟic cakaṭa – maṇaṉamaṭut turaṟu mōcaik kaḻaṉik – karuṅkāl veṇkuruku verūu – miruṅkaḻic cērppiṟṟam miṟaiva ṉūrkkē.” eṉavarum. “viḻuntaṇ mārip peruntaṇ cāraṟ – kūtirk kūvirat talari nāṟu – mātar vaṇṭiṉa nayavaru tīṅkuraṉ – maṇanāṟu cilampi ṉacuṇa mōrkku – muyarvarai nāṭaṟ ṟuṟatta loṉṟō – tuyarmaruṅ kaṟiyā vaṉṉaik kinnōy – taṇiyumā ṟituveṉa vuraitta loṉṟō – ceyyā yākaliṟ koṭiyai tōḻi – maṇikeḻu neṭuvarai yaṇipeṟa nivanta – ceyalai yantaḷi raṉṉaveṉ – pataṉiṉ māmaip pacalaiyuṅ kaṇṭē.” itu aṟattoṭu niṟkumāṟu tōḻikkut talaivi kūṟiyatu. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē tōḻiyait talaivi āṟṟuvittaluṅ koḷka. “ñekiḻnta tōḷum vāṭiya variyun – taḷirvaṉap piḻanta niṟaṉu nōkki – yāñceyva taṉṟiva ṭuyareṉa vaṉpiṉā – ṉaḻāal vāḻi tōḻi vāḻaik – koḻumaṭa lakalilait taḷitalaik kalāvum – perumalai nāṭaṉ kēṇmai namakkē – viḻuma māka vaṟiyi ṉaṉṟeṉak – kūṟuvai maṉṉō nīyē – tēṟuveṉ maṉyā ṉavaruṭai naṭpē.” eṉavarum. “tuṟuka layalatu māṇaka mākkoṭi – tuñcukaḷi ṟivaruṅ kuṉṟa nāṭa – ṉeñcu kaḷaṉāka niṉaiyalēṉ yāṉeṉa – naṟṟōṇ maṇanta ñāṉṟai maṟṟavaṉ – ṟavāa vañciṉa muraittatu – nōyō tōḻi niṉvayi ṉāṉē.” ituvum atu. (22)
114. nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamu moḻukkamu muṇṭiyuñ
ceyviṉai maṟaippiṉuñ celaviṉum payilviṉum
puṇarcci yetirppā ṭuḷḷuṟuttu varūu
muṇarcci yēḻiṉu muṇarnta piṉṟai
meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinilai piḻaiyātu
palvēṟu kavaruporu ṇāṭṭat tāṉuṅ
kuṟaiyuṟaṟ ketiriya kiḻavaṉai maṟaiyuṟap
perumaiyiṟ peyarppiṉu mulakurait toḻippiṉu
marumaiyi ṉakaṟciyu mavaḷaṟi vuṟuttup
piṉvara veṉṟalum pētaimai yūṭṭalu
muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟainiṟut turaittalu
mañciyac cuṟuttalu muraittuḻik kūṭṭamō
ṭeñcātu kiḷanta virunāṉku kiḷaviyum
vanta kiḻavaṉai māyañ ceppip
poṟutta kāraṇaṅ kuṟitta kālaiyum
puṇarntapi ṉavaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkaṟ kaṇṇuṅ
kuṟaintavaṭ paṭariṉu maṟaintava ḷarukat
taṉṉoṭu mavaḷoṭu mutaṉmūṉ ṟaḷaiip
piṉṉilai nikaḻum palavēṟu maruṅkiṉu
naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuri yiṭattiṉu
meṇṇakum paṉṉakai kaṇṇiya vakaiyiṉum
puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum vēṇṭāp piriviṉum
vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya viṭattum
puṇarntuḻi yuṇarnta vaṟimaṭac ciṟappiṉu
mōmpaṭaik kiḷavip pāṅkiṉ kaṇṇuñ
ceṅkaṭu moḻiyāṟ citaivuṭait tāyiṉu
meṉpunekap pirintōḷ vaḻicceṉṟu kaṭaii
yaṉpu talaiyaṭutta vaṉpuṟaik kaṇṇu
māṟṟatu tīmai yaṟivuṟu kalakkamuṅ
kāppiṉ kaṭumai kaiyaṟa variṉuṅ
kaḷaṉum poḻutum varainilai vilakkik
kātaṉ mikuti yuḷappaṭap piṟavu
nāṭu mūru milluṅ kuṭiyum
piṟappuñ ciṟappu miṟappa nōkki
yavaṉvayiṟ ṟōṉṟiya kiḷaviyoṭu tokaii
yaṉainilai vakaiyāṉ varaital vēṇṭiṉu
maiyac ceykai tāykketir maṟuttup
poyyeṉa māṟṟi meyvaḻik koṭuppiṉu
mavaṉvilaṅ kuṟiṉuṅ kaḷampeṟak kāṭṭiṉum
piṟaṉvarai vāyiṉu mavaṉvaraivu maṟuppiṉu
muṉṉilai yaṟaṉeṉap paṭutaleṉ ṟiruvakaip
puraitīr kiḷavi tāyiṭaip pukuppiṉum
varaivuṭaṉ paṭṭōṟ kaṭāval vēṇṭiṉu
māṅkataṉ ṟaṉmaiyiṉ vaṉpuṟai yuḷappaṭap
pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭu vakaiyun
tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṟ ṟōḻi mēṉa.
itu muṟaiyāṉē tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum iṭampalavun tokuttuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) talaivaṉ peṭṭavāyil peṟṟu iravuvaliyuṟṟu muṉṉuṟupuṇarcciyai uraippiṉ iṉṉatu nikaḻumeṉṟu aṟiyātu añcik karantu matiyuṭampaṭuppat tōḻi matiyuṭampaṭuṅkāl avaḷ ārāyum ārāycciyellām nāṭṭamām; atu eṭṭām: avaṟṟuṇ muṉṉuṟupuṇarcciyai uṇartaṟkuk kāraṇam eḻuvakaiya. avai nāṟṟamutaliya ēḻum. nāṟṟamāvatu, ōtiyum nutalum pētaipparuvattut taka nāṟātu talaivaṉkūṭṭattāṉ māṉmataccāntu mutaliyaṉavum palapūkkaḷum viravināṟutal: tōṟṟamāvatu, nīṇṭum piṟaḻntum piḷḷaipparuvattu veḷḷainōkkiṉṟi uḷḷoṉṟu koḷḷa nōkkuṅ kaṇṇun, tannilai tirintu tuṇaittu melkip paṇaittukkāṭṭun tōḷum mulaiyumeṉṟu iṉṉōraṉṉa: oḻukkamāvatu, paṇṇaiyāyattōṭu muṟṟilāṉ maṇaṟkoḻittuc cōṟamaittaṉ mutaliyaṉa muṉinta kuṟippiṉaḷāyp peṇṭaṉmaikku ēṟpa oḻukutal: uṇṭiyāvatu, paṇṭu pāṉmutaliya koṇṭu oṟuttu ūṭṭa uṇṭuvarukiṉṟāḷ, ippoḻutu ācāramum nāṇuṅ kātalum mītūra ataṉmēl uvappu āṇṭu iṉṟi mika oṟutta uḷḷattaḷātal: ceyviṉaimaṟaittalāvatu, muṉpupōlātu ikkālattu niṉaivuñ ceyalun talaivaṉōṭu paṭṭaṉavēyākalāṉ avai piṟarkkup pulaṉākāmai maṟaittal; atu āyattōṭu kūṭātu iṭantalaippāṭṭiṟku ētuvāka nīṅkiniṟṟalām. celavāvatu, paṇṭupōl vēṇṭiyavāṟu naṭavātu cīrpeṟa naṭantu ōriṭattuccēṟal: payilvāvatu, cevilimulaiyiṭattut tuyil vēṇṭātu peyarttu vēṟōriṭattup payiṟal. iṉ, cāriyai. puṇarcci etirppāṭu = etirppāṭṭup puṇarcciyeṉa māṟuka, aḵtu koṭuppārum aṭuppārumiṉṟi iruvarun tamiyarāy etirppaṭṭup puṇarum puṇarcci. ivvoḻukkam pāṅkaṟkūṭṭattiṟku ētuvākā.
uḷḷuṟuttu varūum uṇarcci ēḻiṉum uṇarnta piṉṟai = appuṇarcciyetirppāṭu nikaḻntataṉait tōḻi taṉṉuḷḷattuḷḷē viṉāvivarukiṉṟa aiyavuṇarviṉai avvēḻaṉāṉun teḷintu puṇarcci uṇṭeṉpataṉai uṇarntapiṉṟai: iṉṉatum iṉṉatumākiya ēḻeṉka. aṅṅaṉan tuṇintapiṉṉallatu talaivimāṭṭut tōḻi coṉṉikaḻttāḷeṉṟaṟkup piṉṟaiyeṉṟārya umya “kāmpivar tōḷuṅ karumatar maḻaikkaṇum – vīṅkiḷa mulaiyum vēṟupaṭ ṭaṉavē – tāṅkaru nāṟṟan talaittalai ciṟantu – pūṅkoṭik kivarnta pukaṟciyeṉa vāṅkiṟ – pakaluṅ kaṅkulu makalā toḻukumē – ṉaṉṟi yaḷavait taṉṟi – tevaṉkoṉ maṟṟivaṭ keytiya vāṟē.” itaṉuḷ nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamuñ celavum vantaṉa. “kaṇṇuñ cēyari parantaṉṟu nutalu – nuṇviyar poṟittu vaṇṭārk kummē – vāṅkamai meṉṟōṇ maṭantai – yāṅkā yiṉaḷko leṉṉumeṉ ṉeñcē.” “teyvatti ṉāyatuko ṟeṇṇīr puṭaiyuṭutta – vaiyattu makkaḷi ṉāyatuko – ṉaivuṟṟu – vaṇṭārpūṅ kōtai varivaḷaikkai vāṇutalāḷ – paṇṭaiya ḷallaḷ paṭi.” ivaiyum avai. “ēṉal kāva livaḷu mallaṇ – māṉvaḻi varukuva ṉivaṉu malla – ṉarantaṅ kaṇṇi yivaṉō ṭivaḷiṭaik – karanta vuḷḷamoṭu karutiyatu piṟitē – nammu ṉāṇunar pōlat tammuṇ – matumaṟain tuṇṭōr makiḻcci pōla – vuḷḷat tuḷḷē makiḻpa – collu māṭupa kaṇṇi ṉāṉē.” itu puṇarcci uṇarntatu. “ivaḷvayiṟ celiṉē yivaṟkuṭampu vaṟitē – yivaṉvayiṟ celiṉē yivaṭku maṟṟē – kākkai yirukaṇi ṉorumaṇi pōlak – kuṉṟukeḻu nāṭaṟkuṅ koṭiccikku – moṉṟupōṉ maṉṉiya ceṉṟuvā ḻuyirē.” ituvum atu.
meyyiṉum poyyiṉum vaḻinilai piḻaiyātu palvēṟu kavarporuḷ nāṭṭattāṉum = aṅṅaṉam uṇarntapiṉ tōḻi talaiviyuṭaṉ ārāyuṅkāṟ taṉmaṉattu nikaḻntaṉavaṟṟai maṟaittuk kūṟavēṇṭutalil uṇmaipporuḷāṉum poypporuḷāṉum virāvivariṉum avaṭkuk kuṟṟēval ceyyun taṉmaiyiṟ tappātavāṟṟāṉ vēṟupal kavarporuḷpaṭakkūṟi ārāyum ārāyccikkaṇṇum: nāṇāl iṟantupaṭāmaṟ kūṟutaṟku vaḻinilaipiḻaiyāteṉṟār. piṟaitoḻuvāmeṉavuṅ, kaṇaikuḷitta puṇkūr yāṉai kaṇṭaṉeṉevun, taṉperumaikku ēlāta ciṟucoṟkūṟik kuṟaiyuṟṟu niṟkiṉṟāṉ oruvaṉuḷaṉ avaṉai nīyuṅ kāṇṭal vēṇṭumeṉavum, avaṉ eṉṉait taḻuvikkoṇṭu kuṟaikūṟavum nāṉmaṟuttu niṉṟēṉeṉṟāṟ pōlavum meyyum poyyum virāyum piṟavāṟākavuṅ kūṟuvaṉa palvēṟu kavarporuḷām. um. “muṉṉun toḻattōṉṟi muḷḷeyiṟṟā yatticaiyē – yiṉṉun toḻattōṉṟiṟ ṟītēkāṇ – maṉṉum – porukaḷimāl yāṉaip pukaḻkkiḷḷi pūṇpōṟ – perukoḷiyāṉ mikka piṟai.” itu paṇṭu kūṟiyavāṟu kūṟaliṉ meyyum piṟaitoḻāmai aṟintukūṟaliṟ poyyumāy vaḻinilai piḻaiyāta kavarporuḷāyiṟṟu. “paṇṭip puṉattup pakaliṭat tēṉaluṭ – kaṇṭik kaḷiṟṟai yaṟivaṉmaṟ – ṟiṇṭik – katiraṉ paḻaiyaṉūrk kārnīlak kaṇṇā – yutira muṭaittitaṉ kōṭu.” naṭuṅka nāṭṭam; itu iṟantupāṭu payattaliṟ kantaruvattiṟku amaiyātu. “toyyil vaṉamulaiyun tōḷuṅ kaviṉeytit – teyvaṅ kamaḻumā laimpālu – maiyuṟuval – poṉṉaṅ koṭimaruṅkuṟ pūṅkayaṟ kaṇṇiṉāyk – keṉṉai yituvanta vāṟu.” eṉa vaḻinilai piḻaiyāmaṟ kavarporuḷāka neṟipaṭunāṭṭam nikaḻntavaḻit talaivi cuṉaiyāṭiṉēṟku iṅṅaṉam āyiṟṟeṉṉum. atukēṭṭut tōḻiyum yāṉum āṭikkāṇpal eṉṉum. um. “paiyuṇ mālaip paḻumaram paṭariya – novvuppaṟai vāva ṉōṉciṟa kēykku – maṭicevik kuḻavi taḻīip paiyān – tiṭukukavuṇ maṭappiṭi yevvaṅ kūra – ventiṟa lāḷi verīic cantiṉ – poriyarai miḷirak kutti vāṉkē – ḻuruva veṇkō ṭuyakkoṇṭu kaḻiyuṅ – kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉai kāluṟa voṟṟaliṟ – kōvā vāram vīḻnteṉak kuḷirkoṇṭu – pēe nāṟun tāḻnīrp paṉiccuṉai – tōṉē relvaḷai teḷirppa niṉpōl – yāṉu māṭik kāṇkō tōḻi – varaivayiṟu kiḻitta niḻaṟikaḻ neṭuvēṟ – ṟikaḻpūṇ murukaṉ ṟīmpuṉa lalaivāyk – kamaḻpūm puṟaviṟ kārpeṟṟuk kaḷitta – voṇpoṟi maññai pōlvatōr – kaṇkavar kārikai peṟutaluṇ ṭeṉiṉē.” eṉavarum. “neruna lellai yēṉaṟ ṟōṉṟit – tirumaṇi yoḷirvarum” eṉṉum akappāṭṭuṅ koḷka. “eḻāa vākali ṉeḻiṉalan tolaiya – vaḻāa tīmō notumalar talaiyē – yēṉal kāvalar māvīḻttup paṟitta – pakaḻi yaṉṉa cēyari maḻaikka – ṇalla peruntō ḷōyē kolla – ṉeṟipoṟ pitirviṟ ciṟupal kāya – vēṅkai vīyuku mōṅkumalaik kaṭci – mayilaṟi vaṟiyā maṉṉō – payilkural kavarum paimpuṟak kiḷiyē.” talaivi vēṟupāṭu kaṇṭu ārāyun tōḻi taṉatu ārāycciyai maṟaittuk kūṟiyatu. nāṭṭattāṉum eṉṉum ummai muṟṟu, nāṭṭamellān taḻīiyiṉamaiyiṉ. āṉurupu iṭapporuṭṭām. aḵtu iṭamāka varukiṉṟa eṭṭaṉaiyum iṭattiyal poruḷāka uraikkavē avaḷai nāṭukiṉṟa kālamum mēlvarukiṉṟa eṭṭaṉaiyun talaivaṟku uraikkuṅkālamum oruṅku nikaḻumeṉpatām. iṅṅaṉam nāṭi matiyuṭampaṭukkun tuṇaiyun talaivaṟku ikkuṟai muṭippal eṉṉātu oḻukum.
kuṟaiyuṟaṟku etiriya kiḻavaṉai maṟaiyuṟap perumaiyiṟ peyarppiṉum = iravuvaliyuṟṟuk kuṟaikūṟat toṭaṅkiya talaivaṉait tōḻi tāṉ ivvoḻukalāṟu aṟintum aṟintilaḷpōlat talaivaṉ perumai kūṟi avaṉkuṟippiṉai māṟṟiṉum: tāṉ aṟintataṉai maṟaittukkūṟutalaṉṟi aṉpiṉmai orutalaiyāka uṭaiyaḷallaḷ. um. “kallōṅku cāraṟ kaṭipuṉaṅ kāttōmpu – nalkūrntār māṭṭu nayantoḻukit – tolavanta – vāṉṟōy kuṭikku vaṭucceyta ṟakkatō – tēṉṟōypūṅ kaṇṇiyīr nīr.” “maṟuvoṭu paṭṭaṉa māmalai nāṭa – ciṟukuṭiyōr ceyvaṉa pēṇār – ciṟukuṭikku – mūtāṉ puṟattiṭṭa cūṭēpō ṉillātē – tātāṭu mārpa paḻi.” “takaimāṇ ciṟappiṟ cāṉṟōrk kotta – vakaiyamai vaṉappiṉai yākali ṉulakamoṭu – pāṟpaṭaṟ pālai maṉṉō kāppuṭut – tōṅkuya raṭukkattuc cāntuvaḷar naṉantalai – neṭuvarai maruṅkiṟ kuṭimai cāṉṟō – riṉṉa reṉṉā tiṉpam veḵkip – piṉṉilai muyaṟciyiṉ varuntiṉu – nummō raṉṉōrkkut taruvatō vaṉṟē.” eṉavarum. “ivaḷē, kāṉa naṇṇiya kāmar ciṟukuṭi – nīṉiṟap peruṅkaṭal kalaṅka vuḷpukku – mīṉeṟi paratavar makaḷē nīyē – neṭuṅkoṭi nuṭaṅku niyama mūtūrk – kaṭuntērc celvaṉ kātaṉ makaṉē – naṇṇic cuṟāvaṭutta vuṇaṅkal vēṇṭi – yiṉappu ḷōppu memakkunala ṉevaṉō – pulavu nāṟutuñ celaniṉ ṟīmō – perunīr viḷaiyuḷeñ ciṟunal vāḻkkai – nummoṭu puraivatō vaṉṟē – yemma ṉōriṟ cemmalu muṭaittē.” ituvum atu. 
ulaku uraittu oḻippiṉum = avvoḻukkam aṟiyāḷ pōṟ karanta tōḻi ulakattāraippōl varaintukoḷḷeṉak kūṟit talaivaṉai nīkkiṉum: um. “kōṭī relvaḷaik koḻumaṇik kūnta – lāytoṭi maṭavaral vēṇṭuti yāyiṟ – ṟeṇkaḻic cēyiṟāṟ paṭūun – taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa varaintaṉai koṇmō.”
arumaiyiṉ akaṟciyum = avai kēṭṭup piṟṟaiñāṉṟum vantavaṉ māṭṭuc ciṟitu neñcunikaḻnta tōḻi aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟātu ivaḷ ariyaḷeṉak kūṟutalum: iruvarumuḷvaḻiyavaṉ varavuṇartaliṉ iruvaruḷḷamum uṇarntu aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. “nerunalu muṉṉā ḷellaiyu moruciṟaip – putuvai yākaliṟ kiḷatta ṉāṇi – nēriṟai vaḷaittō ṇiṉṟōḻi ceyta – vārañar varuttaṅ kaḷaiyā yōveṉa – veṟkuṟai yuṟuti rāyiṟ coṟkuṟai – yempatat teḷiya ḷalla ḷemakkōr – kaṭkāṇ kaṭavu ḷallaḷō peruma – vāykōṉ miḷakiṉ malaiyaṅ koḻuṅkoṭi – tuñcupuli varippuṟan taivaru – mañcucūḻ maṇivarai maṉṉavaṉ makaḷē.” “taḻaiyoṭu taṇkaṇṇi taṉmaiyāṟ kaṭṭi – viḻaiyavuṅ kūṭumō veṟpa – viḻaiyārn – tilaṅkaruvi poṉkoḻikku mīrṅkuṉṟa nāṭa – kulaṅkaruti vāḻvār makaḷ.”
avaḷ aṟivuṟuttup piṉ vara eṉṟalum = talaivaṉai nōkki nī kātalittavaṭku nīyēceṉṟu aṟivittup piṉṉar eṉmāṭṭuvaruka veṉṟalum: avaṉaṟivuṟutteṉṟu pāṭamōtuvārum uḷar. um. “taṉṉaiyun tāṉāṇuñ cāyalāṭ kiḵturaippi – ṉeṉṉaiyu nāṇap paṭuṅkaṇṭā - .yeṉṉaiyē – vēyērmeṉ ṟōḷikku vēṟā yiṉiyorunā – ṇīyē yuraittu niṟu.” itu nīyēyuraiyeṉṟatu. “nāḷvēṅkai poṉcoriyu naṉmalai naṉṉāṭa – kōḷvēṅkai pōṟkoṭiya reṉṉaimār – kōḷvēṅkai – yaṉṉaiyā ṉīyu miruntaḻaiyā mēlāmaik – keṉṉaiyō nāḷai yeḷitu.” itu kaiyuṟaimaṟuttup piṉ varuka eṉṟatu. ivai orukūṟṟāka varuvaṉavuḷavēṟ kāṇka.
pētaimai ūṭṭalum = aṅṅaṉam piṉ varukaveṉṟuḻi muṉvantāṉai aṟiyāmai ēṟṟik kūṟalun talaiviyaiyum aṅṅaṉam aṟiyāmaiyēṟṟik kūṟalum: “neṭuntēr kaṭaiit tamiyarāy niṉṟu – kaṭuṅkaḷiṟu kāṇirō veṉṟīr – koṭuṅkuḻaiyār – yāṉai yataruḷḷu niṟparō tampuṉat – tēṉaṟ kiḷikaṭiku vār.” “vēṅkai kamaḻum veṟikamaḻ taṇcilampiṉ – vāṅkamai meṉṟōṭ kuṟavar makaḷirēñ – cōrnta kuruti yoḻukamaṟ ṟippuṟam – pōnta tilaiyak kaḷiṟu.” ivai talaivaṉaip pētaimai ūṭṭiṉa. “naṟuntaṇ ṭakaram vakuḷa mivaṟṟai – veṟumputalpōl vēṇṭātu veṭṭi – yeṟintuḻutu – centiṉai vittuvār taṅkai piṟarnōykku – nontiṉaiya vallaḷō nōkku.” ivai talaiviyaip pētaimaiyūṭṭiyatu. “iḷaiyaḷ viḷaivilaḷ” eṉpatūum itaṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. “puṉṟalai mantik kallā vaṉpaṟaḻ – kuṉṟuḻai naṇṇiya muṉṟiṟ pōkā – teriyakain taṉṉa vītatai yiṇara – vēṅkaiyam paṭuciṉai poruntik kaiya – tēmpey tīmpāl vauvaliṟ koṭicci – yeḻuteḻil citaiya vaḻuta kaṇṇē – tērvaṇ cōḻaṟ kuṭantai vāyiṉ – māriyaṅ kiṭaṅki ṉīriya malarnta – peyaluṟu nīlam pōṉṟaṉa viralē – pāa yavvayi ṟalaitta liṉṉā – tāṭumaḻai tavaḻuṅ kōṭuyar potiyi – ṉōṅkiruñ cilampiṟ pūtta – kāntaḷaṅ koḻumukai pōṉṟaṉa civantē.” eṉavarum. 
muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟainiṟuttu uraittalum = aṅṅaṉam pētaimaiyūṭṭiyavaḻi ivaḷ ikkuṟaimuṭippaḷeṉṟu irantu oḻukiṉēṟku ivaḷ puṇarcciyaṟintilaḷpōṟ kūṟiṉāḷeṉa āṟṟāṉāyavaṉai yāṉ appuṇarccinikaḻntamai aṟivaleṉṟukūṟi varuttantīrttalum: um. “naṟuntaṇ kūntaṟ kuṟuntoṭi maṭantai – ciṟumutuk kuṟaivi yāyiṉaḷ periteṉa – niṉṉetir kiḷattalu mañcuva leṉakkē – niṉṉuyi raṉṉa ḷāyiṉun – taṉṉuṟu viḻumaṅ kātta lāṉē.” niṉṉetirkiḷattal añcuval nī avaṭku uraittiyeṉak karutiyeṉṟaliṟ puṇarcciyuṇarntamai kūṟiṉāḷ.
añci accu uṟuttalum = aṅṅaṉam āṟṟuvittuṅ kaṭitukuṟaimuṭiyāmaiyaik karutun tōḻi kuravarait tāṉ añcit talaiviyum avarai añcuvaḷeṉakkūṟalum: añcutal acceṉṟāyiṟṟu. ivvaccaṅkūṟavē avaṉ āṟṟum. um. “yāaṅ koṉṟa marañcuṭ ṭiyaviṟ – karumpu maruṇmutala paintāṭ centiṉai – maṭappiṭit taṭakkai yaṉṉa pālvārpu – karikkuṟaṭ ṭiṟaiñciya ceṟitāṭ peruṅkuraṟ – paṭukiḷi kaṭikañ cēṟu maṭupō – reḵku viḷaṅku taṭakkai malayaṉ kāṉat – tāra nāṟu mārpiṉai – vāraṉmaṟ ṟaiya varukuvaḷ yāyē.” iḵtu yāyai añciyatu. “yāṉai yuḻalu maṇikiḷar nīḷvaraik – kāṉaka vāḻkkaik kuṟavar makaḷirē – mēṉaluḷaiya varutaṉmaṟ ṟeṉṉaikol – kāṇiṉuṅ kāyva remar.” itu tamarai añcik kūṟiyatu
uraittuḻik kūṭṭamōṭu eñcātu kiḷanta irunāṉku kiḷaviyum = āyattiṉīṅkit taṉṉōṭu niṉṟa talaiviyai avaṉōṭu kūṭṭavēṇṭi avaḷiṉīṅkit talaivarkku iṉṉuḻi etirppaṭutiyeṉa uraittaviṭattuk kūṭuṅkūṭṭattōṭē oḻiyāmaṟ kiḷanta eṇvakaik kiḷaviyum: um. “neytaṟ parappiṟ pāvai kiṭappi – niṉkuṟi vantaṉe ṉiyaṟērk koṇka – celkañ celaviyaṅ koṇmō valkalu – māra laruntum vayiṟṟa – nārai mitikku meṉmaka ṇutalē.” vantaṉaṉeṉṟum eṉmakaḷeṉṟum orumai kūṟic celkameṉṟa uḷappāṭṭup paṉmaiyāṟ talaivivaravuṅ kūṟi iṭattuyttavāṟumuṇarttiṉāḷ. celaviyaṅkoṇmō eṉṟatu nīyē avaḷaip pōkaviṭuvāy eṉṟatām. nāṭṭan taṉmaṉattu nikaḻāniṟṟalum avaṉmaṉattuk kuṟaiyuṇarttutal nikaḻāniṟṟalumeṉṉum iraṇṭiṉaiyum eñcāmaṟ taḻīiniṟkum ivveṭṭumeṉṟaṟku eñcāteṉṟār.
vanta kiḻavaṉai māyañ ceppip poṟutta kāraṇaṅ kuṟitta kālaiyum = taṉmuṉṉar vantu niṉṟa talaivaṉait tōḻi etirppaṭṭuniṉṟēyum vārātāṉpōla māyamēṟṟi ataṉaip poṟutta kāraṇaṅ kuṟippiṉāṟ koḷḷak kūṟuṅkālaikkaṇṇum: kāraṇamāvatu nī ariyaiyātaliṉ ivaḷ āṟṟāḷāmeṉṟu etirkoḷkiṉṟēmeṉṟal, kūṭṭam nikaḻntapiṉ tōḻi ivvāṟu kūṟutaṟku uriyaḷeṉṟu ataṉpiṉ vaittār. itu avaṉvaravai virumpiyatu, varaivukaṭāyataṉṟu. um. “neṭuṅkayiṟu valatta kuṟuṅka ṇavvalaik – kaṭalpā ṭaḻiya viṉamīṉ mukantu – tuṇaipuṇa ruvakaiyar parata mākka – ḷiḷaiyaru mutiyaruṅ kiḷaiyuṭaṉ ṟuvaṉṟi – yuppē yumaṇa raruntuṟai pōkku – moḻukai nōṉpaka ṭoppak kuḻīi – yayirtiṇi yaṭaikarai yolippa vāṅkip – peruṅkaḷan tokutta vuḻavar pōla – virantōr vaṟuṅkala makala vīcip – pāṭupala vaittuk koḷḷai cāṟṟik – kōṭuyar tiṇimaṇaṟ ṟuñcun tuṟaiva – perumai yeṉpatu keṭumō vorunāṇ – maṇṇā mutta marumpiya puṉṉait – taṇṇaṟuṅ kāṉal vantunum – vaṇṇa mevaṉō veṉṟiṉir celiṉē.” tammāṉ iṭaiyūṟeyti varuntukiṉṟāṉai orunāḷ vantilireṉa māyañceppiyavāṟum nīrvārāmaiyiṉ vaṇṇam vēṟupaṭumeṉa ēṟṟukkōṭumeṉak kāraṇaṅ kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka. tammēṟ tavaṟiṉṟākak kūṟuṅkālattu itu kūṟuvareṉṟaṟkuk kuṟittakālaiyeṉṟār.
puṇarntapiṉ avaṉvayiṉ vaṇaṅkaṟkaṇṇum = akkūṭṭattiṉpiṉ muṟkālattup paṇintu piṉṉiṉṟōṉait tōḻitāṉē paṇintoḻukumiṭattum: um. “ivaḷē, niṉcoṟ koṇṭa veṉcoṟ ṟēṟip – pacunaṉai ñāḻaṟ palakiḷai yoruciṟaip – putunala ṉiḻanta pulampumā ruṭaiya – ḷutukkāṇ ṭeyya vuḷḷal vēṇṭu – nilavu miruḷum pōlap pulavuttiraik – kaṭaluṅ kāṉalun tōṉṟu – maṭalcūḻ peṇṇaiyeñ ciṟunal lūrē.” “vāṅku kōṉel” eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcikkaliyuḷ, “araviṉ poṟiyu maṇaṅkum puṇarnta – vuraviṉ mēlacait takaiyai yorāṅku – niraivaḷai muṉkaiyeṉ ṟōḻiyai nōkkip – paṭukiḷi pāyum pacuṅkura lēṉal – kaṭitiṉ maṟappittā yāyi ṉiṉinī – neṭituḷḷa lōmputal vēṇṭum” eṉavum, “kaṭumā kaṭavuṟūuṅ kōlpō leṉaittuṅ – koṭumai yilaiyāva taṟintu maṭupeyal – vaḻaivaḷar cāral varuṭai naṉmāṇ – kuḻavi vaḷarppavar pōlappā rāṭṭiyu – muḻaiyiṟ piriyiṟ piriyu – miḻaiyaṇi yalkuleṉ ṟōḻiyatu kaviṉē.” eṉavum varum. ittuṇaiyum ōrkūṭṭaṅ kūṟiṉār.
kuṟaintu avaṭ paṭariṉum = talaivaṉ irantu piṉṉiṉṟamaikaṇṭu tōḻi maṉam ñekiḻntu tāṉkuṟaintu talaiviyiṭattē ceṉṟu kuṟaikūṟiṉum: um. “vaḷaiyaṇi muṉkai vāleyiṟ ṟiṉṉakai – yiḷaiyā rāṭun taḷaiyaviḻ kāṉal – virunteṉa viṉavi niṉṟa – neṭuntō ḷaṇṇalaik kaṇṭikum yāmē.” eṉa varum.
maṟaintavaḷ aruka = nāṇmikutiyāṟ taṉatu vēṭkai maṟainta talaivi akkūṟṟiṟku uṭampaṭātu niṟṟalāl: taṉṉoṭum avaḷoṭum mutaṉmūṉṟu aḷaii = talaivaṉōṭun talaiviyōṭum nikaḻnta iyaṟkaippuṇarcci mutaliya mūṉṟaṉaiyun tāṉ aṟintamai kuṟippāṉ uṇartti: piṉṉilai palavēṟu nikaḻum maruṅkiṉum = irantu piṉṉiṟṟal palavāy vēṟupaṭṭu naṭakkumiṭattum: avai peruntaṉmaiyāṉoruvaṉ yāṉai mutaliyaṉa viṉāyun taḻaiyuṅ kaṇṇiyuṅ koṇṭum ippuṉattu vārāniṉṟāṉeṉavum, avaṉ eṉmāṭṭup perituṅ kuṟaiyuṭaiyaṉeṉavum, avaṉ kuṟaimuṭiyāmaiyiṉ varuntāniṉṟāṉeṉavum, attaḻai nī ēṟṟalvēṇṭumeṉavum, akkuṟaimuṭittaṟku iḵtiṭameṉavum, yāṉ kūṟiyatu koḷḷāyāyiṉ niṉakkuc ceṟintāruṭaṉ ucāvik kuṟaimuṭippāyeṉavum, maṟuppiṉa maṭalēṟuvaṉeṉavum varaipāyvaṉeṉavum, piṟavāṟṟāṉuṅ kūṟik kuṟainayappittalām. um. “puṉaipūn taḻaiyalkuṟ poṉṉaṉāy cāraṟ – ṟiṉaikāt tiruntēmyā māka – viṉaivāyttu – māviṉavu vārpōla vantavar nammāṭṭut – tāmviṉava luṟṟatoṉ ṟuṇṭu.” “kaitaiyan taṇkāṉaṟ kālaiyu mālaiyu – meyta variṉu mituveṅ kuṟaiyeṉṉāṉ – ceytaḻaiyuṅ kaṇṇiyuñ cērntuḻi vaittiṟantā – ṉuyyalaṉ kollō vuṇaralaṉē yeṉṟiyāl; orunāḷ vārala ṉirunāḷ vāralaṉ – paṉṉāḷ vantu paṇimoḻi payiṟṟiye – ṉaṉṉar neñca nekiḻnta piṉṟai – varaimutir tēṉiṟ pōki yōṉē – yaraicā kentai yāṇṭuḷaṉ kollō – vēṟupula naṉṉāṭṭup peyta – vēṟuṭai maḻaiyiṟ kaluḻume ṉeñcē.” “puṇartuṇaiyō ṭāṭum poṟiyalava ṉōkki – yiṇartatai pūṅkāṉa leṉṉaiyu nōkki – yuṇarvoḻiyap pōṉa volitirainīrc cērppaṉ – vaṇarcuri yaimpālāy vaṇṇa muṇarēṉāl; ōrai yāya maṟiya vūra – ṉalkiṉaṉ ṟanta naṟumpūn taṇṭaḻai – yāṟupaṭi ṉevaṉō tōḻivīṟu ciṟantu – neṭumoḻi viḷakkun tolkuṭi – vaṭunām paṭutta lañcutu meṉavē.” itu kaiyuṟai ēṟpat talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu. “cilampiṉ mēynta ciṟukōṭṭuc cētā – valaṅkukulaik kānta ṭīṇṭit tātukak – kaṉṟutāy maruḷuṅ kuṉṟa nāṭa – ṉuṭukkun taḻaitan taṉaṉē yavaiyā – muṭuppiṉ yāyañ cutumē koṭuppiṟ – kēḷiṭaik kēṭañ cutumē yāyiṭai – vāṭal kollō tāmē yāymalaip – pōruṭai varuṭaiyum pāyāc – cūruṭai yaṭukkatta koyaṟkarun taḻaiyē.” ituvum atu. “ilaicūḻceṅ kānta ḷerivāy mukaiyaviḻtta vīrntaṇ vāṭai – kolaivē ṉeṭuṅkaṭ koṭicci katuppuḷaruṅ kuṉṟa nāṭa – ṉulaipaṭu vennō yuḻakkumā lantō – mulaiyiṭai nērpavar nēru miṭamitu moykuḻalē.” “avvaḷai veriniṉ ṟarakkīrt taṉṉa – cevvari yitaḻa cēṇāṟu naṟavi – ṉaṟuntā tāṭiya tumpi pacuṅkēḻp – poṉṉurai kalli ṉaṉṉiṟam peṟūum – vaḷamalai nāṭa ṉeruṉa ṉammoṭu – kiḷaimali ciṟutiṉaik kiḷikaṭin tacaiic – colliṭam peṟāaṉ peyarntaṉaṉ peyarnta – talla laṉṟatu kātalan tōḻi – tātuṇ vēṭkaiyiṟ pōtunerit tūtā – vaṇṭō raṉṉa taṇṭāk kāṭci – kaṇṭuṅ kaḻaṟoṭi valittaveṉ – paṇpil ceyti niṉaippāy niṉṟē.” “māyō ṉaṉṉa mālvaraik kavāaṉ – vāliyō ṉaṉṉa vayaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi – yammalaik kiḻavō ṉannayan teṉṟum – varuntiṉa ṉeṉpatōr vāyccoṟ ṟēṟāy – nīyuṅ kaṇṭu numaroṭu meṇṇi – yaṟivaṟin taḷaval vēṇṭu maṟutaraṟ – kariya vāḻi tōḻi periyōr – nāṭi naṭpi ṉallatu – naṭṭu nāṭārtam moṭṭiyōr tiṟattē.” “maṟaval vāḻi tōḻi tuṟaivar – kaṭalpurai peruṅkiḷai nāppaṇ – maṭalpuṉain tēṟiniṟ pāṭum poḻutē.” eṉavarum. iṉṉum vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām īṇṭu aṭakkikkoḷka.
naṉṉayam peṟṟuḻi nayampuriyiṭattiṉum = aṅṅaṉan tōḻi kūṟiya kuṟaiyiṉai avaḷ aruḷap peṟṟavaḻi ataṉait talaivaṟkuk kūṟutaṟku virumpumiṭattum: avai talaivaṉāṟṟāmai kaṇṭu tōḻi kaiyuṟaiyetirtalum, iravukkuṟiyum pakaṟkuṟiyum nērtaluṅ, kuṟiyiṭaṅkāṭṭalum, piṟavumām. um. “poṉmeliyu mēṉiyāḷ pūñcuṇaṅkiṉ meṉmulaika – ḷeṉmeliya vīṅkiṉavē pāvameṉ – ṟeṉmeliviṟ – kaṇkaṇṇi vāṭāmai yāṉalla veṉṟuraittā – luṇkaṇṇi vāṭā ḷuṭaṉṟu.” itaṉuṭ kaṇkaṇṇi, kuṟuṅkaṇṇi. “nilāvi ṉilaṅku maṇaṉmali maṟukiṟ – pulālañ cērip pulvēy kurampai – yūreṉa vuṇarāc ciṟumaiyoṭu nīruṭut – tiṉṉā vuṟaiyī ṭāyiṉu miṉpa – morunā ḷuṟaintici ṉōrkkum vaḻināṭ – ṭampati maṟakkum paṇpi ṉempati – vantaṉai ceṉmō vaḷaimey parappap – pommaṟ paṭutirai kammeṉa vuṭaitaru – māṉōṅ koruciṟaip palapā rāṭṭi – yellai yemmoṭu kaḻīi yelluṟa – naṟṟēr kūṭṭalu muriyi raṟṟaṉṟu – cērntaṉir celkuvi rāyiṉ yāmu – memvarai yaḷavaiyiṟ peṭkuva – nummop patuvō virittici ṉemakkē.” “kaṭumpulāl puṉṉai kaṭiyun tuṟaiva – paṭumpulāl puṭkaṭivāṉ pukka – vaṭumpulān – tāḻaimā ñāḻaṟ ṟakaintuyarnta tāḻpoḻi – lēḻaimā ṉōkki yiṭam.” eṉavarum. iṉṉum nayam puriyiṭattumeṉṟataṉāl avaṉvaraviṉait talaivikkuk kūṟutaluṅ koḷka. “ivarpari neṭuntēr maṇiyu micaikkum – peyarpaṭa viyaṅkiya viḷaiyaru molippar – kaṭalāṭu viyalaṇip polinta naṟuntaḻait – titalai yalku ṉalampā rāṭṭiya – varumē tōḻi vārmaṇaṟ cērppa – ṉiraipaṭa vōṅkiya muḻavumutaṟ puṉṉai – maravarai maṟaikam vammati pāṉāṭ – pūviri kāṉaṟ puṇarkuṟi varuntuṇai – melliṇar naṟumpoḻiṟ kāṇā – valla larumpaṭar kāṇkanāñ ciṟitē.” itu tōḻi talaivikkup pakaṟkuṟikkaṭ talaivaṉ varukiṉṟamai kāṭṭi avaṉ varuttaṅ kāṇa yām maṟaintu niṟpāṉ vammōveṉak kūṟiyatu. 
eṇṇarum paṉṉakai kaṇṇiya vakaiyiṉum = talaivaṉ iḷivantoḻukutaṟkup poṟāta tōḻi avaṉ iḷivaravu uṇarttuṅ karuttiṉaḷāyc ceyaṟkaiyāka ārāytaṟku ariyavāy varum oṉṟallāp palanakaikuṟitta pakutikkaṇṇum: avai eṉṉai maṟaittalevaṉākiyareṉṟalum aṟiyāḷpōṟaluṅ kuṟiyāḷ kūṟalum paṭaittumoḻikiḷaviyuṅ kuṟippuvēṟukoḷalum piṟavumām. um. “niṟaittiṅkaḷ cērntōṭu nīṇmalai nāṭa – maṟaikkap paṭātēṉai maṉṉu – maṟaittukkoṇ – ṭōṭiṉā yātalā loṇṭoṭiyā ṭaṉpakkaṅ – kūṭak kiṭantatoṉ ṟil.” “maṉṉērmaṉ cāya lavaruṇ maruṭīra – viṉṉārka ṇeṉpa taṟiyēṉāṉ – miṉṉūruṅ – kārkeḻu tōṉṟaṟ kaṇamalai naṉṉāṭa – yārkaṇṇa tākuṅ kuṟai.” “taṉṉevvaṅ kūṟiṉunī ceyta varuḷiṉmai – yeṉṉaiyu maṟaittāḷeṉ ṟōḻi yatukēṭṭu – niṉṉaiyāṉ piṟarmuṉṉarp paḻikūṟṟā ṉāṇi.” itu paḻikūṟuvēṉeṉṟu talaivi kuṟiyātatoṉṟait tōḻikūṟiṉatu. “viruntiṉa rātaliṉ viṉavuti rataṉetir – tiruntumoḻi māṟṟan tarutalu miyalpeṉak – kūṟuva tammayā ṉūṟupala varumeṉa – lañcuvaṉ vāḻi yaṟaiya veñcā – teṇṇila reṇṇiyatu muṭippar – kaṇṇilar koṭiyariva ṭaṉṉai mārē.” itu nikaḻātu nikaḻvatākap paṭaittumoḻintatu. “neṟinī riruṅkaḻi nīlamuñ cūṭāḷ – poṟimāṇ variyalava ṉāṭṭalu māṭṭāḷ – ciṟunutal vērarumpac cintiyā niṉṟāṭ – keṟinīrttaṇ cērppayā ṉeṉcollic celkō.” itu kuṟippu vēṟu koṇṭāḷeṉṟatu.
puṇarcci vēṇṭiṉum = talaivaṉ pakaṟkuṟiyaiyum iravukkuṟiyaiyum virumpik kūṟumiṭattum: tōḻimēṉa kiḷavi. avai palavakaiya. um. “naṉṉalañ citaiya nāṭoṟum pulampap – poṉṉiṇar vēṅkai tuṟukaṟṟāa – yirumpiṭi verūu nāṭaṟkōr – peruṅka ṇōṭṭañ ceytaṉṟō vilamē.” itu tōḻi talaiviyaip pakaṟkuṟinayappittatu. “māyavaṉun tammuṉum pōla maṟikaṭaluṅ – kāṉalcēr veṇmaṇaluṅ kāṇāyō – kāṉa – liṭaiyelā ñāḻalun tāḻaiyu mārnta – puṭaiyelām puṉṉai pukaṉṟu.” “ūrkku maṇittē poykai poykaikkuc – cēyttu maṉṟē ciṟukāṉ yāṟē – yiraitēr veṇkuru kalla tiyāvarun – tuṉṉal pōkiṉṟāṟ poḻilē yāmeṅ – kūḻaik kērmaṇaṅ kuṟukañ cēṟu – māṇṭum varukuvaḷ perumpē taiyē.” ivai pakaṟkuṟinērntu iṭaṅkāṭṭiyaṉa. “ceṅkaḷam paṭakkoṉ ṟavuṇart tēytta – ceṅkō lampiṉ ceṅkōṭ ṭiyāṉaik – kaḻaṟoṭic cēya kuṉṟaṅ – kurutip pūviṉ kulaikkān taṭṭē.” itu tōḻi talaiviyai iṭattuyttunīṅkiyatu. “ārkali veṟpaṉ mārpu puṇaiyākap – pāṭiṉ ṉaruvi yāṭuta liṉitē – niraiyitaḻ poruntāk kaṇṇō ṭiraviṟ – pañci veṇṭiric ceñcuṭar nalliṟ – piṉṉuvīḻ ciṟupuṟan taḻīi – yaṉṉai muyaṅkat tuyiliṉ ṉātē.” itu iravukkuṟi nayanta talaivaṉ ciṟaippuṟamākap pakaṟkuṟi nērvāḷpōl iravukkāppumikuti kūṟiyatu. pāṭiṉṉaruvi āṭa eṉṟāḷ ataṉkaṇ utaviṉāṉeṉpatu paṟṟi; allatu kaḷaviṟku uṭaṉ āṭutaliṉṟu. “ciṟuvark kuṟukai yākat teṟuvara – vīṅkum varupavō tēmpāy tuṟaiva – ciṟunā veṇmaṇi viḷari yārppak – kaṭumā neṭuntēr nēmi pōkiya – viruṅkaḻi neytal pōla – varuntiṉa ḷaḷiyaṇi yaruntici ṉōḷē.” itu talaivaṉ iravukkuṟinayantavaṉait tōḻi maṟuttatu. “nākupiṭi nayanta muḷaikkōṭ ṭiḷaṅkaḷiṟu – kuṉṟa naṇṇik kuṟava rārppa – maṉṟam pōḻu nāṭaṉ ṟōḻi – cuṉaippūṅ kuvaḷait toṭalai tantun – tiṉaippuṉa maruṅkiṟ paṭukiḷi yōppiyuṅ – kālai vantu mālaip poḻuti – ṉallaka nayantutāṉ mayaṅki – collu mākā taḵki yōṉē. ” itu tōḻi kiḻattiyai iravukkuṟi nayappittatu. “taṇṇan tuṟaivaṉ kaṭumpari māṉṟēr – kālai vantu mālai peyariṉum – peritu pulampiṉṟē kāṉal – ciṟitu pulampiṉamāṟ ṟōḻi nāmē.” itu talaiviyatu āṟṟāmai kaṇṭu namvaruttan tīrtaṟku iravukkuṟiyum vēṇṭumeṉṟatu. “ēṉa miṭantiṭṭa vīrmaṇikoṇ ṭelliṭaik – kāṉavar makkaḷ kaṉaleṉak kaikāyttum – vāṉuyar veṟpaṉ varuvāṉko ṟōḻinam – mēṉi pacappuk keṭa.” itu talaivaṉ varuvaṉeṉṟatu. “cuṟavuppiṟa ḻiruṅkaḻi nīnti vaikalu – miravukkuṟik koṇkaṉ vantaṉaṉ – viravumaṇik koṭumpūṇ viḷaṅkiḻai yōyē.” iḵtu iravukkuṟi. talaivaṉ vantamai talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu. “aṉṉāy vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉainam paṭappait – taṉṉayat tamaṉṟa kūtaḷaṅ kuḻaiya – viṉṉicai yaruvi yōṭu meṉṉatūuṅ – kēṭṭiyō vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉainam paṭappai – yūṭṭi yaṉṉa voṇṭaḷirc ceyalai – yōṅkuciṉait toṭutta vūcal pāmpeṉa – muḻumuta ṟumiya vurumeṟin taṉṟē – yiṉṉuṅ kēṭṭiyō veṉavumaḵ taṟiyā – ḷaṉṉaiyuṅ kaṉaituyiṉ maṭintaṉa ḷataṉṟalai – maṉṉuyir maṭintaṉṟāṟ poḻutē kātalar – varuva rāyiṟ paruva miḵteṉac – cuṭarntilaṅ kelvaḷai ñekiḻnta namvayiṟ – paṭarnta vuḷḷam paḻutiṉ ṟāka – vantaṉar vāḻi tōḻi yantarat – timiḻpeya ṟalaiiya viṉappala koṇmūt – tavirvil veḷḷan talaittalai ciṟappak – kaṉṟu kālōyuṅ kaṭuñcuḻi nīttam – puṉṟalai maṭappiṭip pūcal palavuṭaṉ – veṇkōṭ ṭiyāṉai viḷipaṭat tuḻavu – makalvāyp pāntaṭ paṭāap – pakalu mañcum paṉikkaṭuñ curaṉē.” itu tāyatu tuyiluṇarntu talaivaṉvantamai tōḻi talaivikkuk kūṟik kuṟivayiṟceṉṟatu. “cēyvicum pivarnta ceṅkatir maṇṭila – mālvarai maṟaiyat tuṟaipulam piṉṟē – yiṟavarunti yeḻunta karuṅkāl veṇkuruku – veṇkōṭ ṭucciṟait tāa yakkaraik – karuṅkōṭṭup puṉṉai yiṟaikoṇ ṭaṉavē – kaṇaikkāṉ māmalar karappa malkukaḻit – tuṇaiccuṟā vaḻaṅkalum vaḻaṅku māyiṭai – yellumiḻ paṉikkaṭaṉ malkucuṭark koḷīi – yemarum vēṭṭam pukkaṉa rataṉāṟ – ṟaṅki ṉevaṉō teyya poṅkupicir – muḻavicaip puṇari yeḻutaru – muṭaikaṭaṟ paṭappaiyem muṟaivi ṉūrkkē.” iḵtu iravukkuṟi vēṇṭiya talaivaṟkut tōḻi uṭaṉpaṭṭuk kūṟiyatu. “āmpa ṉuṭaṅku maṇittaḻaiyu māramun – tīmpuṉa lūraṉ makaḷiva – ḷāyntanaṟun – tēmalar nīlam piṇaiyal ceṟimalart – tāmarai taṉṉaiyar pū.” iḵtu iravukkuṟi nērnta tōḻi innilattiṉkaṇ nī varuṅkāl iṉṉapeṟṟiyāṉ varuvāyākaveṉṟatu. “kaṭaṟkāṉaṟ cērppa kaḻiyulāy nīṇṭa – vaṭaṟkāṉaṟ puṉṉaitāḻn tāṟṟa – maṭaṟkāṉa – laṉṟi lakavu maṇineṭum peṇṇaittē – muṉṟi liḷamaṇaṉmēṉ moyttu.” iḵtu iravukkuṟiyiṭaṅ kāṭṭit tōḻi kūṟiyatu. “emmūr vāyi loṇṭuṟait taṭaiiya – kaṭavuṇ mutumarat tuṭaṉuṟai paḻakiya – teyyā vaḷaivāyt teṉkaṭ kūrukir – vāyppaṟai yacāvum valimuntu kūkai – maiyūṉ ṟerinta neyyuṇ puḻukka – lelivāṉ cūṭṭoṭu maliyap pēṇutu – meñcāk koḷkaiyeṅ kātalar varanacaiit – tuñcā talamvarum poḻuti – ṉañcuvarak kaṭuṅkural payiṟṟā tīmē.” iḵtu iravukkuṟit talaivaṉ ciṟaippuṟamākak kūkaikku uraippāḷāyt tōḻi kūṟiyatu. “nilavu maṟaintaṉ ṟiruḷum paṭṭaṉ – ṟōvat taṉṉa viṭaṉuṭai varaippiṟ – pāvai yaṉṉa niṟpuṟaṅ kākkuñ – ciṟanta celvat taṉṉaiyun tuñciṉaḷ – keṭuttuppaṭu naṉkala meṭuttukkoṇ ṭāṅku – naṉmār paṭaiya muyaṅki meṉmelak – kaṇṭaṉam varukañ ceṉmō tōḻi – kīḻu mēluṅ kāppōr nītta – vaṟuntalaip peruṅkaḷiṟu pōlat – tamiyaṉ vantōṉ paṉiyalai nilaiyē.” itu talaivaṉaikkaṇṭu muyaṅkukam vammō eṉṟatu.
vēṇṭāp piriviṉum = talaivaṉṟāṉ puṇarcciyai virumpātu pirivai virumpiya iṭattum: appirivu taṇṭātirattalai muṉinta maṟṟaiyavaḻi iṭṭuppirivum arumaiceytayarttalumām; āṇṭut talaivaṟkun talaivikkuṅ kūṟuvaṉakoḷka. um. “mutta marumpu muṭattāṇ mutirpuṉṉai – tattun tiraiyalaikkun taṇṇaṅ kaṭaṟcērppa – cittirap pūṅkoṭiyē yaṉṉāṭ karuḷiyāy – vittakap paimpūṇiṉ mārpu.” “iṟavup puṟattaṉṉa piṇarpaṭu taṭavumutaṟ – cuṟavukkōṭ ṭaṉṉa muḷḷilait tāḻai – peruṅka ḷiṟṟu maruppi ṉaṉṉa varumpu – mutirpunaṉ māṉuḻaiyiṉ vēṟupaṭat tōṉṟi – viḻavukkaḷaṅ kamaḻu muravunīrc cērppa – viṉamaṇi neṭuntēr pāka ṉiyakkac – celiya cēṟi yāyi ṉivaḷē – varuvai yākiya ciṉṉāḷ – vāḻvā ḷāta laṟintaṉai ceṉmē.” itu pirivuvēṇṭiyavaḻik kūṟiyatu. “cāraṟ palaviṉ koḻuntuṇar naṟumpaḻa – miruṅkal viṭaraḷai vīḻntaṉa veṟpiṟ – peruntē ṉiṟāloṭu kīṟu nāṭaṉ – pēramar maḻaikkaṇ kaluḻattaṉ – cīruṭai naṉṉāṭṭuc cellu maṉṉāy.” eṉavumvarum.
vēḷāṇ peruneṟi vēṇṭiya iṭattum = talaivaṟkut tāñ cila koṭuttalait talaivi vēṇṭiyaviṭattum: atu talaivi vēḷāṇetirum viruntiṉkaṭ tōḻi kūṟuvatām. um. “paṉṉā ḷevvan tīrap pakalvantu – puṉṉaiyam potumpa riṉṉiḻaṟ kaḻippi – mālai mālkoḷa nōkkip paṇṇāyntu – valavaṉ vaṇṭē riyakka nīyuñ – celavuvirup puṟuta loḻikati lamma – cellā nallicaip polampūṭ ṭiraiyaṉ – palpūṅ kāṉaṟ pavattiri yaṉṉaviva – ṇalleḻi liḷanalan tolaiya volleṉak – kaḻiyē yōta malkiṉṟu vaḻiyē – vaḷḷeyiṟ ṟiṟavoṭu vayamīṉ koṭkuñ – ceṉṟōṉ maṉṟa māṉṟaṉṟu poḻuteṉa – niṉṟiṟat tavalam vīṭa viṉṟivaṭ – ceppi ṉevaṉō pūkkēḻ pulampap – pacumī ṉeṭutta veṇṇeṉmāt tayirmiti – mitavai māvār kunavē niṉakkē – vaṭavar tanta vāṉkēḻc cāntaṅ – kuṭapula vuṟuppiṟ kūṭṭupu nikaḻtti – vaṇṭimir naṟuñcān tayarkuvan tiṇṭimi – lelluttoḻiṉ maṭutta valviṉaip paratavar – kūruḷik kaṭuvicai maṇṭaliṟ pāyntuṭaṉ – kōṭcuṟāk kiḻitta koṭumuṭi neṭuvalai – taṇkaṭa lacaivaḷi yeṟitoṟum viṉaiviṭṭu – muṉṟiṟ ṟāḻait tūṅkun – teṇṭiraip parappiṉem muṟaivi ṉūrkkē.” itaṉuṭ taṉakkum puravikkuṅ koṭuppaṉa kūṟit taṭuttavāṟukāṇka. “nāḷvalai mukanta” eṉpatum atu.
puṇarntuḻi uṇarnta aṟimaṭac ciṟappiṉum = iyaṟkaip puṇarcci nikaḻntakālattu avaṉ tīṅku uṇarātavaṉai naṉṟāka uṇarnta aṟiviṉatu maṭappaṅ kūṟit taṅ kātaṟciṟappu uraitta iṭattum: atu “curañcel liyāṉaik kalluṟu kōṭṭiṟ – ṟeṟṟeṉa viṟiyarō vaiya maṟṟiyā – ṉummoṭu nakka vālveḷ ḷeyiṟē – pāṇar pacumīṉ corintamaṇṭai pōla – vemakkum perumpula vāki – nummum peṟēe miṟiyare ṉuyirē.” iḵtu avaṉōṭu nakutaṟkut tōṉṟiya uṇarvu iṉṟiyamaiyāmai kūṟik kātaṟciṟappu uraittatu.
ōmpaṭaik kiḷavip pāṅkiṉ kaṇṇum = talaivaṟkut talaiviyaip pātukāttukkoḷḷeṉak kūṟuṅ kiḷaviyatu pakutikkaṇṇum: tōḻimēṉa kiḷavi. pakutiyāvaṉa varaiviṭaippirivu mutaliyapiriviṭattum puṉattiṭaip puṇarcciyiṉṟi nīṅkumiṭattum piṟaviṭattuṅ kūṟuvaṉavām. um. “naṉaimutir ñāḻaṟ ciṉaimaru ṭiraḷvī – neytaṉ māmalarp peyta pōla – vūtai tūṟṟu muravunīrc cērppa – tāyuṭaṉ ṟalaikkuṅ kālaiyum vāyviṭ – ṭaṉṉā yeṉṉuṅ kuḻavi pōla – viṉṉā ceyyiṉu miṉitutalai yaḷippiṉu – niṉvaraip piṉaḷeṉ ṟōḻi – taṉṉuṟu viḻumaṅ kaḷaiñarō vilaḷē.” “perunaṉ ṟāṟṟiṟ pēṇāru muḷarō – vorunaṉ ṟuṭaiya ḷāyiṉum purimā – ṉāṭu pulavitīr vaḷimati yilaikavaru – pāṭamai yoḻukiya taṇṇaṟuñ cāraṉ – meṉṉaṭai maraiyā tuñcu – naṉmalai nāṭa niṉṉala tilaḷē.” “eṟintemar tāmuḻuta viṉkura lēṉaṉ – maṟantuṅ kiḷiyiṉamum vārā – kaṟaṅkaruvi – māmalai nāṭa maṭamoḻi taṉkēṇmai – nīmaṟava ṉeñcakattuk koṇṭu.” “aḷiya tāmē cevvāyp paiṅkiḷi – kuṉṟak kuṟavar koytiṉaip paiṅkā – liruvi nīḷpuṉaṅ kaṇṭum – pirita ṟēṟṟā pēraṉ piṉavē.” itu tiṉai arintuḻik kiḷiyai nōkkik kūṟuvāḷpōṟ ciṟaippuṟamāka ōmpaṭuttatu. iṉṉum ōmpaṭaikkiḷaviyeṉṟataṟku ivaḷai nī pātukāttukkoḷḷeṉṟu talaivaṉ kūṟuṅ kiḷaviyatu pakutikkaṇṇumeṉṟum poruḷkūṟuka. “piṇaṅkaril vāṭiya paḻaviṟa ṉaṉantalai – yuṇaṅkū ṇāyat tōrāyeṉ ṟeṇmaṇi – paipaya vicaikku mattam vaiyeyiṟ – ṟivaḷoṭuñ celiṉō naṉṟē kuvaḷai – niraicūḻ māmala raṉṉa kaṇṇaḻak – kalaiyoḻi piṇaiyiṟ kalaṅki māṟi – yaṉpili rakaṟi rāyi ṉeṉpara – mākuva taṉṟiva ḷavala nākat – taṇaṅkuṭai yavartan talaiyuṭa livaṇō – pārkali nallēṟu tiritaruṅ – kārcey mālai varūum poḻutē.” itu varaiviṭaip pirikiṉṟāṉ āṟṟuvittukkoṇṭiru eṉṟāṟkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
ceṅkaṭumoḻiyāṟ citaivuṭaittāyiṉum = tōḻi cevvaṉaṅ kūṟuṅ kaṭuñcoṟkaḷāṟ talaivi neñcu citaivuṭaittāyiṉum: āṇṭun tōḻimēṉa kiḷavi. avai talaivaṉai iyaṟpaḻittalun talaiviyaik kaḻaṟalumām. um. “kauvaiyam perumpaḻi tūṟṟa nalaṉaḻintu – paitalañ ciṟunutal pacalai pāya – nammikaṟ paṭutta vavariṉu mavarnāṭṭuk – kuṉṟaṅ koṭiyako ṟōḻi – yoṉṟun tōṉṟā maḻaimaṟain taṉavē.” varaiviṭaip paruvaṅkaṇṭu aḻinta tōḻi iyaṟpaḻittatu. “mācaṟak kaḻīiya yāṉai pōlap – perumpeya luḻanta virumpiṇart tuṟukaṟ – paita lorutalai cēkku nāṭa – ṉōytan taṉaṉē tōḻi – pacalai yāṉṟaṉa kuvaḷaiyaṅ kaṇṇē.” “kēḻa luḻuta karippuṉak kollaiyuḷ – vāḻai mutukāy kaṭuvaṉ pukaittayarun – tāḻaruvi nāṭaṉ ṟeḷikoṭuttā ṉeṉṟōḻi – nērvaḷai neñcūṉṟu kōl.” iḵtu avaṉcūḷuṟavu poyttateṉa iyaṟpaḻittatu. “makiḻnaṉ mārpē vēyai yalarnī – yaḻiyal vāḻi tōḻi naṉṉa – ṉaṟumā koṉṟu nāṭṭiṟ pōkkiya – voṉṟumoḻik kōcar pōla – vaṉkaṭ cūḻcciyum vēṇṭumāṟ ciṟitē.” “meyyiṟ ṟīrā mēvaru kāmamo – ṭeyyā yāyiṉu muraippa ṟōḻi – koyyā muṉṉuṅ kuralvār mututiṉai – yaruvi yārnta paiṅkā ṟōṟu – miruvi tōṉṟiya palavē nīyē – muruku muraṇkoḷḷun tēmpāy kaṇṇip – paripaṉ ṉāyoṭu paṉmalaip paṭarum – vēṭṭuvaṟ peṟalō ṭamaintaṉai yāḻaniṉ – pūkkeḻu toṭalai nuṭaṅka veḻuntu – kiḷḷait teḷviḷi yiṭaiyiṭai payiṟṟi – yāṅkāṅ koḻukā yāyi ṉaṉṉai – ciṟukiḷi kaṭita ṟēṟṟā ḷivaḷeṉap – piṟar tantu niṟukkuna ḷāyi – ṉuṟaṟkari tākumavaṉ malarnta mārpē.” eṉavarum. itaṉāṉē varaiyum paruvamaṉṟeṉak kūṟutaluṅ koḷka.
eṉpu nekap pirintōḷ vaḻicceṉṟu kaṭaii aṉpu talaiyaṭutta vaṉpuṟaikkaṇṇum = eṉpu urukumāṟu talaivaṉāṟ piriyappaṭṭa talaivikku vaḻipāṭāṟṟic ceṉṟu tāṉkūṟum moḻiyai avaḷmaṉattē celuttit talaivaṉ aṉpai avaḷiṭattē cērttukkūṟiya vaṟpuṟuttutaṟkaṇṇum: appirivu varaintukōṭaṟkup poruḷvayiṟpiritalum vēntarkkuṟṟuḻippiritaluṅ kāvaṟkuppiritalumām. āṇṭu vaṟpuṟuttuṅkāl iyaṟpaḻittum iyaṟpaṭamoḻintum piṟavāṟum vaṟpuṟuttum. muṉ ceṅkaṭumoḻiyāleṉpuḻi iyaṟpaḻittaṉavum vaṟpuṟuttutal payaṉākak kūṟiyaṉaveṉṟuṇarka. um. “yāñcey tolviṉaik kevaṉpē tuṟṟaṉai – varuntal vāḻi nīyeṉa yāñceṉ – ṟuraittaṉam varuka veḻumati puṇartiraik – kaṭalviḷai yamutam peyaṟkēṟ ṟāaṅ – kuruki yalkuta lañcuva latukoṇ – ṭammeṉ koṭumai namvayi ṉēṟṟi – nayamperi tuṭaimaiyiṟ ṟāṅkal cellātu – kaṇṇī raruvi yāka – vaḻumē tōḻiyavar paḻamutir kuṉṟē.” itu piriviṭait tōḻi iyaṟpaḻittu vaṟpuṟuttatu. “tōḷun tolkaviṉ ṟolaintaṉa nāḷu – maṉṉaiyu maruntuya ruṟṟaṉa ḷalarē – poṉṉaṇi neṭuntērt teṉṉar kōmā – ṉeḻūut tiṇitō ḷiyaṟērc ceḻiya – ṉērā veḻuva raṭippaṭak kaṭanta – vālaṅ kāṉat tārppiṉum periteṉa – vāḻal vāḻi tōḻi yavarē – māal yāṉai maṟappōrp pullik – kāmpuṭai neṭuvarai vēṅkaṭat tumpa – raṟaiyiṟan takaṉṟaṉa rāyiṉu niṟaiyiṟan – tuḷḷā rākalō varitē celaveṉa – muḷḷūr maṉṉaṉ kaḻaṟoṭik kāri – cellā nallicai niṟutta valvi – lōrik koṉṟu cēralark kītta – cevvērp palaviṉ payaṅkeḻu kolli – nilaipeṟu kaṭavuḷark kīyāp – palarpukaḻ pāvai yaṉṉaniṉ ṉalaḷē.” “aḻiyalāyiḻai” eṉṉuṅ kuṟuntokaiyuṅ koḷka. “peruṅkai yiruṅkaḷi ṟaivaṉa māntik – karuṅkāṉ marāmpoḻiṟ pācaṭait tuñcuñ – curumpimir cōlai malaināṭaṉ kēṇmai – poruntiṉārk kēmāp puṭaittu.” ivai iyaṟpaṭa moḻintu vaṟpuṟuttaṉa. iṉṉum aṉputalaiyaṭutta vaṉpuṟai eṉṟataṉār piṟavuṅ koḷka. “perumutu celvar poṉṉuṭaip putalvar – ciṟutōṭ kotta cevvarip paṟaiyiṉ – kaṇṇakat teḻutiya kuṟiyē pōlak – kōlkoṇ ṭalaikkap paṭīiyar mātō – viraiveṇ māṉa veḷiyaṉ ṟīttaṉ – muracumuṟ koḷīiya mālai viḷakkiṉ – veṇkō ṭiyampa nuṇpaṉi yarumpak – kaiyaṟa vanta poḻutoṭu meycōrn – tavala neñciṉam peyara vuyartirai – nīṭunīrp paṉittuṟaic cērppa – ṉōṭutēr nuṇṇuka nuḻainta māvē.” itu pakaṟkuṟi vantu pōkiṉṟa talaivaṉ puṟakkiṭai nōkki āṟṟāta talaivi talaivi kuṟippaṟintu māviṉmēlvaittu vaṟpuṟuttatu. “viḷāyā ṭāyamo ṭōrai yāṭā – tiḷaiyō rivviṭat tiṟceṟin tirutta – laṟaṉu maṉṟē yākkamun tēymeṉak – kuṟunurai cumantu naṟumala runtip – poṅkivaru putunīr neñcuṇa vāṭukam – vallitiṉ vaṇaṅkic collunarp peṟiṉē – colleṉa
