viṭunaṇmaṟ kollō vellumiḻn turavuru muraṟu maraiyiru ṇaṭunāṭ – koṭinuṭaṅ kilaṅkiṉa miṉṉi – yāṭumaḻai yiṟuttaṉṟavar kōṭuyar kuṉṟē.” itu varaivu nīṭa āṟṟāta talaivi vēṟupāṭu puṟattārkkup pulaṉākāmaiyum iyaiyum iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟuvāraip peṟiṉeṉak kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttatu. “maṟukupu pukalu neñca nōyiṉ – ṟiṟukap pulli muyaṅkukañ – ciṟupuṉ mālai ceyirppa nāmē.” piriviṭaiyāṟṟāta talaiviyait tōḻi naṉṉimittaṅkūṟi vaṟpuṟuttatu.
āṟṟatu tīmai aṟivuṟu kalakkamum = talaivaṉ varukiṉṟa neṟiyiṉatu tīmaiyait tāṅkaḷ aṟitaluṟṟataṉāṉē eytiya kalakkattiṉkaṇṇum: tōḻimēṉa kiḷavi. um. “kaḻutukāl kiḷara viyaṉakark kāṉavar tuñcār – vayakkaḷiṟu poruta vāḷvari yuḻuvai – kaṉmukaic cilampiṟ kuḻūu maṉṉō – meṉṟō ṇekiḻntunām varuntiṉu miṉṟavar – vārā rāyiṉu naṉṟumaṟ ṟilla – vuyarvarai yaṭukkat toḷiṟupu miṉṉip – peyalkāṉ mayaṅkiya poḻutukaḻi pāṉāṭ – ṭirumaṇi yaravuttērn tēṟiya – vurumuccivan tuḻalu mōṅkuvarai yāṟē.” “koṭuvari vēṅkai piḻaittuk kōṭpaṭṭu – maṭicevi vēḻam verīi – yaṭiyōcai – yañci yotuṅku mataruḷḷi yāriruṭ – ṭuñcā cuṭarttoṭi kaṇ.” ivai iravukkuṟivaravāṟ talaivi varuntuvaḷeṉṟatu. “karaiporuṅ kāṉyāṟṟaṅ kallatar” eṉpatum atu. “kūrukireṇkiṉ” eṉṉum akappāṭṭuṅ kāṇka. kalakkameṉap potuppaṭak kūṟiyavataṉāl āṟṟiṭai ētamiṉṟic ceṉṟamai tōṉṟa āṇṭu ōrkuṟiceyyeṉak kūṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. “kāṉa māṉatar yāṉaiyum vaḻaṅkum – vāṉa mīmicai yurumunaṉi yuraṟu – maravum puliyu mañcataka vuṭaiya – viravuc ciṟuneṟi taṉiyai varuti – varaiyiḻi yaruvip pāṭoṭu piraca – muḻavuccēr narampi ṉimmeṉa vimirum – paḻaviṟa ṉaṉantalaip payamalai nāṭa – maṉṟal vēṇṭiṉum peṟukuvai yoṉṟō – viṉṟutalai yāka vāral variṉē – yēmuṟu tuyara nāmiva ṇoḻiya – veṟkaṇṭu peyaruṅ kālai yāḻaniṉ – kaṟkeḻu ciṟukuṭi yeytiya piṉṟai – yūtal vēṇṭumāṟ ciṟitē vēṭṭoṭu – vēypayi laḻuvattup pirintaniṉ – ṉāypayiṟ kuṟinilai koṇṭa kōṭē.” eṉṉum nittilakkōvaiyuḷ, variṉē ēmuṟu tuyaram nāmivaṇoḻiya, niṉṉāypayiṟ kuṟinilai koṇṭa kōṭṭai ūtalvēṇṭumāṟ ciṟitē, eṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. 
kāppiṉ kaṭumai kaiyaṟa variṉum = kāttaṟṟoḻilāl uṇṭāṅ kaṭuñcoṟkaḷ kaḷavoḻukkattiṭattē ellaiyaṟa varumiṭattum: tōḻimēṉa kiḷavi; avai palavakaiya. um. “kaṭaluṭa ṉāṭiyuṅ kāṉa lalkiyun – toṭalai yāyamoṭu taḻuvaṇi yayarntu – notumalar pōlak katumeṉa vantu – muyaṅkiṉaṉ celiṉē yalarntaṉṟu maṉṉē – tuttip pāntaḷ paittaka lalkuṟ – ṟiruntiḻai tuyavukkōṭ ṭacaitta pacuṅkaḻait – taḻaiyiṉu muḻaiyiṟ pōkāṉ – ṟāṉṟan taṉaṉyāy kāttōm pallē.” itu pakaṟkuṟikkaṭ ciṟaippuṟamākac ceṟippaṟivuṟīiyatu. “kaṇamukai kaiyeṉak kāntaḷ kaviṉa – maṇamukaiyeṉ ṟeṇṇi mantikoṇ ṭāṭum – viṟaṉmalai nāṭa varavaritāṅ kollō – puṉamu maṭaṅkiṉa kāppu.” itu tiṉaiviḷaintamai kūṟic ceṟippaṟivuṟīiyatu. “aṟaiyaruvi yāṭā ṭiṉaivaḷamuṅ kāvāḷ – poṟaiyuyar taṇcilampiṟ pūntaḻaiyuṅ koyyā – ḷuṟaikavuḷ vēḻamoṉ ṟuṇṭeṉṟā ḷaṉṉai – maṟaiyāti vāḻiya maiyiruṅ kuṉṟē.” itu kuṉṟattiṟkuk kūṟuvāḷākac ceṟippaṟivuṟīiyatu. “cānta meṟintuḻuta cāraṟ ciṟutiṉaic – cānta vitaṇa micaiccārntu – cāntaṅ – kamaḻak kiḷikaṭiyuṅ kārmayi laṉṉā – ḷimiḻak kiḷiyeḻā vārttu.” itu piṟaraik kāttaṟku iṭuvareṉac ceṟippaṟivuṟīiyatu. “pallōr tuñcu naḷḷeṉ yāmat – turavukkaḷiṟu pōlvan tiravukkatavu muyaṟal – kēḷē mallēṅ kēṭṭaṉem peruma – vōri muruṅkap pīli cāya – naṉmayil valaippaṭ ṭāṅkiyā – muyaṅkutoṟu muyaṅku maṟaṉil yāyē.” itu iravukkuṟik kāppiṉ kaṭumai kūṟiyatu. “viṉaiviḷaiyac celvam viḷaivatupō ṉīṭāp – paṉaiviḷaivu nāmeṇṇap pātti – tiṉaiviḷaiya – maiyār taṭaṅkaṇ mayilaṉṉāy tīttīṇṭu – kaiyār pirivittal kāṇ.” itu ivvoḻukkattiṉai vēṅkai nīkkiṟṟeṉat talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu. iṉṉum vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉkaṇ aṭakkuka. “puṉṉaiyam pūmpoḻilē pōṟṟavē pātukā – vaṉṉap peṭaiyē yaṟamaṟavaṉ – maṉṉuṅ – kaṭumputarmāṉ kāvali kāṉalañ cellūr – neṭuṅkaṭalē nīyu niṉai.” itu puṉaṅ kaiviṭṭup pōkiṉṟuḻic ciṟaippuṟamākat tōḻi kūṟiyatu. “paṇṭaikko ṇalviṉai yillēm patippeyartuṅ – kaṇṭaṟ kulaṅkāḷ kaḻiyarukēr – muṇṭakaṅkā – ṇāṇi yirātē nayantaṅ kavarkkuraimiṉ – pēṇi yavarceṟitta lāṉ.” itu talaivaṟkuk kūṟumiṉeṉṟatu.
kaḷaṉum poḻutum varainilai vilakkik kātaṉmikuti uḷappaṭa = kātaṉmikuti uḷappaṭak kaḷaṉum poḻutum varainilai vilakki; avaḷiṭattuk kātaṉmikuti maṉattu nikaḻāniṟka iruvakaiyiṭattaiyum iruvakaik kālattaiyun tām varaintu kūṟum nilaimaiyait tavirttu avaṉvayiṟ tōṉṟiya kiḷaviyaiyum: piṟavum = kūṟiyavāṟaṉṟip piṟavāṟāka avaṉvayiṟ tōṉṟiya kiḷaviyaiyum: nāṭum ūrum illuṅ kuṭiyum piṟappum ciṟappum iṟappum nōkki avaṉvayiṟ tōṉṟiya kiḷaviyoṭu tokaii = avaṉpiṟantanāṭum ataṉpakutiyākiya kuṭiyiruppum avvūrkku iruppākiya maṉaiyum pārppār mutaliya nālvakaivaruṇamum avvaruṇattuḷ iṉṉavaḻi ivaṉeṉṟalum oruvayiṟṟuppiṟantōr palaruḷḷuñ ciṟappittukkūṟalum piṟariṉ ovvātiṟantaṉavātal nōkkit talaivaṉiṭattē tōḻi kūṟiya kiḷaviyōṭē kūṭi: aṉainilaivakaiyāṉ varaital vēṇṭiṉum = attaṉmaittākiya nilaimaiyiṉ kūṟupāṭṭāṉē varaintukōṭalai virumpiya vaḻiyum: tōḻimēṉa kiḷavi. “pakaṟpuṇarkaḷaṉē” “iravukkuṟiyēyil” “kuṟiyeṉappaṭuvatu” eṉṉuñcūttiraṅkaḷāṟ kaḷaṉum poḻutum uṇarka. um. “puṉṉai kāttu maṉṉa mōppiyum – paṉiyiruṅ kāṉal yāmviḷai yāṭa – malalam pērūr maṟuki – ṉalkalu mōvā talarā kiṉṟē.” itu pakaṟkuṟi vilakkiyatu. “neṭumalai naṉṉāṭa niṉvē ṟuṇaiyāk – kaṭuvicai vāla ruvinīnti – naṭuviru – ḷiṉṉā varavara vīrṅkōtai mātarā – ḷeṉṉāvā ḷeṉṉume ṉeñcu.” itu iravukkuṟi vilakkiyatu. “iravu vāralai yaiya viravuvī – yakalaṟai varikkuñ cāraṟ – pakalum peṟutiyiva ṭaṭameṉ ṟōḷē.” itu pakaṟkuṟi nērvāḷpōla iravukkuṟi vilakkiyatu. “tiraimēṟpōn teñciya teṇkaḻik kāṉal – viraimēvu pākkam viḷakkāk – karaimēl – viṭuvāyp pacumpuṟa vippikāṉ muttam – paṭuvā yiruḷakaṟṟum pārttu.” ituvum atu. ivai varuvunilai vilakkiṉa. “vēral vēli vērkkōṭ palaviṉ – cāra ṉāṭa cevviyai yākumati – yāraḵ taṟintici ṉōrē cāraṟ – ciṟukōṭṭup perumpaḻan tūṅkiyāṅ – kivaḷuyir tavacciṟitu kāmamō peritē.” itu kātaṉmikuti kūṟiyatu. piṟavumeṉṟataṉāl, “kōṭī rilaṅkuvaḷai ñekiḻa nāṭoṟum – pāṭil kaluḻun tuḷiyā ṉāvē – tuṉṉaru neṭuvarait tatumpiya varuvi – taṇṇeṉ muḻavi ṉimiḻicai kāṭṭu – maruṅkiṟ koṇṭa palaviṟ – peruṅka ṉāṭaniṉ ṉayantōḷ kaṇṇē.” itu yāṉ varaiyuntuṇaiyum āṟṟuvaḷō eṉṟāṟku āṟṟāḷeṉṟatu. “neṭuvarai micaiyatu kuṟuṅkāl varuṭai – tiṉaipāy kiḷḷai verūu nāṭa – vallaimaṉ ṟamma poyttal – vallāy maṉṟanī yallatu ceyalē.” itu talaivaṉaip paḻittatu. “kāṉaṟ kaṇṭaṟ kaḻaṉṟuku paiṅkāy – nīṉiṟa viruṅkaḻi yuṭpaṭa vīḻnteḻa – vuṟukā ṟūkkat tūṅki yāmpal – ciṟuveṇ kākkai yāvit taṉṉa – veḷiya viriyun tuṟaiva veṉṟu – maḷiya periyōr kēṇmai numpōṟ – cālpetir koṇṭa cemmai yorutti – tēṟā neñcaṅ kaiyaṟupu vāṭi – nīṭiṉṟu virumpā rāyiṉ – vāḻataṉmaṟ ṟevaṉō tēykamā teḷivē.” āṟṟāta talaiviyaik kaṭitiṉ varaivēṉeṉṟu talaivaṉ teḷivikkap pukkuḻit tōḻi teḷiviṭai vilakkiyatu. “kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kātaṉ maṭamakaṇ – meṉṟōṭ koṭicciyaip peṟaṟkaritu tilla – paimpuṟap paṭukiḷi yōppalavar – puṉpula mayakkattu viḷaintaṉa tiṉaiyē.” puṉaṅkāval iṉiyiṉṟu eṉṟatu. “eṉṉāṅko ṉīṭi liṉavēṅkai nāḷuraippap – poṉṉām pōla vēlavar tāmpurin – teṉṉē – maruviyā mālai malaināṭaṉ kēṇmai – yiruviyā mēṉa liṉi.” itu tiṉai arikiṉṟamaiyuñ cuṟṟattār poruḷvēṭkaiyuṅ kūṟiyatu. “veṟikamaḻ veṟpaveṉ meynnīrmai koṇṭa – taṟiyāṇmaṟ ṟaṉṉai yaṇaṅkaṇaṅkiṟ ṟeṉṟu – maṟiyīrn – tutirantūy vēlaṟ ṟarīi – veṟiyō ṭalamvarum yāy.” itu veṟiyaccuṟuttiyatu. “iṉamī ṉiruṅkaḻi yōta mulāva – maṇinīr parikkun tuṟaiva takumō – kuṇanīrmai kuṉṟāk koṭiyaṉṉāḷ pakka – niṉanīrmai yillā voḻivu.” itu aruḷavēṇṭum eṉṟatu. “mūttō raṉṉa veṇṭalaip puṇari – yiḷaiyō rāṭum varimaṉai citaikkun – taḷaiyaviḻ tāḻaik kāṉalam peruntuṟaic – cilacevvit tākiya puṇarcci yalareḻa – vilvayiṟ ceṟittamai yaṟiyāy paṉṉāḷ – varumulai varutta vampakaṭṭu mārpiṟ – ṟerumara luḷḷamoṭu varuntu niṉvayi – ṉīṅkuka veṉṟiyāṉ yāṅkaṉa moḻikō – varuntiṟaṟ kaṭavuḷ cellūrk kuṇātu – peruṅkaṭaṉ muḻakkiṟ ṟāki yāṇa – rirumpiṭam paṭutta vaṭuvuṭai mukattar – kaṭuṅkaṭ kōcar niyama māyiṉu – muṟumeṉak koḷkuva rallar – naṟunuta larivai pāciḻai vilaiyē.” itu poruṇmikak koṭuttalvēṇṭumeṉṟatu. iṉṉum varaivukaṭāvutaṟ poruṭṭāy vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām itaṉāṉ amaikka. “kōḻilai vāḻaik kōṇmiku peruṅkulai – yūḻuṟu tīṅkaṉi yuṇṇunart taṭutta – cāraṟ palaviṉ cuḷaiyoṭu paṭupa – pāṟai neṭuñcuṉai viḷainta tēṟa – laṟiyā tuṇṭa kaṭuva ṉayalatu – kaṟivaḷar cānta mēṟal cellātu – naṟuvī yaṭukkattu makiḻntukaṇ paṭukkuṅ – kuṟiyā viṉpa meḷiteṉiṉ malaippala – vēṟu vilaṅku meytu nāṭa – kuṟitta viṉpa niṉakkē vaṇariya – veṟutta vēymaruḷ paṇaittō ṇiṟuppa – nillā neñca mōṭu niṉmāṭ – ṭivaḷu miṉaiya ḷākiṟ ṟantai – yaruṅkaṭik kāvalar cōrpata ṉoṟṟik – kaṅkul varutalu muriyai paimputal – vēṅkaiyu moḷḷiṇar virintaṉa – neṭuveṇ ṭiṅkaḷu mūrkoṇ ṭaṉṟē.” vilaṅkum eytu nāṭaveṉṟu annāṭṭiṉai iṟappakkūṟi innāṭuṭaimaiyiṟ kuṟitta iṉpam niṉakkē vaṇariyaveṉa varaitalvēṇṭiyavāṟum vēṅkaivirintataṉāṟ tiṉaiyaṟuttaliṉ iṟceṟippuk kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kaṅkulvarutalum uriyaiyeṉap pakaṟkuṟimaṟuttu iravukkuṟi nērvāḷpōṟ kūṟi neṭuveṇṭiṅkaḷu mūrkoṇṭaṉṟēyeṉṟu ataṉaiyum maṟuttu varaitaṟku nallanāḷeṉak kūṟi varaivukaṭāyavāṟuṅ kāṇka. “kāmaṅ kaṭava vuḷḷa miḷaippa – yāmvantu kāṇpatōr paruva māyi – ṉōṅkit tōṉṟu muyarvaraik – kiyāṅkeṉap paṭuvatu nummūr teyyō.” itu ūrai iṟappak kūṟiyatu. “tuṇaipuṇarn teḻitarum” eṉṉum neytaṟkaliyuṭ “kaṭimalarp puṉṉaikkīḻk kārikai tōṟṟāḷait – toṭinekiḻ tōḷaḷāt tuṟappāyāṉ maṟṟuniṉ – kuṭimaikkaṭ periyatōr kuṟṟamāyk kiṭavātō; āymalarp puṉṉaikkī ḻaṇinalan tōṟṟāḷai – nōymali nilaiyaḷāt tuṟappāyāṉ maṟṟuniṉ – vāymaikkaṭ periyatōr vañcamāyk kiṭavātō; tikaḻmalarp puṉṉaikkīḻt tirunalan tōṟṟāḷai – yitaḻmalark kaṇṇaḷāt tuṟappāyāṉ maṟṟuniṉ – pukaḻmaikkaṭ periyatōr pukarākik kiṭavātō.” ivai oḻukkamum vāymaiyum pukaḻum iṟappakkūṟiyaṉa. kuṭippiṟantārkku immūṉṟuñ ciṟappak kūṟalvēṇṭum. ēṉaiya vantuḻikkāṇka. iṉṉum aṉainilaivakai eṉṟataṉāṉē talaiviyāṟṟāmaikaṇṭu varaivukaṭāvavōveṉṟu talaiviyaikkēṭṭaluñ ciṟaippuṟamākavuñ ciṟaippuṟamaṉṟākavun talaiviyāṟṟāmaikūṟi varaivukaṭāvuvaṉavum piṟavum vēṟupaṭavaruvaṉavum itaṉāṉ amaikka. “kaḻiperuṅ kiḻamai kūṟit tōḻi – yoḻiyā yāyiṉoṉ ṟuraikkō teyya – kaṭavu ṇeṟṟiya karuṅkāl vēṅkai – taṭavunilaip palavoṭu tāḻnta pākkattup piṉṉī rōti yivaṭamark kuraippatōr peṇyāp pāyiṉu māka voṇṇuta – lilaṅkuvaḷai meṉṟōṭ kiḻamai – vilaṅkumalai nāṭanī vēṇṭuti yeṉiṉē.” “nilavu miruḷum pōla neṭuṅkaṭaṟ – kaḻiyuṅ kāṉalu maṇantaṉṟu – nutalun tōḷu maṇintaṉṟāṟ pacappē” eṉavarum. 
aiyac ceykai tāykku etirmaṟuttup poy eṉa māṟṟi meyvaḻik koṭuppiṉum = talaivikkuk kūṭṭam uṇṭukoleṉṟu tāy aiyuṟṟavaḻi avvaiyappaṭṭa ceykaiyait tāykku etirēniṉṟu maṟuttu ataṉaip poyyeṉavē karutumpaṭi avaḷmaṉattiṉiṉṟum pōkki meyyallaṉa cilacoṟkaḷai meyvaḻippaṭuttu aṟivukoḷḷak koṭuppiṉum: um. “urumuraṟu karuviya perumaḻai talaiip – peyalāṉ ṟavinta tūṅkiru ṇaṭunāṇ – miṉṉunimirn taṉṉa kaṉaṅkuḻai yimaippap – piṉṉuviṭu neṟiyiṟ kiḷaiiya kūntalaḷ – varaiyiḻi mayili ṉolkuva ḷotuṅki – miṭaiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉe ṉivaḷeṉa – valaiyal vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉainam paṭappaic – cūruṭaic cilampiṟ cuṭarppū vēyntu – tāmvēṇ ṭuruvi ṉaṇaṅkumār varumē – naṉaviṉ vāyē pōlat tuñcunark – kaṉavāṇṭu maruṭṭalu muṇṭē yivaṭāṉ – cuṭariṉṟu tamiyaḷāyp paṉikkum veruvara – maṉṟa mārtta kūkai kuḻaṟiṉum – neñcaḻin taraṇañ cēru mataṉṟalaip – pulikkaṇat taṉṉa nāytoṭar viṭṭu – muruki ṉaṉṉa cīṟṟattuk kaṭuntiṟa – lentaiyu millā ṉāka – vañcuva ḷallaḷō vivaḷitu ceyalē.” miṭaiyai ēṟi iḻintāḷeṉṟatu kāraṇamāka aiyuṟṟa tāyaik kaṉavumaruṭṭalum uṇṭeṉṟatu mutalākap poyyeṉamāṟṟi aṇaṅkum varumeṉa meyvaḻik koṭuttatu. itu ciṟaippuṟamākak kūṟi varaivukaṭātaliṉ ataṉpiṉ vaittār. “vēṅkai naṟumalar veṟpiṭai yāṅkoytu – māntaḷir mēṉi viyarppamaṟ – ṟāṅkeṉaittum – pāyntaruvi yāṭiṉē mākap paṇimoḻikkuc – cēntaṉavāñ cēyarik kaṇ.” ituvum atu.
avaṉ vilaṅkuṟiṉum = taṉṉāṉun talaiviyāṉum iṭaiyīṭu paṭutaliṉṟit talaivaṉāṟ kūṭṭattiṟku iṭaiyūṟu tōṉṟiṉum: atu varaiviṭaip poruṭpirivum vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻiyuṅ kāvaṟpirivumām. um. “cevviya tīviya colli yavaṟṟoṭu – paiya muyaṅkiya vaññāṉ ṟavaiyellām – poyyātal yāṉyāṅ kaṟikōmaṟ ṟaiya – vakaṉakar koḷḷā valartalait tantu – pakaṉmuṉi veñcura muḷḷa laṟintēṉ – makaṉallai maṉṟa viṉi.” “nuṇñāṇ valaiyiṟ paratavar pōttanta – paṉmī ṉuṇaṅkal kavarun tuṟaivaṉaik – kaṇṇiṉāṟ kāṇa viyaiyuṅko leṉṟōḻi – vaṇṇantā veṉkan toṭuttu.” eṉavarum.
kaḷampeṟak kāṭṭiṉum = kāppumikutiyāṉuṅ kātaṉmikutiyāṉun tamar varaivumaṟuttatiṉāṉun talaivi āṟṟāḷāyavaḻi iteṟṟiṉāṉāyiṟṟeṉac cevili aṟivaraik kūuy avar kaḷattaip peṟāniṟkat talaiviyai avarkku veḷippaṭak kāṭṭiṉum: kaḷamāvatu kaṭṭuṅ kaḻaṅkum iṭṭuraikkum iṭamum veṟiyāṭṭiṭamumām. um. “poypaṭu paṟiyāk kaḻaṅka meyyē – maṇivaraik kaṭci maṭamayi lālu – malarnta vaḷḷiyaṅ kāṉaṅ kiḻavō – ṉāṇṭakai viṟalvē ḷallaṉivaḷ - pūṇṭāṅ kiḷamulai yaṇaṅki yōṉē.” itu kaḻaṅkupārttuḻik kūṟiyatu. “kaṟivaḷar cilampiṟ kaṭavuṭ pēṇi – yaṟiyā vēlaṉ veṟiyeṉak kūṟu – matumaṉaṅ koḷkuvai yaṉṉaiyivaḷ – putumalar maḻaikkaṇ pulampiya nōykkē.” itu tāyaṟiyāmai kūṟi veṟivilakkiyatu. “amma vāḻi tōḻi paṉmalar – naṟuntaṇ cōlai nāṭukeḻu neṭuntakai – kuṉṟam paṭāa ṉāyi – ṉeṉpayaṉ ceyyumō vēlaṟku veṟiyē.” itu talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu. “neyta ṉaṟumalar ceruntiyoṭu viraiik – kaipuṉai naṟuntār kamaḻu mārpa – ṉaruntiṟaṟ kaṭavu ḷallaṉ – peruntuṟaik kaṇṭiva ḷaṇaṅki yōṉē.” itu vēlaṟkuk kūṟiyatu. “kaṭavuṭ kaṟcuṉai yiṭaiyiṟan taviḻnta – maṟiyāk kuvaḷai malaroṭu kāntaṭ – kuruti yoṇpū vurukeḻak kaṭṭip – peruvarai yaṭukkam poṟpac cūrmaka – ḷaruvi yiṉṉiyat tāṭu nāṭaṉ – mārputara vanta paṭarmali yarunōy – niṉṉaṇaṅ kaṉmai yaṟintu māṇātu – kārnaṟuṅ kaṭampiṉ kaṇṇi cūṭi – vēlaṉ vēṇṭa veṟimaṉai vantōy – kaṭavu ḷāyiṉu māka – maṭavai maṉṟa vāḻiya murukē.” itu murukaṟkuk kūṟiyatu. “aṉṉai tantapu vākuva taṟivaṉ – poṉṉakar varaippiṟ kaṉṉan tūkki – murukeṉa moḻiyu māyi – ṉaruvarai nāṭaṉ peyarkolō vatuvē.” itu tamarkēṭpak kūṟiyatu.
piṟaṉvaraivu āyiṉum = notumalar varaiyak karutiyavaḻit talaivi cuṟṟattār avvaraiviṉai ārāyiṉum: tōḻi talaivaṟkun talaivikkuṅkūṟum. um. “kaṇṭal vēlik kaḻicūḻ paṭappai – muṇṭakam vēynta kuṟiyiṟaik kurampaik – koḻumīṉ koḷpavar pākkaṅ kalleṉa – neṭuntēr paṇṇi varalā ṉātē – kuṉṟat taṉṉa kuvavumaṇa ṉīnti – vantaṉar peyarvarko ṟāmē yalka – liḷaiyaru mutiyaruṅ kiḷaiyuṭaṉ kuḻīik – kōṭcuṟā veṟinteṉac curuṅkiya narampiṉa – muṭamutir paratavar maṭamoḻik kuṟumakaḷ – valaiyun tūṇṭilu maṟīiya peruṅkāṟ – ṟiraiyeḻu pauva muṉṉiya – kolaiveñ ciṟāar pāṟpaṭ ṭaṉaḷē.” itu notumalarvaraivu malintamai tōḻi ciṟaippuṟamākak kūṟiyatu. pāṟpaṭṭaṉaḷeṉat teḷivupaṟṟi iṟantakālattāṟ kūṟiṉāḷ. iṉi, “yāaṇ ṭaiyaṉō tōḻi kuṉṟattup – paḻaṅkuḻi yakaḻnta kāṉavaṉ kiḻaṅki – ṉēe rakaṉamai tuṇipeṟūu nāṭa – ṉaṟivukāḻk koḷḷu maḷavaic ceṟitoṭi – yemmil varukuvai nīyeṉap – pomma lōti nīvi yōṉē.” itu tāy kēṭpat tōḻi talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu.
avaṉ varaivu maṟuppiṉum = talaivi cuṟṟattār talaivaṟku varaivumaṟuttavaḻiyum: tōḻi aṟattoṭunilaiyāṟ kūṟum. um. “alakaṉ maḻaipoḻinta vakaṉka ṇaruvi – yāṭumaḻai yaṭukkat tiḻiyu nāṭaṉ – peruvarai yaṉṉa tiruviṟal viyaṉmārpu – muyaṅkātu kaḻinta nāḷivaṇ – mayaṅkitaḻ maḻaikkaṇ kaluḻu maṉṉāy.” “kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ kātaṉ maṭamaka – ḷaṇimayi laṉṉa vacainaṭaik koṭicciyaip – peruvarai nāṭaṉ varaiyu māyiṟ – koṭuttaṉe māyiṉō naṉṟē – yiṉṉu māṉātu naṉṉuta ṟuyarē.” eṉavarum. talaivikkuk kūṟuvaṉavuṅ koḷka. “amma vāḻi tōḻi nammūrp – pirintōrp puṇarppavava riruntaṉar kollō – taṇṭuṭaik kaiyar veṇṭalaic citavalar – naṉṟunaṉ ṟeṉṉu mākkaḷō – ṭiṉṟuperi teṉṉu māṅkaṇa tavaiyē.” itu tamar varaivu maṟupparōveṉak kavaṉṟāṭkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu. “nuṇṇēr puruvatta kaṇṇu māṭu – mayirvār muṉkai vaḷaiyuñ ceṟūuṅ – kaḷiṟukōṭ piḻaitta katañciṟan teḻupuli – yeḻutaru maḻaiyiṟ kuḻumum – peruṅka ṉāṭaṉ varuṅko laṉṉāy.” itu tamar varaivumaṟuttuḻi āṟṟāta talaivikkut tōḻi tīyakuṟi nīṅki naṟkuṟi taṉakkuc ceyyakkaṇṭu kaṭitiṉvaraivareṉak kūṟiyatu.
muṉṉilai aṟaṉ eṉap paṭutal eṉṟu iruvakaip puraitīr kiḷavi tāyiṭaip pukuppiṉum = aṟaṉeṉappaṭutal iruvakaip puraitīr muṉṉilaiyeṉṟu kiḷavi tāyiṭaip pukuppiṉum; aṟaṉeṉṟu collappaṭun taṉmai iruvarkaṇṇuṅ kuṟṟantīrnta etirppāṭeṉṟu ceviliyiṭattē kūṟi akkiḷaviyai naṟṟāyiṭattuñ celuttiṉum: eṉṟatu puṉaṟarupuṇarcciyum pūttarupuṇarcciyuṅ kaḷirutarupuṇarcciyum pōlvaṉa cevilikkuk kūṟi avaḷ naṟṟāykkuk kūṟutalai nikaḻttuvittalām. eṉavē, avaḷ tantaikkun taṉṉaiyarkkum uṇarttutalum ataṉai mīṇṭuvantu talaivikku uṇarttutalum peṟṟām. avvaṟattoṭunilai eḻuvakaiya eṉap poruḷiyaluṭ kūṟupa. um. “kāmar kaṭumpuṉal kalantemmō ṭāṭuvāḷ” eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcikkaliyuḷ, “terumantu cāyttār talai.” eṉap puṉaṟarupuṇarcciyāṟ tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭuniṟpac cevili naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭuniṟpa avaḷ ēṉaiyōrkku aṟattoṭu niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. “vāṭāta cāṉṟōr varavetir koṇṭīrāyk – kōṭātu nīrkoṭuppa tallatu – kōṭā – veḻilu mulaiyu miraṇṭaṟku munnīrp – poḻilum vilaiyāmō pōntu.” “cāṉṟōr varuntiya varuttamu numatu – vāṉṟōy vaṉṉa kuṭimaiyu nōkkit – tirumaṇi varaṉṟuṅ kuṉṟaṅ koṇṭivaḷ – varumulai yākam vaḻaṅkiṉō naṉṟē – yaḵtāṉ, ṟaṭaiporuḷ karutuvi rāyiṟ kuṭaiyoṭu – kaḻumalan tanta naṟṟērc cempiyaṉ – paṅkuṉi viḻaviṉ vañciyō – ṭuḷḷi viḻavi ṉuṟantaiyuñ ciṟitē.” ivai naṟṟāy tantai taṉṉaiyarkku aṟattoṭuniṉṟaṉa. “aṉṉāy vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉai yeṉṉai – tāṉu malaintā ṉemakkun taḻaiyāyiṉa – poṉvī maṇiyarum piṉavē – yeṉṉa māṅkolavar cāra lavvē.” itu taḻaitantamai kūṟiṟṟu. “cuḷḷi cuṉainīlañ cērpā likaiceyalai – yaḷḷi yaḷakattiṉ mēlāyntu – teḷḷi – yitaṉāṟ kaṭiyoṭuṅkā vīrṅkaṭā yāṉai – yutaṉāṟ kaṭintā ṉuḷaṉ.” itu kaḷiṟṟiṭaiyutavi kūṟiṟṟu. “villār viḻaviṉum vēlāḻi cūḻulaki – ṉallār viḻavakattu nāṅkāṇē – nallā – yuvarkkat teṟitiraic cērppaṉō ṭoppār – cuvarkkat tuḷarāyiṟ cūḻa.” itu cevili talaiviyaik kōlañceytu ivaḷ nalattiṟku oppāṉa oruvaṉaip peṟavēṇṭumeṉṟāṭkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu. “peruṅkaṭaṟ ṟiraiyatu ciṟuveṇ kākkai – tuṟaipaṭi yampi yakamaṉai yīṉun – taṇṇan tuṟaiva ṉalki – loṇṇuta larivai pālā rummē.” notumalarvaraivuḻi āṟṟātu paciyaṭa niṉṟuḻi itaṟkuk kāraṇameṉṉeṉṟa cevilikkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu. “entaiyum yāyu muṇarak kāṭṭi – yoḷitta ceytiyiṉ veḷippaṭak kiḷantapiṉ – malaikeḻu veṟpaṉ ṟalaivan tiṟappa – naṉṟupuri koḷkaiyi ṉoṉṟā yiṉṟē – muṭaṅka liṟaiya tūṅkaṇaṅ kurīi – nīṭirum peṇṇait toṭutta – kūṭṭiṉu mayaṅkiya maiya lūrē.” itu aṟattoṭu niṉṟamai talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu.
varaivu uṭaṉpaṭṭōṟ kaṭāval vēṇṭiṉum = talaivi tamarvaraivuṭaṉpaṭat tāṉum varaivuṭaṉpaṭṭa talaivaṉ varaiviṭaivaittup pirintu nīṭṭittuḻi iṉi nīṭṭikkaṟpālaiyallaiyeṉak kaṭuñcoṟ kūṟi varaivukaṭāval vēṇṭiya iṭattum: um. “māmalar muṇṭakan tillaiyō ṭoruṅkuṭaṉ – kāṉa laṇinta vuyarmaṇa lekkarmēṟ – cīrmiku ciṟappiṉōṉ maramuta lacaitta – nīrmali karakampōṟ paḻantūṅku muṭattāḻaip – pūmalarn tavaipōlap puḷḷalkun tuṟaivakēḷ; āṟṟuta leṉpatoṉ ṟalantavark kutavutal – pōṟṟuta leṉpatu puṇarntāraip piriyāmai – paṇpeṉap paṭuvatu pāṭaṟin toḻukuta – laṉpeṉap paṭuvatu taṉkiḷai ceṟāmai – yaṟiveṉap paṭuvatu pētaiyār coṉṉōṉṟal – ceṟiveṉap paṭuvatu kūṟiyatu maṟāmai – niṟaiyeṉap paṭuvatu maṟaipiṟa raṟiyāmai – muṟaiyeṉap paṭuvatu kaṇṇōṭā tuyirvauva  lāṅkatai yaṟintaṉi rāyiṉeṉ ṟōḻi naṉṉuta ṉalaṉuṇṭu tuṟattal koṇka – tīmpā luṇpōr kalamvaraita liṉṟu – niṉṟalai varuntiyā ṭuyarañ – ceṉṟaṉai kaḷaimō pūṇkaniṉ ṟērē.” itu muṟkālattu varaivukaṭāvumāṟu pōlaṉṟi varaivukaṭāyatu. “yārai yeluva yāmē nīyemak – kiyāraiyu mallai notuma lāḷaṉai – yaṉaittāṟ koṇkavem miṭaiyē niṉaippiṟ – kaṭumpakaṭ ṭiyāṉai neṭuntērk kuṭṭuvaṉ – vēntaṭu kaḷattiṉ muracatirn taṉṉa – vōṅkaṟ puṇari pāyntāṭu makaḷi – raṇintiṭu palpū marīi yārntavar – pulampuku tarūum pēricai mālaik – kaṭalkeḻu māntai yaṉṉavem – vēṭṭaṉai yallaiyā ṉalantantu ceṉmē.” itu nalantolaivuraittu varaivukaṭāyatu. 
āṅku ataṉ taṉmaiyiṉ vaṉpuṟai uḷappaṭa = aṅṅaṉaṅ kaṭāviyavaḻi avvaraintukōṭaṉ meyyāyiṉamaiyiṉ vatuvai muṭiyumaḷavum āṟṟutaṟku vaṟpuṟuttuk kūṟutal uḷappaṭa: taṉmai, meymai.  eṉavē, muṉ poymaiyāṉa vaṟpuṟuttalum peṟṟām. um. “neykaṉi kuṟumpūḻ kāya māka – vārpatam peṟuka tōḻi yattai – peruṅka ṉāṭaṉ varainteṉa vantetira – naṉṟō makaṉē yeṉṟaṉa – ṉaṉṟē pōlu meṉṟurait tōṉē.” itu talaivaṉ kuṟṟēvaṉmakaṉāṉ varaivumalinta tōḻi talaivikkuraittatu. “kūrmuṇ muṇṭakak kūrmpaṉi māmalar – nūlaṟu mukattiṟ kāloṭu pāṟit – tuṟaitoṟum parakkun tūmaṇaṟ cērppaṉai – yāṉuṅ kātalēṉ yāyu naṉiveyya – ḷentaiyuṅ koṭīiyar vēṇṭu – mampa lūru mavaṉoṭu moḻimō.” eṉavarum. “koṭicci kākku maṭukkaṟ paintiṉai – muntu viḷaiperuṅ kuralkoṇṭa manti – kallāk kaṭuvaṉoṭu nalvarai yēṟi – yaṅkai niṟaiya ñemiṭik koṇṭutaṉ – ṟiraiyaṇaṟ koṭuṅkavu ṇiṟaiya mukki – vāṉpeya ṉaṉainta puṟatta nōṉpiyar – taiyū ṇirukkaiyiṟ ṟōṉṟu nāṭaṉ – vantaṉaṉ vantaṉaṉ vāḻi tōḻi – yulaṅkayaṅ kaṇaṟṟa paitaṟu kālaip – pīḷoṭu tiraṅkiya nelliṟku – naḷḷeṉ yāmattu maḻaipoḻin tāṅkē.” itaṉuṭ tiṉaiviḷaikālam vatuvaikkālamāyiṉum vampamāri iṭaiyiṭutalaṉṟi yāṉkūṟiya varaivu poyttaṉarēṉum iṉṟu meyyākavē vantaṉareṉṟāḷ. “uravuttirai poruta piṇarpaṭu taṭavumuta – laravu vāya muḷḷilait tāḻai – poṉṉēr tātiṟ puṉṉaiyoṭu kamaḻum – palapūṅ kāṉaṟ pakaṟkuṟi vantunam – meykaviṉ citaiyap peyarntaṉa ṉāyiṉuṅ – kuṉṟiṟ ṟōṉṟuṅ kuvavumaṇa lēṟik – kaṇṭaṉam varukañ ceṉmō tōḻi – taṇṭā rakalam vaṇṭimir pūtap – paṭumaṇik kalimā kaṭaii – neṭunīrc cērppaṉ varūu māṟē.” itu talaivaṉ varaivoṭuvarukiṉṟamai kāṇkam vammōveṉṟatu.
pāṅkuṟa vanta nāleṭṭum = pāṅkiyar palaruḷḷum pāṅkāntaṉmai ciṟappak kūṟiya muppattiraṇṭum: nāleṭṭumeṉa ummai virikka; vakaiyum = ikkūṟṟukkaḷilē vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavum: tāṅkaruñ ciṟappiṟ tōḻi mēṉa = poṟuttaṟkariya ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya tōḻiyiṭattaṉa.  e-ṟu. eṉavē, oṉṟiya tōḻikkaṇaṉṟi ēṉaiyōrkku ikkūṟṟu iṉṟeṉṟāṉ.  tāyattāyk koṇṭuvaruñ ciṟappum iruvar tuṉpamun tāṉ uṟṟāḷākak karutuñ ciṟappum uṭaimaiyiṟ tāṅkaruñ ciṟappeṉṟāṉ. uraittuḻik kūṭṭattōṭē akaṟciyum eṉṟalum ūṭṭalum uraittalum accuṟuttalum eñcāmaṟ kiḷanta irunāṉku kiḷaviyum pāṅkuṟavanta eṉka. nāṭṭattiṉ kaṇṇum eñcāmaṟ kiḷanta eṉka. eṉṟatu, ārāycciyuṭaṉē ivveṭṭuṅ kūṟumeṉṟāṉ. peyarppiṉum oḻippiṉum uraittuḻik kūṭṭattōṭu eñcāmaṟ kiḷanta eṉka. ēṉaipporuḷkaḷ ēḻaṉurupum viṉaiyeccamumāy niṉṟavaṟṟaip pāṅkuṟavanta eṉpataṉōṭu muṭittu appeyareccattiṉai nāleṭṭeṉṉum peyarōṭu muṭittu ataṉait tōḻimēṉaveṉa muṭikka.
iṉi vakaiyāṟkoḷvaṉa varumāṟu. “aṉṉai vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉai neyta – ṉīrppaṭar tūmpiṉ pūkkeḻu tuṟaiva – ṉentō ṭuṟanta kālai – yevaṉkolpaṉ ṉāḷvaru mavaṉaḷitta pōḻtē.” itu aṟattoṭuniṉṟapiṉ varaivāṉ pirintu nīṭṭittuḻi aiyuṟṟacevili avaṉ nummait tuṟantāṉpōlum nuṅkaṭku avaṉ kūṟiyatiṟam yāteṉṟāṭkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu. “puṉṉai vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉai kaḻaṉiya – muṇṭaka malarun taṇkaṭaṟ cērppa – ṉentō ṭuṟantaṉa ṉāyi – ṉevaṉkoṉ maṟṟava ṉayanta tōḷē.” itu aṟattoṭuniṉṟapiṉ varaivunīṭa maṟṟoru kulamakaḷai varaiyuṅkoleṉṟu aiyuṟṟa cevili kuṟippaṟinta tōḻi avaṭkuk kūṟiyatu. “aṉṉai vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉai puṉṉaiyoṭu – ñāḻal pūkkun taṇṇaṟun tuṟaiva – ṉivaṭkamain taṉaṉāṟ ṟāṉē – taṉakkamain taṉṟivaḷ māmaik kaviṉē.” itu vatuvai nikaḻāniṉṟuḻit tāykkuk kāṭṭit tōḻi kūṟiyatu. “kaṉṉavi ṟōḷāṉ kaṭināḷ vilakkutaṟ – keṉṉai poruṇiṉaintā rēntiḻāy – piṉṉa – ramarēṟṟuk koḷḷumeṉ ṟañciṉē ṉañcār – namarēṟṟuk koḷḷāta ñāṉṟu.” itu cuṟṟattār poruḷvēṇṭi maṟuttāreṉṟatu. “notuma lāḷar koḷḷā rivaiyē – yemmoṭu vantu kaṭalāṭu makaḷiru – neytalam pakaittaḻaip pāvai puṉaiyā – ruṭalakaṅ koḷvō riṉmaiyiṟ – ṟoṭalaik kuṟṟa cilapū viṉarē.”  itu kaiyuṟai maṟuttatu. “amma vāḻi tōḻi nammalai – varaiyār piḻiyak kōṭa ṉīṭak – kātalarp pirintōr kaiyaṟa naliyun – taṇpaṉi vaṭantai yāccīr – muntu vantaṉarnaṅ kāta lōrē.” itu varaiviṭaippirintōṉ kuṟitta paruvattiṟkumuṉ varukiṉṟamai yaṟinta tōḻi talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu. (23)
115. kaḷavala rāyiṉuṅ kāmameyp paṭuppiṉu
maḷavumikat tōṉṟiṉun talaippeytu kāṇiṉuṅ
kaṭṭiṉuṅ kaḻaṅkiṉum veṟiyeṉa viruvaru
moṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytik kaṇṇu
māṭiya ceṉṟuḻi yaḻivu talaivariṉuṅ
kātal kaimikak kaṉavi ṉaraṟṟalun
tōḻiyai viṉavalun teyvam vāḻttalum
pōkkuṭa ṉaṟintapiṟ ṟōḻiyoṭu keḻīik
kaṟpi ṉākkattu niṟṟaṟ kaṇṇum
pirivi ṉeccattu makaṇeñcu valippiṉu
mirupāṟ kuṭipporu ḷiyalpiṉ kaṇṇu
miṉṉa vakaiyiṟ patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyō
ṭaṉṉavai piṟavuñ cevili mēṉa.
itu cevili kūṟṟu nikaḻumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu: akkūṟṟuc cevili tāṉē kūṟappaṭuvaṉavun talaiviyun tōḻiyuṅ koṇṭukūṟṟākak kūṟappaṭuvaṉavumeṉa iruvakaiyavām: ikkūṟappaṭṭa patiṉmūṉṟukiḷaviyum avaipōlvaṉa piṟavuñ cevili tāṉē kūṟappaṭuvaṉavun talaiviyun tōḻiyum avaḷ kūṟṟāyk koṇṭeṭuttu moḻiyappaṭuvaṉavumāyc cevilikkuriyavā meṉṟavāṟu. iṉṉavakaiyeṉṟār taṉkūṟṟuṅ koṇṭukūṟṟumāy nikaḻumeṉṟaṟku. (i-ḷ.) kaḷavu alar āyiṉum = kaḷavoḻukkam puṟattārkkup pulaṉāy alar tūṟṟappaṭiṉum. um. “pāvaṭi yurala pakuvāy vaḷḷai – yētiṉ mākka ṇuvaṟalu nuvalpa – vaḻiva tevaṉkolip pētai yūrkkē – perumpūṭ poṟaiyaṉ pēemutir kollik – karuṅkaṭ ṭeyvaṅ kuṭavarai yeḻutiya – nalliyaṟ pāvai yaṉṉaveṉ – melliyaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pāṭinaṉ kuṟiṉē.” itu cevili tāṉē kūṟiyatu. “amma vāḻi tōḻi neṉṉa – lōṅkutirai veṇmaṇa luṭaikkun tuṟaivaṟ – kūrār peṇṭeṉa moḻiya veṉṉai – yatukēṭ, ṭaṉṉā yeṉṟaṉa ḷaṉṉai – paipaya vemmai yeṉṟaṉeṉ yāṉē.” peṇṭeṉṟataṉaikkēṭṭu aṉṉāyeṉṟaṉaḷ aṉṉaiyeṉa alartūṟṟiṉamaikaṇṭu cevilikūṟiya kūṟṟiṉait talaivikoṇṭukūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.
kāmam meyppaṭuppiṉum = talaivi karantoḻukuṅ kāmantāṉē akkaḷaviṉai naṉṟāyun tītāyum meykkaṇ veḷippaṭuppiṉum: um. “maṇiyuṭ ṭikaḻtaru nūlpōṉ maṭantai – yaṇiyiṟ ṟikaḻvatoṉ ṟuṇṭu.” kāmattāṟ tikaḻnta poliviṉaic cevili tāṉē kūṟiyatu. 
aḷavumikat tōṉṟiṉum = kaṇṇun tōḷum mulaiyum piṟavum puṇarcciyāṟ katirttuk kārikainī ravāy avaḷiṭattu aḷavai mikak kāṭṭiṉum. um. “kaṇṇiṟainta kārikaik kāmpērtōṭ pētaikkup - peṇṇiṟainta nīrmai peritu.” itu katirppukkaṇṭu cevili tāṉē kūṟiyatu.“piṉṉuviṭa neṟitta kūntalum poṉṉeṉa – vākat tarumpiya cuṇaṅkum vampuṭaik kaṇṇurut teḻutaru mulaiyu nōkki – mellivaḷ periteṉap paṉmāṇ kūṟip – peruntō ḷaṭaiya muyaṅki nīṭuniṉain – taruṅkaṭip paṭuttaṉaḷ  ñāṉṟē.” tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟiyatu.
talaippeytu kāṇiṉum = iruvarkkuṅ kūṭṭam nikaḻtalāṉē talaivaṉai illiṭattē varakkāṇiṉun talaiviyaip puṟattuppōkak kāṇiṉum: peyteṉpatu kāraṇakāriyapporuṭṭāya piṟaviṉai koṇṭatu. um. “irumpuli tolaitta peruṅkai vēḻattup – pulavunāṟu pukarnutal kaḻuvak kaṅku – laruvi tanta vaṇaṅkuṭai neṭuṅkōṭ – ṭañcuvaru viṭarmuṉkai yāriru ḷakaṟṟi – miṉṉoḷi reḵkañ ceṉṉeṟi viḷakkat – taṉiyaṉ vantu paṉiyalai muṉiyā – ṉīraḻi maruṅki ṉāriṭat tamaṉṟa – kuḷaviyoṭu miṭainta kūtaḷaṅ kaṇṇi – yacaiyā nāṟṟa macaivaḷi pakarat – tuṟuka ṉaṇṇiya kaṟiyivar paṭappaik – kuṟiyiṟaik kurampainam maṉaivayiṟ pukutaru – meymali yuvakaiya ṉannilai kaṇṭu – murukeṉa vuṇarntu mukamaṉ kūṟi – yuruvac centiṉai nīroṭu tūuy – neṭuvēṭ paravu maṉṉai yaṉṉō – veṉṉā vatuko ṟāṉē poṉṉeṉa – malarnta vēṅkai yalaṅkuciṉai poliya – maṇiniṟa maññai yakavu – maṇimalai nāṭaṉō ṭamaintanan toṭarpē.” immaṇimiṭaipavaḻattut talaivaṉaic cevilikaṇṭu murukeṉap parāviṉamai tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟiṉāḷ. “urumuraṟu karuviya” eṉpataṉuḷ “miṭaiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉa ḷivaḷeṉa – valaiyal vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉai” eṉṟatu talaivi puṟattuppōkak kaṇṭu cevili kūṟiyataṉait tōḻi koṇṭukūṟiṉāḷ. tāṉē kūṟuvaṉa vantuḻikkāṇka.
kaṭṭiṉum kaḻaṅkiṉum veṟiyeṉa iruvarum oṭṭiya tiṟattāṟ ceytikkaṇṇum = kaṭṭuvicciyum vēlaṉun tām pārtta kaṭṭiṉāṉuṅ kaḻaṅkiṉāṉun teyvattiṟkuc ciṟappucceyyākkāl immaiyal tīrāteṉṟu kūṟutaliṉ avviruvarun tammiṉotta tiṟampaṟṟiyataṉaiyē ceyyuñ ceytiyiṭattum: tiṟameṉṟataṉāṉ avar vēṟuvēṟākavuṅ kūṟappaṭum. um. “peymaṇal muṟṟaṅ kaviṉpeṟa viyaṟṟi – malaivāṉ kōṭṭa ciṉaiiya vēlaṉ – kaḻaṅkiṉā ṉaṟikuva teṉṟā – ṉaṉṟā lamma niṉṟaviva ṇalaṉē.” itu vēlaṉ kaḻaṅkupārttamai kūṟiṟṟu. “aṟiyā maiyiṉ veṟiyeṉa mayaṅki – yaṉṉai yaruntuya ruḻantaṉa ḷataṉā – ṉeyyātu viṭutalō koṭitē niṟaiyita – ḻāymala ruṇkaṇ pacappac – cēymalai nāṭaṉ ceyta nōyē.” itu veṟiyeṉa aṉṉaimayaṅkiṉamai kūṟiṟṟu. “aṇaṅkuṭaineṭuvarai” eṉṉum akappāṭṭiṉuṭ kaṭṭukkaṇṭu veṟiyeṭuttamaikūṟiṟṟu. “paṉivarainivanta” eṉṉum akappāṭṭiṉuṭ “pirappuḷarviri” eṉakkaṭṭuvicciyaik kēṭṭavāṟum “eṉmakaṭku” eṉac cevilikūṟṟu nikaḻntavāṟuṅ kāṇka. itaṉuḷ “neṭuvē ṇalkuvateṉṉiṉē” eṉat talaivi añcavēṇṭiyatu iruvarum oṭṭikkūṟāmaṟ teyvantāṉ aruḷumeṉṟu kōṭaliṉ. “ikuḷaikēṭṭiciṉ” eṉṉum maṇimiṭaipavaḻam vitantu kūṟāmaiyiṉ iraṇṭum oruṅku vantatu.
āṭiyaceṉṟuḻi aḻivu talaivariṉum = aṅṅaṉam veṟiyāṭutalvēṇṭiya toḻiṉ muṭintapiṉṉun talaivikku varuttam mikiṉum: um. “vēṅkai yirumpuṉattu vīḻuṅ kiḷikaṭiyāḷ – kāntaṇ mukiḻviralāṟ kaṇṇiyuṅ kaitoṭā – ḷēnteḻi lalkuṟ ṟaḻaipuṉaiyā ḷellēyeṉ – pūntoṭi yiṭṭa pulampu maṟitirō” eṉavum, “puṉaiyiruṅ kuvaḷaip pōtuviri nāṟṟañ – cuṉaiyara makaḷi ravvē ciṉaiya – vēṅkai yoḷvī veṟikamaḻ nāṟṟamoṭu – kānta ṇāṟupa kallara makaḷi – rakilu māramu nāa ṟuveṉa – ṟiṟalaru marapiṟ ṟeyva meṉpa – veṟipuṉaṅ kāva liruntataṟ ṟoṭṭut – tīviya nāṟu meṉmaka – ḷaṟiyēṉ yāṉi tañcutaka vuṭaittē.” eṉavum varum ivai āṟṟāmai kaṇṭu añcic cevili piṟarai viṉāyiṉa. “aṉṉāy vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉai niṉmakaḷ – pālu muṇṇāḷ paḻaṅkaṇ koṇṭu – naṉipacan taṉaḷeṉa viṉavuti yataṉṟiṟam – yāṉun teṟṟeṉa vuṇarēṉ mēṉāṇ – malipūñ cāraleṉ ṟōḻi mārō – ṭoliciṉai vēṅkai koykuvaḷ ceṉṟuḻip – pulipuli yeṉṉum pūca ṟōṉṟa – voṇceṅ kaḻunīrk kaṇpō lāyitaḻ – mūcu pōkiya cūḻcey mālaiyaṉ – pakkañ cērttiya ceccaik kaṇṇiyaṉ – kuyamaṇ ṭākañ ceñcāntu nīvi – varipuṉai villa ṉorukaṇai terintukoṇ – ṭiyātō maṟṟam mātiṟam paṭarkeṉa – viṉavi niṟṟan tōṉē yavaṟkaṇ – ṭemmu ḷemmuṇ meymmaṟai yoṭuṅki – nāṇi niṉṟaṉa mākap pēṇi – yaivakai vakutta kūnta lāynutaṉ – maiyī rōti maṭavīr numvāyp – poyyu muḷavō veṉṟaṉaṉ paiyeṉap – parimuṭuku tavirntō ṉetira maṟuttu – niṉmaka ḷuṇkaṇ paṉmā ṇōkkic – ceṉṟōṉ maṉṟavak kuṉṟukiḻa vōṉē – pakaṉmā yantip paṭucuṭa ramayat – tavaṉmaṟai tēe nōkki maṟṟivaṉ – makaṉē tōḻi yeṉṟaṉa – ḷitaṉaḷa vuṇṭukoṉ matival lōrkkē.” itu cevili kūṟṟiṉait tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟiyatu. 
kātal kaimikak kaṉaviṉ araṟṟalum = talaiviyiṭattuk kātal kaiyikantu perukutalāṟ tuyilāniṉṟuḻiyum oṉṟu kūṟi āṟṟutalum: kaṉavu, tuyil; tuyiliṟ kāṇṭalaik kaṉaviṟ kāṇṭaleṉpa. um. “poḻutumelliṉṟē” eṉpataṉuṭ “putalvaṟpulli aṉṉāy” eṉṟu talaiviyai viḷittatu kaṉaviṉ araṟṟalāyiṟṟu. araṟṟal iṉṉatōr iṉṉākkālattu eṉceykiṉṟāyeṉak kātalpaṟṟi iraṅkutal.
tōḻiyai viṉalum = niṉṟōḻikku ivvēṟupāṭu eṟṟiṉāṉāyiṟṟeṉṟāṟpōlat tōḻiyai viṉāvutalum: um. “neṭuvēlēnti nīyemak kiyāar – toṭuta lōmpeṉa vāṟṟalu māṟṟuṅ – kaṭavuḷ vēṅkaiyuṅ kāntaḷu malainta – toṭalaik kaṇṇi pariyalu meṉṉum – pāmpupaṭa nivanta payamaḻait taṭakkaiya – pūmpoṟik kaḻaṟkā lāaya kuṉṟattuk – kuṟuñcuṉai malarnta kuvaḷai nāṟic – ciṟutēṉ kamaḻnta vammel lākam – vāḻiyem makaḷai yuraimati yimmalait – tēmpoti kiḷaviyeṉ pētai – yāṅkā ṭiṉaḷō niṉṉoṭu pakalē.” eṉavarum. itu cevili tōḻiyai viṉāyatu. “ōṅkumalai nāṭa voḻikaniṉ vāymai – kāmputalai maṇanta kallatarc ciṟuneṟi – yuṟupakai pēṇā tiraviṉ vantivaḷ – poṟikiḷa rākam pullat tōḷcēr – paṟukāṟ paṟavai yaḷavila moyttaliṟ – kaṇkō ḷāka nōkkip paṇṭu – miṉaiyai yōveṉa viṉaviṉa ḷāyē – yataṉetir collā ḷāki yallān – teṉmuka nōkki yōḷē yaṉṉā – yāṅkuṇarn tuykuvaḷ kolleṉa maṭutta – cānta ñekiḻi kāṭṭi – yiṅkā yiṉavā leṉṟiciṉ yāṉē.” itu cevili viṉāyiṉamaiyait tōḻi koṇṭu kūṟiṉāḷ.
teyvam vāḻttalum = iṉṉatoṉṟu nikaḻntateṉat tuṇintapiṉṉart taṉmakaḷoṭu talaimakaṉiṭai nikaḻnta oḻukkam naṉṉarttākaveṉat teyvattiṟkup parāvutalum: um. “perumalaic cilampiṉ vēṭṭam pōkiya – ceṟipaṭai yampiṉ valviṟ kāṉavaṉ – porutu tolaiyāṉai veṇkōṭu koṇṭa nīrtikaḻ cilampiṉaṉ pōṉa kaḻalōṉ – kaṇporu timaikkun tirumaṇi kiḷarppa – vainnuti vāṉmarup poṭiya vukka – teṇṇī rāli kaṭukku muttamoṭu – mūvēṟu tāramu moruṅkuṭaṉ cāṟṟic cāntam pōṉṟa māmara naṟainār – vēṅkaik kaṇṇiya yaṇitaru nāṭaṟ – kīṉṟīm palavi ṉērkeḻu celvat – tentaiyu metirntaṉaṉ koṭaiyē yalarvā – yampa lūru mavaṉoṭu moḻiyuñ – cāyiṟait tiraṇṭa tōḷpā rāṭṭi – yāyu mavaṉē yeṉṉum yāmum – vallē varuka varainta nāḷeṉa – nalliṟai melviral kūppi – yilluṟai kaṭavuṭ koṭkutum paliyē.” itaṉuṭ tōḷpārāṭṭi yāyumavaṉē eṉṉumeṉṟu yāy teyvam parāyiṉāḷeṉpatupaṭak kūṟi yām atteyvattiṟkup palikoṭuttumeṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.
pōkkuṭaṉ aṟintapiṟ tōḻiyoṭu keḻīik kaṟpiṉ ākkattu niṟṟaṟkaṇṇum = uṭaṉpōkku aṟintapiṉṉarc cevili tōḻiyoṭu matiyuṭampaṭṭuniṉṟu talaiviyatu kaṟpumikutiyē karuti uvanta uvakaikkaṇṇum: atu “emmaṉai muntuṟat tarumō taṉmaṉai yuykkumō yātavaṉ kuṟippē.” eṉṟāṟpōlak kaṟpiṉākkattuk karuttunikaḻtal. um. “muyaṅkukam vārāy tōḻi tayaṅkupu – kaṭalpeyarn taṉṉa kāṉalaṅ kalleṉap – peyalkaṭaik koṇṭa peruntaṇ vāṭai – varumulai veppaṅ koḻunaṟ pōṟṟiya – ceṉṟaṉa ḷammaniṉ ṟōḻi yavaṉō – ṭeṉṟiṉi varūu meṉṟaṉaḷ – valanturai tavirntaṉ ṟalarnta vūrē.” itu cevili kaṟpiṉākkattu niṉṟamai tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
piriviṉ eccattum = talaivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻit tāṉ piṉ cellātē eñcutalum uḷavātaliṉ āṇṭuk kūṟuvaṉavum. um. “teṟuva tamma nummakaḷ viruppē – yuṟutuya ravalamo ṭuyircelac cāayp – pāḻpaṭu neñcam paṭaraṭak kalaṅka – nāṭiṭai vilaṅkiya veṟpiṟ – kāṭiṟan taṉaṇam maṇaṅki yōḷē.” itu piṉcellātu varuntiyirunta ceviliyaik kaṇṭa naṟṟāy kūṟiyatu. itu naṟṟāykūṟṟāyc cevilimēṉa āyiṟṟu.
makaḷ neñcu valippiṉum = uṭaṉpōkkiṟku makaḷ neñcu tuṇiyiṉum: taṉmēl aṉpunīṅkiyatu uṇarntu cevili kūṟum. um. “peyarttaṉeṉ muyaṅkayāṉ viyarttaṉe ṉeṉṟaṉa – ḷiṉiyaṟin tēṉatu tuṉiyā kutalē – kaḻaṟoṭi yāay maḻaitavaḻ potiyil – vēṅkaiyuṅ kāntaḷu nāṟi – yāmpaṉ malariṉun tāṉṟaṇ ṇiyaḷē.” eṉavarum.
irupāṟ kuṭipporuḷ iyalpiṉ kaṇṇum = talaivaṉun talaiviyun tōṉṟiya iruvakaik kuṭiyum nirampivarutal iyalpākap peṟṟavaḻiyum: poruḷeṉṟār, piṟappumutaliya pattaiyuṅkaruti. “kāmarkaṭumpuṉal” eṉṉuṅkaliyuḷ “avaṉuntā, ṉēṉa litaṇat takiṟpukai yuṇṭiyaṅkum – vāṉūr matiyam varaicēri ṉavvarait – tēṉi ṉiṟāleṉa vēṇi yiḻaittirukkuṅ – kāṉaka nāṭaṉ makaṉ – eṉavāṅ, kaṟattoṭu niṉṟēṉaik kaṇṭu – tiṟappaṭa veṉṉaiyark kuytturait tāḷyāy.” eṉat tōḻi tāṉ kūṟiya irupāṟ kuṭipporuḷaik kūṟic cevili aṟattoṭu niṉṟāḷeṉak koṇṭeṭuttu moḻintavāṟu kāṇka.
iṉṉavakaiyiṟ patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyōṭu aṉṉavai piṟavum = ittaṉmaittākiya kūṟupāṭṭaiyaṭaiya patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyōṭē avaipōlvaṉa piṟavāy varuvaṉavum: cevili mēṉa = taṉkūṟṟāyum piṟarkoṇṭu kūṟuṅ kūṟṟāyum kūṟuṅ kūṟṟuc cevilikku uriyavām.  e-ṟu. aṉṉapiṟa eṉṟataṉāl, “paṟaipaṭap paṇila mārppa viṟaikoḷat – toṉmū tālattup potiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiya – naralūrk kōcar naṉmoḻi pōla – vāyā kiṉṟē tōḻi yāykaḻaṟ – ceyalai veḷvēl viṭalaiyoṭu – tokuvaḷai muṉkai maṭantai naṭpē.” itu uṭaṉpōyapiṉ cevili naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭuniṉṟatu. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē “valluraik kaṭuñco laṉṉai tuñcāḷ” eṉṟaluñ “ciṟukiḷi kaṭita ṟēṟṟā ḷivaḷ” eṉṟaluṅ “kaṇkō ḷāka nōkkip paṇṭu miṉaiyaiyō” eṉṟalum pōlvaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka. (24)
116. tāykkum varaiyā ruṇarvuṭam paṭiṉē.
itu cevilikku uriyaṉa kūṟi naṟṟāykku uriya kūṟṟuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) uṇarvu uṭampaṭiṉ = aṅṅaṉañ cevili uṇarntāṅkē = naṟṟāyum matiyuṭampaṭil: tāykkum varaiyār = naṟṟāykkum muṟkūṟiya patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷaviyum piṟavumākak koṇṭu eṭuttu moḻital varaiyār  e-ṟu. tāykkum eṉṟār ivaṭku attuṇai payiṉṟuvārā eṉṟaṟku. atu naṟṟāy illaṟam nikaḻttuṅ karuttu vēṟu uṭaimaiyiṉ uṟṟunōkkāḷ ceviliyē talaiviyai uṟṟunōkki oḻukuvāḷātaliṉ ilakkaṇamuṇmaiyiṉ ilakkiyam vantuḻik kāṇka. (25)
117. kiḻavō ṉaṟiyā vaṟiviṉa ḷivaḷeṉa
maiyaṟu ciṟappi ṉuyarntōr pāṅki
ṉaiyak kiḷavi yaṟitalu murittē.
itu aṅṅaṉaṅ kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉar naṟṟāykkuñ cevilikkum uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu (i-ḷ.) kiḻavōṉ aṟiyā aṟiviṉaḷ ivaḷeṉa aiyakkiḷavi = naṅkulattiṟku otta talaivaṉai aṟintu kūṭāta aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiyaḷ ivaḷeṉṟu tammaṉattai aiyamuṟṟup piṟarōṭu ucāvuṅ kiḷaviyai: maiyaṟu ciṟappiṉ uyarntōr pāṅkiṉ aṟitalum urittē = kuṟṟamaṟṟa ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya antaṇaṉ mutaliyōriṭattē kūṟi atuvum muṟaimaiyeṉṟu avarkūṟa aṟitalum urittu  e-ṟu. eṉṟatu, “mikkō ṉāyiṉuṅ kaṭivarai yiṉṟē” eṉa muṟkūṟiṉamaiyiṟ talaivaṉ taṉkulattiṉ uyarntamai aṟintaviṭattu iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭutal muṟaiyaṉṟeṉṟu aiyuṟṟa ceviliyum naṟṟāyum uyarntōraik kēṭṭu ituvuṅ kūṭumuṟaimai eṉṟuṇarvar eṉpatām. ilakkaṇamuṇmaiyiṉ ilakkiyamum akkālattu uḷaveṉṟuṇarka. (26)
118. taṉṉuṟu vēṭkai kiḻavaṉmuṟ kiḷatta
leṇṇuṅ kālaik kiḻattik killaip
piṟanīr mākka ḷiṉṟiya vāyiṭaip
peynnīr pōlu muṇarviṟ ṟeṉpa.
itu talaivikku uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) piṟanīr mākkaḷiṉ = vēṟuvēṟākat tammiṟ tāṅ kātal ceytu oḻukum aṟivillātāraippōla: kiḻavaṉ aṟiyat taṉṉuṟu vēṭkai muṟkiḷattal kiḻattikku illai = talaivaṉ aṟiyumpaṭiyākat taṉakkuṟṟa vēṭkaiyai avaṉmuṉṉark kūṟutal talaivikku illai: āyiṭai = aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟṟillātaviṭattu: eṇṇuṅ kālaiyum: avaḷ vēṭkaiyai avaṉ ārāyuṅkālaiyum peynīr pōlum uṇarviṟṟu eṉpa = avvēṭkai putukkalattup peyta nīr puṟattup pocintu kāṭṭumāṟupōlum uṇarviṉaiyuṭaitteṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar  e-ṟu. kiḻattikkillaiyeṉavē tōḻikkut talaivi taṉatu vēṭkaiyai etirniṉṟu kūṟutaluḷateṉpatu peṟṟām. talaivikkuk kuṟippāṉaṉṟit talaivaṉmuṉṉiṉṟu kūṟum vēṭkaikkūṟṟiṉmai muṟkūṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷuṭ kāṇka. eṉavē tōḻi muṉṉart talaivikku vēṭkaikkūṟṟu nikaḻtal peṟṟām. um. “cēṇōṉ māṭṭiya naṟumpukai” “īyaṟ puṟṟat tīrmpuṟat tiṟutta” eṉpaṉavum “ivaḷē niṉcoṟ koṇṭa” eṉṟāṟpōl varuvaṉavum muṉṉark kāṭṭiṉām. “kaṭumpuṉaṉ malinta kāvirip pōtan tōḷē” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa talaivivēṭkaiyait talaivaṉ kuṟippāṉ uṇarntaṉa. (27)
119. kāmak kūṭṭan taṉimaiyiṟ politaliṟ
ṟāmē tūtuva rātalu murittē.
itu eytātatu eytuvittatu pāṅkaṉun tōḻiyum nimittamākavaṉṟit tāmē tūtākum iṭamum uṇṭeṉṟaliṉ. (i-ḷ.) kāmakkūṭṭan taṉimaiyiṟ politalil = iyaṟkaip puṇarcciyum iṭantalaippāṭuṅ kūṭṭuvāraiyiṉṟit taṉimaiyāṟ polivupeṟutaliṉ: tāmē tūtuvar ākalum urittē = oruvarukku oruvar tūtuvarāki oruvar oruvaraik kūṭutalum āṇṭurittu  e-ṟu. atu meyppāṭṭiṉuṭ pukumukampuritaṉmutaliya paṉṉiraṇṭāṉum aṟika. itaṉpayaṉ ikkūṭṭattiṉpiṉṉar varaitalum uṇṭeṉpatām. (28)
120. avaṉvaram piṟatta laṟantaṉak kiṉmaiyiṟ
kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviya tākun
tāṉcelaṟ kuriyavaḻi yāka lāṉa.
itu muṉṉark kaḷaṉum poḻutum eṉṟavaṟṟuṭ kaḷaṅkūṟutaṟkum uriyāḷ talaiviyeṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) avaṉvarampu iṟattal = talaivaṉ kūṟiya kūṟṟiṉ ellaiyaik kaṭattal: taṉakku aṟamiṉmaiyiṉ = talaivikku uritteṉak kūṟiya tarumanūliṉmaiyiṉ: kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatu ākum = talaimakaṉai iṉṉaviṭattu varukaveṉṟu ōriṭattait tāṉ karutik kūṟuṅkūṟṟu avaṉkuṟippuvaḻi oḻukun talaiviyatām: tāṉ celaṟku uriyavaḻi ākalāṉ = tāṉ ceṉṟu kūṭutaṟkuriya iṭan tāṉē uṇarvaṉa ātalāṉ.  e-ṟu. cuṭṭukkiḷavi eṉṟataṉ karuttut talaivaṉ iruvakaikkuṟiyum vēṇṭiyavaḻi avaṉai maṟātu tāṉ aṟintaviṭattiṉaik kūṟṟāṉaṉṟik kuṟippāṉātal ciṟaippuṟattāṉātal tōḻiyāṉātal uṇarttumeṉpatām. talaivaṉ kaḷañcuṭṭumāyiṉ yāṇṭāṉum eppoḻutāṉum akkaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻntu piṟarkkum pulaṉāyk kuṭippiṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut takātām. “viriyiṇar vēṅkai” eṉṉum akappāṭṭut talaivi kaḷañcuṭṭiyatu. “maṟanticiṉ yāṉē” eṉṟaliṉ itu kuṟippāṉ uṇarttiṟṟu. piṟavum vantuḻik kāṇka. (29)
121. tōḻiyiṉ muṭiyu miṭaṉumā ruṇṭē.
itu tōḻikkuṅ kaḷañcuṭṭuk kiḷavi uritteṉṟu eytātatu eytuvittatu. (i-ḷ.) kaḷañcuṭṭukkiḷavi talaivikuṟippāṟ tōḻi kūṟutalaṉṟit tāṉēyuṅ kūṟappeṟum orōvaḻi  e-ṟu. tōḻi kuṟitta iṭamun talaivi tāṉcēṟaṟkuriya iṭamāmeṉpatu karuttu. um. “cevvi ñāḻaṟ karuṅkōṭ ṭañciṉait – taṉippārp puḷḷiya taṇpaṟai nārai – maṇikkēḻ neytaṉ mākkaḻi nivappa – viṉippulam piṉṟē kāṉalu naḷikaṭaṟ – ṟiraiccura muḻanta tiṇṭimil viḷakkiṟ – paṉmīṉ kuṭṭa meṉṉaiyark kāṭṭiya – ventaiyuñ cellumā riravē yanti – laṇaṅkuṭaip paṉittuṟai kaitoḻu tētti – yāyu māyamō ṭayaru nīyun – tēmpā yōti tirunuta ṉīvik – kōṅku mukaittaṉṉa kuvimulai yākat – tiṉṟuyi lamarntaṉai yāyiṉ vaṇṭupaṭa – virinta cerunti veṇmaṇaṉ muṭukkarp – pūvēy puṉṉaiyan taṇpoḻil – vāvē teyya maṇantaṉai celaṟkē.” eṉat tōḻi kaḷañcuṭṭiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka. (30)
122. munnā ḷallatu tuṇaiyiṉṟu kaḻiyā
tannā ḷakattu matuvarai viṉṟē.
ituvum atikārattāṟ talaivikkeytiyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) munnāḷ allatu tuṇaiyiṉṟu kaḻiyātu = pūppeytiya mūṉṟunāḷum allatu kūṭṭamiṉṟi ikkaḷavoḻukkaṅ kaḻiyātu: annāḷakattum atu varaivu iṉṟē – ammūṉṟunāḷiṉ akappaṭṭa nāḷākiya orunāḷiṉum iraṇṭunāḷiṉun tuṇaiyiṉṟik kaḻital nīkkappaṭātu.  e-ṟu. atu eṉṟatu tuṇaiyiṉṟu kaḻitalai, pūppiṉāṟ tuṇaiyiṉṟik kaḻitalporuntiṟṟāyiṉum pūppiṉṟi orunāḷum iraṇṭunāḷun tuṇaiyiṉṟik kaḻital vaḻuvāmeṉakkarutiṉ atuvum puṟattārkkup pulaṉām eṉṟu añcutalāṟ kaḻitaliṉ vaḻuvākāveṉṟaṟku varaiviṉṟeṉṟār. iṉṉōraṉṉa kāraṇan talaivaṟkiṉmaiyiṉ avaṉāṟ tuṇaiyiṉṟik kaḻital iṉṟāyiṟṟu. um. “kukkū veṉṟatu kōḻi yataṉetir – tuṭkeṉ ṟaṟṟeṉ ṟūya neñcan – tōṭōy kātalarp pirikkum – vāḷpōla vaikaṟai vantaṉṟā leṉavē.” itu munnāḷaippirivākiya pūppiṭaippirivu vantuḻit talaivi kūṟiyatu. iṉi allakuṟippaṭṭuḻi orunāḷum iraṇṭunāḷum iṭaiyīṭāmeṉṟuṇarka. pūppunikaḻāta kālattuk kaḷavoḻukkam pūppu nikaḻkālam varaiyappaṭṭateṉṟu uraippārumuḷar. ivviti antaṇarkkuk kūṟiyataṉṟu. aracar vaṇikarātiyavarkkuc ciṟupāṉmaiyākavum ēṉaivēḷāḷar āyar vēṭṭuvar mutaliyōrkkup perumpāṉmaiyākavuṅ kūṟiya vitiyeṉṟuṇarka. eṉṉai? pūppu nikaḻuṅkālattu varaiyātu kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttiṉārkku “antarat teḻutiya veḻuttiṉ māṉa – vanta kuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa voḻukalum” eṉpataṉāṟ pirāyccittam vitipparātaliṉ. itaṉāṉē antaṇarmakaḷirkkum pūppeytiyakkāl aṟattoṭu niṉṟum varaital peṟutum. (31)
123. paṉṉūṟu vakaiyiṉun taṉvayiṉ varūu
naṉṉaya maruṅki ṉāṭṭam vēṇṭaliṟ
ṟuṇaiccuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviya tākun
tuṇaiyōr karuma māka lāṉa.
itu talaivikku uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) taṉvayiṉ varūum naṉṉaya maruṅkiṉ = talaiviyiṭattē tōḻikkuñ cevilikkum varukiṉṟa aṉpumikutikkaṇṇē: pala nūṟu vakaiyiṉum nāṭṭam vēṇṭalil – pala nūṟākiya pakutiyāṉum ākkamuṅ kēṭum ārāytalai avar virumputalālē = tuṇaiccuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatākum = ivaḷ ōr tuṇaiyuṭaiyaḷeṉa avar cuṭṭutaliṭattuk kiḷakkuṅ kiḷavi talaiviyatām: tuṇaiyōr karumam ākalāṉa = akkiḷavi attōḻiyāṉuñ ceviliyāṉum muṭiyuṅkāriyam ātalāṉ  e-ṟu. eṉṟatu, tōḻi palavēṟu kavarporuṇāṭṭam uṟṟavaḻiyuñ cevili kaḷavu alarātal mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ nāṭṭamuṟṟavaḻiyun talaivi aṟattoṭu niṟkumeṉṟu aṟattoṭunilaikku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. tōḻikkut talaivi aṟattoṭuniṟpa, avaḷ cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟpa, avaḷ naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭuniṟkumeṉṟu uṇarka. itaṉāṉē pāṅkaṟku uṟṟaturaittapiṉṉart talaivaṉ uraiyāmaiyum peṟṟām. “puṉaiyiḻai nōkkiyum” eṉṉum marutakkaliyuḷ “viṉavutiyāyiṉ” eṉa nāṭṭam nikaḻntavāṟum ataṉcuritakattuk kūṭṭamuṇmai kūṟutaliṟ tuṇaiccuṭṭuk kiḷavi kiḻaviyatāyavāṟuṅ kāṇka. “koṭiyavuṅ kōṭṭavum” eṉpataṉ curitakattuc cevilikku uraiyāyeṉak kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka. (32)
124. āyperuñ ciṟappi ṉarumaṟai kiḷattaliṟ
ṟāyeṉap paṭuvōḷ cevili yākum.
itu muṟkūṟiya ceviliciṟappuk kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) āy peruñ ciṟappiṉ = tāyattāyk koṇṭu uyir oṉṟāy varukiṉṟāḷeṉṟu ārāyntu tuṇiyappaṭṭa peruñciṟappuk kāraṇamāka. arumaṟaikiḷattaliṉ = kūṟutaṟkariya maṟaipporuḷellāṅ kuṟippāṉaṉṟik kūṟṟāṟ kūṟattakkāḷātaliṉ: tāyeṉappaṭuvōḷ cevili ākum = tāyeṉṟu ciṟappittuc collappaṭuvāḷ ceviliyēyām.  e-ṟu. eṉavē, īṉṟatāyiṉuṅ kaḷaviṉkaṭ ciṟantāḷ ivaḷeṉṟār. kaṟpiṟku iruvarum oppārāyiṟṟu. cevili ciṟantamai cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuṭ palaruṅ kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka. (33)
125. tōḻi tāṉē cevili makaḷē.
itu tōḻiyatu ciṟappuṇarttukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) tōḻi tāṉē = tōḻiyar palaruḷḷum oruttiyeṉap pirikkappaṭuvāḷ. cevili makaḷē = muṟkūṟiya ceviliyuṭaiyamakaḷ.  e-ṟu. itaṟkum arumaṟaikiḷattal atikārattāṟ koḷka. tāyattāyk koṇṭuvarukiṉṟamaiyiṉ uḻuvalaṉpu pōlvatōr aṉpu uṭaiyar iruvarumeṉṟu koḷka. itaṉāṉē kaḷaviṟkut tōḻiyē ciṟantāḷāyiṟṟu; atu cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuṭ kāṇka. (34)
126. cūḻtalu mucāttuṇai nilaimaiyiṟ polimē.
ituvun tōḻiciṟappiṉaiyē kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) ucāttuṇai nilaimaiyiṉ = talaimakaṉun talaimakaḷum ucāvutaṟkut tuṇaimaicāṉṟa nilaimaiyiṉālē cūḻtalum polimē = puṇarcciyuṇmaiyai ēḻuvakaiyāṉuñ cūḻtaṟkaṇṇum polivupeṟum  e-ṟu. eṉavē, immūṉṟu nilaikkun tōḻi uriyaḷ eṉṟār. utāraṇam muṟkāṭṭiyavaṟṟuṭkāṇka. (35)
127. kuṟaiyuṟa vuṇartaṉ muṉṉuṟa vuṇarta
liruvaru muḷvaḻi yavaṉvara vuṇartaleṉa
matiyuṭam paṭutta lorumū vakaittē.
itu attōḻi cūḻcci ittuṇaippakutittu eṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) kuṟaiyuṟa uṇartal = talaivaṉ tōḻiyai irantu kuṟaiyuṟṟavaḻi uṇartal: muṉ uṟa uṇartal = muṉṉam mikavuṇartal. iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉ vara uṇartal = talaiviyun tōḻiyum oruṅkiruntavaḻit talaivaṉvarutalāṟ talaivaṉ kuṟippun talaivikuṟippuṅ kaṇṭuṇartal: eṉa matiyuṭampaṭuttal oru mūvakaittē = eṉṟu iruvarkaruttiṉaiyun taṉ karuttiṉōṭu oṉṟupaṭuttuṇartal orumūṉṟu kūṟṟiṉaiyuṭaittu.  e-ṟu. eṇṇutal eṇṇeṉṟāṟpōla muṉṉutal muṉṉeṉa niṉṟatu. uyirkalantoṉṟaliṟ kuṟippiṉṟiyum pākamuṇarvāḷ kuṟippuppeṟṟuḻi mikavuṇarum eṉṟukoḷka. itu mūvarmatiyiṉaiyum oṉṟupaṭuttutaliṉ matiyuṭampaṭuttaleṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu. immūṉṟuṅ kūṭiya piṉṉarallatu matiyuṭampaṭuttaliṉṟeṉṟaṟku mūvakaitteṉṟu orumaiyāṟ kūṟiṉār. muṉṉuṟaveṉṟataṉai muntuṟaveṉṟālōveṉiṉ kuṟaiyuṟutalāṉ uṇartal avaṉvarutalāṉ uṇartaleṉṟu iraṇṭaṟkuṅ kāraṇaṅ kūṟutaliṉ itaṟkuṅ kuṟippumikutalāṉ uṇartaleṉak kāraṇaṅ koṭuttalvēṇṭumeṉṟuṇarka. “nāṟṟamun tōṟṟamum” eṉpataṉuṭ kūṟiyavāṟaṉṟi muṉṉuṟavai iṭaivaittār, avviraṇṭiṉāṉ uṇaruṅkālum ikkuṟippāṉ uṇaravēṇṭumeṉṟaṟku. immūṉṟum muṟkūṟiṟṟēṉum orovoṉṟāṟ kūṭṭamuṇariṟ talaiviyai naṉkumatittilaḷāvaḷeṉṟaṟku immūṉṟum vēṇṭumeṉṟu īṇṭukkūṟiṉār. um. “kōṉē relvaḷai teḷirppa niṉṟōḻi” itu kūṭṭamuṇarātāḷpōla nāṇiṟku māṟākāmaṟ kūṟaliṉ muṉṉuṟavuṇartal. “niṉṉiṉ viṭāniḻaṟpōṟ ṟiritarvā – yeṉṉī peṟāta titeṉ” itu kuṟaiyuṟavuṇartal. “ēṉal kāva livaḷu mallaḷ” eṉpatu avaṉvaravuṇartal. (36)
128. aṉṉa vakaiyā ṉuṇarntapi ṉallatu
piṉṉilai muyaṟci peṟāṉeṉa moḻipa.
itu matiyuṭampaṭṭapiṉṉallatu talaivaṉ kūṟṟunikaḻttap peṟāṉeṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) aṉṉavakaiyāṉ uṇarntapiṉ allatu = ammūvakaiyāṉun tōḻi matiyuṭampaṭuttapiṉṉallatu: piṉṉilai = ivaṉ ōr kuṟaiyuṭaiyaṉeṉṟu tōḻi uyttuṇara niṟkumiṭattu: muyaṟcipeṟāṉ eṉa moḻipa = kūṟṟāṉ akkuṟaimuṭittal vēṇṭumeṉṟu muṭukkutal peṟāṉeṉṟu kūṟupa āciriyar.  e-ṟu. tōḻi taṉṉai vaḻippaṭṭavāṟu kaṇṭu matiyuṭampaṭṭavāṟuṇarntu kūṟṟāṉ uṇarttum. atu “neruṉalu muṉṉāḷ” eṉpataṉuḷ “ārañar varuttaṅ kaḷaiyāyō” eṉṟavāṟu kāṇka. (37) 
129. muyaṟcik kālat tataṟpaṭa nāṭip
puṇartta lāṟṟalu mavaḷvayi ṉāṉa.
itu talaivaṉmuyaṟci kūṟiya muṟaiyē tōḻimuyaṟci piṟakkumiṭaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) muyaṟcikkālattu = talaivaṉ aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭutaṟku muyaṟci nikaḻttuṅkālattē: nāṭi ataṟpaṭap puṇarttalum = talaivi kūṭutaṟku muyaluṅ karuttiṉai ārāyntu akkūṭṭattiṭattē uḷḷampaṭumpaṭi kūṭṭutalum, avaḷ vayiṉ āṉa āṟṟal = tōḻiyiṭattu uṇṭāṉa kaṭaippiṭi. e-ṟu. āṟṟal oṉṟaṉai muṭivu pōkkal. ummai, eccavummai. matiyuṭampaṭuttalēyaṉṟik kūṭṭavum peṟumeṉka.(38)
130. kuṟiyeṉap paṭuva tiraviṉum pakaliṉu
maṟiyat tōṉṟu māṟṟa teṉpa.
aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṭṭukiṉṟavaṭkuk kūṭutaṟkuriya kālamum iṭaṉuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) kuṟiyeṉap paṭuvatu = kuṟiyeṉṟu collappaṭuvatu: iraviṉum pakaliṉum = iraviṉkaṇṇum pakaliṉkaṇṇum: aṟiyat tōṉṟum āṟṟatu eṉpa = talaivaṉun talaiviyun tāṉum aṟiyumpaṭi tōṉṟum neṟiyaiyuṭaiya iṭam. e-ṟu. neṟiyeṉṟār avaṉ varutaṟkuriya neṟi iṭameṉṟaṟku. atuveṉṟu orumaiyāṟ kūṟiṉār, talaippeyvatōriṭameṉṉum potumaipaṟṟi. iravu kaḷaviṟkuc ciṟattaliṉ muṟkūṟiṉār. aṟiyat tōṉṟumeṉṟataṉāṟ ceṉṟukāṭṭal vēṇṭā niṉṟu kāṭṭal vēṇṭumeṉak koḷka. (39)
131. iravuk kuṟiyē yillakat tuḷḷu
maṉaiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkumvaḻi yatuvē
maṉaiyakam pukāak kālai yāṉa.
itu niṟuttamuṟaiyāṉē iravukkuṟiyiṭam uṇarttukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) akamaṉaip pukāk kālai āṉa iravukkuṟiyē = uṇmaṉaiyiṟ ceṉṟukūṭutaṟku urittallāta muṟkālattu uṇṭāṉa iravukkuṟiyē: ēkāram, pirinilai. illakattuḷḷum maṉaiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyatuvē = ilvaraippiṉuḷḷatākiyum maṉaiyōr kūṟiya kiḷavikēṭkum piṟamaṉaiyiṭattatām.  e-ṟu. allakuṟippaṭṭataṉai oruvāṟṟāṉ uṇarttiyakālattu avaṉ atukēṭṭu āṟṟuvaṉeṉpatu karuti maṉaiyōrkiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyateṉṟār. ēkāram īṟṟacai. eṉṟatu, iravukkuṟi. ammuyaṟcikkālattu accanikaḻtaliṉ akamaṉaikkum puṟamatiṟkum naṭuvē puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉṟatām. akamaṉaiyiṟ pukākkālaiyeṉavē iravukkuṟi aṅṅaṉañ cilanāḷ nikaḻntapiṉṉar accamiṉṟi uṇmaṉaiyiṟ ceṉṟu kūṭavumpeṟumeṉpatuṅ kūṟiyatām. um. “añcilam poṭukki yañciṉaḷ vantu – tuñcūr yāmattu muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ” eṉavum, “miṭaiyūr piḻiyak kaṇṭaṉe ṉivaḷeṉa – valaiyal vāḻiveṇ ṭaṉṉai” eṉavum “aṭṭi lōlai toṭṭaṉai niṉmē” eṉavum varuvaṉa piṟavum maṉaiyōr kiḷavi kēṭkum vaḻiyatu. “uḻaimāṉ ṟuppi ṉōṅkutiṉaip perumpuṉattuk – kaḻutiṟ kāṉavaṉ piḻimakiḻntu vatinteṉa – vuraitta canti ṉūra viruṅkatup – paituvā lacaivaḷi māṟṟak kaippeyarā – voliyal vārmayi ruḷariṉaḷ koṭicci – peruvarai maruṅkiṟ kuṟiñci pāṭaka – kuraluṅ koḷḷātu nilaiyiṉum peyarātu – paṭāam paiṅkaṇ pāṭupeṟ ṟoyyeṉa – maṟampukaṉ maḻakaḷi ṟuṟaṅku nāṭa – ṉāra mārpi ṉaṇimiñi ṟārppat – tāraṉ kaṇṇiya ṉeḵkuṭai valattaṉ – kāvala raṟita lōmpip paiyeṉa – viḻāak katava macaiyiṉaṉ pukuntu – tuyaṅkupaṭa rakala muyaṅkit tōṇmaṇan – tiṉco laḷaiip peyarntaṉaṉ ṟōḻi – yiṉṟevaṉ kollō kaṇṭiku maṟṟava – ṉalkā maiyi ṉampa lāki – yoruṅkuvan tulakkum paṇpi – ṉiruñcū ḻōti yoṇṇutaṟ pacappē.” itu maṉaiyakam pukkatu. talaivi puṟattup pōkiṉṟāḷeṉac cevilikku ōr aiyam nikaḻntavaḻip piṉṉar maṉaiyakattup puṇarcci nikaḻumeṉṟuṇarka. (40)
132. pakaṟpuṇar kaḷaṉē puṟaṉeṉa moḻipa
vavaḷaṟi vuṇara varuvaḻi yāṉa.
itu muṟaiyāṉē pakaṟkuṟi uṇarttukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) avaḷ aṟivu uṇara varuvaḻi āṉa pakaṟ puṇar kaḷaṉē = kaḷañcuṭṭiya talaivi aṟintaviṭan talaivaṉ uṇarumpaṭiyāka varuvatōriṭattu uṇṭāṉa pakaṟpuṇaruṅ kuṟiyiṭattai: puṟaṉ eṉa moḻipa = matiṟpuṟattēyeṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar. e-ṟu. aṟivu, ākupeyar. um. “puṉṉaiyaṅ kāṉaṟ puṇarkuṟi vāytta – miṉṉē rōtiyeṉ ṟōḻikku” eṉavum, “pūvē puṉṉaiyan taṇpoḻil – vāvē teyya maṇantaṉai celaṟkē” eṉavum varuvaṉa piṟavuṅ koḷka. (41)
133. allakuṟip paṭutalu mavaḷvayi ṉurittē
yavaṉkuṟi mayaṅkiya vamaivoṭu variṉē.
itu tōḻi alla kuṟippaṭumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. iruvakaik kuṟi piḻaippākiyaviṭattum eṉpuḻit talaivi allakuṟippaṭutal kūṟiṟṟu. (i-ḷ.) avaṉkuṟi = talaivaṉ taṉvaravu aṟivikkuṅ karuvikaḷ: mayaṅkiya amaivoṭu variṉ = avaṉ ceyaṟkaiyāṉaṉṟi iyaṟkaivakaiyāṉē nikaḻntu tōḻi mayaṅkiya amaitiyōṭē varumāyiṉ: allakuṟip paṭutalum = kuṟiyiṭattuk kūṭṭuṅkāl avvallavākiya kuṟiyilē mayaṅkutalum: avaḷvayiṉ urittu = attōḻiyiṭattu urittu. e-ṟu. veṟittal veṟiyāyiṉāṟpōlak kuṟittal kuṟiyāyiṟṟu. akkaruvi puṉalolippaṭuttal mutaliyaṉa. um. “koṭumuṇ maṭaṟṟāḻaik kūṉpuṟa vāṉpū – viṭaiyu ḷiḻutoppat tōṉṟip – puṭaiyelān – teyvaṅ kamaḻun teḷikaṭaṟ ṟaṇcērppaṉ – ceytāṉ ṟeḷiyāk kuṟi.” itu allakuṟippaṭṭamai ciṟaippuṟamākak kūṟiyatu. “eṟicuṟā nīḷkaṭa lōta mulāva – neṟiyiṟāk koṭku nimirkaṭaṟṟaṇ cērppa – ṉaṟivuṟā viṉco laṇiyiḻaiyāy niṉṉiṟ – ceṟivaṟā ceyta kuṟi.” itu avaṉ mēṟkuṟiceykiṉṟamai talaivikkuk kūṟiyatu. “iṭumaṇa lekka rakaṉkāṉaṟ cērppaṉ – kaṭumāṉ maṇiyarava meṉṟu – koṭuṅkuḻai – puḷḷaravaṅ kēṭṭup peyarntāḷ ciṟukuṭiya – ruḷḷarava nāṇuva reṉṟu.” itu ivaṉ iṉi āṇṭuvariṟ cuṟṟattār aṟivareṉṟatu. “vīḻpeyaṟ kaṅkuṉiṉ viḷiyōrtta voṭukkattāl – vāḻunāḷ ciṟantavaḷ varuntutōṭ ṭavaṟuṇṭō – tāḻceṟi kaṭuṅkāppiṟ ṟāymuṉṉar niṉcārā – lūḻuṟu kōṭalpō loḷivaḷai yukupavāl” itu talaivaṟkup piṟṟaiñāṉṟu kūṟiyatu. “aṉṉai vāḻiyō vaṉṉainam paṭappai – miṉṉeṉa nuṭaṅkiṭaik kiṉṉiḻa lākiya – puṉṉai meṉkāy pōkuciṉai yiriya – vāṭuvaḷi tūkkiya vacaiviṟ kollō – teṇṇīrp poykaiyuḷ vīḻnteṉa – veṇṇiṉai yuraimō vuṇarkuval yāṉē.” itu tōḻi tāykkuk kūṟuvāḷāy allakuṟi aṟivittatu. “maṇiniṟa neyta liruṅkaḻic cērppa – ṉaṇinala muṇṭakaṉṟā ṉeṉṟuko lempōṟ – ṟiṇimaṇa lekkarmē lōtam peyarat – tuṇimunnīr tuñcā tatu.” itu tōḻi illuḷiruntu ciṟaippuṟamākak kūṟiyatu. tiṅkaḷmēl vaittuk kūṟuvaṉavum ōtattiṉmēlvaittuk kūṟuvaṉavum piṟavuṅ koḷka. “aravaḷai meṉṟō ḷaṉuṅkat tuṟantu – karavala meṉṟāraik kaṇṭa tilaiyā – liravelā niṉṟāyā līrṅkatirt tiṅkāḷ.” “puṉṉai naṉaippiṉum pūñciṉai tōyiṉum – piṉṉiruṅ kūntaleṉ ṟōḻi naṭaiyokku – maṉṉai naṉaiyāti vāḻi kaṭalōtam” eṉavaruvaṉa piṟavuṅkoḷka. paṭutal etirppaṭāmaiyai uṇarttiṟṟu. āṇṭut taṉmēṟ tavaṟēṟṟātu talaivaṉ poḻutaṟintu vārāmaiyiṉ mayaṅkiṟṟeṉṟu amaivutōṉṟaliṉ amaiveṉṟār. atu “tāṉ kuṟivārāt tappaṟkut – tāmpacan taṉaveṉ ṟaṭameṉ ṟōḷē” eṉṟāṟ pōla varum. itaṉpayaṉ talaivi tuṉpan taṉatākat tuṉpuṟutalāyiṟṟu. (42)
134. āṅkāṅ koḻuku moḻukkamu muṇṭē
yōṅkiya ciṟappi ṉoruciṟai yāṉa.
itu talaivaṉum allakuṟiyāl varuntuvaṉeṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) ōṅkiya ciṟappiṉ = taṉatu mikka talaimaippāṭṭiṉālē poḻutaṟintu vārāmaiyiṉ: oruciṟai āṉa āṅku = tāṉ kuṟi ceyvatōriṭattē taṉṉāṉaṉṟi iyaṟkaiyāṉ uṇṭāṉa avvallakuṟiyiṭattē: āṅku oḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭu = talaiviyun tōḻiyun tuṉpuṟumāṟu pōlat talaivaṉun tuṉpuṟṟu oḻukum oḻukkamum uṇṭu. e-ṟu. muṉṉarniṉṟa āṅku muṉṉiṟcūttirattu allakuṟiyaic cuṭṭiṟṟu; piṉṉarniṉṟa āṅku uvamavurupu. um. “tāvi ṉaṉpoṉ ṟaiya pāvai – viṇṭava ḻiḷaveyiṟ koṇṭuniṉ ṟaṉṉa – mikukavi ṉeytiya tokukura laimpāṟ – kiḷaiyari nāṇaṟ kiḻaṅkumaṇaṟ kīṉṟa – muḷaiyō raṉṉa muḷḷeyiṟṟut tuvarvāy – nayavaṉ ṟaivaruñ cevvaḻi nalyā – ḻicaiyōrt taṉṉa viṉṟīṅ kiḷavi – yaṇaṅkucā larivaiyai nacaiip peruṅkaḷiṟ – ṟiṉampaṭi nīriṟ kalaṅkiya poḻutiṟ – peṟalaruṅ kuraiya ḷeṉṉāy vaikalu – miṉṉā varuñcura nīnti nīyē – yeṉṉai yiṉṉaṟ paṭuttaṉai miṉṉuvacip – puravukkāl kaṭuppa maṟali maintuṟṟu – viravumoḻit takaṭṭūr vēṇṭuvayiṟ koḷīip – paṭainilā vilaṅkuṅ kaṭaṉamaru ṭāṉai – maṭṭaviḻ teriyaṉ maṟappōrk kuṭṭuvaṉ – porumuraṇ peṟātu vilaṅkic ciṉañciṟantu – ceruccey muṉpoṭu munnīr muṟṟi – yoruṅkutiraip pauva nīṅka vōṭṭi  nīrmā ṇeḵka niṟattucceṉ ṟaḻuntat – tāmata ṉaḻiyarō neñcē yāṉā – teḷiya ḷallōṭku varunti – viḷiyā vevvan talaittan tōyē.” “vaṭamalai micaiyōṉ kaṇṇil muṭavaṉ – ṟeṉṟicai yellai viṇpuku potiyiṟ – cūruṭai neṭuñcuṉai nīrvēṭ ṭāṅku – varuntiṉai vāḻiye ṉuḷḷañ cāraṟ – porutu puṟaṅkaṇṭa pūnuta loruttal – cilampiḻi poḻuti ṉattam peyariya – valliya maṭukkat toṭuṅku nalvaraik – kallaka veṟpaṉ maṭamakaṇ – melliyal vaṉamulait tuyiluṟ ṟōyē.” ivai alla kuṟippaṭṭu nīṅkukiṉṟāṉ neñciṟkuk kūṟiyaṉa. (43)
135. maṟainta voḻukkat tōraiyu nāḷun
tuṟanta voḻukkaṅ kiḻavōṟ killai.
itu talaivaṟku uriyatōr ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) ōraiyum nāḷun tuṟanta oḻukkam = tīya irāciyiṉkaṇṇun tīyanāḷiṉkaṇṇuṅ kūṭṭattait tuṟanta oḻukkam: kiḻavōṟku maṟainta oḻukkattu illai = talaivaṟkuk kaḷavoḻukkattiṉkaṇ illai. eṉavē kaṟpiṉkaṇuḷa: e-ṟu. oḻukkamāvatu cīlamātaliṟ cīlaṅ kāraṇattāṟ tuṟappatu tītākiya irāciyum nāḷumeṉpatu peṟṟām. nāḷāvatu avvirāci maṇṭilamuḻuvatum. kiḻatti tuṟanta oḻukkam munnāḷallateṉa muṟkūṟiṟṟu. itaṉāṉ aṉpu mikuti kūṟiṉār. itaṟkup pirāyacittam antaṇar mutaliya mūvarkkum uṇmai “vantakuṟṟam vaḻikeṭa voḻukalum” eṉak kaṟpiyaliṟ kūṟupa. (44)
136. āṟiṉa tarumaiyu maḻivu maccamu
mūṟu muḷappaṭa vataṉō raṉṉa.
ituvun talaivaṟku illaṉa kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) uḷappaṭa = nilavum iruḷum pakaivarum pōlvaṉapaṟṟic celavaḻuṅkutal uḷappaṭa: āṟiṉatu arumaiyum = neṟiyiṉatu arumai niṉaintu kūṭṭanikaḻntavaḻik kūṟutalum: aḻivum = kuṟainta maṉattaṉātalum: accamum = pāmpum vilaṅkum pōlvaṉa naliyumeṉṟu añcutalum: ūṟum = akkarumattiṟku iṭaiyūṟu uḷavāṅkoleṉṟu aḻuṅkutalum: ataṉōr aṉṉa = kiḻavaṟku illai. e-ṟu. kiḻavaṟkillaiyeṉavē kiḻattikkun tōḻikkum uḷavāyiṟṟu. avai muṟkāṭṭiyavaṟṟuṭ kāṇka (45)
137. tantaiyun taṉṉaiyu muṉṉatti ṉuṇarpa.
itu tantaiyun taṉṉaiyuṅ kaḷavoḻukkam uṇarumāṟu kūṟakiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) tantaiyun taṉṉaiyum oruvar kūṟak koḷḷātu uyttukkoṇṭuṇarvar. e-ṟu. naṟṟāy aṟattoṭuniṉṟavaḻiyum “iruvarkaṭ kuṟṟamu millaiyā leṉṟu – terumantu cāyttār talai” eṉṟaliṉ muṉṉarnikaḻnta vekuṭcinīṅki uyttukkoṇṭu uṇarntārāyiṟṟu. (46)
138. tāyaṟi vuṟutal ceviliyō ṭokkum.
itu tantaitaṉṉaikku naṟṟāy kaḷavoḻukkam uṇarttumāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) tāy aṟivuṟutal = naṟṟāy kaḷavoḻukkam uṇṭeṉṟu aṟinta aṟivu tantaikkun taṉṉaikkuñ ceṉṟu uṟuntaṉmai: ceviliyōṭu okkum = cevili naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭuniṉṟu uṇarttiya taṉmaiyōṭu okkum. e-ṟu. eṉṟatu, cevili naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭuniṉṟāṟpōla naṟṟāyun tantaikkun taṉṉaikkum aṟattoṭuniṟkum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. atu “eṉavāṅ, kaṟattoṭu niṉṟēṉaik kaṇṭu – tiṟappaṭa veṉṉaiyark kuṟṟurait tāḷyāy” eṉpataṉāṉ uṇarka. iṉi itaṟku naṟṟāyuñ cevili uṇarntāṟpōla uṇarumeṉṟu poruṭkūṟiṟ “tāykkum varaiyār” eṉṉuñ cūttiram vēṇṭāvām. (47)
139. ampalu malaruṅ kaḷavuveḷip paṭuttali
ṉaṅkataṉ mutalvaṉ kiḻava ṉākum.
itu kaḷavu veḷippaṭutaṟku nimittamāvāṉ talaimakaṉeṉkiṟatu. (i-ḷ.) ampalum alaruṅ kaḷavu veḷippaṭuttaliṉ = mukiḻ mukiḻttalum palaraṟiyac col nikaḻttaluṅ kaḷavoḻukkattiṉai veḷippaṭuttalāṉ: aṅkataṉ mutalvaṉ kiḻavaṉ ākum = avviṭattu avveḷippaṭai nikaḻttutaṟku nimittamāyiṉāṉ talaimakaṉām. e-ṟu. talaivaṉai aṟintapiṉ allatu muṟkūṟiya aiyam nikaḻāmaiyiṉ talaivivaruttam nimittamākā, āṇṭu aiyam nikaḻtalaṉṟit tuṇivu tōṉṟāmaiyiṉ. varaivu nīṭṭippōṉun talaivi tamarkkuk kūṟi veḷippaṭuppōṉun talaivaṉē eṉṟuṇarka. atu “nīrolitta liṉṉa pōlā namakkoḻiya vaḻippirin tōrē” eṉavum “neṟiyaṟi ceṟikuṟi puritiri paṟiyā – vaṟivaṉai muntuṟi” eṉṟāṟpōla varuvaṉavum piṟavum veḷippaṭaiyāmāṟṟāṟ kaṇṭuṇarka. (48)
140. veḷippaṭa varaital paṭāmai varaitaleṉ
ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa varaita lāṟē.
itu varaiyum pakuti iṉaitteṉkiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) veḷippaṭa varaital = muṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉē kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉṉar varaintu kōṭal: paṭāmaivaraital = akkaḷavu veḷippaṭuvataṉ muṉṉar varaintukōṭal: eṉṟu ā iraṇṭu eṉpa varaital āṟē = eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa avviraṇṭē eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar varaintukoḷḷum vaḻiyai. e-ṟu. “cēyuyar veṟpaṉum vantaṉaṉ – pūveḻi luṇkaṇum polikamā viṉiyē.” itu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ varaivunikaḻntatu. “kollaip puṉatta vakilcumantu kalpāyntu – vāṉi ṉaruvi tatumpak kaviṉiya – nāṭa ṉayaṉuṭaiya ṉeṉpataṉā ṉīppiṉum – vāṭaṉ maṟantaṉa tōḷ.” itu veḷippaṭāmal varaivu nikaḻntatu. “emmaṉai muntuṟat tarumō – taṉmaṉai yuykkumō yātavaṉ kuṟippē” eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa veḷippaṭuvataṉ muṉṉark koṇṭutalaikkaḻintuḻik koṭuppōriṉṟiyuṅ karaṇam nikaḻntamaiyiṉ atuvum veḷippaṭāmal varaintatām. (49)
141. veḷippaṭai tāṉē kaṟpiṉo ṭoppiṉu
ñāṅkark kiḷanta mūṉṟu poruḷāka
varaiyātu pirital kiḻavōṟ killai.
itu mutaṟkūṟiya varaivu nikaḻttātu piriyum iṭam ituveṉavum piriyalākā iṭam ituveṉavuṅ kūṟukiṉṟatu. (i-ḷ.) veḷippaṭaitāṉē kaṟpiṉoṭu oppiṉum = muṟkūṟiya veḷippaṭai tāṉē kaṟpiṉuṭ talaivi urimai ciṟantāṅku arumaiciṟantu kaṟpōṭottatāyiṉum: ñāṅkark kiḷanta mūṉṟu poruḷāka = muṟkūṟiya ōtal pakai tūteṉṟa mūṉṟum nimittamāka: varaiyātu pirital kiḻavōṟku illai = varaiviṭaivaittup pirital talaimakaṟkillai. e-ṟu. mūṉṟumeṉa muṟṟummaikoṭātu kūṟiṉamaiyiṉ ēṉaippirivukaḷiṉ varaiyātu piriyappeṟum eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. avai varaitaṟkup poruḷvayiṟpiritalum vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻiyuṅ kāvaṟkuppiritalumeṉa mūṉṟumām. u-m. “poṉṉaṭarn taṉṉa voḷḷiṇarc ceruntip – paṉmalar vēynta nalampeṟu kōtai – yaṇimaṇa laṭaikarai yalava ṉāṭṭi – yacaiyuḷḷi vanta vāytoṭik kuṟumaka – ṇalañcāl viḻupporuḷ kalaniṟai koṭuppiṉum – peṟalaruṅ kuraiya ḷāyi ṉaṟanterintu – nāmuṟai tēe marūuppeyarn tavaṉō – ṭirunīrc cērppi ṉuppuṭa ṉuḻutum – perunīrk kuṭṭam puṇaiyoṭu pukkum – paṭuttaṉam paṇintaṉa maṭuttaṉa miruntapiṟ – ṟarukuvaṉ kollō tāṉē viritiraik – kaṇṭiraṇ muttaṅ koṇṭun tuñcāt – tēṉimir taṇkaraip paṭukkuṅ – kāṉalam peruntuṟaip paratava ṉamakkē.” itaṉuḷ īṇṭuḷḷa poruḷkoṭuttāṟ peṟal ariyaḷāyiṟ taṉṉai vaḻippaṭṭāṟ tantai taruvaṉō? atu namakkaritākaliṉ iṉṉum poruḷ nām mikat tēṭivantu varaitumeṉap poruḷvayiṟpiriyak karutiyavāṟu kāṇka. “pūṅkoṭi maruṅkuṟ polampū ṇōyē – vēntuviṉai muṭittu vantaṉar – kāntaṇ melviraṟ kavaiyiṉai niṉaimē.” itu vēntaṟkuṟṟuḻip pirintāṉ varaivumalintamai tōḻi kūṟiyatu. ēṉaiya vantuḻikkāṇka. ōtutaṟku ēvuvār irumutukuravarātaliṉ avar varaiyāmaṟ pirikaveṉṉār. pakaiveṉṟu tiṟaikōṭaṟkup piriyuṅkāl aṉpuṟu kiḻatti tuṉpuṟṟiruppa varaiyātu piritaliṉṟu. itu tūtiṟkum okkum. maṟaiveḷippaṭutal kaṟpeṉṟu ceyyuḷiyaluṭkūṟutaliṉ itaṉai ivvōttiṉ iṟutikkaṇ vaittār. kaṟpiṉoṭoppiṉum piriviṉṟeṉavē kaṟpiṟkāyiṟ pirivu varaiviṉṟāyiṟṟu. (50)
kaḷaviyaṉ muṟṟiṟṟu.

